### [1] [Multibranched parametric resonance and swallowtail catastrophe in electromechanical oscillators with nonlinear friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27242)
*P. Y. Chan,L. Huang,X. Dong,M. I. Dykman,H. B. Chan*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 该研究实验与理论相结合，证明常规受控非线性摩擦（nonlinear friction）可诱导微机械振荡器（micromechanical oscillator）中共存两对不同的周期二态（period-two states），并通过驱动诱导的共振耦合将两个振动量子转移到快速衰减模式来实现摩擦。多稳态的起始由燕尾突变（swallowtail catastrophe）主导，研究定量描绘了相关的分岔结构，拓展了对参量共振（parametric resonance）的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量振荡器（parametric oscillator）作为典型的驱动-耗散系统，通常被认为只有一对稳定的周期二态，该双稳态行为支撑了自旋哈密顿量模拟和超导猫态制备等应用。然而，单个振荡器中是否可以共存多对这样的稳定态，仍然是一个悬而未决的问题（open question），这构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 研究者通过实验与理论相结合的方式进行验证：采用一种规范的方法实现受控非线性摩擦，即利用驱动诱导的共振耦合（drive-induced resonant coupling），将两个振动量子（two vibrational quanta）从振荡模式转移到衰减更快的模式，从而在微机械振荡器中诱导出两种不同周期的二态对的共存。

Result: 实验与理论结果表明，多稳态（multistability）的起始由燕尾突变（swallowtail catastrophe）支配，研究者定量描绘了相关的分岔结构（bifurcation structure），验证了非线性摩擦机制下多对周期二态共存的可能性。

Conclusion: 这些结果拓宽了对参量共振的理解，并确立了微纳机械振荡器（micro- and nano-mechanical oscillators）作为研究突变论（catastrophe theory）和多稳态非平衡动力学（multistable nonequilibrium dynamics）的多功能平台的潜力。

Abstract: Parametric resonance underpins the operation of a wide range of physical systems, from nanomechanical resonators to quantum-information systems and Ising machines. As an archetypal class of driven-dissipative systems, parametric oscillators are generally expected to exhibit a single pair of stable period-two states with opposite phases. This bistable behavior enables both the simulation of spin Hamiltonians and the preparation of superconducting cat states. Whether multiple pairs of such states can coexist in a single oscillator, however, remains an open question. Here, we show experimentally and theoretically that conventional controlled nonlinear friction can induce the coexistence of two distinct pairs of period-two states in a micromechanical oscillator. The friction is implemented via a canonical approach, utilizing a drive-induced resonant coupling that transfers two vibrational quanta from the oscillatory mode to a faster decaying mode. We demonstrate that the onset of multistability is governed by a swallowtail catastrophe and quantitatively map the associated bifurcation structure. Our results broaden the understanding of parametric resonance and establish micro- and nano-mechanical oscillators as a versatile platform for studying catastrophe theory and multistable nonequilibrium dynamics.

</details>

### [2] [Anomalous Microwave Response in YBCO Resonators beyond the Two-Level-System Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27392)
*Kaiwen Zheng,Nathan J. Johnson,Nathan T. Thobaben,Sidharth Duthaluru,Haochen Shen,Denae T. Cherry,David S. Wisbey,Kater W. Murch*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了由YBCO薄膜制备的共面波导（coplanar-waveguide, CPW）谐振器在约70 mK至40 K温度范围内的微波响应。谐振器在70 mK下内部品质因子Qi处于10^4量级，随温度升高至约6 K时达到最大值；低温行为虽表面类似双能级系统（two-level-system, TLS）缺陷，但未呈现饱和且无功率依赖，需引入顺磁响应（如局域磁矩或Andreev束缚态）加以解释，表明其低温响应无法仅用传统TLS框架理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索YBCO超导薄膜谐振器在毫开尔文（millikelvin）温度下的微波性能，检验其低温损耗机制是否与传统超导体中普遍存在的双能级系统缺陷行为一致。

Method: 对YBCO薄膜制成的共面波导谐振器在约70 mK至40 K温度范围内测量其微波响应，包括内部品质因子Qi、谐振频率分数偏移Δfr/fr以及微波功率依赖关系。

Result: 谐振器在70 mK下Qi约为4×10^3至10^4，在约6 K时升至约8×10^3至1.2×10^4的最大值；低温下Qi和Δfr/fr均随温度升高而增大，但两者均不饱和，且损耗无可见微波功率依赖。低温频率上翘可由缺陷引起的局域磁矩或Andreev束缚态产生的附加顺磁响应更好描述，而低温损耗呈近似对数温度依赖，其微观起源尚未明确。

Conclusion: 这些测量确立了图案化YBCO谐振器在毫开尔文温度下的性能，并表明其低温响应无法仅用传统TLS框架理解，需要更复杂的微观机制模型。

Abstract: We report the microwave response of coplanar-waveguide (CPW) resonators fabricated from $\mathrm{YBa_2Cu_3O_{7-δ}}$ (YBCO) thin films over temperatures from approximately $70~\mathrm{mK}$ to $40~\mathrm{K}$. The resonators exhibit internal quality factors $Q_\mathrm{i}$ in the range of $4\times10^3$ to $10^4$ at 70 mK, which increase to a maximum of approximately $8\times10^3$ to $1.2\times10^4$ near $6~\mathrm{K}$. At low temperatures, both $Q_\mathrm{i}$ and the fractional shift of the resonance frequency $Δf_\mathrm{r}/f_\mathrm{r}$ increases with temperature, qualitatively resembling behavior commonly associated with two-level-system (TLS) defects. However, neither response saturates on the temperature scale set by the resonator frequency, and the loss exhibits no observable microwave-power dependence. We show that low-temperature frequency upturn may be better described by an additional paramagnetic response associated with defect-induced local moments or Andreev bound states, while the low-temperature loss follows an approximately logarithmic temperature dependence whose microscopic origin remains unresolved. These measurements establish the millikelvin performance of patterned YBCO resonators and show that their low-temperature response cannot be understood within the conventional TLS framework alone.

</details>


### [3] [Extended s-wave superconductivity in M-point twisted bilayer SnSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27492)
*Lennart Klebl,Ammon Fischer,Salahudin V. Smailagić,Ming-Rui Li,Henning Schlömer,Haoyu Hu,B. Andrei Bernevig,Dante M. Kennes,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过第一性原理瓦尼尔模型（Wannier model）和泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group）模拟，揭示了M-谷莫尔材料中电子有序态与非传统超导电性的涌现机制，发现不同掺杂浓度下分别对应多种配对对称性的弱耦合超导体和扩展s波自旋单态超导体，并将该平台类比为铁基超导体（iron pnictide superconductors）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究M-谷莫尔材料中电子有序态与非传统超导电性的涌现机制，探究莫尔平带中磁有序与超导态的关联，为该类材料作为量子模拟平台提供理论依据。

Method: 基于AB堆垛扭曲SnSe2的第一性原理瓦尼尔模型，采用泛函重整化群模拟处理（栅极屏蔽的）长程库仑相互作用，通过解析费米面不稳定性的动量结构与能量尺度来研究超导配对机制。

Result: 在半填充（ν=3）的莫尔平带之反铁磁条纹态掺杂后，磁有序让位于由谷选择性自旋涨落（valley-selective spin fluctuations）介导的非传统超导；较大空穴掺杂（ν≈1）产生多种配对对称性的弱耦合超导体，而轻微电子和空穴掺杂（ν≈2,4）稳定了受益于离费米能级失谐的虚拟粒子-空穴态散射的自旋单态扩展s波态。

Conclusion: M-谷莫尔材料可作为与铁基超导体具有现象学类比的量子模拟平台，其超导机制由谷选择性自旋涨落介导，为理解非传统超导电性提供了新的实验体系。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of electronic order and unconventional superconductivity in M-valley moiré materials. Starting from a first-principles Wannier model of AB-stacked twisted SnSe2, we tackle the (gate-screened) long-ranged Coulomb interaction with functional renormalization group simulations resolving the momentum structure and energy scales of the leading Fermi surface instabilities. Upon doping an antiferromagnetic stripe state at half-filling ($ν=3$ electrons per moiré unit cell) of the moiré flat bands, magnetic order gives way to unconventional superconductivity mediated by valley-selective spin fluctuations: Large hole doping ($ν\approx1$) leads to weak-coupling superconductors with various pairing symmetries, while slight electron- and hole-doping ($ν\approx2,4$) stabilizes a spin-singlet, extended s-wave state that benefits from scattering between virtual particle and hole states that are detuned from the Fermi level. These findings establish M-point moiré materials as a quantum simulation platform with phenomenological parallels to the class of iron pnictide superconductors.

</details>


### [4] [Single-crystal structural phase diagram of stoichiometric bilayer nickelate La3Ni2O7 under hydrostatic pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27607)
*Misaki Sasaki,Zhehong Liu,Takeshi Hara,Shunsuke Kitou,Markus Kriener,Haruto Yoshimochi,Shion Yamada,Chieko Terakura,Naohisa Hirao,Hirokazu Kadobayashi,Yusuke Wakabayashi,Yoshinori Tokura,Yasujiro Taguchi,Taka-hisa Arima,Yukako Fujishiro*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文通过在准静水条件下使用氦作为传压介质，对化学计量比的双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇单晶建立了确定的压力-温度相图，明确了超导相的结构，解决了此前关于超导相晶格结构的争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在发现La₃Ni₂O₇在压力下出现高温超导后，其超导相晶体结构仍存在争议，阻碍了对该材料微观起源的深入理解。

Method: 使用氦作为压力传导介质，对化学计量比（stoichiometric）La₃Ni₂O₇单晶在准静水压（hydrostatic）条件下进行高压实验，系统测定压力-温度相图并确认超导区域。

Result: 常压下La₃Ni₂O₇采用极性正交Am2m结构，其特征为不等价Ni位点间的电荷序（charge order）和NiO₆八面体倾斜；加压至约10 GPa时，体系从电荷有序的Am2m相直接转变为四方I4/mmm相，该转变与体相超导的出现同时发生。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了La₃Ni₂O₇的本征结构演化路径，为镍酸盐超导的微观理论提供了结构框架。

Abstract: The bilayer nickelate La3Ni2O7 has attracted intense interest following the discovery of high-temperature superconductivity under pressure, representing the first nickelate superconductor realized in bulk form. However, the crystal structure of the superconducting phase remains under active discussion, complicating efforts to establish its microscopic origin. Here we resolve these structural controversies by establishing a definitive pressure-temperature phase diagram, including the superconducting region of stoichiometric La3Ni2O7 single crystals under hydrostatic conditions using helium as the pressure-transmitting medium. At ambient pressure, La3Ni2O7 adopts a polar orthorhombic Am2m structure characterized by charge order between inequivalent Ni sites and NiO6 octahedral tilting. Upon compression, the system undergoes a direct transition from the charge-ordered Am2m phase to the tetragonal I4/mmm phase near 10 GPa, coinciding with the onset of bulk superconductivity. These results establish the intrinsic structural evolution of La3Ni2O7 and provide a structural framework for microscopic theories of nickelate superconductivity.

</details>


### [5] [Cavity Tuning of the CDW--Superconductivity Interplay in a Kagome Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27769)
*Lan-Ting Shi,I-Te Lu,Xin Wang,Dongbin Shin,Lede Xian,Angel Rubio*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究利用量子电动力学密度泛函理论（quantum electrodynamical density functional theory），发现在kagome金属CsV₃Sb₅中，采用面外偏振的单模腔可实现均衡调控电荷密度波（CDW）序与超导电性：腔耦合可选择性软化CDW相关声子并扩展其不稳定性至更高压力，同时在高压力区重构Eliashberg谱权重、增强电子-声子耦合（EPC）并提升对T_c的Allen-Dynes估算值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome金属中竞争性电子序（CDW与超导）的调控受晶格、电子关联与kagome几何效应的复杂交织所限制，需要寻找一种平衡态（equilibrium）途径来重塑这一竞争平衡。

Method: 采用量子电动力学密度泛函理论，对CsV₃Sb₅施加面外（out-of-plane）偏振的单模腔，系统考察腔耦合对CDW相关声子、Eliashberg谱函数、电子-声子耦合强度及Allen-Dynes超导转变温度估算的影响。

Result: 腔耦合选择性软化CDW相关声子，抵消压力诱导的硬化并使CDW不稳定性向更高压力延伸；在CDW被抑制的高压区，腔耦合使Eliashberg谱权重向低频重分布，增强总电子-声子耦合并提高Allen-Dynes估算的T_c。其机制源于面外光子模式引发的电荷密度重分布，修改晶格恢复力并驱动声子与EPC重整化。

Conclusion: 腔量子电动力学（cavity quantum electrodynamics）被确立为一种可行的平衡态调控途径，可用于调谐kagome材料中相互交织的电荷序、晶格动力学与超导电性。

Abstract: Kagome metals host competing electronic orders, including charge-density-wave (CDW) order and superconductivity, shaped by intertwined lattice, electronic-correlation, and kagome-geometric effects. Here, using quantum electrodynamical density functional theory, we identify an equilibrium cavity route for reshaping this balance in the kagome metal CsV$_3$Sb$_5$. An out-of-plane polarized single-mode cavity selectively softens CDW-related phonons, counteracting pressure-induced hardening and extending the CDW instability toward higher pressures. In the high-pressure regime where the CDW instability is otherwise suppressed, cavity coupling redistributes Eliashberg spectral weight toward lower frequencies, enhances the total electron--phonon coupling (EPC), and increases the EPC-based Allen--Dynes estimate of $T_c$. This response originates from a charge-density redistribution induced by the out-of-plane photon mode, which modifies lattice restoring forces and drives the phonon and EPC renormalization. These results establish cavity quantum electrodynamics as a viable equilibrium route for tuning intertwined charge order, lattice dynamics, and superconductivity in kagome materials.

</details>


### [6] [Symmetry, Sound, and the Quest for Chiral Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28365)
*B. J. Ramshaw*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用弹性模量测量来寻找手性拓扑超导体（chiral topological superconductor）的研究进展，指出多组分超导性是手性超导的前提条件，弹性模量实验可提供明确的热力学证据，但迄今为止所有结果仍为阴性；文章还提供了多组分序参量、手性拓扑超导电性及超导序参量与应变耦合的教学性介绍，并讨论了在重费米子、铁基和Kagome超导体中的研究努力及加速搜索的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体拓扑超导体的证实是现代量子材料研究的核心目标，但至今尚无确认实例，主要困难在于大多数拓扑超导电性的实验信号模糊且间接，因此亟需寻找更明确、更直接的实验证据来缩小搜索范围。

Method: 采用综述性方法，系统梳理利用弹性模量测量来探测多组分超导电性的研究路径；同时提供教学性讲解，涵盖多组分序参量、手性拓扑超导电性以及超导序参量与应变（strain）的耦合机制，并总结重费米子、铁基和Kagome超导体中的实验工作。

Result: 弹性模量实验在原理上能够为多组分超导电性提供无歧义的热力学证据，这被认为是二维和准二维材料中手性超导电性的先决条件；然而目前所有相关实验结果仍为阴性，尚未获得正的确认信号。

Conclusion: 尽管弹性模量测量具有潜力，但现有实验仍未成功；文章建议通过加速搜索流程和开发其他实验工具来继续推进对手性拓扑超导体的探索。

Abstract: The confirmation of a bulk, topological superconductor is a central goal of modern quantum materials research. Despite decades of searching, no confirmed examples yet exist. A primary difficulty is that most experimental signatures of topological superconductivity are ambiguous and indirect. I review efforts to narrow the search for a chiral topological superconductor using measurements of elastic moduli. In principle, these experiments can provide unambiguous thermodynamic evidence for multi-component superconductivity---a pre-requisite for chiral superconductivity in 2D and quasi-2D materials. Despite the potential strength of this program, the results are still negative. I provide a pedagogical introduction to multi-component order parameters, chiral topological superconductivity, and how superconducting order parameters couple to strain. I discuss ongoing efforts in heavy fermion, iron pnictide, and Kagome superconductors, and suggest routes for speeding up the search process, as well as other experimental tools that should be developed for the pursuit of this goal.

</details>

### [7] [Coordinate and Momentum Distributions of a Small Composite System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27244)
*Dmitry Naplekov,Yury Bezruk,Vladimir Yanovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一个由可移动外壳和有限数量内部粒子组成的孤立小系统，在总能量和总动量守恒条件下，精确求解了外壳坐标和动量的分布。发现坐标分布由有限分支构成并可能包含平台区，动量分布对应系统自由度间能量的非均匀分配。关键在于坐标分布依赖于内部粒子数量而不随其运动维度变化，动量分布则依赖自由度数量，因此比较两者可推断系统内部结构。此外还发现外壳的混沌运动在无外部介质时仍持续存在（不同于布朗运动），并得到了外壳均方根偏差的普适解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解小尺度孤立系统中边界与内部粒子间的统计力学行为，为通过外壳可观测量的测量推断系统内部结构提供理论依据。

Method: 在总能量和总动量守恒条件下，针对不同类型的内部粒子运动，采用精确解析方法求解可移动外壳的坐标分布和动量分布，并分析其统计特征及其与内部粒子数、运动维数和自由度数的关系。

It is shown that the coordinate distribution depends on the number of internal particles and remains unchanged when the dimensionality of their motion varies, whereas the momentum distribution depends on the number of degrees of freedom. Consequently, comparison of the coordinate and momentum distributions of the shell makes it possible to draw conclusions about the internal structure of the system. The chaotic motion of the shell caused by impacts from the internal particles is analyzed; unlike Brownian motion, it persists even in the absence of an external medium. A universal expression for the root-mean-square deviation of the shell is obtained explicitly, depending on the number of internal particles and their masses.

Abstract: Information engines exploit feedback to extract work from thermal fluctuations, extending the second law of thermodynamics through information-theoretic bounds. While several such bounds have been proposed, their relative performance under realistic conditions---where measurements are noisy and feedback is temporally correlated---remains largely unclear. Here, we experimentally and theoretically investigate this problem in an underdamped feedback-controlled system with Markovian measurements but non-Markovian control sequences. We compare three representative bounds derived from transfer entropy, unavailable information, and Markovian mutual information, and find that none is universally optimal. Instead, measurement noise preferentially affects information measures that rely on detailed trajectory statistics, while leaving quantities based on instantaneous correlations comparatively robust. As a consequence, trajectory-dependent bounds deteriorate rapidly, giving rise to a crossover in which the Markovian mutual-information bound becomes tighter than the unavailable-information bound over a broad range of measurement noise. Our results reveal a general limitation of information-theoretic descriptions that rely on detailed trajectory statistics in realistic settings and provide a unified perspective on information thermodynamics beyond idealised feedback protocols.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit the metal-insulator transition in a one-dimensional lattice subjected to an on-site quasiperiodic potential, modeled using spin-1/2 particles with tunable nearest-neighbor interactions. To characterize the transition, we examine the power-spectrum, defined as the squared modulus of the Fourier transform of consecutive level spacings. In the absence of interactions, the power-spectrum fails to capture clear signatures of the transition, consistently exhibiting brown-noise-like behavior across regimes. In contrast, the interacting case reveals a marked change: the metallic phase displays spectral fluctuations consistent with pink noise, while the insulating phase is characterized by brown noise. These results are supported by analyses of the level spacing distribution and the level number variance. Conclusion: IQSEP提供了一个统一的框架，能够从相干到非相干的输运行为之间进行连续插值，并发展了超越标准涨落流体动力学范围的量子相干涨落标度理论，为理解相互作用扩散系统中的量子输运提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce and solve the Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process (IQSEP), a family of models describing the stochastic quantum hopping of charged particles along the edges of a lattice, with hopping amplitudes that depend on the occupations of neighbouring sites. In the absence of interactions, they reduce to the standard quantum simple symmetric exclusion process, exhibiting coherent diffusive transport. For interactions of order one, they capture incoherent diffusive transport and its fluctuations, characterized by density-dependent diffusivity and mobility, making contact with the macroscopic fluctuation theory. By rescaling the interaction strength appropriately with the lattice mesh, we define a mesoscopic scaling regime that retains a finite coherence length in the continuous thermodynamic limit. This regime interpolates between coherent behavior at small length scales and incoherent behavior at large scales. The resulting scaling theory accounts for fluctuations of quantum coherences in interacting diffusive systems, going beyond the scope of standard fluctuating hydrodynamics.

</details>

该工作为研究非互易耦合系统如何自发产生非平衡稳态提供了最小化的理论框架，有助于揭示此类相互作用在无对称性破坏时的核心作用。

Abstract: We consider the minimal model of a two dimensional Brownian dimer consisting of two overdamped monomers, trapped in an isotropic harmonic potential and mutually coupled by a non-reciprocal harmonic spring that violates Newton's action-reaction principle. The exact steady state probability distribution and current are explicitly calculated for the zero-rest-length limit of the spring, which eventually maps our model to another non-equilibrium phenomenon, called Brownian gyration. For a spring with finite rest length, these quantities are calculated numerically.

Abstract: Heat flow sustained by a temperature difference generates a gradient of matter as observed, for example, in atomistic simulations of hard-core gases.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, wherein a system prepared farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, has been extensively investigated in a wide range of physical systems. In contrast, its role in biologically relevant non-equilibrium processes remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate anomalous relaxation in the six-state chemomechanical network model of the Kinesin molecular motor under both equilibrium and non-equilibrium conditions. We first establish the existence of the Mpemba effect in chemical equilibrium and show that many of its qualitative features can be understood from the underlying free-energy landscape. We then examine the effects of mechanical and chemical driving, showing that breaking detailed balance primarily reshapes the Mpemba phase diagram without qualitatively altering the relaxation phenomenology over the physically relevant parameter regime. Finally, we demonstrate that the relaxation of the motor velocity also mirrors the anomalous relaxation of the underlying stochastic dynamics, thereby identifying an experimentally accessible signature of the Mpemba effect. Our results establish molecular motors as a promising baseline for studying anomalous relaxation in living systems and suggest a broader framework for exploring the Mpemba effect in non-equilibrium biochemical networks.

</details>


### [14] [Three-Dimensional Kardar--Parisi--Zhang Scaling in Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28106)

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出利用三维激子-极化激元晶体（exciton-polariton crystal）作为观察三维Kardar–Parisi–Zhang（KPZ）普适类（universality）的平台。通过从随机驱动-耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程约化得到3+1维KPZ方程，数值模拟在中间渐进区域获得与KPZ基准指数一致的一阶相干性标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ普适类是由微观细节平衡（detailed balance）破缺产生的宏观标度行为的典型范例，但到目前为止，KPZ标度已在二维体系中得到验证，在三维空间中的实现仍是重大挑战。作者希望找到一个可控的系统来展示三维KPZ普适性。

Method: 从三维光子晶体（photonic crystal）下极化激元带中凝聚体所满足的随机驱动-耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发，消除（eliminate）质量较大的密度模和库（reservoir）模，得到凝聚体相位满足的有效3+1维KPZ方程。随后对KPZ方程和完整驱动-耗散极化激元模型分别进行数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟显示存在中间渐进区域（intermediate-asymptotic regime），其中一阶相干性满足−ln|G(0,Δt)|∝|Δt|^{2β}和−ln|G(Δr,0)|∝|Δr|^{2χ}，且指数与3+1维KPZ基准值β=0.1845、χ=0.3135一致。

Conclusion: 三维极化激元晶体为观测高维非平衡普适性提供了一条可控的量子流体路径，有望推广KPZ普适类的实验验证到第三个空间维度。

Abstract: Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universality provides an example of macroscopic scaling generated by microscopic violation of detailed balance. At high order, the method yields an exceptionally accurate estimate of the jamming density while retaining explicit analytical expressions for the full time-dependent evolution.

Abstract: The physics of quantum chaos and diffusive transport is typically studied in settings with microscopic disorder or many-body interactions. In this Letter, we demonstrate that these phenomena can arise purely from geometric randomness. By studying non-interacting quantum particles on random locally tree-like layered graphs with uniform couplings, we show that the geometric randomness and effective graph dimensionality dictates the presence of chaotic dynamics or lack thereof. These graphs can be considered as structurally disordered generalisations of regular square lattices or ladders, or equivalently as multi-component one-dimensional chains with random links between the components. We find that an extensive layer size yields robust quantum chaos, level repulsion, and diffusive transport. Conversely, in the quasi-one-dimensional limit, we find the coexistence of extensive number of localised and delocalised states -- this leads to suppressed level repulsion accompanied by the latter driving ballistic transport. These results establish geometric randomness as a fundamental and independent mechanism for generating and tuning quantum chaos.

Abstract: We present a framework combining discrete gradient (DG) methods with the Scalar Auxiliary Variable (SAV) approach to construct structure-preserving integrators for dissipative and conservative systems. The key observation is that SAV quadratization lifts the dynamics to an extended state space on which the modified energy has an exact discrete-gradient identity. This viewpoint yields three integrators with different accuracy and cost profiles: a first-order semi-implicit Forward Euler scheme, a second-order self-adjoint Midpoint scheme, and a second-order Predictive scheme with reduced implicitness. The construction extends to almost-Poisson systems and preserves selected Casimir invariants under an enforceable discrete condition. Numerical experiments cover the Allen--Cahn equation, an Ohta--Kawasaki-type nonlocal gradient flow, a double-well Hamiltonian oscillator, and a Poisson system with a nonlinear cubic Casimir.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核与超核（hypernuclei）的产生是研究高能重离子碰撞中弱束缚态形成机制的有力工具。凝聚模型预测，在小碰撞系统中，由于³_ΛH的半径更大，其产生相对于³He会被强烈抑制，这一预言需要通过实验测量进行验证。

Result: 测量得到的比率NtNp/Nd²和S₃显著偏离热模型预期。采用包含d和³_ΛH真实非高斯波函数的凝聚计算能改进对数据的描述；而使用高斯近似描述³_ΛH波函数时，无法同时再现所测S₃值和³_ΛH的结合能。

Abstract: The study of nuclei and hypernuclei production is a powerful tool to investigate the formation mechanism of loosely bound states in high-energy heavy-ion collisions. A key prediction from coalescence models is a strong suppression of the hypertriton (${}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}$) compared to ${}^{3}\rm{He}$ production in small collision systems due to the larger radius of ${}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}$. We find that the ratios $N_{t}N_{p}/N_{d}^{2}$ and $S_{3} =(N_{{}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}}/N_{{}^{3}_{}\rm{He}})/(N_Λ/N_{p})$ deviate significantly from thermal model expectations. Coalescence calculations incorporating realistic non-Gaussian wave functions for the $d$ and ${}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}$ provide an improved description of the data, while Gaussian descriptions of the ${}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}$ wave function fail to simultaneously reproduce the measured $S_{3}$ and the binding energy of ${}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}$. These results suggest that the ${}^{3}_Λ\rm{H}$ wave function contains larger short-distance $d$--$Λ$ probability than implied by a Gaussian ansatz, demonstrating the potential of heavy-ion production measurements as a probe of hypernuclear wave functions and the underlying hyperon--nucleon interaction.

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于并行轨迹回火（Parallel Trajectory Tempering, PTT）的能量模型（Energy-Based Models, EBMs）训练算法，利用优化路径连续性在整个学习过程中维持平衡采样，实现了对高度多模态及数据稀缺科学数据集上的稳定快速训练，并在计算成本上与持续对比散度（Persistent Contrastive Divergence）相当，同时免费提供热化时间估计、平衡样本和精确对数似然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量模型（EBMs）为科学数据生成建模提供了可解释框架，但马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov Chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）混合不良常限制其可靠性，亟需一种能维持平衡采样且计算高效的训练方法。

Method: 引入基于并行轨迹回火（PTT）的训练算法，利用优化路径的连续性在学习全程维持平衡采样；结合蓄水池采样（reservoir sampling）与自适应优化（adaptive optimization），实现与持续对比散度（PCD）相当的计算成本。

Result: 在受限玻尔兹曼机（Restricted Boltzmann Machines）上，PTT持续优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上超越最先进的深度生成模型，生成更高质量样本并展现出更强的抗过拟合和对有限数据的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PTT使能量模型的平衡最大似然训练（equilibrium maximum-likelihood training）变得实用且计算高效，同时以几乎零额外成本提供热化时间直接估计、训练模型的平衡样本及准确对数似然。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>

### [23] [Chaos in reason: How chain-of-thought LLMs can look for an answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27805)
*Gregorio Jaca,Kristóf Benedek,János Török*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本论文运用非线性动力学和混沌理论（chaos theory）的工具分析大语言模型（large language models, LLMs），通过文本与隐状态轨迹分析揭示其内部动力学表现出混沌特征——包括对初始条件的强敏感性、有界演化中的间歇性跳变发散、雅可比矩阵分析、复发图与维度分析等多角度证据，并提出注意力机制引发的非线性耦合是驱动该混沌行为的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在广泛任务上表现卓越，但其内部动力学机制仍缺乏深入理解。作者引入非线性动力学与混沌理论的视角，旨在揭示LLMs内部动态行为的基本性质。

Method: 论文从多个层面展开分析：通过文本和隐状态轨迹分析检测混沌特征；对Transformer子块进行精确的雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）分析以解析各组件对扰动的传播或抑制作用；使用复发图（recurrence plots）与维度分析（dimension analysis）来揭示隐状态空间中的结构特征；并考察不同距离度量下结果的一致性。

Result: LLMs表现出典型的混沌信号，包括对初始条件的强敏感性，体现为邻近轨迹的间歇性跳变发散，同时伴有有界演化；自注意力（self-attention）与前馈网络（feed-forward network）会扩展并传播扰动，而归一化与残差连接则抵消这种扩展并促进稳定性；复发图显示LLMs与洛伦兹吸引子（Lorenz attractor）等典型混沌系统存在结构相似性；维度分析揭示隐状态空间中存在分形结构，尤其在最后几层最为显著。

Conclusion: 作者提出由注意力机制诱导的非线性耦合是驱动LLMs混沌行为的关键机制，为理解大语言模型内部动力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet their internal dynamics remain poorly understood. In this work, we apply the tools of nonlinear dynamics and chaos theory to LLMs. By analyzing both text and hidden state trajectories, we demonstrate that LLMs exhibit hallmark signatures of chaos, including strong sensitivity to initial conditions, manifested as intermittent, jump-like divergence of nearby trajectories combined with bounded evolution, with consistent results across different distance metrics. An exact Jacobian analysis of the Transformer's sub-blocks shows that self-attention and the feed-forward network expand and propagate perturbations, while normalization and residual connections counteract this expansion and promote stability. Recurrence plots show structural similarities between LLMs and canonical chaotic systems such as the Lorenz attractor, while dimension analysis reveals fractal structures in the hidden state space, particularly pronounced in the last layers. We propose that the nonlinear coupling induced by attention mechanisms plays a key role in driving this chaotic behavior.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成的机器学习框架，将基于强化学习（reinforcement learning）的金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）生成与多阶段晶体图卷积神经网络（Crystal Graph Convolutional Neural Network, CGCNN）预测漏斗相结合，用于加速光催化剂的设计与筛选。在生成并筛选12万MOF候选材料后，识别出两个性能显著优于基准材料（如PCN-224(Zr)）的候选体系，并将计算成本降低了4.13倍。

Method: 构建了一个集成机器学习框架，包含基于强化学习的MOF生成模块和由CGCNN构成的预测漏斗，使用13个关键描述符（包括带隙适配性、CO2/H2O选择性、吸附能和结构稳定性）对12万个候选MOF进行逐步筛选。

Result: 预测漏斗将计算成本降低了4.13倍且保持预测鲁棒性；Cr基和Zn基两个最优候选MOF的预测光催化适应度分别比PCN-224(Zr)等基准材料高出1.70±0.25倍和1.20±0.05倍；模拟X射线衍射图谱证实与实验合成MOF高度吻合，且后验分析揭示了与高活性强相关的N262金属簇等结构基序（structural motif）。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法有望显著加速高效耐用光催化剂的发现，为环境与能源相关转化过程提供可实验实现和大规模应用的计算设计MOF基础。

Abstract: The rational design of photocatalysts for environmental remediation and CO2 conversion remains limited by the high computational cost and sparse experimental data describing multi-parameter photocatalytic behavior. This work presents an integrated machine-learning framework that couples reinforcement learning-based metal-organic framework (MOF) generation with a multi-stage Crystal Graph Convolutional Neural Network (CGCNN) prediction funnel to identify photocatalysts optimized across multiple electronic and structural features. 120,000 MOF candidates were generated and screened using 13 key descriptors, including band-gap suitability, CO2/H2O selectivity, adsorption energy, and structural stability. The funnel approach reduced computational cost by 4.13-fold while maintaining predictive robustness. Two top candidates, a Cr-based and a Zn-based MOF, exhibited predicted photocatalytic fitness values of 1.70 +/- 0.25 and 1.20 +/- 0.05 fold higher respectively than benchmark materials such as PCN-224(Zr), demonstrating simultaneous improvements in light absorption, redox energetics, and framework durability. Simulated X-ray diffraction patterns confirmed strong structural agreement with experimentally synthesized MOFs, indicating high synthesizability. Post-hoc analysis revealed recurring structural motifs, such as the N262 metal cluster, that correlated strongly with high predicted photocatalytic activity. These results highlight the potential of data-driven methods to accelerate discovery of efficient and durable photocatalysts for environmental and energy-related transformations, providing a foundation for experimental realization and large-scale implementation of computationally designed MOFs.

</details>


### [25] [Parity-selective spin splitting in coplanar antiferromagnets via bichromatic driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27335)
*Di Zhu,Zhongbo Yan,Mohsen Yarmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过双色（bichromatic）$ω$–$nω$ Floquet驱动打破反铁磁体中自旋劈裂（spin splitting）宇称（parity）的对称性限制，实现了静态或单色驱动下无法获得的不对称自旋织构（spin textures），并揭示了驱动谐波阶数与自旋劈裂宇称之间的清晰对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋劈裂的宇称是反铁磁体中动量依赖自旋劈裂的核心特征，但内禀晶体对称性通常将劈裂限制为偶数或奇数宇称，阻碍了灵活的自旋操控，亟需一种绕过该限制的方案。

Method: 采用共面反铁磁体（coplanar AFM），施加双色（$ω$–$nω$）Floquet驱动，通过调节驱动频率组合和谐波阶数来调控自旋劈裂的宇称特性，并借助光学猝灭（optical quench）探测宏观自旋电流。

Result: $ω$–$2ω$驱动可产生高度可调的奇宇称和混合宇称自旋劈裂，而高阶谐波（$n \ge 3$）仅产生偶宇称态；宏观磁化可通过具体驱动协议和谐波阶数进行切换，不同自旋劈裂态在光学猝灭后表现为定性不同的宏观自旋电流。

Conclusion: 双色Floquet驱动为反铁磁体中自旋劈裂宇称的灵活调控提供了自然途径，产生的自旋劈裂态可通过宏观自旋电流这一输运特征加以鉴别，并可由自旋与角度分辨光电子能谱（spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）直接可视化验证。

Abstract: Parity is a central characteristic of momentum-dependent spin splitting in antiferromagnets (AFMs). Yet, intrinsic crystal symmetries typically restrict the splitting to either even or odd parity, preventing flexible spin control. Using a coplanar AFM, we demonstrate that bichromatic ($ω$--$nω$) Floquet driving offers a natural way to bypass this constraint. This mechanism generates asymmetric spin textures unattainable in static AFMs or under monochromatic driving. For the specific AFM considered here, we reveal an elegant relation between spin-splitting parity and harmonic hierarchy: $ω$--$2ω$ fields generate highly tunable odd- and mixed-parity spin splittings, whereas higher-order harmonics ($n \ge 3$) exclusively produce even-parity states. The macroscopic magnetization can also be toggled via the specific driving protocol and harmonic $n$. These distinct spin splitting states manifest in qualitatively different macroscopic spin currents generated after an optical quench---a definitive transport signature complementing direct visualization via spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy.

</details>

*Satya Kokonda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合强化学习（reinforcement learning）与晶体图卷积神经网络（Crystal Graph Convolutional Neural Network, CGCNN）的材料设计方法，以光催化为案例，生成了60,000种金属有机框架（Metal-Organic Frameworks, MOFs）候选材料，通过预测器漏斗系统筛选出两个最具前景的MOFs，并揭示了影响催化性能的关键设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多关键过程过于复杂而无法进行计算建模，需要通过实验来识别有前景的材料。本文旨在为这类"不可建模"的应用场景提供一种计算材料设计方法，以光催化作为具体案例进行验证。

Method: 方法包括三个核心组成部分：首先，利用强化学习生成60,000种针对CO/H2O选择性优化的新型MOFs；其次，构建预测器漏斗系统（predictor funnel system），迭代剔除低评分MOFs，将候选集缩减至10,986个，计算效率提升276%；最后，训练CGCNN模型预测特征，构建包含稳定性、催化能力、材料成本、可持续性和吸附性等指标以及应用特定设计准则的适应度函数（fitness function），用于模拟光催化性能并筛选出最有利的候选材料。

Result: 方法筛选出两种最有前景的MOFs：一种是Cr基MOF，其光催化评分比对照组高230%；另一种是Zn基MOF，在所有相关指标上均优于最佳对照组，表现出对适应度函数变化的鲁棒性。本研究共设计了20种材料，每种都比该应用的对照组好125%。此外，分析揭示了有意义的设计模式，如金属簇N262对催化性能的显著影响。

Conclusion: 通过纳入工业应用相关特征（如成本和稳定性），本方法成功为药物递送等原本不可建模的过程设计了具有工业前景的材料，并开创了一种多目标优化方法，其纳入的特征数量比先前工作多260%。

Abstract: Many crucial processes are too complex for computational modeling, requiring experimentation to identify promising materials. Here, a methodology for material design is presented, while photocatalysis is presented as a specific case-study. Metal-Organic Frameworks (MOFs) are a subset of highly promising porous nanomaterials, used in a variety of unmodellable applications. Reinforcement learning generated 60,000 novel MOFs optimized for CO/H20 selectivity. A predictor funnel system was created, iteratively removing low-scoring MOFs to 10,986 potential candidates, improving computational efficiency by 276%. While trained Crystal Graph Convolutional Neural Network (CGCNN) models predicted features for creating a fitness function incorporating stability, catalytic ability, material cost, sustainability, and adsorption while allowing the inclusion of application specific design criterion. This designed function provides a computational method to model photocatalytic performance- and filtered down to two promising MOFs which each pass a myriad of synthesis criteria, first a Cr-based MOF with photocatalyst score 230% higher than the control. Second, a Zn-based MOF outperforms the best control across all relevant metrics, demonstrating robustness against variable fitness functions. This work designed 20 materials, each 125% better than the control for this application. Furthermore, analysis revealed insightful design patterns, such as the significant influence of metal cluster N262 on catalytic performance, providing a method for future work to narrow the chemical space. By incorporating industrially applicable features such as cost or stability of the material, this work successfully designs industrially promising materials in otherwise unmodellable processes such as drug delivery, while paving a method for multi-objective optimization incorporating 260% more features than prior work.

*Soumya Mishra,Suchandrima Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过分子动力学模拟系统研究了钨中刃位错与空位环的相互作用，揭示了不同缺陷形貌和几何构型下的作用机制与障碍强度变化，并展示了如何将原子尺度的障碍强度转化为介观尺度本构参数。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 平行取向的空位环表现出强几何依赖性，根据相互作用几何的不同分别发生完全湮灭、转变为较弱残留缺陷或缺陷输运；倾斜取向的空位环则通过伯格斯矢量反应形成不可动的<100>位错段，产生显著更高的钉扎强度，且对交叉位置不敏感。

Conclusion: 研究建立了从原子尺度到介观模型的转化框架，为未来显式考虑辐照缺陷群体演化的介观尺度本构定律提供了物理基础，从而提升超越标定条件下的预测能力。

Abstract: Structural materials for fusion reactors undergo neutron irradiation, generating defect populations that govern their mechanical response through irradiation hardening. Physically based mesoscale constitutive models require accurate descriptions of dislocation defect interactions and, critically, how both defect morphology and obstacle strength evolve during plastic deformation as the irradiation-induced defect landscape changes. Vacancy loops provide an ideal prototype for addressing this problem because they are among the most prevalent irradiation-induced defects in tungsten, yet their interaction mechanisms with dislocations and subsequent evolution remain poorly understood. Molecular dynamics simulations are used to systematically investigate interactions between edge dislocations and vacancy loops in tungsten by varying loop size, crystallographic orientation, and dislocation loop intersection geometry. Parallel loops exhibit strong geometry dependent behaviour, undergoing complete annihilation, transformation into weaker remnant defects, or defect transport depending on the interaction geometry. In contrast, inclined loops interact through Burgers vector reactions that form sessile <100> dislocation segments, producing substantially higher pinning strengths with little sensitivity to the intersection position. Finally, a framework is demonstrated for translating atomistically determined obstacle strengths into constitutive parameters, such as irradiation hardening, for mesoscale models. The mechanistic understanding developed here provides the physical basis for future mesoscale constitutive laws that explicitly account for the evolution of irradiation-induced defect populations and the resulting changes in dislocation-defect interaction mechanisms and obstacle strength, thereby improving predictive capability beyond calibrated conditions.

</details>

*Wenderson R. F. Silva,Robson C. O. Guedes,Gilberto Rodrigues-Junior,Eduarda P. M. Campos,Angelo Malachias,Joaquim B. S. Mendes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次将导电聚合物聚(3,4-亚乙基二氧噻吩)磺酸盐（PEDOT）用于磁弹性（magnetoelastic, ME）湿度传感器功能化涂层，通过滴涂法在Metglas谐振器上沉积PEDOT薄膜，利用湿度诱导的聚合物结构变化实现高灵敏度、高分辨率的无线湿度传感，灵敏度达155 Hz/% RH，优于此前报道的ME湿度传感器。

Result: 表征结果显示湿度诱导PSS富集区溶胀、聚合物链迁移率增强，层间距在95%相对湿度下从23.5 Å增至24.2 Å；谐振频率随湿度系统性下移（源于水吸附、质量加载和粘弹性阻尼的综合效应），优化器件在20–70% RH范围内灵敏度达155 Hz/% RH，分辨率优于0.1% RH，响应与恢复时间优异。

Abstract: Magnetoelastic (ME) resonators provide an attractive platform for passive and wireless sensing, particularly for monitoring relative humidity (RH) in sealed or hard-to-access environments. In this work, we introduce ME humidity sensors functionalized with poly(3,4-ethylenedioxythiophene) sulfonate (PEDOT), marking the first application of this conductive polymer in ME-based humidity sensing. PEDOT films were deposited onto Metglas resonators via a simple drop-casting process, producing uniform and mechanically stable coatings. Comprehensive structural and morphological characterization by SEM, EDX, Raman spectroscopy, AFM/KPFM, and XRD confirmed the structural integrity of the polymer and revealed humidity-induced swelling of the PSS-rich domains, accompanied by enhanced polymer-chain mobility and an increase in the lamellar spacing from 23.5 Å to 24.2 Å at 95% RH. These structural changes directly affected the dynamic response of the resonators, leading to a systematic resonance-frequency downshift driven by the combined effects of water adsorption, mass loading, and viscoelastic damping. The optimized device achieved a sensitivity of 155 Hz/% RH in the 20 - 70% RH range, outperforming previously reported ME humidity sensors, while exhibiting a resolution better than 0.1% RH together with excellent response and recovery times. These results establish PEDOT as a highly effective functional coating for magnetoelastic humidity sensors and demonstrate a simple, low-cost, wireless sensing platform with high sensitivity and strong potential for practical environmental and industrial monitoring applications.

</details>


### [29] [Optimization of Epitaxial Mn4N Thin Films Grown by Sputtering for Spintronic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27419)
*Teodor Apetrei,Emre Demiroglu,Caner Deger,Can Onur Avci,Silvia Damerio*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Altermagnets combine collinear antiferromagnetic order with nonrelativistic spin splitting, enabling spintronic functionalities without relying on spin--orbit coupling. Abstract: This work presents a new approach to evaluating the toughness, described by the critical energy release rate ($G_c$), and ultimate tensile strength ($σ_c$) of amorphous silica (SiO$_2$ glass), combining microbeam tests and phase-field calculations. We found $G_c = 5.1$~J/m$^2$ (critical stress intensity factor $K_{IC} = 0.61$~MPa$\cdot$m$^{1/2}$), an intrinsic material length scale $\ell_c = 9.1$~nm, and $σ_c = 6.8$~GPa, consistent with previously reported brittle properties of silica glass. Abstract: An equation-of-state (EOS)-based construction of the Grüneisen function is developed and assessed through predictions of finite-temperature bulk moduli. These results demonstrate that latent graph-based AE-GCN-LSTM forecasting provides a scalable and computationally efficient surrogate for long-horizon simulation of multicomponent, multiphase microstructures and offers a promising foundation for high-throughput alloy design.

Since the strong coupling is a prerequisite for achieving coherent quantum control of magnons and preparing magnonic quantum states, we start by introducing representative strong-coupling experiments in cavity magnonics, then review a series of protocols for creating various magnonic quantum states, such as Fock, cat, squeezed, and entangled states, and discuss their potential applications in macroscopic quantum studies, quantum information science, quantum sensing, magnonic quantum devices, dark matter detection, and so on. Finally, we summarize the review and give an outlook for the future study of quantum magnonics.

Abstract: We show that global optimal control can drastically suppress the impact of decoherence in globally driven superconducting quantum computing architectures, taking as a prototype a recently proposed quasi-two-dimensional ladder geometry. TL;DR: 本文基于群扩展（group extensions）和图的提升（graph lifts）引入了一类系统性的提升乘积（lifted product, LP）码族构造方法，在增大码长时保持Tanner图的局部结构不变，并通过链映射和上链映射建立码字参数、逻辑算子和容错逻辑操作装置之间的关系。该构造首次获得了优于以往报告的LP码参数，并展示了码手术（code surgery）装置可跨有限提升迁移且在某些情形下降低空间开销，同时提出将提升作为定义无欧几里得格点背景下代数定义qLDPC码热力学族的第一步。

Abstract: Lifted product (LP) codes form an important class of quantum error correcting codes with favorable code parameters. We introduce a systematic construction of LP code families based on group extensions and graph lifts. The construction increases the code size while preserving the local structure of the Tanner graph, and relates code parameters, logical operators, and fault-tolerant logical-operation gadgets within the families through chain and cochain maps.

We then demonstrate that code-surgery gadgets can be transferred across the selected finite lifts through chain maps and, in several cases, implemented with lower space overhead. We also develop parallel product surgery for lifted clustered cyclic codes. Finally, we propose lifting as a systematic first step toward defining thermodynamic families for algebraically defined qLDPC codes without an underlying Euclidean lattice. For several base codes and selected lifts, coherent information exhibits finite-size crossings, while our results also indicate that additional conditions are needed to determine a unique family.

Abstract: We study the general form of meromorphic amplitudes that are compatible with unitarity, analyticity, crossing symmetry, polynomial boundedness and the hidden-zero and corresponding splitting conditions. We argue that, in the absence of an accumulation point in the energy levels, the analytic boundary we derive is the largest possible boundary in the primal construction of meromorphic amplitudes. Importantly these conflicting scenarios for cosmic birefringence could be experimentally distinguished by a search for, or exclusion of, birefringence anisotropies, that could be measured by POLARBEAR, Simons Observatory and LiteBIRD. Abstract: We apply the framework of Soft Collinear Effective Theory (SCET) to the theory of the geometric scalar field. Abstract: We consider three families of semiclassical string solutions on AdS$_5\times S^5/\mathbb{Z}_L$ orbifold backgrounds which correspond to `twisted' single-trace operators in the SU$(2)$, SU$(3)$ and SL$(2)$ sectors, respectively, of the dual 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theory. Abstract: We revisit the well-known nonlinear sigma model (NLSM), an effective field theory describing the scattering of $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Goldstone bosons and characterized by an infinite tower of two-derivative interactions. Primordial black holes exhibit unique and novel phenomenology, and may constitute the observed dark matter. Helicity conjugation fixes which one: the exponentiating source obeys $\overline{S^{(s)}_{+,{\rm exp}}}=(-1)^sS^{(s)}_{-,{\rm exp}}$, so the tower fixes the electric projection of the source at even levels and the magnetic projection at odd ones, matching the alternation of the Kerr mass and current moments; for aligned spin the projection is exhaustive and we solve the tower in closed form. We explain the corresponding hard flux charges and show how their external-state action gives the Ward representation of the soft theorem. The polynomial Poisson algebra on $T^\ast S^2$, with local $w_{1+\infty}$-type reductions, then acts on these frame-changing generators; it does not close on the Kerr-selected data alone. This gives the physical role of the $w_{1+\infty}$-like structure in Kerr black-hole scattering: it moves the intrinsic/canonical dictionary. Its observable imprint begins with displacement memory at $s=0$ and spin memory at $s=1$, followed by higher electric and magnetic memory moments.

Conclusion: 该分析具有完全普适性，可直接应用于近幺正（unitarity）囚禁费米子和共振任意子（resonant anyons）等系统，前者可提供这些动力学相的实验信号。此外，作者从全息（holographic）视角注释了受驱动的非相对论CFT的几何对应。

Abstract: We develop a formalism for studying Floquet dynamics for systems with {\it non-relativistic} conformal invariance in $d$ spatial dimensions. Our analysis indicates the existence of two dynamical phases, hyperbolic and elliptic, separated by a parabolic transition surface. We demonstrate this by studying the expectation value of a conformal generator in the many body ground state during the drive, as well as the fidelity of this state. Stroboscopically, they behave exponentially in the hyperbolic phase, show oscillatory behavior in the elliptic phase, and exhibit power-law on the transition surface. Our analysis is completely universal and can be directly applied to several systems including trapped fermions near unitarity and resonant anyons. The former can provide experimental signatures of these dynamical phases. We also comment on a holographic perspective of such driven non-relativistic CFTs and demonstrate that the hyperbolic phase is associated with a timelike stationary-limit surface, such as an ergosphere, in the bulk, while the parabolic phase corresponds to an extremal Killing horizon.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect in rainbow space-time, focusing on leading-order corrections to the Casimir energy and force. Starting with the scalar field Lagrangian in rainbow space-time, with parallel plates introduced through delta-function potentials, we find the corresponding energy-momentum tensor. We obtain the vacuum expectation value of this energy-momentum tensor by expressing it as a quadratic operator acting on the Green's function. By solving the Euler-Lagrange equation of a scalar field in rainbow space-time, we obtain the Green's function solutions. Employing these Green's function solutions in the vacuum expectation value of the energy-momentum tensor, we obtain the modified Casimir energy and Casimir force expressions in rainbow space-time. We study the variation of the deformed Casimir force and energy with the distance between the plates for different choices of rainbow functions. Our results show that for two choices of rainbow functions, the absolute value of the Casimir energy and force is decreasing or increasing, whereas for one specific choice of rainbow functions, it remains the same as the standard result in Minkowski space-time. Comparing our result with experimentally measured value of Casimir force, we obtain the bound on the rainbow parameter dependent terms to be of the order of 10^-24.

Abstract: In this work, we analyze the differences and similarities between two recent constructions, which are distinct in their methodologies for extending the Wald entropy of stationary black holes to non-stationary situations in general higher-derivative gravity. One of them, denoted by $S_\text{Wall}$, is constructed by exploiting the boost symmetry of the near-horizon geometry, whereas the other, denoted by $S_\text{dyn}$, is obtained from a covariant phase-space analysis based on the Wald-Iyer Noether charge formalism. While $S_\text{dyn}$ is, by construction, defined only for linearized fluctuations around a stationary black hole solution, $S_\text{Wall}$ does not require such a linearization for its construction. Although the linearization is necessary to interpret $S_\text{Wall}$ as a well-defined notion of entropy, the construction itself naturally contains terms that are higher order in the dynamical fluctuations. By comparing the technical structures underlying the two constructions, we clarify the fundamental differences between the methods on which they are based. We demonstrate that while the construction of $S_\text{dyn}$ given the $S_\text{Wall}$ is straightforward, the converse is more subtle. We develop an algorithm to obtain a local expression for $S_\text{Wall}$ from a known expression for $S_\text{dyn}$ in a generic diffeomorphism-invariant theory of gravity, provided certain technical conditions are satisfied. We justify our analytical findings with explicit demonstrations in a particular case: the Riemann-squared example of the higher-derivative theory of gravity.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对一级相变中气泡膨胀的流体动力学分析大多基于共形（conformal）假设或简单的袋模型，而实际物理系统（如QCD）具有非共形热力学性质。准确建模这些动力学对于预测相变产生的引力波谱至关重要，因此需要系统研究非共形效应对气泡膨胀解的影响。

Method: 作者分析了一类宽泛的状态方程（equation of state），该类别在电弱相变常用的袋模型描述与类QCD理论之间进行插值；以纯SU(3)杨-米尔斯（Yang-Mills）理论作为具体基准，求解气泡解。此外，还展示了如何通过改变物质场对几何的背景作用（backreaction）在全息（holographic）框架下实现袋模型与类QCD极限之间的插值。

Result: 作者发现了一组新的流体动力学阻碍，这些阻碍出现在气泡壁处和流体流动过程中，可部分或完全排除原本允许的解；识别了一类含附加冲击波插入稀疏波（rarefaction wave）中的"冲击爆轰"新解；在类QCD理论中，所有爆轰（detonation）解（包括冲击爆轰）均被排除，气泡壁速度空间中出现了间隙。

Conclusion: 这些阻碍和约束会强烈影响流体的动能预算和相应的引力波信号，可能抑制最高效的相变配置，凸显了非共形动力学对精确建模相变过程及预测引力波谱的重要性。相关构建气泡解的代码已公开。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrodynamics of expanding bubbles in first-order phase transitions with non-conformal thermodynamics. We analyze a broad class of equations of state interpolating between bag-model descriptions, commonly used for electroweak transitions, and QCD-like theories. As a concrete benchmark, we determine the bubble solutions for pure SU(3) Yang-Mills theory. We uncover a new set of hydrodynamic obstructions to bubble expansion. These obstructions arise both at the bubble wall and along the fluid flow, and can partially or completely eliminate otherwise allowed solutions. We also identify a new class of solutions, which we dub "shocked detonations", consisting of ordinary detonations with an additional shock inserted in the rarefaction wave. As a consequence of these obstructions, the space of admissible bubble wall velocities is significantly constrained, with gaps appearing between different expansion regimes. For example, for QCD-like theories, all detonation solutions, including shocked detonations, are excluded. We show that these effects can strongly impact the kinetic energy budget of the fluid and, therefore, the resulting gravitational-wave signal, potentially suppressing the most efficient configurations. Our results highlight the importance of non-conformal dynamics for accurately modeling phase transitions and the resulting gravitational-wave spectrum. The code used to construct the bubble solutions is publicly available. For completeness, we also show how the interpolation between the bag-model and QCD-like limits can be realized holographically by varying the backreaction of matter fields on the geometry.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将 FMR 表述为受调节的副本加权朗道规范在温度趋于零时的极限；在有限逆温度 β 下，测度局域化于绝对最小值，主导修正由逆 Faddeev–Popov 算符（Faddeev–Popov operator）决定。通过种群退火（population annealing）和集体盆地跳跃（collective basin hopping）实现小规模 SU(2) 基准计算；此外，该构造还允许在 FMR 上以规范固定真空波泛函（gauge-fixed vacuum wave functional）进行哈密顿量诠释。

Result: 该构造成功定义了无需参数化 FMR 边界的绝对朗道规范，并通过小规模 SU(2) 基准计算提供了实际可行的数值实现；同时可自然推广至完整 QCD，并具备哈密顿量层面的物理解释。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 FMR 构造避免了边界参数化的需求，兼具数值可实施性与理论普适性，为绝对朗道规范在 QCD 中的应用提供了一条新的路径。

Abstract: We formulate the Fundamental Modular Region (FMR) as the zero-temperature limit of regulated copy-weighted Landau gauges. At finite $β$, the measure localizes on absolute minima, and the leading correction is dominated by the inverse Faddeev--Popov (FP) operator. A small $SU(2)$ benchmark provides a computational realization through population annealing and collective basin hopping. Our construction defines absolute Landau gauge without parametrizing the FMR boundary and extends naturally to full quantum chromodynamics (QCD). It also admits a Hamiltonian interpretation in terms of the gauge-fixed vacuum wave functional on the FMR.

</details>

Method: 利用群流形（group manifold）上的调和分析将循环图上的KM型模型重述为受Schur测度控制的随机配分模型；在大N_c极限下精确求解基本表示（fundamental representation）情形；从谱曲线（spectral curve）推导出液滴图像（droplet picture）。

Result: 在大N_c极限下精确求解循环图上的KM型模型，证明Gross-Witten-Wadia相变精确对应于Young图极限形状触及容许表示空间边界；阐明该相变与玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚密切相关；揭示强/弱耦合对偶对应Young图与其补图间的交换，反映Artin-Ihara L-函数的函数方程（functional equation）；建立了酉矩阵特征值密度（eigenvalue density）与随机配分Maya图密度（Maya diagram density）之间的确定关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过将KM型规范理论映射为随机配分模型，建立了规范理论相变、随机组合结构与代数几何对象（Artin-Ihara L-函数）之间的深层联系，为理解大N极限下规范理论与可积系统的交叉提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We introduce Kazakov-Migdal (KM)-type gauge theories on graphs via the Artin-Ihara $L$-function, providing a unified description of the models proposed in prior works. Using harmonic analysis on the group manifold, we reformulate the KM-type model on the cycle graph as a random partition model governed by the Schur measure. We exactly solve the KM-type model on the cycle graph in the fundamental representation as the random partition model in the large $N_c$ limit and demonstrate that the Gross-Witten-Wadia phase transition occurs precisely when the limiting shape of the Young diagram touches the boundary of the allowed representation space. We further clarify that this phase transition is intimately related to Bose-Einstein condensation, and the strong/weak coupling duality possesses a natural combinatorial interpretation as the exchange between the Young diagram and its complement, reflecting the functional equation of the Artin-Ihara $L$-function. We also establish a definitive relationship between the eigenvalue density of the unitary matrix and the Maya diagram density of the random partitions by deriving a droplet picture from the spectral curve.

</details>

Method: 作者构造了一个同时包含Lorentz扇区与规范扇区的"大空间"世界sheet流代数，推广Poláček–Siegel方案，计算扩展生成元的对易子并确定广义联络（generalized connections），以几何方式将杂化Chern–Simons结构嵌入广义vielbein与曲率之中。

Conclusion: 本文建立了杂化弦理论中Lorentz与规范联接在T-对偶框架下统一的代数结构，为理解α'修正中的几何与反常消除机制提供了统一的代数视角。

Abstract: We study the Lorentz and gauge connections in heterotic string theories within a framework of manifest T-duality. A central motivation is the Bergshoeff--de Roo (BdR) type parallelism between the torsionful Lorentz and Yang-Mills connections in heterotic $α'$-corrections. We formulate worldsheet current algebras in a mega-space that incorporates the Lorentz and gauge sectors simultaneously. By generalizing the Poláček-Siegel scheme, we evaluate the commutators of the extended generators and determine the generalized connections. We show that this mega-space current algebra geometrically embeds the heterotic Chern--Simons structures into the generalized vielbeins and curvatures. We also show that the Green-Schwarz anomaly cancellation condition follows from the Jacobi identity for the algebra of stringy covariant derivatives, the ``nachos'' operators $\triangleright_{\mathcal{M}}$.

</details>


### [89] [Quantum Trigonometric Spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider Models from $K$-theoretic Coulomb Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28043)

TL;DR: 本文通过4维N=2颤抖规范理论的K-理论Coulomb分支代数对三角函数自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型进行量子化，建立了其与可积自旋链之间的联系，并推导了量子运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角函数自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型是经典可积系统，其量子化一直是一项重要挑战。近年来该经典模型被发现可以用4维N=2颤抖规范理论的K-理论Coulomb分支来描述，这为量子化提供了新的代数工具与途径。

Method: 利用K-理论Coulomb分支代数中由微小电荷(minscule charge)的abelianized monopole算子导出的L-算子代数，将项链颤抖(necklace quiver)转化为可积自旋链，由此构造一族对易的Hamilton量，并利用量子行列式与Bethe子代数进行分析。

Result: 证明了最低阶Hamilton量与K-理论Coulomb分支代数内水平量子loop代数的量子行列式第一模一致，且其Bethe子代数生成最大族对易Hamilton量；同时推导了量子化物理自旋变量的对易关系与量子运动方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为N个粒子、各具有ell个自旋态的三角函数自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型提供了完整的量子化方案，并揭示了其与可积自旋链、量子loop代数之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We quantize the trigonometric spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider model of $N$ particles each with $\ell$ spin states using the recently developed description of the classical model in terms of the $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch of the 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theory for the necklace quiver with $\ell$ nodes of rank $N$. The main algebraic tool is an algebra of $L$-operators derived from abelianized monopole operators of minuscule charge, which turns the necklace quiver into an integrable spin chain by producing a family of commuting Hamiltonians. We show that the lowest Hamiltonian coincides with the first mode of the quantum determinant of the horizontal quantum loop algebra living inside the $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch algebra, whose Bethe subalgebra generates a maximal family of commuting Hamiltonians. Finally, we derive the commutation relations and quantum equations of motion of the quantized physical spin variables.

</details>


### [90] [Interior zeros of supersymmetric indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28143)
*Yu Nakayama,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出超对称指标（supersymmetric index）具有内部零点（interior zeros，即 |q|<1 处的零点）的一个充要判据，指出其与底层超对称ζ函数（supersymmetric zeta function）的算术系数指数增长直接相关，并展示了内部零点可通过有限个 q-级数（q-series）系数探测，从而排除自由或 s-限制（s-confining）的红外描述；同时借助巨引力子展开（giant graviton expansion）论证内部零点是有限 N 效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称指标中的零点结构可能揭示理论的非微扰红外信息，但此前缺乏系统判定内部零点是否存在的方法，也无法将其与 IR 自由或 s-限制的物理描述直接联系起来。

Method: 证明超对称指标存在内部零点当且仅当超对称ζ函数的算术系数 δ(ν) 以由最近零点决定的速率指数增长；利用有限个 q-级数系数即可从展开式中直接探测内部零点；并通过巨引力子展开将内部零点定位为有限 N 效应，其位置由单个巨引力子的能量决定。

Result: 在 4 维 N=1 SU(2) SQCD 中，内部零点的存在排除了自由或 s-限制的红外描述；对 N=4 U(N) Schur 指标，内部零点被确认为有限 N 效应，其位置通过巨引力子展开验证。

Conclusion: 内部零点为超对称指标提供了可由 q-级数系数直接判定的精确判据，并可作为探测非微扰红外物理结构（如排除自由或 s-限制描述）的有效工具；同时表明该现象本质上属于有限 N 效应，可由巨引力子能量标度。

Abstract: A supersymmetric index has interior zeros ($|q|<1$) if and only if the arithmetic coefficients $δ(ν)$ underlying the supersymmetric zeta function grow exponentially at a rate set by the nearest zero. Each $δ(ν)$ follows from finitely many $q$-series coefficients, so interior zeros are detectable from the expansion alone and obstruct a free or s-confining infrared description, as we show for 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ $SU(2)$ SQCD. With a giant graviton expansion interior zeros are a finite $N$ effect, located by the energy of one giant graviton, verified for the $\mathcal{N}=4$ $U(N)$ Schur index.

</details>


### [91] [Nonlocal four-fermion theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28160)
*F. M. Belchior,J. R. Nascimento,A. Yu. Petrov,P. J. Porfirio*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建并分析了一类非局域四费米子理论（nonlocal four-fermion theory），其中通常的Dirac算子被一个整函数形式的非局域形状因子（form factor）所形变。通过引入辅助标量场推导出自洽态有效作用量（mean-field effective action）和动力学质量的间隙方程（gap equation），并对两种代表性形状因子进行了详细分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非局域形变下四费米子理论（如NJL型模型）的动力学质量生成行为，理解不同形状因子对间隙积分（gap integral）和临界耦合（critical coupling）的系统性影响。

Method: 通过引入辅助标量场推导平均场有效作用量和间隙方程；由于非局域形状因子是Dirac算子的矩阵函数，逆传播子（inverse propagator）须在由恒等算子和动量算子生成的闭代数（closed algebra）中处理。采用红外/紫外匹配方法（IR/UV matching），在中间尺度M≪Ω≪Λ处拆分动量积分并分别在红外和紫外区域做解析展开。有限温度和密度情形通过Matsubara求和及修正的围道表示（contour representation）扩展。

Result: 对双曲型形状因子f_I=exp(−∂/Λ)和振荡型形状因子f_II=exp(−i∂/Λ)获得了间隙核的显式表达式。结果表明双曲型形状因子增强了间隙积分并降低了临界耦合，而振荡型形状因子则抑制间隙积分并提高临界耦合。在Λ→∞极限下恢复了局域的NJL/Gross-Neveu热间隙方程。

Conclusion: 非局域形状因子的具体形式对四费米子理论的动力学质量生成具有定性不同的影响：单调衰减型因子促进对称性破缺，振荡型因子则抑制之；局域结果作为Λ→∞的极限被自然恢复。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate and analyze a nonlocal four-fermion theory in which the usual Dirac operator is deformed by an entire nonlocal form factor. After introducing an auxiliary scalar field, we derive the mean-field effective action and the corresponding gap equation for the dynamical mass. A central technical point of the analysis is that the nonlocal form factor is a matrix function of the Dirac operator, so the inverse propagator must be treated as an element of the closed algebra generated by the identity and $\not{\!p}$ operators, rather than as a purely scalar quantity. We obtain explicit expressions for the gap kernel for two representative choices of a form factor, $f_{I}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-\not{\partial}/Λ}$ and $f_{II}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-i\not{\partial}/Λ}$. Following the IR/UV matching method used in the recent Dirac-like nonlocal spinor theory [1], the momentum integral is split at an intermediate scale $M\ll Ω\ll Λ$, expanded analytically in the infrared and ultraviolet regions, and compared with the usual local NJL/Gross-Neveu result. We show that the hyperbolic form factor enhances the gap integral and lowers the critical coupling, whereas the oscillatory form factor suppresses it and raises the critical coupling. The finite-temperature and finite-density extension is formulated through Matsubara sums and a corrected contour representation, with the local thermal gap equation recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [92] [Light-Front approach to $4d$ massless Higher-Spin interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28183)
*Mattia Serrani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文在光锥（Light-Front）框架下研究四维无质量高自旋（higher-spin）相互作用，通过分析庞加莱代数（Poincaré algebra）在四阶（quartic order）的闭合性，系统分类了所有幺正的局部高自旋理论，并厘清了OPE结合性、散射振幅消失、雅可比恒等式与光锥全纯约束之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自旋相互作用的动力学缺失是基础物理中的核心难题之一。作者旨在通过光锥方法系统考察四阶约束的可解性，从而确定哪些高自旋局域理论是自洽的，并厘清不同数学条件（如OPE结合性、雅可比恒等式、光锥全纯约束）之间的逻辑联系。

Method: 首先在平直空间中求解光锥四阶全纯（holomorphic）约束，分类所有单导数和双导数理论；随后对比OPE结合性、树级振幅消失、动力学代数（kinematical algebra）的雅可比恒等式与光锥全纯约束的关系；最后研究包含MHV和反MHV顶点的非全纯四阶约束，并利用旋量-螺旋度形式（spinor-helicity formalism）结合因子化（factorisation）一致性确定全部局域四振幅。

Result: 发现存在无限多个相互作用的局域高自旋理论（场数目有限或无限），单/双导数理论对应规范与引力相互作用的推广，属于手性高自旋引力（Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity）的一致子扇区。在非全纯情形下，恢复杨-米尔斯（Yang-Mills）理论与引力，并证明多引力子相互作用的不一致性；引入高阶导数三次顶点后可获得完整四阶约束的非平凡解。最终分类出所有幺正局域高自旋理论，并识别出新一类局域准手性（quasi-chiral）理论。

Conclusion: 光锥四阶约束为高自旋相互作用的分类提供了统一的框架；OPE结合性、振幅消失条件、雅可比恒等式与光锥全纯约束在一致理论间相互依赖。在引入高阶导数顶点后，存在新的局域准手性高自旋理论族，其全部四阶振幅可通过因子化条件唯一确定。

Abstract: This thesis studies $4d$ massless higher-spin interactions in the Light-Front approach by analysing the closure of the Poincaré algebra at quartic order. We first solve the light-cone quartic holomorphic constraint in flat space and show the existence of infinitely many interacting local higher-spin theories with either a finite or infinite number of fields. We classify all one- and two-derivative theories, corresponding to higher-spin extensions of gauge and gravitational interactions. These are consistent subsectors of higher-spin extensions of self-dual Yang--Mills and gravity, themselves truncations of Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity. We then clarify the relation between the OPE associativity in celestial CFT, the vanishing of tree-level amplitudes for generic kinematics, the Jacobi identity of the associated ''gauge algebra'' (kinematical algebra), and the light-cone holomorphic constraints. Finally, we investigate the non-holomorphic quartic constraint involving both MHV and anti-MHV vertices. We recover the existence of Yang-Mills theory and gravity, and the inconsistency of interacting multi-graviton theories. We then show that once higher-derivative cubic vertices are included, nontrivial solutions to the full quartic constraint exist. Result: 完整的表示包含满足 C_2=m^2=κρ-β^2>0（2D）和 C_2=m^2=κρ-β⃗²>0（3D）的扇区，这些扇区可能描述平面全息中的质量态。与普通共形对称不同，Carroll 共形对称一般只能将关联函数约束到 Carroll 不变量的任意函数而非常数。

Conclusion: Carroll 共形对称的完整表示结构比普通共形对称更为丰富，其关联函数受约束程度较弱，且包含可刻画质量态的新扇区，这为平面全息中的质量态描述提供了新的理论框架。

Unlike ordinary conformal symmetry, Carrollian conformal symmetry generally determines the correlators only up to arbitrary functions of Carrollian invariants rather than constants. The complete representation contains sectors with $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-β^2>0$ for 2D and $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-\vecβ^{\,2}>0$ for 3D, which may describe massive states in flat holography.

Result: 协变变分保持度规、缩并和霍奇对偶，但其对易子因反常而不封闭；结合Kosmann导数后可覆盖同时具有时空与洛伦兹结构的场。在四维零超曲面上，沿超旋转的协变变分反常产生了电磁螺旋度通量；该算子及其反常亦适用于高维张量场，尤其关注d=2p+2维度中的p形式（p-forms）。

Abstract: We define a covariant variation of tensor fields by combining its Lie derivative with the metric variation. This operator preserves the metric, contractions, and Hodge duality, but its commutator is not closed due to an anomaly. We derive its algebraic and geometric properties, and compare it with the Kosmann derivative. Combining the covariant variation with Kosmann derivative gives total covariant variation for the fields with both spacetime and Lorentz structure, all of which belong to the metric Lie derivative. Moreover, we introduce families of extended operators which contain the affine connection, Lie derivative, and covariant variation. From the anomaly of the covariant variation along the superrotation, an electromagnetic helicity flux appears at null hypersurfaces in four dimensions. We also apply the covariant variation and its anomaly to tensor fields in arbitrary spacetime dimensions, and especially focus on the $p$-forms in $d=2p+2$ dimensions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Hayden-Preskill恢复方案中，注入后的扰频器与初始状态不相关。本文旨在扩展该框架，探究当扰频器与初态通过SWAP门相关联时，以及在有限温度条件下，信息恢复是否依然可行，并考察温度对恢复质量的影响。

Method: 修改后的协议引入SWAP门使扰频器与初始状态相关联，并考虑有限温度恢复。在均匀算子扩散（uniform operator spreading）假设下推导后选择概率与条件保真度的晚时解析估计，并与数值结果对比。

Result: 后选择概率与条件保真度均与温度成正比，反映低温下初始态纠缠减少。解析估计与数值结果吻合良好，表明强扰频（strong scrambling）对信息恢复至关重要。在IBM超导处理器上使用N=8个Majorana的二进制稀疏SYK哈密顿量实现该协议，观察到数据保留定性恢复动力学特征，且基于SWAP的误差缓解方案能同时改善后选择概率与条件保真度。

Conclusion: 通过将扰频器与初始状态相关联并在有限温度下扩展Hayden-Preskill协议，信息仍能成功恢复，且恢复效率与温度正相关。实验验证进一步表明强扰频是信息恢复的关键因素，SWAP基误差缓解方案可提升恢复性能。

Abstract: In the original Hayden--Preskill recovery, the post-injection scrambler and initial state are {\it not related}. We extend this setup in two ways: by using a SWAP gate so that the scrambler and initial state are {\it related}, and by considering recovery at {\it finite} temperature. For this modified protocol, we show that the information is successfully recovered in the sense that the postselection probability is non-negligible and the conditional fidelity is large. We find that both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity are proportional to temperature, reflecting the reduced entanglement of the initial state at lower temperatures. We also derive their late-time analytic estimates under the assumption of uniform operator spreading and show that they agree well with the numerical results. This demonstrates that strong scrambling is important for successful information recovery. Implementing the protocol on an IBM superconducting processor using a binary sparse SYK Hamiltonian with $N = 8$ Majoranas, we observe that the data retain the qualitative recovery dynamics and that a SWAP-based error-mitigation scheme improves both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity.

</details>


### [96] [Soft charges and zero modes at null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28543)
*Dušan Đorđ ević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了零边界（null boundaries）上的全局零模（global zero-mode）歧义对场论正则结构的修正，指出这一零模会产生新的准局域边界可观测量（quasilocal edge observable），并对电荷代数（charge algebra）产生重要影响，且该效应可出现在任意类型的零边界（包括无穷远零边界和有限距离的黑洞视界）上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零边界上的渐近对称性（asymptotic symmetries）为场论的软扇区（soft sector）提供了重要视角，揭示了超越体动力学（bulk dynamics）的守恒荷（conserved charges）和无限维对称代数（infinite-dimensional symmetry algebra）。尽管这些结构通常与剩余规范变换（residual gauge transformations）相关联，但零边界还表现出一种具有特征性的全局零模歧义（global zero-mode ambiguity），其物理后果此前探索较少。

Method: 论文通过分析零边界上的全局零模如何修正场论的正则结构（canonical structure）来进行研究，重点考察该零模对边界可观测量的影响及其在电荷代数中的角色。

Result: 研究表明，全局零模会引发一种新的准局域边界可观测量（edge observable），并对电荷代数产生相应后果。该效应具有普遍性，可出现在任何类型的零边界上，既包括无穷远零边界（null infinity），也包括有限距离的零边界，如黑洞视界（black hole horizon）。

Conclusion: 全局零模歧义不仅是零边界的一个技术性特征，还具有实质性的物理意义：它引入了新的准局域边界可观测量并影响电荷结构。这一发现为理解零边界上的对称性与守恒律提供了新的视角，且其适用范围涵盖从渐近无穷远到黑洞视界等多种几何场景。

Abstract: Asymptotic symmetries at null boundaries provide a powerful window into the soft sector of field theories, revealing conserved charges and infinite-dimensional symmetry algebras beyond the standard bulk dynamics. While these structures are usually associated with residual gauge transformations, null boundaries also exhibit a characteristic global zero-mode ambiguity, whose physical consequences are less explored. We discuss how this zero mode modifies the canonical structure of field theories at null boundaries. In particular, we show that it gives rise to a new quasilocal edge observable, with consequences to the charge algebra. It can arise on any kind of null boundaries, both at the null infinity and the finite distance such as a black hole horizon.

</details>


### [97] [Learning to Trace Seiberg Dualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28628)
*Jonathan J. Heckman,Shani Meynet,Alessandro Mininno,Gary Shiu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习方法判定超对称箭图规范理论（supersymmetric quiver gauge theories）中的西伯格对偶性（Seiberg duality），将问题数学化为箭图（quiver）的突变（mutation）判断，并比较不同网络架构（transformer与多层感知机）在该任务上的表现，发现其优于确定性算法，结合路径规划算法可进一步提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶性（duality）在物理系统中广泛存在且重要，但即使已知所有规则，判定两个系统是否对偶在计算上仍具挑战性。本文旨在回答：面对两个系统，如何高效地确立它们确实是对偶的？

Method: 使用机器学习方法建立箭图突变（quiver mutation）的判定模型，这类似于"学习解结"（learning to unknot）问题的变体。具体测试了transformer和多层感知机（multi-layer perceptron）等不同网络架构，并尝试将其与成熟的路径查找算法（pathfinder algorithm）结合以提升搜索效率与准确性。

Result: 对于节点数在10量级的箭图，由transformer和多层感知机构成的不同网络架构倾向于优于确定性算法。向网络补充成熟的路径查找算法（可类比为"面向箭图的Google地图"）可进一步改善搜索策略的效率与准确性。

Conclusion: 这类对偶性判定问题有望成为前沿AI模型应用于理论物理领域的有用基准（benchmark），同时本研究也揭示了不同网络架构如何学习追踪西伯格对偶性的内在机制。

Abstract: Dualities play an important role in establishing both microscopic and emergent phenomena in a wide range of physical systems. In practice, though, it can often be computationally challenging to establish when two systems are dual, even when all of the "rules of the game" are well-known. Said differently, when confronted with two systems, how can one efficiently establish that they are in fact dual? In this paper we use machine learning methods to address this question for Seiberg dualities of supersymmetric quiver gauge theories. Mathematically, this involves establishing mutations of quivers, which is in turn a variation on the theme of "learning to unknot". On the one hand, this leads us to a practical tool for establishing the computational complexity of different dualities. On the other hand, it also allows us to study how different network architectures learn how to trace Seiberg dualities. We find that for quivers with a modest number of quiver nodes (of order $10$), different network architectures consisting of transformers and multi-layer perceptrons tend to outperform deterministic algorithms. Supplementing the network by well-established pathfinder algorithms (essentially "Google Maps for quivers") leads to an additional improvement in the efficiency and accuracy of the search strategy. We anticipate that this class of questions can serve as a useful benchmark for frontier AI models applied to theoretical physics.

</details>

### [98] [Mean-Field Theory of Chiral Active Model B: Arrested Coarsening and Chiral Fingering Instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27305)
*Kristian Blom,Uwe Thiele*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文从手性伊辛模型（chiral Ising model）的主方程出发，推导并分析了一种平均场理论。通过空间离散演化和连续化过程，揭示了手性偏置（chiral bias）如何影响畴粗化（coarsening）并导致各向异性生长和手性诱导的粗化停滞（arrested coarsening），进一步通过线性稳定性分析发现了手性指状不稳定性（chiral fingering instability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在手性活性物质（chiral active matter）中，微观旋转偏置如何通过粗化过程塑造凝聚体的形貌，并揭示其非平衡动力学的基本机制。

Method: 从顺时针与逆时针旋转的2×2自旋块主方程出发，推导空间分辨平均磁化强度的离散演化方程；随后取连续极限得到活性场论（active field theory），其结构为弛豫型Model-B动力学附加手性流；最后利用连续理论对径向对称团簇进行线性稳定性分析。

Result: 在离散层面证明手性偏置会强烈影响相粗化：畴呈各向异性生长、近似矩形，最终出现手性诱导的粗化停滞；连续化明确展示了微观旋转偏置如何产生局域化在界面处的切向流；线性稳定性分析识别出手性指状不稳定性，其中界面角向扰动被放大，最终导致径向不对称的旋转态或无序态。

Conclusion: 微观手性偏置通过界面局域切向流驱动系统永久远离平衡，并在粗化过程中产生各向异性形貌与稳定的非对称旋转态，为理解手性活性物质中的凝聚体形貌提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: We derive and analyze a mean-field theory of the chiral Ising model recently introduced by Wang, Pietzonka, and Jülicher in "Edge Currents Shape Condensates in Chiral Active Matter", arXiv:2603.20064. Starting from the master equation for clockwise and counterclockwise rotations of 2x2 spin blocks, we first obtain spatially discrete evolution equations for the spatially resolved average magnetization. On this discrete level, we show that a chiral bias strongly affects phase coarsening: domains coarsen anisotropically, develop nearly rectangular shapes, and eventually display chirality-induced arrested coarsening. Taking the continuum limit of these equations yields an active field theory that has the structure of a relaxational Model-B-type dynamics supplemented by a chiral current that permanently drives the system out of equilibrium. The coarse graining explicitly shows how microscopic rotational bias generates tangential currents localized at interfaces. Using this continuum theory, we perform a linear stability analysis of radially symmetric clusters and identify a chiral fingering instability in which angular perturbations of the interface are amplified and eventually lead to radially asymmetric rotating states or disordered states.

</details>
