### [99] [Co-design of ground-based gravitational wave detector networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27254)
*Daniel Lanchares,Lysiane Mornas,Luigi Toffolatti,Pietro Vischia*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出IfoScout，一种基于先进机器学习（machine learning）技术的下一代地面引力波探测器协同设计方法。该方法采用两阶段模拟流程：先利用强化学习（reinforcement learning）在物理和地理约束下确定虚构L形干涉仪网络的长度与朝向，再通过微分编程（differential programming）优化腔内稳定性相关内部参数以提升灵敏度，从而在降低成本的同时为下一代探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜、宇宙探索者）的设计提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代地面引力波探测器的设计理念与选址仍在讨论之中，传统设计流程难以在物理和地理多重约束下高效权衡灵敏度与成本。作者希望借助机器学习技术实现探测器协同设计（co-design），在满足约束的同时优化灵敏度并间接降低成本。

Method: 作者提出两阶段模拟框架：第一阶段利用强化学习（reinforcement learning, RL）基于两个代表性选址的公开数据确定虚构L形干涉仪网络的长度和朝向；第二阶段采用微分编程（differential programming, DP）优化与腔体稳定性相关的内部探测器参数，以达成最佳灵敏度。

Result: 该框架在物理和地理约束下成功实现探测器网络灵敏度的优化，并通过优化设计间接减少了建设成本。作者论证了在具备精确选址数据（如地理和地质图）以及详细逼真的干涉仪模拟的前提下，IfoScout有望对下一代探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜Einstein Telescope、宇宙探索者Cosmic Explorer等）的最终设计产生积极影响。

Conclusion: IfoScout展示了机器学习技术在引力波探测器设计中的可行性与潜力，表明结合强化学习和微分编程的协同设计方法能够在多约束条件下优化灵敏度并降低成本，为未来新一代探测器的设计决策提供方法论支撑。

Abstract: Discussions around the design philosophy and location of the next generation of ground-based gravitational wave detectors are still underway. In this context, we propose IfoScout, an innovative methodology for detector co-design based on state-of-the-art machine-learning (ML) techniques. We present a two-stage simulation of a network of fictional L-shaped interferometers whose sensitivity is optimized within physical and geographical constraints, indirectly resulting in reducing the costs. To achieve this, we gather publicly available data for two token locations and establish the length and orientation with reinforcement learning (RL). Next, we optimize the internal detector parameters related to cavity stability to achieve the best possible sensitivity by means of differential programming (DP). We make the case that IfoScout could have a positive impact on the final design of new generation detectors (e.g. the Einstein Telescope, the Cosmic Explorer, etc.), given precise data (e.g. geographical and geological maps of chosen sites) and detailed, realistic simulations of the interferometers.

</details>


### [100] [Assessing the Impact of Instrumental Requirements on the Scientific Performance of the Einstein Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27311)
*Ulyana Dupletsa,Francesco Iacovelli,Mikhail Korobko,Valeria Sequino,Alessandro Agapito,Manuel Arca Sedda,Biswajit Banerjee,Nicolò Cibrario,Andrea Cozzumbo,Francesco Crescimbeni,Alessio Ludovico De Santis,Gabriele Franciolini,Yufeng Li,Michele Mancarella,Benedetta Mestichelli,Niccolò Muttoni,Lavinia Paiella,Ippocratis D. Saltas,Filippo Santoliquido,Pawan Tiwari,Cristiano Ugolini,Marica Branchesi,Archisman Ghosh,Jan Harms,Michele Maggiore,Fiodor Sorrentino*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了第三代替引力波天文台——爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）的仪器设计参数与其科学性能之间的关系，建立了将噪声预算退化映射到各项科学目标能力损失的综合评估框架，并在考虑最严重退化情况下发现ET的科学目标整体仍然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着第三代引力波观测站的设计和基础设施正在最终确定阶段，需要系统评估不同的技术设计方案（决定不同的噪声预算）如何影响探测器的科学能力，为最终设计决策提供量化依据。

Method: 定义了一组涵盖致密双星并合（CBC）探测与参数估计、随机引力波背景、孤立自旋中子星和核塌缩超新星等来源的综合性能指标，构建了将特定噪声成分和频段的退化与科学能力损失相关联的比较参考框架，并对代表性技术参数（如镀膜与悬架温度、低频仪器的滤波腔长度、高频仪器的束斑尺寸）进行了敏感性评估。

Result: 低于30 Hz的灵敏度影响大质量和高红移源的可探测性及长时程CBC信号的恢复，进而影响双中子星（BNS）的早期预警和天区定位；30-450 Hz范围主导大部分CBC参数估计指标；约450 Hz以上的高频灵敏度主要影响BNS并合后研究和超新星可探测性，对探测率影响有限。即使在最显著退化下，ET科学案例整体保持稳健。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了连接科学目标与仪器需求的综合基准框架，对第三代观测站的最终设计和基础设施建设具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between instrumental requirements and the scientific performance of the Einstein Telescope (ET), a third-generation (3G) gravitational-wave (GW) observatory. Different technical design choices result in distinct noise budgets, ultimately shaping the detector's scientific capabilities. To systematically assess and compare their impact, we define a comprehensive set of performance metrics spanning compact binary coalescence (CBC) detection and parameter estimation, as well as other sources, including stochastic GW backgrounds, isolated spinning neutron stars, and core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe). We build a comparative reference framework that links degradations in specific noise contributions and frequency bands to losses in scientific capabilities. We consider a representative selection of technical parameters, such as coating and suspension temperatures, the filter cavity length in the low-frequency instrument, and the beam size in the high-frequency instrument. We evaluate how sensitivity variations across specific frequency bands affect different scientific objectives. We quantify how the sensitivity below 30 Hz impacts the detectability of massive and/or high-redshift sources and the reconstruction of long-duration CBC signals, affecting early warning and sky localization for binary neutron stars (BNSs). Sensitivity in the 30-450 Hz range governs most CBC parameter-estimation metrics, while high-frequency sensitivity above ~450 Hz predominantly impacts BNS post-merger studies and CCSN detectability, with modest effects on detection rates. Even with the most significant degradations considered, the ET science case remains robust overall. Our results provide a comprehensive benchmark linking scientific objectives to instrumental requirements, particularly important as the final design and infrastructure of 3G observatories are being defined.

</details>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Regularized Bulk Universality versus Bounded-Disorder Nonuniversality for Annealed Complexity of Spherical $p$-Spin Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27613)
*Taegyun Kim*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文针对纯球面p自旋哈密顿量（pure spherical p-spin Hamiltonian），证明了具有光滑密度且紧支集的非高斯无规分布可以匹配前2p阶高斯矩，却仍然在正则化的体观普适性（regularized bulk universality）与无限制退火计数（unrestricted annealed complexity）之间产生分离。障碍源于约N^{1/p}个坐标的相干块，有限阶矩匹配不足以恢复未正则化的退火普适性；但矩匹配到足够高阶时，正则化压力量（regularized pressure）仍然实现普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自旋玻璃与高维随机几何交叉领域，一个核心问题是：无规分布（disorder distribution）需满足何种条件才能使可观测量（observables）呈现仅由前几阶矩决定的高斯普适行为？此前的工作多聚焦于正则化泛函（regularized functionals）的普适性，但对未受正则化保护的临界点计数等可观测量，其普适性条件尚不明确。

Method: 作者采用Kac-Rice公式（Kac-Rice formula）分析临界点计数的期望值，分别考虑正则化单点泛函与无限制临界点数。构造一类匹配前2p个高斯矩的对称、紧支且光滑密度的非高斯无规分布，并通过相干块（coherent block）结构构造反例来证明非普适性；同时推导匹配前m个高斯矩时正则化泛函对数期望比的上界估计，并引入高斯变分极限与二次能量-游走（energy-excursion）上界。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 匹配前2p个高斯矩的分布可使正则化的Kac-Rice泛函期望接近高斯值，但无限制临界点计数率指数严格高于高斯极限，从而建立分离；(2) 匹配前m个矩的次指数分布，正则化泛函的期望对数比受约束于N q_N^{m-1}量级，因矩匹配到m=2（即匹配均值与方差）即实现p≥3下的正则化压力普适性；(3) 当(m-1)(p-2)>2时期望比值趋于1；(4) 在Gaussian-rate profile-tail条件下，证明了高斯二次能量-游走的均匀上界；(5) 给出条件性约化到精确普适性的方案：当两个单侧去正则化缺陷消失时，精确最大值公式使局部化补集的控制成为充要条件，且在高斯模型中这些缺陷确实消失。

Conclusion: 论文表明提升矩匹配阶数可以恢复正则化泛函的普适性，但由于相干块的阻碍，即使匹配到前2p个矩也无法恢复无限制退火计数的普适性。这一分离结果深化了对多体随机能量景观中普适性边界的理解，并为精确普适性提供了基于去正则化缺陷消失的条件性判据。

Abstract: Fix $p\ge 3$. We establish a separation between regularized bulk universality and unrestricted annealed complexity for the pure spherical $p$-spin Hamiltonian with independent non-Gaussian tensor coordinates. There is a symmetric, compactly supported disorder law with a smooth density, matching the first $2p$ Gaussian moments, for which the expectation of a positive, frame-averaged regularization of the one-point Kac-Rice functional is asymptotic to its Gaussian counterpart, while the unrestricted critical-point count in a compact energy window has a lower exponential rate strictly above the Gaussian limit. The obstruction is a coherent block involving on the order of $N^{1/p}$ coordinates, so neither finite moment matching nor a uniform entry bound restores unregularized annealed universality. For uniformly subexponential disorder matching the first $m$ Gaussian moments, the regularized functional on incoherent frames satisfies $\left|\log\left(\mathbb{E}\mathcal{Z}_{N,L}^J/\mathbb{E}\mathcal{Z}_{N,L}^G\right)\right|\le C N q_N^{m-1}$, where $q_N=L^{p-1}N^{-(p-2)/2}(\log N)^{(p-1)/2}$. Thus mean and variance matching imply regularized pressure universality for every $p\ge 3$, and the expectation ratio tends to one when $(m-1)(p-2)>2$. We identify the Gaussian variational limit and prove the Gaussian quadratic energy-excursion upper bound uniformly over profiles asymptotically supported on $o(N)$ coordinates under a Gaussian-rate profile-tail condition. Finally, we give a conditional reduction to exact universality: once two one-sided de-regularization defects vanish, an exact max formula makes control of the localized complement necessary and sufficient. The defects vanish in the Gaussian model.

</details>


### [102] [Perturbed Beta Corners Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27810)
*Leonid Petrov,Jiaming Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究扰动β-角过程（perturbed β-corners process），它是经典β-角过程的一种形变。在经典β值（1,2,4）下，该扰动过程对应给GOE/GUE/GSE矩阵加上确定性对角矩阵后的特征值分布。作者通过多元Bessel函数（multivariate Bessel functions）将构造推广到所有β>0，并分析了β→∞时的晶化（crystallization）行为，证明了两种情形下的大数定律和中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典β-角过程是描述高斯随机矩阵（GOE/GUE/GSE）主子矩阵特征值联合分布的模型。通过引入确定性对角矩阵扰动，可以研究外部源（external source）对特征值交错阵列（interlacing array）极限行为的影响，特别是在结晶极限下的结构变化。这一研究旨在理解扰动如何与特征值排斥相互作用，以及如何在非经典β下统一处理。

Method: 通过多元Bessel函数将扰动β-角过程的构造从经典β值推广到所有β>0。在结晶极限（β→∞）下，分别对两种情形进行分析：固定扰动的弱外部源情形和扰动随β线性增长（a_i~β）的强外部源情形。利用最优性方程（optimality equations）刻画形变格点，并利用离散高斯自由场（discrete Gaussian free field）描述涨落。

Result: 在固定扰动情形下，特征值排斥占主导，交错阵列在与未扰动情形相同的多项式导数根格点上晶化，涨落也由相同的离散高斯自由场控制。在a_i随β线性增长的情形下，外部源在首阶上与排斥竞争，阵列冻结在由耦合最优性方程组刻画的形变格点上，涨落仍由离散高斯自由场控制但依附于形变格点。对于最后一个坐标上的单尖峰（single spike）特殊情形，形变格点方程解耦，可通过对函数应用一次移位导数算子D_c f = f' + cf后再进行迭代普通导数来显式获得。

Conclusion: 扰动β-角过程的结晶极限在不同扰动强度下展现不同的相行为：弱扰动下保持与未扰动情形相同的晶体结构和涨落特征，强扰动下则出现形变格点，但涨落普适性地仍由离散高斯自由场描述。这揭示了外部源与特征值排斥在首阶竞争时的新现象，并为理解带外部源随机矩阵的极限结构提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We introduce and study the perturbed $β$-corners process, a deformation of the classical $β$-corners process. The latter is a probability measure on interlacing arrays of real numbers that, for the classical values $β=1,2,4$, describes the joint distribution of eigenvalues of principal submatrices of a Gaussian random matrix with the corresponding GOE/GUE/GSE symmetry.
  For classical $β$, the perturbed process arises from adding a deterministic diagonal matrix $A=diag(a_1,...,a_N)$ to a GOE/GUE/GSE matrix. The eigenvalues of the resulting random matrix depend symmetrically on $a_1,...,a_N$, but this symmetry does not extend to the whole corners process.
  We extend the construction to all $β>0$ via multivariate Bessel functions, and analyze the crystallization ($β\to\infty$) of the resulting interlacing array, proving a law of large numbers and a central limit theorem in two regimes. For fixed perturbation, the eigenvalue repulsion dominates, and the array crystallizes on the same lattice of polynomial-derivative roots, with the same discrete Gaussian free field fluctuations, as in the unperturbed case treated by Gorin and Marcus (arXiv:1706.07393). New phenomena appear in the second regime, where the $a_i$'s grow linearly in $β$. Then the external source competes with the repulsion at leading order. Here the array freezes on a deformed lattice characterized by a coupled system of optimality equations. The fluctuations are governed by the same discrete Gaussian free field, now attached to the deformed lattice.
  The deformed lattice equations decouple in the special case of a single spike in the last coordinate. Then the deformed lattice is obtained explicitly by applying one shifted derivative $D_c f = f' + cf$ followed by iterated ordinary derivatives.

</details>


### [103] [On two differing geometric descriptions of the passage from microscopy to macroscopy in Markov diffusion theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28578)
*Dalton A R Sakthivadivel*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在黎曼流形上构造了一个中心对象，将扩散现象中微观—介观—宏观的三级层次结构部分统一，连接了粒子速度到测度演化的连续性算子在不同希尔伯特完备化下的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机过程如何过渡到抛物型偏微分方程，并进一步转化为涉及梯度和变分计算的函数不等式是多个领域共同关注的问题；扩散现象存在从粒子、力场、经验测度到光滑律这一描述层级结构，但干预这一层级时对连续性算子如何将粒子速度转化为测度演化存在选择空间，需要一中心对象来中介两种不同描述。

Method: 将闭黎曼流形上光滑正概率密度空间视为Fréchet流形，其全连续余切空间由非常值分布构成、正则余切空间由充分正则的非常值函数构成；以基流形微分同胚的无穷小提升算子为起点，对该密度空间进行两种不同的希尔伯特（Hilbert）完备化，以涵盖广泛相关对象。

Result: 两种希尔伯特完备化能够涵盖一大类相关对象，从而实现了马尔可夫分析（Markov analysis）中微观—介观—宏观层级的部分普适化（partial universalisation）。

Conclusion: 通过在中介密度空间的两种完备化之间建立联系，本文为马尔可夫扩散描述的统一框架提供了部分普适的构建途径。

Abstract: In various disparate settings one studies how random processes give way to parabolic partial differential equations and in turn to functional inequalities involving gradients and variational calculus. Particles subject to gradient forces, the fields which move them, the empirical measures which they form, the smooth laws which those measures approximate, and differentiable functionals of these laws constitute a hierarchy of descriptions of diffusion. Intervening on this hierarchy is a choice of how a conintuity operator converts particle velocity to the evolution of measures. Here a central object is constructed mediating two different such descriptions. The space of smooth positive probability densities on a closed Riemannian manifold is treated as a Fréchet manifold whose full continuous cotangent space consists of nonconstant distributions and whose regular cotangent space consists of sufficiently regular nonconstant functions. Beginning from the operator on this space arising as the infinitesimal lift of diffeomorphisms of the base manifold, two different Hilbert completions of this space of densities account for a wide class of objects relevant, leading to a partial universalisation of the microscopic--mesoscopic--macroscopic hierarchy in Markov analysis.

</details>

### [104] [Gaussian non relativistic spontaneusly stochastic hydrodynamica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27540)
*David Montenegro,Giorgio Torrieri*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯协变流体动力学（Gaussian covariant hydrodynamics）的非相对论极限。作者主张不可压缩性提供了与相对论流体动力学相匹配的额外对称性，但不可压缩性必须在"微观"尺度上被打破。论文随后针对该尺度发展平均量与涨落的重整化群方程（renormalization group equations），以理解其在流体动力学让位于统计力学（statistical mechanics）的分子间距离尺度上的效应。所得动力学自然地将自发随机性（spontaneous stochasticity）纳入为统计力学涨落的宏观反作用，并呈现出与反常耗散（anomalous dissipation）及"野解"（wild solutions）相似的重整化群对消项（counterterms）特征。论文同时从现象学角度讨论流体动力学适用极限，以及物理学如何为湍流的数学问题提供启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图弥合流体动力学与统计力学在介观尺度上的鸿沟，并探究不可压缩性假设失效的物理后果，进而为湍流中已知的数学疑难（如非唯一性、反常耗散）提供物理层面的物理解释框架。

Method: 论文从高斯协变流体动力学的非相对论极限出发，论证不可压缩性的额外对称性及其在微观尺度的必然破缺；随后构建针对该破缺尺度的重整化群方程，分别处理平均流动与统计涨落的随尺度演化行为。

Result: 研究发现该重整化群框架自然产生自发随机性作为统计力学涨落的宏观反作用；同时，反常耗散与"野解"等现象以重整化群对消项的形式出现，表明这些数学特征可能具有物理实在性。

Conclusion: 论文将上述结果分别置于流体动力学适用极限的现象学讨论，以及物理学对湍流相关数学问题（如解的非正则性与非唯一性）可能提供的启示两个层面加以总结，表明介观尺度重整化可能是连接宏观流体与微观统计力学、并解释湍流异常行为的关键桥梁。

Abstract: We study the non-relativistic limit of Gaussian covariant hydrodynamics [1]. We argue that the condition of incompressibility provides additional symmetries matching relativistic hydrodynamics but incompressibility must break down at a ``microscopic`` scale. We then develop the renormalization group equations for average and fluctuations w.r.t. that scale, to understand its effect on flows at intermolecular distances where hydrodynamics gives way to statistical mechanics. The resulting dynamics naturally incorporates spontaneous stochasticity as a macroscopic back reaction of statistical mechanics fluctuations, as well as features reminiscent of anomalous dissipation and ``wild solutions`` as renormalization group counterterms. We frame these considerations into both a phenomenological discussion of the limits of applicability of fluid dynamics, and a discussion of where physics might shed some light on the mathematical issues associated with turbulence.

</details>

### [105] [Study of $t\bar{t}H$ and $tH$ production in the $H\toττ$ channel in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27276)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS探测器在LHC上记录的质心能量为13 TeV和13.6 TeV的质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度分别为140 fb⁻¹和161 fb⁻¹），在τ轻子对衰变道中研究了与顶夸克对（t̄tH）或单顶夸克（tH）联合产生的希格斯玻色子，测得的信号强度分别为μ_t̄tH = 1.51⁺⁰·⁷¹₋₀.₆₂和μ_tH = -0.4⁺⁵·⁶₋₅.₁，并在简化模板截面框架下对t̄tH截面进行了微分测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量希格斯玻色子与顶夸克对或单顶夸克的联合产生截面，可用于验证标准模型（Standard Model）对粒子产生率的预言，并对CKM矩阵元（CKM matrix element）和顶夸克-希格斯玻色子汤川耦合（Yukawa coupling）等关键参数进行约束。

Method: 分析基于顶夸克和τ轻子均为全强子衰变的末态，利用ATLAS探测器记录的质子-质子对撞数据，在简化模板截面框架（simplified template cross-section framework）下对t̄tH截面在希格斯玻色子横动量（transverse momentum）的三个区间内进行微分测量。

Result: 测得的t̄tH信号强度为μ_t̄tH = 1.51⁺⁰·⁷¹₋₀.₆₂，tH信号强度为μ_tH = -0.4⁺⁵·⁶₋₅.₁，其中信号强度定义为实测截面与标准模型预言的比值。

Conclusion: t̄tH信号强度的测量结果与标准模型预言一致，而tH信号的测量不确定性较大，尚不能给出明确结论。

Abstract: A study of the production of the Higgs boson in association with either a top-quark pair ($t\bar{t}H$) or a single top quark ($tH$) in the $τ$-lepton-pair decay channel is presented. The analysis relies on final states featuring fully hadronic decays of the top quarks and the $τ$-leptons. It employs data samples of proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ and 13.6 TeV recorded with the ATLAS detector at the CERN Large Hadron Collider and corresponding to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 161 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. The measured signal strength ($μ$), defined as the measured cross-section normalised to the Standard Model prediction, is $μ_{t\bar{t}H}=1.51^{+0.71}_{-0.62}$ for $t\bar{t}H$ and $μ_{tH}=-0.4^{+5.6}_{-5.1}$ for $tH$. Additionally, the $t\bar{t}H$ cross-section is measured differentially in three bins of the Higgs boson transverse momentum in the simplified template cross-section framework.

</details>


### [106] [Search for heavy Majorana neutrinos in vector boson scattering with $τ$-lepton final states with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27307)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上通过矢量玻色子散射（vector boson scattering）搜索耦合到第三代轻子的重型马约拉纳中微子（heavy Majorana neutrino）的研究。利用ATLAS探测器记录的质心能量为13 TeV、积分亮度为140 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，在具有同号ττ、eτ或μτ对的末态中进行搜索，未观察到显著超出标准模型背景的偏离，并在92 GeV至6.5 TeV的质量范围内对耦合参数|V_{τN}|²及二维参数空间施加了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型马约拉纳中微子是超越标准模型（Standard Model）的重要候选粒子，其存在可为中微子质量来源（跷跷板机制）提供实验线索。此前对重型马约拉纳中微子的搜索主要集中在前两代轻子耦合，本文旨在填补第三代轻子（τ轻子）耦合通道的空白，并探索尚未被覆盖的质量区间。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器在质心能量√s = 13 TeV下记录的质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度140 fb⁻¹），选择矢量玻色子散射过程产生的末态，要求包含一对同号（same-sign）带电轻子（ττ、eτ或μτ组合），并据此构建信号区域进行统计分析与模型无关的背景估计。

Result: 搜寻结果与标准模型背景预期一致，未发现显著的信号偏离。在重型马约拉纳中微子质量介于92 GeV至6.5 TeV的范围内，推导出|V_{τN}|²的上限约束，同时设定了(|V_{eN}|², |V_{τN}|²)及(|V_{μN}|², |V_{τN}|²)的二维约束。这些结果在质量大于1.0 TeV时优于此前对第三代轻子耦合的极限，并在超过1.5 TeV的未探索质量区间首次设置了排除界限。

Conclusion: 本研究显著提升了对耦合到第三代轻子的重型马约拉纳中微子的实验约束能力，尤其在高质量区间（>1.0 TeV）取得了更严格的极限，并首次将排除范围拓展至1.5 TeV以上的未知质量区域，为未来更高亮度数据下的进一步搜索提供了重要参考基准。

Abstract: A search for heavy Majorana neutrinos coupling to third-generation leptons in vector boson scattering at the LHC is presented. The search is performed in the final state with a same-sign $ττ$, $eτ$ or $μτ$ pair using data of proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, recorded with the ATLAS detector and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 $\text{fb}^{-1}$. No significant deviation from the Standard Model background is observed. Constraints on the squared mixing matrix element between the $τ$-lepton and the heavy Majorana neutrino ($|V_{τN}|^2$) are derived for heavy Majorana neutrino masses between 92 GeV and 6.5 TeV. Additionally, two-dimensional constraints on ($|V_{eN}|^{2}, |V_{τN}|^{2}$) and ($|V_{μN}|^{2}, |V_{τN}|^{2}$) are set. These results improve the limits on heavy Majorana neutrinos coupling to third-generation leptons for heavy Majorana neutrino masses above 1.0 TeV, and extend the exclusion limits to an unexplored mass range above 1.5 TeV.

</details>


### [107] [First Experimental Bounds on Transverse Plasmon Solar Axions with ANAIS-112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27346)
*S. J. Hollick,M. Giannotti,J. Ruz,J. K. Vogel,R. M. Alkaddah,J. Amaré,J. Apilluelo,S. Bharat,S. Cebrián,D. Cintas,I. Coarasa,E. García,M. Martínez,Y. Ortigoza,A. Ortiz de Solórzano,T. Pardo,J. Puimedón,M. L. Sarsa,C. Seoane*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文报告了首个针对太阳轴子类粒子（ALPs）通过日冕内部横向等离激元（transverse plasmon）共振产生机制开展的实验搜索。利用ANAIS-112探测器625.75 kg·年的曝光数据，通过逆Primakoff机制搜索由地球-太阳距离变化引起的年调制信号，首次给出了包含共振横向等离激元通量的轴子-光子耦合约束：g_aγ < 1.32×10⁻⁹ GeV⁻¹（90%置信水平，m_a ≈ 150 eV），在35–280 eV的质量范围内将此前未探索的产生通道纳入灵敏度覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的轴子产生机制主要是Primakoff过程（太阳内部光子与电磁场的电弱耦合），而横向等离激元在太阳内部磁场中通过共振转换产生ALPs是一种互补的、此前未经实验检验的产生通道。该通道在特定质量范围内预期的通量比Primakoff通量大两个数量级，因此有必要对此进行首次实验搜索。

Method: 利用ANAIS-112探测器累积的625.75 kg·年曝光数据，在1–20 keV能量范围内按1 keV能量窗拟合年调制幅度（由地球-太阳距离的周期性变化引起）。检测依赖于探测器中的逆Primakoff转换，即太阳ALPs转换回光子而被观测。

Result: 各能量窗拟合得到的调制振幅与无调制假设一致，未观测到显著信号。由此给出轴子-光子耦合的首次包含共振横向等离激元通量的约束，在m_a ≈ 150 eV处g_aγ < 1.32×10⁻⁹ GeV⁻¹（90%置信水平），覆盖质量范围35–280 eV。

Conclusion: 该研究将ANAIS-112的灵敏度扩展到了包含共振横向等离激元产生通道的质量区间，表明在预期的太阳磁场模型中，此通道的通量可超过Primakoff通量最多两个数量级，为未来更高灵敏度实验提供了新的探索方向。

Abstract: Solar axion-like particles (ALPs) can be resonantly produced through the conversion of transverse plasmons in the magnetic field of the solar interior, introducing a production mechanism complementary to the conventional Primakoff process. In this Letter, we report the first experimental search for solar ALPs produced through this mechanism. Using an exposure of 625.75 kg$\times$yr from ANAIS-112, we search for the annual modulation of the flux induced by the variation of the Earth--Sun distance, assuming detection through inverse Primakoff conversion in the detector. Modulation amplitudes fitted in 1 keV bins between 1--20 keV are consistent with the absence of modulation, yielding the first constraints on the axion--photon coupling that include the resonant transverse-plasmon flux, $g_{aγ} < 1.32\times 10^{-9} \mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ (90\% C.L.) at $m_a \simeq 150$ eV. This previously unexplored production channel extends the sensitivity of ANAIS-112 in the mass range 35--280 eV, where, for the expected solar magnetic-field models, the transverse-plasmon flux exceeds the Primakoff flux by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [108] [Search for $\textit{tH}$ production in the $ H \to ττ$ decay mode, and a combination with other searches, using $\textit{pp}$ collisions at 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27708)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了ATLAS探测器对希格斯玻色子与单个顶夸克联合产生（tH）过程的多轻子末态搜索，基于140 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，并与此前不同末态的结果组合，得到信号强度μ_tH = 3.3±1.2（统计）⁺¹·¹₋₁·₀（系统）倍标准模型预言，观测（期望）显著性为2.3（0.8）个标准偏差，这是迄今对tH产生最精确的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tH产生过程对探测希格斯玻色子与顶夸克的汤川耦合符号具有重要敏感性，可为标准模型外新物理提供约束。该过程截面极小且背景复杂，需要大统计量数据与多末态组合才能获得具有统计意义的测量结果。

Method: 利用LHC上ATLAS探测器采集的140 fb⁻¹、质心能量√s=13 TeV的质子-质子碰撞数据，搜索包含τ轻子的多轻子末态中H→ττ衰变（至少一个τ轻子强子衰变）的tH产生信号。随后将该结果与ATLAS此前在相同产生过程但不同末态发表的分析进行组合，后者分别使用140 fb⁻¹（13 TeV）和164 fb⁻¹（13.6 TeV）的数据。

Result: 单次测量未观测到超出预期背景的显著过剩，95%置信水平下观测（期望）信号强度上限为标准模型预言的8.6（9.3）倍。组合测量得到信号强度μ_tH = 3.3⁺¹·⁷₋₁·₅ = 3.3 ± 1.2（统计）⁺¹·¹₋₁·₀（系统）倍标准模型期望，对应观测（期望）显著性为2.3（0.8）个标准偏差，组合的95%置信水平观测（期望）上限为6.1（2.7）倍标准模型预言。

Conclusion: 当前的组合测量未达到确认tH产生过程的统计显著性水平，但已构成迄今为止对tH产生最精确的测量，对未来更高统计量的实验数据具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: A search for the production of a Higgs boson in association with a single top quark in multi-lepton final states including $τ$-leptons is presented, using an integrated luminosity of $140\,\text{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13\,\text{TeV}$ collected by the ATLAS detector at the LHC. The analysis targets the $H \to ττ$ decay, where at least one $τ$-lepton decays hadronically, to indicate the presence of the Higgs boson. No significant excess over the expected background is observed, and an observed (expected) upper limit at the 95% confidence level on the signal strength is set at 8.6 (9.3) times the Standard Model prediction. This measurement is combined with previous publications of the ATLAS Collaboration targeting the same production process but in different final states, using $140\,\text{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s} = 13\,\text{TeV}$ and $164\,\text{fb}^{-1}$ at $13.6\,\text{TeV}$. The combined measurement of the signal strength yields $μ_{tH} = 3.3^{+1.7}_{-1.5} = 3.3 \pm 1.2\,(\text{stat.})^{+1.1}_{-1.0}\,(\text{sys.})$ times the Standard Model expectation, corresponding to an observed (expected) $\textit{tH}$ significance of 2.3 (0.8) standard deviations. The combined observed (expected) upper limit at the 95% confidence level on the signal strength is set at 6.1 (2.7) times the Standard Model prediction. This combination represents the most precise measurement of $tH$ production to date.

</details>


### [109] [Comment on the preprint: First Limits on Axion Dark Matter from a DALI Prototype arXiv:2603.21951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28095)
*Erika Garutti,Anton Ivanov,Béla Majorovits*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对DALI装置探测轴子类粒子（axion-like particle, ALP）暗物质的灵敏度进行了独立评估，基于预印本提供的信息和MADMAX合作组发展的形式体系，未发现12 GHz以下与轴子场存在显著耦合的模式以支持所报告的灵敏度，并提出了介电腔（dielectric haloscope）实验中评估电磁模式轴子耦合的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对DALI装置声称的轴子暗物质探测灵敏度进行独立核实。基于预印本信息，使用MADMAX合作组发展的形式体系进行交叉评估，以检验其报告结果的可信度。

Method: 采用MADMAX合作组内部发展的形式体系（formalism）作为独立分析框架，对DALI装置预印本中描述的实验配置进行模式分析，逐一检验各电磁模式与轴子场的耦合强度。

Result: 在12 GHz以下未识别出任何与轴子场具有显著耦合的电磁模式，因此无法支撑DALI所报告的探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: DALI装置所声称的灵敏度缺乏充分依据；本文建议介电腔实验中评估电磁模式轴子耦合时应采用更严谨的方法，并给出了相应建议。

Abstract: We report on an independent assessment of the sensitivity of the DALI setup to axion-like particle (ALP) dark matter, as described in [1]. Our analysys is based on the information provided in the preprint and using the formalism developed within the MADMAX collaboration. We do not identify any mode below 12 GHz with significant coupling to the axion field to account for the reported sensitivity. We provide recommendations for assessing the axion coupling of electromagnetic modes in dielectric haloscope experiments.

</details>


### [110] [Search for high-mass dilepton resonances in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV combined with 13 TeV results using the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28334)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用ATLAS探测器在LHC上记录的13.6 TeV（165 fb⁻¹）和13 TeV（140 fb⁻¹）两批质子-质子对撞数据，对高质量区自旋为1（spin-1）的ee和μμ共振态进行搜寻。对双轻子不变质量谱进行功能性拟合以建模标准模型背景，未观察到显著偏离，因此在95%置信水平下对自旋1共振态的基准截面乘以分支比设定上限，并提取了多个Z'基准模型的质量下限，其中Z′_SSM为5.5 TeV，是迄今为止最严格的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高轻子对不变质量共振态是探测超出标准模型新物理（如额外的规范玻色子Z'）的直接途径，本研究旨在利用更大数据量和高能质子-质子对撞来推动对这类共振态的质量搜索极限。

Method: 使用ATLAS探测器两批质子-质子对撞数据集（13.6 TeV、165 fb⁻¹，以及13 TeV、140 fb⁻¹）进行搜寻，并对两批结果进行统计合并（statistical combination）。双轻子不变质量谱采用函数形式拟合以建模标准模型背景贡献，使用通用自旋1信号形状评估观测偏离背景预期的显著性。

Result: 未观察到显著偏离背景预期的信号。在95%置信水平下，对自旋1共振态在ee、μμ终态及其组合下分别施加基准截面乘以分支比的上限。从13 TeV和13.6 TeV合并结果中提取基准模型玻色子质量下限，包括Z′_SSM（5.5 TeV）、Z′_χ（5.1 TeV）和Z′_ψ（4.8 TeV）。

Conclusion: 这些结果代表了迄今为止对所考虑的Z'模型在高质量双轻子共振态搜寻方面最严格的限制，表明在当前数据量下未发现新共振态的证据。

Abstract: A search for spin-1 $ee$ and $μμ$ resonances in the high-mass range is presented. Two proton-proton ($pp$) collision datasets recorded by the ATLAS detector at CERN's Large Hadron Collider are utilised. A search is first performed using a dataset recorded at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6 TeV between 2022 and 2024, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 165 fb$^{-1}$. The results of this search are subsequently statistically combined with results using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of data recorded between 2015 and 2018 at 13 TeV. The dilepton invariant mass spectrum is fitted with a functional form to model the contribution from Standard Model background processes. A generic spin-1 signal shape is used to determine the significance of observed deviations from the background expectation. No significant deviation is observed, therefore upper limits are placed at the 95% confidence level on the fiducial cross-section times branching ratio for a spin-1 resonance with various width hypotheses, individually for $ee$, $μμ$ final states and for their combination. From the combined 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV results, mass limits are extracted for various benchmark model bosons, including a $Z^{\prime}_{\text{SSM}}$ (5.5 TeV), $Z^{\prime}_χ$ (5.1 TeV) and $Z^{\prime}_ψ$ (4.8 TeV). These results represent the most stringent limits to date on high-mass dilepton resonances on the considered $Z'$ models.

</details>


### [111] [Search for pair-production of vector-like $T$ quarks decaying into a top quark and a spin-0 particle in the diphoton final state in proton proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28381)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了对成对产生的矢量类T夸克（vector-like T quark，VLQ）的搜寻。每个VLQ假设衰减为一个顶夸克（top quark）和一个奇异（伪）标量粒子（pseudo)scalar），并通过包含共振$S \to \gamma\gamma$衰变的最终态进行搜索。基于ATLAS探测器在$\sqrt{s}$=13 TeV质子-质子对撞中2015至2018年记录的140 fb$^{-1}$积分亮度数据，未发现超出标准模型背景的显著信号，并给出基准截面乘以分支比的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找成对产生的矢量类T夸克及其新颖衰变模式，探索超越标准模型（Standard Model）的物理现象，特别是首次在该特定的$S \to \gamma\gamma$最终态中对VLQ进行搜索。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器2015至2018年在$\sqrt{s}$=13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的积分亮度为140 fb$^{-1}$的数据。搜索事件要求至少一个共振$S \to \gamma\gamma$衰变，以及来自其他最终态粒子的大横向动量（transverse momentum）总和与大的缺失横向动量（missing transverse momentum）。分析在很大程度上是模型无关的，但针对级联衰变到包含电弱玻色子或$t\bar{t}$的最终态。

Result: 未观察到超过标准模型背景的显著超出，该结果以基准截面（fiducial cross-section）乘以分支比（branching ratio）的上限形式给出，作为VLQ质量与自旋零粒子质量的函数。

Conclusion: 本研究是首次在该最终态中进行的VLQ搜索，虽然未发现显著信号，但提供了新的排除界限，基准区域（fiducial volume）由自旋零粒子衰变产生的光子在运动学上的要求定义。

Abstract: A search for pair-produced vector-like T quarks (VLQs) is presented. Each VLQ is assumed to decay into a top quark and an exotic (pseudo)scalar S. The search targets events with at least one resonant $S \to γγ$ decay and large summed transverse momentum and missing transverse momentum from other final-state particles. The analysis uses data from $pp$ collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector from 2015 to 2018 and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. This is the first VLQ search performed in this final state. No significant excess over the Standard Model background is observed. The analysis is model-independent to a large extent but targets cascade decays into final states with electroweak bosons or $t\bar{t}$. Results are provided in the form of upper limits on the fiducial cross-section times branching ratio as a function of the masses of the VLQ and the spin-0 particle. The fiducial volume is defined by kinematic requirements on the photons from the spin-0 decay.

</details>


### [112] [A measurement of the Higgs boson mass in the diphoton decay channel in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28396)
*CMS Collaboration*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高希格斯玻色子质量测量的精度，此前测量结果存在进一步改进空间，需要通过改进探测器标定和新的分析技术来缩小不确定性。

Method: 使用CMS探测器在质心能量13 TeV下记录的数据（2016-2018年，积分亮度138 fb^-1），在双光子（diphoton）衰变道中测量希格斯玻色子质量，并采用精化探测器标定与新的分析技术提升测量精度。

Result: 质心能量13 TeV下测得质量m_H = 125.13 ± 0.10（统计） ± 0.12（系统） GeV；与7和8 TeV双光子末态质量测量组合后得到m_H = 125.06 ± 0.09（统计） ± 0.11（系统） GeV。

Conclusion: 新的测量结果相较此前实验在精度上有所提升，且与7、8 TeV数据的组合结果保持一致，进一步精确限定了希格斯玻色子质量。

Abstract: A measurement of the Higgs boson mass in the diphoton decay channel is performed using proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. The data set recorded with the CMS detector between 2016 and 2018 is used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. A refined detector calibration and new analysis techniques are employed to improve the precision of the results compared to earlier measurements. The Higgs boson mass is measured to be $m_\mathrm{H}$ = 125.13 $\pm$ 0.15 GeV = 125.13 $\pm$ 0.10 (stat) $\pm$ 0.12 (syst) GeV. In addition, a combination with the mass measurement at center-of-mass energies of 7 and 8 TeV in the diphoton final state is performed resulting in $m_\mathrm{H}$ = 125.06 $\pm$ 0.14 GeV = 125.06 $\pm$ 0.09 (stat) $\pm$ 0.11 (syst) GeV.

</details>


### [113] [Search for Lorentz-boosted di-$τ$ resonances produced in association with top quark pairs in $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV pp collisions with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28484)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了 ATLAS 探测器对洛伦兹增强的赝标量共振 a（质量范围 20–85 GeV）在顶夸克对伴生产生并衰变为 τ 轻子对过程中的搜寻结果。基于 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度 140 fb⁻¹），未发现显著信号超出，并在 95% 置信水平下对可见截面给出了 0.19 fb 的模型无关上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在搜寻低质量赝标量共振（pseudoscalar resonance）在顶夸克对（t\bar{t}）伴生产生并衰变为 τ 轻子对的过程，从而检验超出标准模型（Standard Model）的新物理信号，尤其是涉及 Two-Higgs-Doublet 模型的预测。

Method: 利用 ATLAS 探测器在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上采集的质心能量为 13 TeV 的质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度 140 fb⁻¹），两个 τ 轻子均以强子衰变模式重建。由于洛伦兹增强（Lorentz-boosted）产生导致衰变产物角间距很小，采用专门定制的增强型双 τ 对象（boosted di-τ），包含自定义重建、能量刻度与鉴别程序。

Result: 未观察到相对于标准模型背景的显著偏差。在 95% 置信水平下，对可见截面给出模型无关上限 0.19 fb；在 Two-Higgs-Doublet Model 信号场景下，对 σ(t\bar{t}a)×BR(a→ττ) 给出从 m_a=20 GeV 时 0.4 pb 到 m_a=85 GeV 时 0.05 pb 的限制。

Conclusion: 在当前数据统计量下未发现该过程的新物理证据，所给出的截面限制对赝标量共振在 20–85 GeV 质量区间的存在性提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: A search for a Lorentz-boosted pseudoscalar resonance $a$ in the mass range between 20 GeV and 85 GeV, produced in association with a top-antitop quark pair and decaying into a $τ$-lepton pair, is presented. Both $τ$-leptons are reconstructed in the hadronic decay mode. The boosted production of $a$ results in a small angular separation between its decay products, requiring the use of dedicated boosted di-$τ$ objects with custom reconstruction, energy calibration, and identification procedures. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass-energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV collected with the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. No significant deviation is observed over the expected Standard Model background, and a model independent upper limit of 0.19 fb is placed at the 95% confidence level on the visible cross-section. Limits on $σ(t\bar{t}a) \times BR(a \to ττ)$ are also obtained for a Two-Higgs-Doublet Model signal scenario, ranging from 0.4 pb at $m_a=20$ GeV to 0.05 pb at $m_a=85$ GeV.

</details>


### [114] [Extraction of $σ_{TT}$ for Proton, Neutron, Deuteron and $^3$He from Quasi-real Photon Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28612)
*YiLei Li,B. Callahan,M. M. Dalton,A. Deur,O. Larson,A. Rask,D. W. Upton,X. Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文基于美国杰斐逊实验室（Jefferson Lab）的E97-110（³He）与CLAS EG4（质子与氘核）实验数据，通过将电子散射数据外推到实光子点（real photon point），提取了质子、氘核、中子和³He的极化光产生截面（polarized photoproduction cross-section）。利用弱束缚近似（weak binding approximation）从氘核或³He数据中提取中子信息。与实光子直接测量结果对比发现，质子结果一致，而氘核与中子在Δ(1232)共振区显示出更强的强度，且与质子的结果相比较更符合同位旋对称性（isospin symmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过电子散射数据外推的方法提取实光子点的极化光产生截面，以补充直接测量数据的不足，并为检验同位旋对称性和核子激发态结构提供更完整的信息。

Method: 将杰斐逊实验室E97-110（³He）和CLAS EG4（质子和氘核）的电子散射数据外推到实光子点来获得极化光产生截面；弱束缚近似被用于从氘核或³He数据中提取中子的相关信息。

Result: 与实光子直接测量数据比较显示，质子结果一致；氘核和中子在Δ(1232)区域表现出更大强度，且与质子结果相比更符合同位旋对称性。

Conclusion: 利用外推方法得到的氘核和中子数据与实光子测量存在差异，提示在Δ(1232)区域可能存在未被直接实验充分捕捉的物理效应，且结果更支持同位旋对称性的假设。

Abstract: We report on an extraction of the polarized photoproduction cross-section for the proton, deuteron, neutron and $^3$He, obtained by extrapolating electron scattering data to the real photon point. The data are from the Jefferson Lab E97-110 ($^3$He) and CLAS EG4 (proton and deuteron) experiments. Information on the neutron is extracted from the deuteron or $^3$He data using the weak binding approximation. Comparing with data obtained with real photons, we find that while the proton results agree, our results on the deuteron and the neutron exhibit a larger strength in the $Δ(1232)$ region, and are more consistent with isospin symmetry when compared with the proton results.

</details>

### [115] [Volume Stability for Hyperbolic Manifolds and Applications to General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27666)
*Puskar Mondal,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭双曲三维流形的尖锐体积稳定性定理：若光滑度量满足标量曲率下界R≥-6且体积趋于极小体积，则除去体积趋于零的集合外，度量在张量C⁰意义下收敛到双曲度量。作为应用，建立了Fischer–Moncrief约化哈密顿量在洛伦兹锥基态附近的稳定性，即CMC归一化后，双曲拓扑类中近极小的紧致真空数据在除去零体积集合后收敛到双曲洛伦兹锥几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双曲三维流形上，经典的尖锐体积不等式给出体积下界，但等号附近的稳定性（即近等号时度量是否趋于双曲度量）此前缺乏严格的张量C⁰收敛刻画。此外，Fischer–Moncrief提出的约化哈密顿量在洛伦兹锥基态附近的渐近图像需要严格的体积主导（volume-dominance）表述来给出数学基础。

Method: 方法基于对闭双曲流形(M,h)上满足R(g_i)≥-6且体积趋于Vol_h(M)的度量序列g_i，证明存在体积趋于零的集合Z_i与光滑域K_i以及微分同胚ψ_i:K_i→M∖Z_i，使得‖ψ_i^*g_i−h‖_{C^0(K_i,h)}→0。这一结果将度量序列的张量C⁰收敛与除零体积区域的体积收敛性精确对应起来。

Result: 证明了尖锐体积稳定性定理：在标量曲率下界及体积收敛条件下，近等号的度量序列在除去体积趋于零的集合后，于张量C⁰范数下收敛到双曲度量。作为推论，建立了Fischer–Moncrief约化哈密顿量在洛伦兹锥基态处的稳定性，即CMC归一化后的近极小紧致真空数据在双曲拓扑类中收敛到双曲洛伦兹锥几何。

Conclusion: 该结果将尖锐双曲体积界附近的行为精确刻画为"体积主导"的张量C⁰收敛，为Fischer–Moncrief关于洛伦兹锥基态渐近图像的猜想提供了严格的数学表述与证明。这为广义相对论中双曲拓扑类真空时空的稳定性研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We prove a sharp volume-stability theorem for closed hyperbolic three-manifolds. Let \((M,h)\) be closed hyperbolic with \(\operatorname{Ric}_h=-2h\), and let \(g_i\) be smooth metrics on \(M\) satisfying $R(g_i)\geq -6, \operatorname{Vol}_{g_i}(M)\longrightarrow \operatorname{Vol}_h(M)$. After passing to a subsequence, there exist \(Z_i\subset M\), smooth domains \(K_i\subset M\), and diffeomorphisms $ψ_i:K_i\longrightarrow M\setminus Z_i $
such that $\operatorname{Vol}_{g_i}(Z_i)\longrightarrow0, \operatorname{Vol}_h(M\setminus K_i)\longrightarrow0, $
and $
\|ψ_i^*g_i-h\|_{C^0(K_i,h)}\longrightarrow0. $
Thus near-equality in the sharp hyperbolic volume bound forces tensorial \(C^0\)-convergence to the hyperbolic metric outside regions of vanishing volume. As an application, we establish stability of the Fischer--Moncrief reduced Hamiltonian at the Lorentz-cone ground state: after CMC normalization, near-minimizing compact vacuum data in the hyperbolic topological class converge, modulo sets of vanishing volume, to the hyperbolic Lorentz-cone geometry in tensorial \(C^0\). This provides a rigorous volume-dominance formulation of the Fischer--Moncrief asymptotic picture.

</details>


### [116] [SU(3)-structures on quotients of 3-Sasakian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28337)
*Quentin Peres,Dimitrios Tsimpis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究7维拟正则3-Sasakian轨道流形（quasi-regular 3-Sasakian orbifold）的商空间，证明其继承了一个由两个实参数刻画的SU(3)结构，且该结构为LT结构（LT-structure）；在某些参数取值下可得到近Kähler结构（nearly Kähler structure），并给出了正则、拟正则和轨道情形的若干例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 7维3-Sasakian流形（3-Sasakian manifold）与SU(3)几何之间存在密切联系，理解其商空间上的几何结构有助于揭示特殊几何（special geometry）之间的构造与分类关系。

Method: 从拟正则3-Sasakian轨道流形出发，通过选取其中一个Reeb向量场（Reeb vector field）取商空间，构造出由两个实参数族化的SU(3)结构，并通过计算其挠率（torsion）验证该结构满足LT结构的条件。

Result: 商空间继承的SU(3)结构为LT结构；在特定参数取值下，该结构退化为近Kähler结构作为构造的特例；分别在正则、拟正则和轨道情形提供了具体实例。

Conclusion: 该构造建立了从7维3-Sasakian轨道几何到6维近Kähler几何之间的一族连续参数化桥梁，丰富了SU(3)结构及LT结构的例子来源。

Abstract: We show that if $(S,g,ξ_i,η_i,Φ_i)$ is a quasi-regular $3$-Sasakian orbifold of dimension $7$, then its quotient $Z$ by one of the Reeb vector fields inherits an $SU(3)$-structure parametrized by two real parameters. We compute its torsion and show that it is an LT-structure. Moreover, for certain values of the parameters, we can provide a nearly Kähler structure on $Z$ appearing as a special case of our construction. We give several examples in the regular, quasi-regular and orbifold cases.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Clifford-Appell formulation of a Dirac-type Kronig-Penney model in condensed matter physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27920)
*L. Aceto,P. A. Grassi,H. Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在(N+1)维Clifford分析框架下研究推广的Kronig-Penney模型的定态Dirac方程。通过引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统改写为耦合一阶系统，再利用特征超复变量将其化为解耦的广义Helmholtz方程，并借助双变量Clifford-Appell多项式基显式构造级数解，建立了自由传播问题的统一Clifford解析表述及其与Appell多项式系统的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为(N+1)维Clifford分析中的推广Kronig-Penney模型提供显式解析求解框架，弥补高维Clifford函数论中Dirac方程系统求解方法的不足，并建立Clifford分析与Appell多项式理论之间的桥梁。

Method: 方法包括：引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统改写为两个Clifford值场的一阶耦合方程组；通过特征超复变量约化为两个解耦的广义Helmholtz方程；引入与广义Cauchy-Riemann算子相适应的双变量Clifford-Appell多项式基；利用Appell展开将微分问题转化为代数递推关系，从而得到解的显式级数表示。

Result: 获得了Kronig-Penney模型自由Dirac方程解的显式级数表示，建立了广义Helmholtz方程、Appell多项式系统与超复函数论之间的明确联系，提供了一个统一的Clifford解析框架来处理自由传播问题。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维Clifford分析中Dirac方程的解析求解提供了统一途径，并揭示了Clifford-Appell多项式在超复函数论中求解椭圆型方程的潜在价值，为后续处理含相互作用项的完整Kronig-Penney模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the stationary Dirac equation associated with a generalized Kronig-Penney model in (N+1)-dimensional Clifford analysis. Away from the interaction sites, the free Dirac system is reformulated by introducing suitable generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators, leading to a coupled first-order system for two Clifford-valued fields. By means of characteristic hypercomplex variables, this system is reduced to a pair of decoupled generalized Helmholtz equations. To construct explicit solutions, we introduce a bivariate Clifford-Appell polynomial basis adapted to the generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators. The Appell expansions transform the differential problem into an algebraic recurrence for the expansion coefficients, yielding explicit series representations of the solutions. The proposed framework provides a unified Clifford-analytic formulation of the free propagation problem and establishes an explicit connection between generalized Helmholtz equations, and Appell polynomial systems in hypercomplex function theory.

</details>


### [118] [Consistent symmetry breaking and topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28181)
*Yuezhao Li,Guo Chuan Thiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于对称群约化（symmetry breaking）原理，建立了等变指标（equivariant indices）的正则弱/强二分法（weak/strong dichotomy），并探讨了其与粗几何（coarse-geometric）或宏观指标的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变指标在对称群及其有限指标子群下需保持一致性，这为建立指标分类提供了基本原理；作者希望借鉴凝聚态物理中拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）的思想，为等变指标建立系统的弱/强分类框架。

Method: 利用对称群不变算子在其任意有限指标子群下仍不变的性质，结合对称破缺下的等变指标一致性条件，构造等变指标的正则弱/强二分法，并进一步研究其与粗几何（宏观）指标的关联性。

Result: 建立了等变指标的正则弱/强分类方案，揭示了对称破缺背景下指标一致性约束所导致的弱/强结构，并将该框架与粗几何指标建立联系。

Conclusion: 通过对称破缺一致性原理，实现了等变指标的弱/强二分法，这种分类框架起源于固态物理中拓扑绝缘体的理论思想，并与粗几何指标理论相互关联，为等变指标的分类研究提供了系统性视角。

Abstract: Operators invariant under a symmetry group are also invariant under any finite-index subgroup. The equivariant indices of such operators must be consistent under symmetry breaking. We use this principle to establish a canonical weak/strong dichotomy for equivariant indices, building on an idea originating in the theory of topological insulators in solid-state physics. We also study the relationship to coarse-geometric, or macroscopic, indices.

</details>


### [119] [The reduced Dirac structure of General Relativity on manifolds with corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28262)
*Alberto S. Cattaneo,Filippo Fila-Robattino,Manuel Tecchiolli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了四维Palatini-Cartan引力理论中角落（corner）泊松结构（Poisson structure）的精确形式，通过边界约束代数构建了预Dirac结构（pre-Dirac structure），并经由约化过程得到极大Dirac结构（maximal Dirac structure），最终识别为泊松双矢量场（Poisson bivector field）的图。该结构等价的仿射泊松描述将约化角落理论自然呈现为BF-型理论（BF-like theory），并给出BF²V表述，为统合Palatini-Cartan引力的体（bulk）、边界（boundary）与角落结构提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力理论中带边界流形的经典描述需要边界与角落处的规范对称性和辛结构。通过边界约束代数（boundary constraint algebra）推导角落处的泊松结构，有助于建立引力体-边界-角落结构的统一框架。

Method: 基于带边界流形上Palatini-Cartan重力的经典描述，特别是边界约束代数，在角落场（corner fields）空间上构造预Dirac结构；通过约化程序（reduction procedure）获得极大Dirac结构，并将其识别为泊松双矢量场的图。进一步证明该泊松结构具有等价的仿射泊松描述（affine Poisson description），导出BF-型理论与BF²V表述。

Result: 成功推导出四维Palatini-Cartan引力角落泊松结构；约化后得到极大Dirac结构（即泊松双矢量场的图）；发现该结构等价的仿射泊松形式使角落理论呈现为BF-型理论并给出BF²V表述。

Conclusion: 所得结果提供了统一描述Palatini-Cartan引力体、边界与角落结构的框架基础，揭示了角落理论与BF-型理论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In this paper, the corner Poisson structure of four-dimensional Palatini-Cartan gravity is derived. Building on the classical description of gravity on manifolds with boundary, specifically on the boundary constraint algebra, a pre- Dirac structure on the space of corner fields is obtained together with a reduction procedure that yields a maximal Dirac structure, identified as the graph of a Poisson bivector field, on the reduced space of corner fields. It is further shown that this Poisson structure admits an equivalent affine Poisson description, which naturally exhibits the reduced corner theory as a $BF$-like theory and leads to a BF$^2$V formulation. This provides the basis for a unified framework for the bulk, boundary, and corner structures of Palatini-Cartan gravity.

</details>


### [120] [Integrability in Asymptotic Symmetries of Spacetime: the $\text{BMS}_3$ scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28454)
*Corentin Vitel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Fuentealba等人（JHEP, 2018）提出的$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层级（integrable hierarchy），采用不同的结构方法论：在多项式符号环（ring of polynomial symbols）上的变分复形（variational complex）中构造$\mathfrak{bms}_3$双哈密顿结构（bi-Hamiltonian structure），并附着Nijenhuis算子；对$\mathrm{AdS}_3$情形进行了类似论证，并验证其平坦极限（flat limit）恢复渐近平坦情形。此外，通过Lie-Poisson描述表明该层级并非唯一，且对一类能量依赖的薛定谔算子（energy-dependent Schrödinger operators），其Lax流可由$\mathfrak{bms}_3$的余伴随轨道（coadjoint orbits）描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验Fuentealba等人于2018年提出的$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层级证明，采用不同的结构方法论来验证和深化该结果。

Method: 在多项式符号环上的变分复形中构造$\mathfrak{bms}_3$双哈密顿结构并附加Nijenhuis算子；对$\mathrm{AdS}_3$情形进行类似论证并检查平坦极限；引入$τ$-方案描述；通过Lie-Poisson描述分析层级唯一性；对能量依赖薛定谔算子的Lax流用$\mathfrak{bms}_3$余伴随轨道刻画。

Result: $\mathfrak{bms}_3$双哈密顿结构与Nijenhuis算子得以构造；$\mathrm{AdS}_3$情形的平坦极限恢复渐近平坦情形；$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层级并非唯一；能量依赖薛定谔算子的Lax流可由$\mathfrak{bms}_3$余伴随轨道描述。

Conclusion: 通过多种等效结构方法重新证明了$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层级的存在，揭示了该层级并不唯一，并将一类能量依赖薛定谔算子与$\mathfrak{bms}_3$的余伴随轨道联系起来。

Abstract: We revisit the proof of a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$integrable hierarchy introduced in Fuentealba et al. (JHEP, 2018), using different structural methodologies. Specifically, from the variational complex on the ring of polynomial symbols, a $\mathfrak{bms}_3$ bi-Hamiltonian structure is constructed on which a Nijenhuis operator can be attached. A similar argument is made for the $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ case, where we check that the flat limit of the latter recovers the asymptotically flat situation. An alternative $τ-$scheme description is also presented. Moreover, a Lie-Poisson description suggests that such a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$hierarchy is not unique. For a subclass of so-called energy-dependent Schrödinger operators, it is shown that their Lax flows are described by the coadjoint orbits of $\mathfrak{bms}_3$.

</details>


### [121] [Redundant moments of non-melonic random tensor models: simplifying the tensor bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28518)
*Carlos I. Perez-Sanchez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在任意张量模型（tensor model）中，大-N极限下所谓的"melonisch族"（melonic family）矩是冗余的，即任何melonisch算符的期望值仅依赖于其阶数（degree）。该结果还推广到melonisch矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程，且适用范围不限于melonisch张量模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量自举（tensor bootstrap）方法的核心依赖于构建正半定矩阵，而减少这些矩阵中独立元素的数量是提高自举效率的关键。本文旨在通过证明melonisch族矩的冗余性来缩小这类矩阵的维度。

Method: 作者在任意张量模型中严格证明大-N极限下melonisch族矩的冗余性，即任何melonisch算符C在大-N极限下的期望值仅通过其阶数依赖C；同时证明类似结论对melonisch矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程成立。

Result: 成功证明melonisch族在大-N张量模型中作为冗余自由度可以减少张量自举核心正半定矩阵中的独立条目数量，并证明该结论同样适用于Schwinger-Dyson方程，且适用范围超出melonisch张量模型本身。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了melonisch族矩在大-N极限下的冗余性，为张量自举方法的简化提供了严格的理论基础，并具有超越melonisch张量模型的更广泛适用范围。

Abstract: We prove the redundancy of a family (the so-called melonic family) of large-$N$ moments in arbitrary tensor models. This reduces the number of independent entries in the positive semidefinite matrices that build the core of the tensor bootstrap (for tensor models there are already several generalizations of the positive semidefinite Toeplitz and Hankel matrices of lattice gauge theory and random matrix bootstrap, respectively). More concretely, we prove that any melonic operator $C$ at large-$N$ has an expectation value that depends on $C$ exclusively through its degree. A similar statement is shown here to hold for the Schwinger-Dyson equations of melonic moments. The strength of our result is also its scope, which is not limited to melonic tensor models.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Design and beam-test characterization of the CRILIN semi-homogeneous crystal calorimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28477)
*S. Ceravolo,M. Cavallina,V. Ciccarella,E. Di Meco,E. Diociaiuti,R. Gargiulo,Q. Han,E. Leonardi,D. Lucchesi,M. Moulson,L. Palombini,N. Pastrone,I. Raghib,A. Russo,A. Saputi,I. Sarra,S. Salamino,L. Sestini,S. Squerzanti,D. Zuliani*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文报道了CRILIN——一种基于纵向分段PbF₂晶体矩阵、由紫外扩展硅光电倍增管（SiPM）读出的大面积原型电磁量能器——在CERN SPS束流测试中的性能。在10–120 GeV电子束下实现了50 ps以下（高于60 GeV时低于20 ps）的时间分辨率，能量分辨率随机项为(6.58±0.04)%/√(E/GeV)，常数项为(0.23±0.02)%。纵向分段能力允许基于辐射簇射发展的逐事件修正，显著改善了能量分辨率；光产额约为0.54光电/MeV，Geant4模拟与实测能量分辨率一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向未来轻子对撞机实验，需要兼具快速契伦科夫响应、精细横向颗粒度、纵向簇射信息和抗辐照能力的电磁量能器；CRILIN架构旨在满足这些需求。

Method: 构建了包含五个7×7 PbF₂晶体矩阵、总深度约22X₀的大面积原型，每个晶体由4个3×3 mm² SiPM集成至单一电子通道读出。利用CERN SPS束流测试收集10–120 GeV电子数据和150 GeV缪子数据，并结合Geant4模拟加以验证。

Result: 电子能量≥10 GeV时时间分辨率优于50 ps，60 GeV以上优于20 ps；能量分辨率为(6.58±0.04)%/√(E/GeV)⊕(0.23±0.02)%，噪声项由噪声底（pedestal）数据固定；纵向分段可进行基于簇射发展的逐事件修正，显著改善能量分辨率；光产额约0.54光电/MeV；Geant4模拟复现了实测能量分辨率。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了CRILIN架构作为面向未来对撞机实验的紧凑、快速、纵向分段电磁量能器的可行性。

Abstract: CRILIN is a high-granularity semi-homogeneous electromagnetic calorimeter based on longitudinally segmented PbF$_2$ crystal matrices read out by UV-extended silicon photomultipliers. The concept combines fast Cherenkov response, fine transverse granularity, longitudinal shower information, and radiation tolerance for future lepton-collider experiments. This paper reports the construction of a large-area prototype and its performance measured in beam tests at the CERN SPS. The detector comprises five $7\times7$ PbF$_2$ crystal matrices, has a depth of about $22X_0$, and is read out by four $3\times3~\mathrm{mm}^2$ SiPMs per crystal integrated in a single electronic channel.
  Electron data between 10 and 120~GeV and dedicated 150~GeV muon data were used to characterize the detector response. A time resolution below 50~ps is achieved for electron energies above 10~GeV, reaching values below 20~ps above 60~GeV.
  The energy resolution is described by a stochastic term of $(6.58\pm0.04)\%/\sqrt{E/\mathrm{GeV}}$ and a constant term of $(0.23\pm0.02)\%$, with an additional noise contribution fixed from pedestal data. The longitudinal segmentation enables event-by-event corrections based on the reconstructed shower development, resulting in a significant improvement of the energy resolution. A light yield of approximately 0.54~photoelectrons/MeV is measured consistently using both electron showers and minimum-ionizing particles. A Geant4-based simulation incorporating the relevant experimental effects reproduces the measured energy resolution. These results validate the CRILIN architecture as a compact, fast, and longitudinally segmented electromagnetic calorimeter for future collider experiments.

</details>

### [123] [Data Field Theory: Theory and Applications of the Functional Renormalization Group for Signal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27236)
*Riccardo Finotello,Vincent Lahoche,Dine Ousmane Samary,Parham Radpay*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文综述了重整化群（renormalization group）框架在高维数据信号检测中的应用，该框架适用于信号具有广延秩（extensive rank）且不与噪声本体（noise bulk）分离为孤立尖峰的情形，通过追踪谱形变（spectral deformations）并在谱尾部（spectral tail）测试高斯不动点（Gaussian fixed point）的稳定性来识别信号，给出了不依赖尖峰分离的检测下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法如Baik-Ben Arous-Péché阈值要求特征值与本征谱（bulk）清晰分离，无法处理信号以广延秩形式嵌入准连续谱区域的情形，因此需要更通用的检测框架。

Method: 采用重整化群方法直接追踪谱形变，构建用于谱尾部集体自由度行为的有效场论（effective field theory），通过检验高斯不动点的稳定性来判定信号是否存在；同时考察由信号诱发的量纲（canonical dimension）标度依赖性。

Result: 该框架提供了一种概念上简单的判据，能在近随机矩阵普适类（random-matrix universality class）的准连续谱区域区分信号与噪声，并一致地给出检测下限（limit of detection），无需依赖尖峰分离。

Conclusion: 重整化群方法将信号检测问题转化为对谱尾部有效理论的稳定性分析，其中信号导致的量纲标度依赖性体现为维度相变（dimensional phase transition），从而扩展了可检测信号的范围。

Abstract: We review the renormalization group framework for signal detection in high-dimensional data, tailored to the regime where the signal may be of extensive rank and does not separate from the noise bulk as isolated spikes. The framework provides a conceptually simple criterion for distinguishing signal from noise within a quasi-continuous spectral region near a random-matrix universality class. This scenario lies beyond the reach of standard methods such as the Baik-Ben Arous-Péché threshold, which requires eigenvalues to be cleanly separated from the bulk. The renormalization group approach, by contrast, directly tracks spectral deformations and consistently yields a lower limit of detection without relying on spike separation. We review results that identify the presence of a signal by testing the stability of the Gaussian fixed point of an effective field theory for the collective behaviour of the degrees of freedom in the spectral tail, where the signal resides. We also discuss how the scale dependence of the canonical dimension, induced by the signal, manifests as a dimensional phase transition.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Nanobubbles, pristine emulsions, high ionic strength electrokinetics -- paradoxes of Colloid and Interface Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27513)
*Andrei Dukhin,Renliang Xu,Darrell Velegol*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文回顾了胶体与界面科学中三大实验悖论（高离子强度下的电动学现象、纳米气泡和原始乳液在无表面稳定条件下的超长寿命），指出经典理论模型与大量实验证据之间存在根本矛盾，并综述了以疏水界面结构化水层为核心假设的新理论模型，认为这一共同特征可为三大现象提供统一解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量实验证据表明经典理论模型无法解释胶体与界面科学中的三大悖论现象，这种实验与理论之间的矛盾要求引入新的理论框架，从而推动了对结构化水层假说及相关模型的系统综述。

Method: 作者系统回顾了来自多个国家众多科研团队的数十篇实验论文，将这些数据视为三类现象存在的明确证据；随后综述了相关新理论模型，识别出其中最有前景模型的共同特征——疏水界面处存在结构化水层，并进一步梳理了该假设百年来的实验与理论文献。

Result: 综述发现，解释高离子强度下电动学现象的理论模型已有数十年历史，而解释纳米气泡和原始乳液异常寿命的理论模型则较新，两者均可通过结构化水层与双电层（electric double layer）的相互作用来统一解释三大悖论现象。

Conclusion: 结构化水层假说获得了大量实验与理论文献的支持，将其与双电层相互作用相结合，为三大实验悖论提供了一致且可行的理论解释框架。

Abstract: There are three paradoxes in the modern Colloid and Interface Science supported by large bulk of experimental evidence and contradicting classical theoretical models: - Electrokinetics at high ionic strength; - Nanobubbles having life span on scale of days and weeks without any surface stabilization; - Pristine emulsions having life span on scale of days and weeks without any surface stabilization.
  We overview many dozens of experimental papers by broad spectrum of scientific groups from many countries. We consider this vast experimental data as unambiguous evidence that these phenomena exist. On other hand, classical theoretical models deny such possibility.
  This contradiction between experiment and theory justifies introduction of new theoretical models. We overview these models. It turns out that there is one common feature between most promising of them -- assumption regarding structured water layer at hydrophobic interface. That is why we combine these three phenomena in this review. There is extensive literature on century old idea of the structured water layer, experimental and theoretical. We overview this literature, which provides convincing support to this hypothesis. We discuss existing theoretical models that incorporate the structured interfacial water layer for the successful explanation of these phenomena in more detail. Theoretical model of electrokinetics at high ionic strength was developed several decades ago. Theoretical model explaining paradoxical longevity of nanobubbles and pristine emulsions by interaction between structured water layer and electric double layer is more recent.

</details>


### [125] [Phase transitions and microphases in elastomers. I. Emergence of stable domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27516)
*Manu Mannattil,Haim Diamant,David Andelman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文（两部分论文的第一部分）提出，溶胀弹性体（swollen elastomers）中的微相分离（microphase separation）可由体积守恒导致的非局域热力学-弹性耦合（nonlocal thermodynamic-elastic coupling）来解释，该理论复现了实验观察到的相变温度和畴尺寸对弹性体刚度的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性效应在调控物理系统中的相分离方面起关键作用，近期实验表明弹性效应可用于控制溶胀弹性体中的微相分离，但其背后的理论机制尚需阐明。

Method: 利用传统弹性理论（conventional elasticity theories），通过引入由体积守恒（volume conservation）引起的非局域热力学-弹性耦合，建立微相形成的理论框架。

Result: 该理论成功复现了各向同性溶胀弹性体中相变温度和畴尺寸（domain size）随弹性体刚度（stiffness）变化的实验观测结果。

Conclusion: 微相分离源于弹性相互作用与热力学相互作用的特征长度尺度不匹配，该机制可通过经典弹性理论结合非局域耦合加以解释；后续（第二部分）将研究各向异性溶胀和非均匀弹性模量的影响。

Abstract: Elasticity often plays a key role in regulating phase separation in physical systems. Recent experiments have shown that elastic effects can be used to control microphase separation in swollen elastomers. Here, microphase separation arises from a mismatch between the characteristic length scales of elastic and thermodynamic interactions. In this first part of a two-part paper, we show that microphase formation in elastomers can be explained using conventional theories of elasticity through a nonlocal thermodynamic-elastic coupling arising from volume conservation. Our theory reproduces the observed dependence of phase transition temperature and domain size on elastomer stiffness in isotropically swollen elastomers. In the companion paper, we investigate the effects of anisotropic swelling and inhomogeneous elastic moduli.

</details>


### [126] [Design principles for energy dissipation in viscoelastic network metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27525)
*Niranjan Sarpangala,Sean Fancher,Prashant K. Purohit,Eleni Katifori*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于图拉普拉斯（graph Laplacian）的谱方法框架，用于高效分析黏弹性桁架网络（viscoelastic truss networks）中的机械能耗散。研究发现随机重新分配截面积通常降低耗散，而基于梯度的优化可产生非平凡架构，其最优质量分布从驱动节点以材料固有衰减长度（attenuation length）为尺度递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化材料的机械能耗散在振动隔离到冲击防护等应用中至关重要，但使用传统有限元方法（finite element methods）在大型无序桁架网络中寻找最优耗散结构在计算上难以实现，亟需更高效的数值框架。

Method: 论文构建了一个基于图拉普拉斯的谱框架（spectral framework）来处理黏弹性桁架网络，该框架精确保留每根杆件的连续介质动力学，且计算规模仅随节点（joint）数量而非单元离散点数增长。在此基础上，研究通过重分配网络中截面面积（不改变材料组成）来控制能量耗散，并采用基于梯度的优化方法寻找最优架构。

Result: 随机重新分配截面面积通常相对于均匀基线减少耗散，而梯度优化可生成非平凡架构，其形式由基材固有衰减长度决定。在驱动频率接近网络全局共振模式时，最优质量分布从驱动节点以衰减长度尺度向外衰减；在衰减长度很小时，最优架构与边界条件无关。

Conclusion: 这些结果为基于耗散长度尺度（dissipation length scale）的设计原则在更复杂无序架构中的应用提供了动机，同时该高效计算框架为大规模探索此类结构开辟了途径。

Abstract: Mechanical energy dissipation in networked materials is relevant for applications from vibration isolation to impact protection, yet identifying optimal dissipative architectures in large disordered truss networks is computationally prohibitive with conventional finite element methods. We develop an efficient graph Laplacian-based spectral framework for viscoelastic truss networks, in which the full continuum dynamics of each rod are retained exactly and the problem size scales with the number of joints rather than element-level discretization points. Using this framework, we investigate how redistributing cross-sectional areas within a network (without changing material composition) controls energy dissipation. We find that random redistribution typically reduces dissipation relative to a uniform baseline, while gradient-based optimization yields nontrivial architectures whose form is governed by the intrinsic attenuation length of the base material. Focusing on driving frequencies near a global resonant mode of the network, we show that the optimal mass distribution decays from the source (driven joint) with the attenuation length scale, and at small attenuation lengths the optimal architecture is independent of the boundary conditions. These results motivate future studies of dissipation length scale based design principles on more complex disordered architectures and provide an efficient computational framework for exploring such structures at scale.

</details>


### [127] [Partial vision leads to an unexpected emergent collective behavior in active aligning particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27819)
*Raúl Molina-Prados Lallena,José Martín-Roca,Chantal Valeriani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文基于Vicsek模型提出了一个包含两个不重叠角视锥的广义模型，系统研究了孔径面积和前后朝向偏差对非平衡相行为的影响，揭示了限制侧向视野会破坏全局有序、诱导高密度行波带，而前后对称性的破缺引入非互易相互作用，分别产生"跟随着领"的强聚集态和均匀的绒毛态，同时体积排斥作用对这些新相的结构完整性具有高度敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Vicsek模型假设各向同性的相互作用场，但自然界中许多生物系统具有有限的感知范围和视觉盲区，因此现有模型无法准确描述这类现实约束下的集体运动行为。

Method: 提出一个推广的Vicsek模型，引入两个不重叠的角视锥（angular vision cones），通过调节锥体的孔径面积（aperture area, α）和前后朝向偏差（front-back orientation, β）两个参数，系统研究体系的非平衡相行为，并进一步引入短程体积排斥（short-range volume exclusion）考察物理约束的影响。

Result: 限制侧向视野区域会破坏全局有序、移动临界噪声并诱导高密度行波带；前向偏置视野通过"跟随领"对齐机制驱动强聚集，而后向偏置视野稳定了密度涨落被抑制的异常均匀绒毛态；这些新相的结构完整性对短程体积排斥相互作用高度敏感。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解活性物质（active matter）中非互易感知（non-reciprocal perception）、空间各向异性（spatial anisotropy）与物理约束之间的相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: The Vicsek Model represents a paradigmatic framework for understanding the collective motion of active aligning particles, traditionally assuming isotropic interaction fields. Inspired by biological systems characterized by limited perception and blind spots, we propose a generalized Vicsek model featuring two distinct, non-overlapping angular vision cones. We systematically investigate the non-equilibrium phase behavior of this system by tuning the aperture area (α) and the front-back orientation (\b{eta}) of the cones. Our results reveal that restricting the lateral vision area destabilizes global order, shifts the critical noise, and induces highly dense traveling bands. Furthermore, breaking the front-back symmetry introduces non-reciprocal interactions that profoundly alter the emergent spatial structures: forward-biased vision drives strong clustering through "follow the leader" alignment, whereas backward-biased alignment stabilizes an exceptionally homogeneous flocking state with suppressed density fluctuations. Finally, we incorporate short-range volume exclusion, demonstrating that the structural integrity of these novel tightly-clustered phases is highly sensitive to steric interactions. Our work provides new insights into the interplay between non-reciprocal perception, spatial anisotropy, and physical constraints in active matter.

</details>


### [128] [Molecular Hyperpolarisability as a Screening Descriptor for Second-Order Nonlinear Optics in Ferroelectric Nematic Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27986)
*Charles Parton-Barr,Nerea Sebastian,Richard Mandle*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文评估了利用电子结构计算（electronic-structure calculations）预测铁电向列（ferroelectric nematic）液晶材料非线性光学（nonlinear optical）性能的可行性。研究采用多种DFT方法计算分子超极化率张量，并通过取向气体模型（oriented-gas model）转换为宏观d系数，发现绝对值的预测强烈依赖方法选择，而相对趋势更适合作为筛选依据。将最优的单构象计算协议应用于更广泛的极性液晶数据集，识别出具有增强非线性光学响应的候选材料，并揭示了基于供体-受体不对称性、共轭长度和连接基团选择的简单设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电向列液晶兼具流动性和宏观极性有序，具有重要的光学应用潜力。然而现有铁电向列材料大多未在设计中考虑光学非线性，且缺乏系统的理论筛选方法，因此需要评估电子结构计算是否能用于鉴别具有优越非线性光学性能的候选材料。

Method: 使用多种DFT方法和基组计算频率相关的分子超极化率张量（hyperpolarisability tensors），再利用取向气体模型并结合经验值<p1>和<p3>转换为宏观d系数。计算结果与代表性材料的实验d33、d15和d13/d31系数进行基准对比，并研究构象平均的影响。

Result: 计算所得的绝对值强烈依赖于所用方法和体相参数假设，而相对趋势更适合用于材料筛选；显式的构象平均并不能一致地提升与实验的吻合度。将最优单构象协议应用于更大的极性液晶数据集后，识别出具有增强预测非线性光学响应的候选材料。

Conclusion: 基于相对趋势的电子结构计算可以作为筛选极性液晶非线性光学材料的高效工具，并据此提出了基于供体-受体不对称性、共轭长度和连接基团选择的普适设计规则，为该类材料的理性设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Ferroelectric nematic liquid crystals combine fluidity with macroscopic polar order. Although the archetypal NF material RM734 exhibits large nonlinear optical coefficients, most known ferroelectric nematics were designed without consideration of optical nonlinearity. Here, we assess whether electronic-structure calculations can be used to identify promising nonlinear optical candidates within known polar liquid-crystal materials. Frequency-dependent molecular hyperpolarisability tensors were calculated using a range of DFT methods and basis sets, then converted to macroscopic d-coefficients using an oriented-gas model with empirical <P1> and <P3> values. Calculated values were benchmarked against available experimental d33, d15, and d13/d31 coefficients for representative materials. We find that absolute values depend strongly on method and bulk-parameter assumptions, whereas relative trends are more useful for screening. Explicit conformer averaging does not consistently improve agreement with experiment. Applying the best-performing single-conformer protocols to a broader polar liquid-crystal dataset identifies candidate materials with enhanced predicted nonlinear optical response and reveals simple design rules based on donor-acceptor asymmetry, conjugation length, and linker choice.

</details>


### [129] [Rheology of dense suspensions of granular spherocylinders by particle-based simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28206)
*Alex Dixon,John Hone,Gavin Melaugh,Christopher Ness*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于粒子的模拟方法，用于研究棒状颗粒致密悬浮液（dense suspensions of rod-shaped granular particles）在简单剪切流（simple shear flow）下的黏度与微观结构，模型预测黏度随体积分数和长径比（aspect ratio）系统性增加，并与有限的实验流变学数据相吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒状颗粒致密悬浮液在自然界和工业制造中广泛存在，其流体力学性质往往至关重要，但目前缺乏针对此类体系的精确预测工具。

Method: 研究开发了一种基于粒子的模拟方法，追踪球柱形（spherocylindrical）棒状颗粒在短程摩擦接触力和水动力（hydrodynamic）作用下的运动轨迹，并引入计算球柱体间润滑力（lubrication forces）的新方案以及时间步长的动态确定机制。

Result: 在长径比高达20的条件下，模型预测剪切启动时出现黏度尖峰，随后达到稳态黏度，且稳态黏度随体积分数和长径比的增大而系统性上升；颗粒取向度随体积分数增加，直至达到依赖长径比的临界点。

Conclusion: 模型验证了棒状颗粒悬浮液有限的实验流变学数据，并为深入研究此类广泛存在材料的流体力学、微观结构与流变学提供了基础性研究工具。

Abstract: Dense suspensions of rod-shaped granular particles are widespread in nature and manufacturing, where their fluid mechanical properties are often paramount. We have developed a particle-based simulation that models such suspensions under simple shear flow, providing predictions of the viscosity and microstructure for a given solids volume fraction and particle aspect ratio. The model tracks the trajectories of spherocylindrical rods under the action of short-range frictional contact and hydrodynamic forces, inspired by similar tools that have generated new insight into suspensions of granular spheres. It incorporates new schemes for the computation of lubrication forces between spherocylinders and the dynamic determination of the timestep. For aspect ratios up to 20, the model predicts a viscosity spike at shear start-up, giving way to steady state viscosities that increase systematically with volume fraction and aspect ratio. Likewise, particle alignment increases with volume fraction up to an aspect-ratio-dependent critical point. Our model corroborates the limited experimental rheology data available for suspensions of granular rods, and offers a tool for fundamental exploration of the fluid mechanics, microstructure and rheology of this widespread material.

</details>


### [130] [Influence of Rotational Diffusion on Macromolecular Self-Assembly Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28325)
*Prabeen Kumar Pattnayak,Aloke Kumar,Gaurav Tomar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算模拟对比线形链与四臂、七臂星形聚合物两类不同构型（architecture）的补丁大分子（patchy macromolecule）的自组装过程，在两者平移扩散系数近乎一致的情况下，揭示了大分子内部构型与旋转扩散对其自组装动力学和途径的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶液中大分子自组装动力学本质上是随机过程，由平移与旋转扩散共同决定；然而大多数计算研究将大分子简化为补丁球形胶体（patchy spherical colloid），忽略了聚合物构型和内部构象动力学的影响，而这些因素在自组装动力学中的作用尚不清楚。

Method: 选取两种不同构型的大分子——线形链（linear chain）以及四臂和七臂的星形聚合物（star polymer），将其流体力学半径（hydrodynamic radius）调至近乎相等以匹配平移扩散系数，从而隔离旋转扩散对自组装过程的单独影响；并通过反应路径密度分析（reactive path density analysis）探索自组装途径。

Result: 补丁大分子的结合概率强烈依赖于其内部构型；反应路径密度分析进一步表明，自组装途径受单个大分子旋转扩散系数的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究在大分子的平衡动力学与其自组装动力学之间建立了联系，凸显了聚合物内部构型在自组装过程中的重要地位。

Abstract: Macromolecular self-assembly underlies a plethora of biological processes and provides a versatile route for fabricating functional soft materials. The kinetics of self-assembly in solution are inherently stochastic and are fundamentally governed by the interplay of translational and rotational diffusion of the constituent macromolecules. While most computational studies model macromolecules as patchy spherical colloids, thereby neglecting the influence of polymer architecture and internal conformational dynamics, the role of these factors in macromolecular self-assembly kinetics remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate the self-assembly of two patchy macromolecules with different architectures, namely linear chains and star polymers with four and seven arms. The hydrodynamic radii of the macromolecules are chosen to be nearly identical, thereby matching their translational diffusion coefficients and thus isolating the influence of rotational diffusion on the self-assembly process. The binding probability of the patchy macromolecules is found to depend strongly on their internal architecture. Furthermore, reactive path density analysis reveals that self-assembly pathways are influenced by the rotational diffusion coefficient of the individual macromolecules. Overall, this study establishes a bridge between the equilibrium dynamics of macromolecules and their self-assembly kinetics, highlighting the importance of polymer internal architecture in the process of self-assembly.

</details>


### [131] [Thermodynamics and Kinetics of a Three-Arm Star Polymer Translocating through a Nanopore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28340)
*Bhavesh R. Sarode,Harshwardhan H. Katkar*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过朗之万动力学（Langevin dynamics）模拟和元动力学（metadynamics）模拟，研究了带电长链线形聚合物与三臂星形聚合物在纳米孔中的电压驱动穿膜（translocation）过程。发现三臂星形聚合物在低于阈值电压时表现出偏离线形聚合物标度关系的显著偏差，并通过自由能景貌分析识别出由熵贡献和静电相互作用引起的第二势垒，构建的福克-普朗克（Fokker-Planck）模型能准确预测该偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物穿过纳米孔的穿膜过程在生物技术（如DNA测序）中具有重要意义，但带分支结构的聚合物（如星形聚合物）的穿膜动力学与线形聚合物有本质差异，其电压依赖性和底层机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用粗粒化模型（coarse-grained model）和半隐式纳米孔表示方法，对均匀带电的长链线形聚合物和三臂星形聚合物进行朗之万动力学模拟；利用元动力学模拟估算穿膜过程的自由能景貌（free-energy landscape）；基于自由能景貌建立福克-普朗克模型来描述穿膜动力学。

Result: 线形聚合物的平均穿膜时间在宽电压范围内与施加电压成反比；三臂星形聚合物在低于阈值电压时显著偏离该标度关系。阈值电压几乎不依赖分子量，但依赖于纳米孔尺寸和盐浓度。自由能景貌在阈值电压以下表现出明显的第二势垒，源于熵贡献和孔内拖尾臂段之间的静电相互作用。福克-普朗克模型使用一个与电压无关的拟合参数即能准确预测该偏差，且与不同纳米孔半径、分子量和盐浓度下的模拟结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了星形聚合物穿膜动力学与线形聚合物的重要差异，自由能景貌中的第二势垒是导致标度关系偏差的关键机制。福克-普朗克模型能够定量预测穿膜动力学，且可通过简单扩展自由能景貌来预测更高分子量聚合物的穿膜动力学，无需额外的高计算成本模拟。

Abstract: In this work, voltage-driven translocation of uniformly charged long linear and three-arm star polymers through narrow nanopores is investigated. Langevin dynamics simulation is performed using a coarse-grained model of the polymer and a semi-implicit representation of the nanopore. The mean translocation time of a linear polymer is found to be inversely proportional to the applied voltage over a wide range of voltages. In contrast, the mean translocation time of a three-arm star polymer of the same molecular weight exhibits a pronounced deviation from this scaling relation below a threshold voltage. The threshold voltage is found to be nearly independent of the molecular weight of the polymer, but depends on the size of the nanopore and salt concentration. Metadynamics simulation is used to estimate the free-energy landscape for the translocation of the three-arm star polymer. Below the threshold voltage, the free energy exhibits a pronounced second barrier resulting from an entropic contribution and electrostatic interactions between segments of the trailing arm inside the nanopore. A Fokker-Planck model developed using the estimated free-energy accurately predicts the deviation from the scaling relation below the threshold voltage and shows a remarkable agreement with the Langevin dynamics simulation results using a voltage-independent fitting parameter. The agreement between the theory and the Langevin dynamics simulation results is seen for different nanopore radii, molecular weights of the polymer and salt concentrations studied. A simple extension of the free energy landscape is suggested to predict translocation kinetics for higher molecular weights of the polymer without performing additional computationally expensive simulations.

</details>
