### [132] [Spin-incoherent Mott-Thouless pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27321)
*Ajesh Kumar,Urban F. P. Seifert,Erez Berg,Achim Rosch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类莫特–图勒斯泵（Mott–Thouless pump），它能在高熵、自旋不相干的莫特态（spin-incoherent Mott states）中实现指数级保护的量子化电荷输运，尽管系统具有巨大的熵。研究发现，泵浦电荷位于一个有能隙的扇区，与热自旋自由度动态解耦，从而保证输运保护；同时，另一类缺乏这种保护的莫特–图勒斯泵中，自旋激发会快速破坏量子化输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的图勒斯泵（Thouless pump）要求系统在整个泵浦过程中保持基态且熵为零，这在实际高熵系统中很难满足。本文旨在探索在高熵条件下仍能保持量子化输运的泵浦机制，并理解哪些条件下这种保护会失效。

Method: 作者从解析上推导了莫特–图勒斯泵中输运的量子化保护机制，指出泵浦电荷所处的有能隙扇区与热自旋自由度在动态上解耦；通过与近期实验对比，识别出缺乏保护的另一类系统；并用有限系统的精确实时数值模拟（numerically exact real-time simulations）验证了解析结果。

Result: 解析分析表明，在高熵的莫特态中，泵浦电荷的量子化输运依然受到指数级保护（exponentially protected），因为电荷与自旋动力学在时间尺度上分离。相反，在另一类系统中，自旋激发能够高效产生电荷激发，导致量子化输运迅速崩溃；数值模拟证实了这两类行为的区别。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了高熵系统中量子化输运保护的新机制，表明熵本身不一定破坏量子化输运，关键在于电荷与自旋自由度是否在动态上解耦；同时指出实验上某些莫特–图勒斯泵会因自旋–电荷耦合而失去保护，为设计稳健的量子泵浦器件提供了理论和实验指导。

Abstract: A Thouless pump describes a system in which a quantized amount of charge is transported by one lattice spacing per cycle when the parameters of a Hamiltonian are varied slowly and periodically. In the standard case, this quantization requires the system to remain in its ground state with vanishing thermodynamic entropy throughout the pumping process. Here, we introduce a class of Mott-Thouless pumps, which operate in highly entropic, spin-incoherent Mott states. We show analytically that these states exhibit exponentially protected quantized transport despite their extensive entropy, because the pumped charge resides in a gapped sector that remains dynamically decoupled from the hot spin degrees of freedom. By contrast, motivated by a recent experimental realization, we identify other classes of Mott-Thouless pumps that lack this protection. In these systems, spin excitations can efficiently generate charge excitations, leading to a rapid breakdown of quantized transport. Our analytical results are supported by numerically exact real-time simulations of finite systems.

</details>


### [133] [Altermagnetism from a Cu-Fe Lieb Lattice in FeSe/Cuprate Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27331)
*Ying Li,Augustin Davignon,Peng Rao,Runhan Li,Maia G. Vergniory,Roser Valentí,Johannes Knolle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出FeSe/铜氧化物超导体异质结是实现交错磁性（altermagnetism）的平台，通过Cu与Fe层的45°扭转形成有效CuFe₂ Lieb晶格，以及FeSe中两个Se位点不等价两种机制产生d波自旋劈裂，密度泛函理论计算验证了两种机制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高临界温度（high-Tc）铜氧化物超导体体系中实现交错磁性，可为在无净磁化条件下研究自旋劈裂电子带及其与非传统超导（unconventional superconductivity）的相互作用提供直接途径。

Method: 提出FeSe/铜氧化物异质结构平台，构造45°扭转的Cu与Fe层形成有效CuFe₂ Lieb晶格；通过最小紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）论证机制的普适性；利用密度泛函理论（DFT）对FeSe/Bi₂Sr₂CuO₆异质结构进行第一性原理计算验证两种机制。

Result: DFT计算证实两种交错磁性产生机制均可行：其一源于CuFe₂ Lieb晶格中Fe磁有序与Cu-Fe杂化诱导的d波自旋劈裂；其二源于衬底导致的FeSe中两个Se位点不等价，使Fe层产生交错磁性并通过近邻效应（proximity effect）转移至铜氧化物层。计算结果还揭示了增强自旋劈裂的方法。

Conclusion: 研究确立了超导铜氧化物/过渡金属硫族化物异质结构作为工程化交错磁性并研究其与非传统超导耦合的可行平台。

Abstract: Realizing altermagnetism in high-$T_c$ cuprate-based systems would provide a direct route for studying spin-split electronic bands in the absence of net magnetization and investigate their interplay with unconventional superconductivity. Here, we propose that FeSe/cuprate heterostructures offer such a platform, where a 45$^\circ$ twist of Cu and Fe layers creates an effective CuFe$_2$ Lieb lattice in which Fe magnetic order and Cu-Fe hybridization through the ligands induces altermagnetic $d$-wave spin splitting. A minimal tight-binding model shows that this mechanism is generic. Furthermore, a substrate-induced inequivalence of the two Se sites in FeSe provides a second route in which altermagnetism originates in the Fe layer and is transferred to the cuprate layer by proximity. Density functional theory calculations for FeSe/Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CuO$_6$ heterostructures confirm the viability of both mechanisms and reveal ways to enhance the spin splitting. These results establish superconducting cuprate/transition metal chalcogenide heterostructures as a promising setting for engineering altermagnetism and studying its coupling to unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [134] [Flat-band formation and chiral superconductivity in driven topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27355)
*Suman Jyoti De,Leo Goutte,Kartiek Agarwal,T. Pereg-Barnea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用圆偏振光在三维拓扑绝缘体（3D TIs）表面通过Floquet工程（Floquet engineering）实现近平坦带或墨西哥帽（Mexican-hat）形电子能带，并在纯排斥库仑相互作用下诱导拓扑超导，估计临界温度（$T_c$）可达约7 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在凝聚态物理中，平坦带是增强电子关联效应、实现高温超导等强关联现象的重要平台；作者希望通过光驱动手段在三维拓扑绝缘体表面系统地调控能带几何，从而在无需声子或磁机制的情况下，仅凭纯排斥库仑相互作用实现拓扑超导。

Method: 作者利用圆偏振光对三维拓扑绝缘体表面态进行Floquet工程，通过光驱动不仅关闭狄拉克锥（Dirac cone），还能减弱甚至翻转表面态色散在狄拉克点以外的固有曲率，从而构造近平坦带或墨西哥帽形能带；同时配合合适放置的屏蔽金属栅极（screening metallic gate），在低电子密度条件下评估超导性和Wigner结晶的风险。

Result: 计算表明，在典型三维拓扑绝缘体的参数下，合理的电场强度即可达到平坦带极限；通过改变驱动场强度实现的能带在能量与空间分布上类似于菱面体石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）在变化位移场下的结果。在加入适当屏蔽栅后，该体系中可在约$10^{11}$–$10^{12}/\text{cm}^2$的低电子密度范围内获得约$T_c \sim 7$ K的超导转变温度，同时避免Wigner结晶。

Conclusion: 圆偏振光驱动是一种可行的、可调的控制手段，可在三维拓扑绝缘体表面实现与菱面体石墨烯等已知系统类似的平坦带物理，从而为仅依靠纯排斥库仑相互作用的拓扑超导开辟了新的平台。

Abstract: We demonstrate that circularly polarized light can be used to Floquet-engineer nearly flat or Mexican-hat like electronic bands on the surface of three-dimensional topological insulators (3D TIs), which under suitable conditions, can support topological superconductivity via purely repulsive Coulomb interactions. The driving acts not merely by gapping out the Dirac cone on the surface of the 3D TI, but can be used to diminish, and even flip in sign, the intrinsic curvature of the surface state dispersion away from the Dirac point. Using parameters for canonical 3D TIs, we find that the flat band limit is attained for reasonable electric fields and the bands realized by changing the strength of the driving field have a similar energetic and spatial profile to those obtained in rhombohedral graphene under varying displacement field, where the case for superconductivity with purely repulsive interactions has recently been made. We find that, with the aid of appropriately placed screening metallic gate, one can obtain $T_c \sim 7 $K in this setup while avoiding Wigner crystallization for low electron densities in the range of $10^{11}-10^{12}/\text{cm}^2$.

</details>


### [135] [Excited state optimization for strongly correlated quantum defects using ensemble variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27377)
*Kevin G. Kleiner,Lucas K. Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用系综变分蒙特卡洛（ensemble variational Monte Carlo）方法，系统地优化了金刚石中氮-空位和硅-空位缺陷中心以及氮化铝中替代铁和铬杂质的波函数。研究发现混合泛函PBE0轨道优于半局域PBE轨道，激发能变化最高达0.5 eV，进一步直接优化目标函数可将激发能再改动最高达0.2 eV。最关键的是，不同缺陷体系所需的最重要优化参数各不相同，表明对强关联缺陷的激发态计算必须全面优化所有参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联点缺陷（如金刚石中的色心、氮化铝中的过渡金属杂质）的激发态精准描述是材料计算的重大挑战，传统方法如密度泛函理论（DFT）在描述强关联体系时存在系统误差。本文旨在系统评估变分蒙特卡洛方法中各参数（行列式展开、轨道、Jastrow关联因子）的优化对缺陷激发态精度的影响，以厘清最优计算策略。

Method: 作者采用近期提出的系综变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）方法，对四个强关联缺陷体系进行波函数优化研究。分别考察了完全优化的行列式展开参数、单粒子轨道以及Jastrow相关系数的作用，并对比了基于PBE0与PBE泛函生成的轨道起始点在优化后的目标函数（objective functional）表现。

Result: PBE0泛函生成的轨道经过优化后，其目标函数值显著优于PBE半局域泛函，导致激发能计算差异最高达0.5 eV。进一步直接优化目标函数可再带来最高0.2 eV的激发能变化。不同缺陷体系中主导作用的优化参数各不相同，不存在统一的最重要单一参数。

Conclusion: 对于强关联点缺陷的激发态计算，轨道来源（PBE0优于PBE）和直接优化目标函数都会对激发能产生不可忽略的影响，且各参数的重要性因缺陷类型而异。因此，要获得准确的激发态描述，必须对行列式展开、轨道和Jastrow因子进行所有参数的联合优化，不能仅依赖单一参数。

Abstract: Using the recently introduced ensemble variational Monte Carlo (VMC), we study optimized wave functions for strongly correlated point defects, including nitrogen-vacancy and silicon-vacancy centers in diamond and substitutional iron and chromium impurities in aluminum nitride. We study the effects of fully optimized determinant expansion parameters, orbitals, and Jastrow correlation factors on these systems. We find that orbitals from the hybrid functional PBE0 are much better (have lower objective functional) than semilocal PBE, which results in changes in the excitation energies up to 0.5 eV. Further improvements can be made by directly optimizing the objective functional, resulting in changes in excitation energies up to 0.2 eV. The most important parameter varies from defect to defect, reinforcing the necessity of optimizing all parameters to obtain accurate excited states in strongly correlated defects.

</details>


### [136] [Universality of Energy-Space Entanglement in Quantum Impurity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27394)
*Geng-Dong Zhou,Zhi-Da Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子杂质模型（quantum impurity models）中基于能量空间划分的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy, EE），证明其在费米液体固定点下趋于与模型参数无关的普适常数（为ln 2的整数倍加仅依赖对数离散参数Λ的修正），并通过一维拓扑带理论对固定点进行分类，还将非费米液体临界点的EE行为与其普适类联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵通常通过实空间二分（real-space bipartition）进行研究，但能量空间划分的普适行为尚未得到系统理解。作者受“穷人缩放”（poor man's scaling）启发，提出可通过能量空间的二分展示量子杂质模型中的普适性现象。

Method: 对浴（bath）做对数离散化（logarithmic discretization），划分为高能和低能两个扇区，研究其纠缠熵。对于费米液体固定点，利用固定点波函数在能量空间的尺度不变性映射到一维链的有效平移不变性，从而用一维拓扑带理论进行分类。通过双轨道Anderson模型中的局域单态-近藤单态（local-singlet–Kondo-singlet）转变研究不同固定点之间的跃迁。

Result: 费米液体固定点（包括Anderson模型、完全屏蔽和欠屏蔽Kondo模型）的低能纠缠熵流化为不依赖模型参数的常数，为ln 2的整数倍加仅依赖对数离散参数Λ的修正。低能手征对称性（chiral symmetry）下每个ln 2贡献源于一个拓扑边缘模；局域单态相纠缠熵接近零，而Kondo单态相每自旋和轨道均有大于ln 2的有限纠缠熵。非费米液体临界点处EE呈现不稳定平台，其Λ依赖性类似于过度屏蔽双通道Kondo模型。

Conclusion: 能量空间二分的纠缠熵为量子杂质模型提供了一种新的普适量，其固定点可通过一维拓扑带理论分类。费米液体与局域单态相之间的转变对应有效浴链的拓扑转变；非费米液体临界点的EE行为支持其与超屏蔽双通道Kondo模型同属一个非费米液体普适类。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is commonly studied using real-space bipartitions. We show that, in quantum impurity models, an energy-space bipartition, equivalent to the momentum-space bipartition of the bath, can display universal behavior. Motivated by poor man's scaling, we logarithmically discretize the bath and partition it into high- and low-energy sectors. For models with Fermi-liquid fixed points, including the Anderson model and fully screened or underscreened Kondo models, the low-energy EE flows to constants independent of model parameters. These constants are integer multiples of $\ln 2$ plus corrections that depend only on the logarithmic discretization parameter $Λ$. We show that scale invariance of the fixed-point wavefunction in energy space maps to effective translation invariance along a one-dimensional chain, allowing the fixed points to be classified by one-dimensional topological band theory. With low-energy chiral symmetry, each $\ln 2$ contribution originates from a topological edge mode. We also study transitions between distinct Fermi-liquid fixed points using the local-singlet--Kondo-singlet transition in a two-orbital Anderson model driven by an inter-orbital antiferromagnetic coupling. The local-singlet phase has an effectively decoupled impurity and nearly vanishing EE, whereas the Kondo-singlet phase has finite EE larger than $\ln 2$ per spin and orbital. When chiral symmetry holds at low energies, this distinction corresponds to a topological transition of the effective bath chain. At the non-Fermi-liquid critical point, the EE develops an unstable plateau. Its $Λ$ dependence resembles that of the overscreened two-channel Kondo model, supporting universality within the same non-Fermi-liquid universality class.

</details>


### [137] [Iterative minimization in reduced density matrix functional theory for periodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27679)
*Kai Luo,Jingang Han,Peize Lin,Daye Zheng,Mohan Chen,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基无关的约化密度矩阵泛函理论（reduced density matrix functional theory, RDMFT），并实现了基于平面波基组的周期性固体计算方法，通过谱投影梯度（spectral projected gradient, SPG）和显式-隐式（explicit-by-implicit, EBI）两种迭代方案优化自然占据数，结合复Stiefel流形上的黎曼优化更新自然轨道。基准测试表明SPG方法在能量收敛和稳定性上优于EBI，并能可靠地应用于多种实际体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMFT为强关联体系提供了一条超越Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（Kohn-Sham density functional theory, KS-DFT）的途径，但此前尚无法实际应用于周期性固体计算，因此需要发展面向扩展体系的可行实现方案。

Method: 该工作以基无关的方式表述用于扩展体系的RDMFT，利用平面波基组和迭代最小化方法处理周期性固体；非局域交换关联泛函通过已有的自适应压缩交换（adaptive compressed exchange）机制求值。自然占据数在N-可表示性（N-representability）约束下使用SPG方法或一阶EBI映射优化，自然轨道则通过在复Stiefel流形（complex Stiefel manifold）上的黎曼优化（Riemannian optimization）更新。

Result: 以Hartree-Fock（HF）泛函对H2、硅和钠进行基准测试显示，SPG方法能够复现收敛的杂化参考结果，而EBI在占据数趋近0或1时可能停滞。使用幂泛函（power functional）和Müller泛函时，SPG比EBI得到更低的能量和更稳定的收敛。应用于分数电荷LiH、解离的H2和N2分子以及硅的状态方程（equation of state）表明该算法可靠且稳健。

Conclusion: 所提出的RDMFT平面波实现方案在周期性固体计算中是可靠和稳健的，其中SPG方法在收敛性能和最终能量方面均优于EBI方法，为RDMFT在强关联扩展体系中的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Reduced density matrix functional theory (RDMFT) offers a route beyond Kohn-Sham density functional theory for strongly correlated systems, yet practical calculations for periodic solids are still out of reach. We formulate RDMFT for extended systems in a basis-independent way and present a planewave implementation using iterative minimization for periodic solids, evaluating nonlocal exchange-correlation functionals through the existing adaptive compressed exchange machinery. Natural occupations are optimized under N-representability constraints with a spectral projected gradient (SPG) method or an first-order explicit-by-implicit (EBI) map, while natural orbitals are updated by Riemannian optimization on the complex Stiefel manifolds. Benchmarks on typical systems of \ce{H2}, silicon, and sodium with the Hartree-Fock functional show that SPG reproduces converged hybrid references, whereas EBI can stall when occupations approach $0$ or $1$. With the power and Müller functionals, SPG yields lower energies and more stable convergence than EBI. Applications to fractionally charged \ce{LiH}, dissociating \ce{H2} and \ce{N2} molecules, and equation of state of silicon show that the algorithm presented in this implementation is reliable and robust.

</details>


### [138] [Trion Excitations in Twisted Bilayer Graphene: A Quantum Monte Carlo Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27745)
*Shibo Shan,Cheng Huang,Patrick Ledwith,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过连续场动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）方法，在扭曲双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）的有限温度正常态中发现了奇异的"狄拉克三子"（Dirac trion）激发——由两个电子和一个空穴组成的三粒子束缚态，其零动量处无隙且与高能电子完全正交，尽管由重成分构成却具有任意轻的有效质量，且光谱可通过扭转角和层间跳跃强度进行调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定电荷载流子的本质是强关联电子系统（strongly correlated electron systems）研究中的基本目标，而扭曲双层石墨烯在有限温度正常态中的激发性质尚不明确，需要无偏的量子多体计算来揭示。

Method: 采用连续场（continuous-field）动量空间量子蒙特卡洛方法，对投影相关平带（projected correlated flat-band）框架下的扭曲双层石墨烯进行无偏量子多体计算，研究其有限温度正常态中的激发谱。

Result: 基态为对称性破缺的绝缘体，具有约20 meV带隙的电子类激发；但当温度升至约3 meV（远低于相互作用尺度）时，系统进入强涨落对称正常态，该正常态承载由两个电子和一个空穴组成的无隙三粒子束缚态——狄拉克三子。这些激发在零动量无隙点与高能电子完全正交，且由重成分构成却具有极轻的质量，其光谱可通过扭转角与层间跳跃强度调节。

Conclusion: 该无偏量子多体计算揭示了强关联拓扑能带（topological bands）中的狄拉克三子激发，超越了朗道能级（Landau levels）以外的强关联拓扑带中多体激发的研究，为后续深入探索此类系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Determining the nature of charge carriers is a fundamental goal in the study of strongly correlated electron systems. Here, we employ the continuous-field momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo method to reveal exotic "Dirac trion" excitations in the finite-temperature normal state of twisted bilayer graphene. While the ground state is a symmetry-breaking insulator with gapped ($\sim$ 20 meV) electron-like excitations, we show that a small temperature ($\sim$ 3 meV), well below the interaction scale, drives the system into a strongly fluctuating symmetric normal state. We demonstrate that this normal state hosts gapless excitations consisting of three-particle bound states, two electrons and one hole, that are exactly orthogonal to the higher-energy electrons at the zero-momentum gapless point. These Dirac trions have the remarkable property of being arbitrarily light despite being composed of heavy constituents, and their spectra can be easily tuned by varying the twist angle and interlayer hopping strength. Our unbiased quantum many-body computation sheds light on the Dirac trions in a projected correlated flat-band setting and opens the door for further investigation of many-body excitations in strongly correlated topological bands beyond Landau levels.

</details>


### [139] [Finite-size effects and interaction-driven crossovers in quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model: Exact diagonalization, DMRG and machine-learning analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27916)
*Md Fahad Equbal,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）、密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group）以及无监督机器学习方法，研究了有限宽度圆柱格点上的四分之一填充吸引哈伯德模型（attractive Hubbard model），揭示了由弱关联费米子到紧束缚单态成对（singlet pair）区域的连续相互作用驱动交叉（crossover），并验证了该行为与热力学极限下的BCS–BEC交叉一致。机器学习方法（主成分分析与UMAP）独立识别出与传统观测量一致的交叉区域，且有限尺寸标度分析表明这些特征随系统尺寸增大保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在系统刻画四分之一填充吸引哈伯德模型中从弱关联费米子到强配对态的转变过程，并希望不依赖人为定义的序参量（order parameter）对多体关联的重组进行无偏描述，从而验证该转变的本质及其在热力学极限下的性质。

Method: 论文采用多种互补方法：精确对角化和密度矩阵重整化群用于计算基态能量、局域观测量及相关函数；通过空穴束缚能（hole-binding energy）提供配对的直接能量证据；同时将主成分分析（principal component analysis, PCA）与均匀流形逼近与投影（uniform manifold approximation and projection, UMAP）两种无监督机器学习技术应用于实空间关联矩阵，实现无偏的关联景观（correlation landscape）刻画。

Result: 研究发现从弱关联费米子到紧束缚单态配对区存在连续的相互作用驱动交叉，源于动能驱动的费米子游动性与相互作用驱动的在位配对之间的竞争。双空穴束缚能在整个吸引区内保持负值，而三空穴束缚仅在足够强的吸引下出现且呈现显著有限尺寸依赖性。PCA揭示关联方差的系统性再分布，UMAP则清晰区分弱配对与强配对两个区域，两种方法独立识别出与传统观测量一致的交叉区域。有限尺寸标度分析表明配对结构因子（pairing structure factor）和主PCA方差比等特征随系统尺寸增大保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了吸引哈伯德模型中存在与BCS–BEC交叉一致的连续配对转变，并证明基于机器学习的无监督方法能够在不需要先验序参量的情况下可靠识别多体关联中的关键转变区域，为复杂强关联体系的表征提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model on finite-width cylindrical lattices using exact diagonalization (ED), density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and unsupervised machine-learning-based techniques. Analysis of the ground-state energetics, local observables and correlation functions reveals a continuous interaction-driven crossover from weakly correlated fermions to a regime dominated by tightly bound singlet pairs. This crossover originates from the competition between kinetic-energy-driven fermionic itinerancy and interaction-driven onsite pair formation and exhibits behavior consistent with the BCS--BEC crossover in the thermodynamic limit. Hole-binding-energy calculations provide direct energetic evidence for pair formation: the two-hole binding energy remains negative throughout the attractive regime whereas three-hole binding emerges only at sufficiently strong attraction and exhibits pronounced finite-size dependence. To obtain an unbiased characterization of the correlation landscape, we apply principal component analysis (PCA) and uniform manifold approximation and projection (UMAP) to the real-space correlation matrices. PCA reveals a systematic redistribution of correlation variance whereas UMAP identifies a clear separation between weak- and strong-pairing regimes. Both machine-learning-based approaches independently identify the same crossover region inferred from conventional observables while providing an order-parameter-independent characterization of the underlying reorganization of many-body correlations. Finite-size scaling analyses of the pairing structure factor and the leading PCA variance ratio demonstrate that these signatures remain robust with increasing system size.

</details>


### [140] [Orbital-Selective Mott Transition and Correlation-Amplified Charge Ordering in the Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28029)
*Xiuhua Chen,Yilin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT+DMFT计算，揭示了交替磁体（altermagnet）CsCr₂S₂O中Verwey型金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）的微观机制：S位点畸变经由Cr-d_yz与S-p轨道杂化引发微小的Cr位电荷不对称，而动力学电子关联将其显著放大，最终驱动MIT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体CsCr₂S₂O经历由晶格畸变和Cr亚晶格上条纹电荷序驱动的Verwey型金属-绝缘体转变（MIT），与Fe₃O₄中的物理机制类似，但原子畸变仅发生在配位体S位点而非Cr位点，Cr位点间为何出现显著电荷不平衡仍是未解之谜。

Method: 采用DFT+DMFT（动力学平均场理论）计算，分析CsCr₂S₂O的电子结构、轨道分辨的电子关联效应，并对比S与Te置换后的行为。

Result: 识别出一个轨道选择性Mott转变（orbital-selective Mott transition），使关联金属性的d_yz轨道主导低能物理；S位畸变通过Cr-d_yz和S-p轨道杂化触发微小Cr位电荷不对称，该不对称被动力学电子关联显著放大，导致不同Cr位点间电荷和电子关联出现巨大差异，进而引发局域自旋极化的显著分化并驱动MIT。相反，用Te替换S会削弱电子关联，减弱该放大效应从而无法诱导MIT。

Conclusion: 多体效应能大幅放大配位体不稳定性并重塑交替磁体的电子结构，表明配位体工程（ligand engineering）对实现稳健的金属性交替磁性至关重要。

Abstract: Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O undergoes a Verwey-type metal-to-insulator transition (MIT) driven by lattice distortion and a stripe charge order on the Cr sublattice, reminiscent of the physics in Fe$_3$O$_4$. However, atomic distortions occur exclusively at the ligand sites rather than the Cr sites. What drives such a pronounced charge imbalance between Cr sites thus remains a mystery. Utilizing DFT+DMFT calculations, we identify an orbital-selective Mott transition, which leaves the correlated metallic $d_{yz}$ orbital governing the low-energy physics. We demonstrate that S-site distortions trigger an initial, tiny charge asymmetry between Cr sites via Cr-$d_{yz}$ and S-$p$ orbital hybridization. Crucially, this asymmetry is significantly amplified by dynamical electronic correlations, resulting in a large discrepancy in both the charge and electron correlations of the Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbital between distinct Cr sites. This further induces a substantial differentiation in local spin polarizations in the altermagnetic state, ultimately driving the MIT. In contrast, we predict that replacing S with Te weakens this correlation-amplification effect and fails to induce an MIT due to weaker electron correlations. Our findings demonstrate that many-body effects can drastically amplify ligand instabilities to reshape the electronic structure of altermagnets, highlighting that ligand engineering is of paramount importance for realizing robust metallic altermagnetism.

</details>


### [141] [Thermal spin transport in easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnets controlled by magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28085)
*Yuliia I. Gusieva,Kostiantyn V. Yershov,Jeroen van den Brink,Volodymyr P. Kravchuk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究易平面型d波交替磁体（如金红石结构的NiF₂）中交替磁体产生的磁振子磁矩，发现该磁矩具有动量依赖性和d波对称性，导致磁振子驱动的自旋分流效应及可调热自旋电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnets）中磁振子支在非相对论极限下即发生自旋劈裂，这一效应对磁振子输运有强烈影响，在易轴型d波交替磁体中已发现热磁振子分流效应（thermal magnon splitter effect）。然而该效应是否存在于易平面型体系中尚不明确，因为此类体系中磁振子支不携带恒定磁矩，本文旨在探讨这一问题。

Method: 以金红石型易平面d波交替磁体（如NiF₂）为研究对象，理论分析交替磁体诱导的磁振子磁矩的动量依赖性和对称性，并考察温度梯度下磁振子驱动的自旋流（即磁矩流）的涌现，以及外部磁场对该体系热自旋电导率的调控作用。

Result: 研究证明易平面d波交替磁体中涌现出交替磁体产生的磁振子磁矩，该磁矩具有动量依赖性并呈现d波对称性，由此导致自旋分流效应（spin-splitter effect），即温度梯度下产生磁振子驱动的自旋流；同时发现垂直施加于易平面的外磁场可有效调控相应的热自旋电导率。

Conclusion: 易平面型d波交替磁体同样可实现磁振子驱动的自旋分流效应，且其热自旋输运性质可通过外部磁场进行有效调节，这为交替磁体系中的自旋热输运操控提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Altermagnets constitute a novel class of collinear spin-compensated materials in which magnon branches are spin-split even in the non-relativistic limit. The latter is the result of a more complex symmetry operation (compared to conventional antiferromagnets) that connects the two sublattices. Altermagnetic splitting strongly affects the magnon transport properties, leading, in particular, to the thermal magnon splitter effect in easy-axial $d$-wave altermagnets. Whether this effect also appears in easy-planar systems is not obvious, since the magnon branches do not carry a constant magnetic moment in this case. Here, we consider the easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnet of a rutile-type, e.g., NiF$_2$, and demonstrate the emergence of the altermagnetically generated magnon magnetic moment, which is momentum-dependent and possesses $d$-wave symmetry. This then leads to the spin-splitter effect, i.e., the emergence of a magnon-driven spin current (the flow of magnetic moment) in response to the applied temperature gradient. We also demonstrate that the corresponding thermal spin conductivity can be effectively tuned by an external magnetic field perpendicular to the easy plane.

</details>


### [142] [Coupled Spin-Density-Wave and Bond-Order Driven Metal-Insulator Transition in Altermagnetic CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28329)
*Chenchao Xu,Wansheng Bai,Guo-Xiang Zhi,Yi Liu,Xiaoqun Wang,Jianhui Dai,Chao Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在CsCr₂S₂O中识别出一种由键序（bond order, BO）与次级自旋密度波（spin-density wave, SDW）耦合驱动的金属-绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transition, MIT）。该耦合源于预先存在的C型反铁磁（C-type antiferromagnetic, C-AFM）序导致的时间反演对称性破缺，并与轨道选择性（orbital-selective）物理相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CsCr₂S₂O中金属-绝缘体转变的微观驱动机制，特别是预先存在的反铁磁序、电子关联与轨道自由度如何协同引发结构相变。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算，分析Cr-d_yz与Cr-d_xz轨道的选区占据行为，并结合在位相互作用（on-site interaction）考察次级自旋密度波不稳定性及其与键序的耦合效应。

Result: 计算表明Cr-d_yz轨道形成局域磁矩并建立交变磁（altermagnetic）序，而Cr-d_xz轨道保持金属性并与S-p_z轨道杂化；在位相互作用增强了d_xz电子的sSDW不稳定性，该sSDW与Cr-d_xz-S-p_z键序耦合，同时产生实验观测到的结构畸变、电荷歧化（charge disproportionation）、局域Cr磁矩调制及能隙开启。

Conclusion: 研究建立了轨道选择性机制：预先存在的交变磁性（altermagnetism）与电子关联协同作用，驱动了结构性的金属-绝缘体转变。

Abstract: A metal-insulator transition (MIT) driven by bond order (BO) coupled with a secondary spin-density wave (SDW) is identified in CsCr$_2$S$_2$O. Such coupling is enabled as a result of the broken time-reversal symmetry due to the pre-existing C-type antiferromagnetic (C-AFM) order. First-principles calculations reveal an orbital-selective physics that Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbitals form local moments and establish the altermagnetic order, while the Cr-$d_{xz}$ orbitals remain metallic and hybridize with S-$p_z$. Thus the low-energy physics is governed by the Cr-$d_{xz}$ and S-$p_z$ orbitals. On-site interactions then enhance a secondary SDW ($s$SDW) instability of the itinerant $d_{xz}$ electrons, which couples to the Cr-$d_{xz}$-S-$p_z$ bonding order. The resulting coupled $s$SDW-BO simultaneously produces experimentally observed structural distortion, charge disproportionation, local Cr-moment modulation, and gap opening. Our results establish an orbital-selective mechanism upon which pre-existing altermagnetism and electronic correlations cooperate to drive a structural MIT.

</details>


### [143] [Field-Selected Topological Buffering in a Disordered Skyrmion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28458)
*Wenyu Su,Nvsen Ma,Chen Cheng,Hong-Gang Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本论文通过大规模模拟三角晶格手性磁体（chiral magnet）中随机DM相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interactions）的影响，揭示了磁场会选择两种不同的无序化路径：高场下存在一个"拓扑缓冲区间"（topological buffer），包含类 Bragg 玻璃态（Bragg-glass-like）的斯格明子态和随后出现的斯格明子玻璃态（skyrmion-glass）；低场下该缓冲区间被压缩甚至消失。研究将磁场确立为调控晶体无序化与拓扑荷损失的"控制旋钮"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火无序（quenched disorder）可以破坏晶体有序性而不立即摧毁其拓扑纹理结构，但斯格明子晶体（skyrmion crystal）中这两个过程之间的关系此前尚不明确。作者旨在厘清晶体无序化与拓扑结构损失之间的关联机制。

Method: 采用三角晶格手性磁体的大型数值模拟（large-scale simulation），在体系中引入随机DM相互作用以代表淬火无序；通过有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）、空间关联函数（spatial correlations）、缺陷统计（defect statistics）以及自旋自关联函数（spin autocorrelations）等工具来刻画不同无序强度下的结构转变与玻璃态特征。

Result: 高场下，全局平移相干性在弱无序尺度即丧失，但六重键取向序（sixfold bond-orientational order）可存活到更大的无序强度，且总拓扑荷（total topological charge）被近锁定到更大的尺度；由此形成一个拓扑缓冲区间，先出现类Bragg玻璃态的斯格明子态，再过渡到斯格明子玻璃态。低场下，键取向无序化与拓扑重构几乎同时发生，斯格明子玻璃窗口被消除、缓冲区被压缩。

Conclusion: 磁场被确认为一个控制旋钮，可用来分离晶体解序与拓扑荷损失；拓扑缓冲效应（topological buffering）被确立为一种机制，使拓扑纹理在结构无序介质中仍能保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quenched disorder can disrupt crystalline order without immediately destroying the topology of its constituent textures, but the relation between these processes in skyrmion crystals remains unclear. Using large-scale simulations of a triangular-lattice chiral magnet with random DM interactions, we show that the magnetic field selects between two disordering routes. At high fields, global translational coherence is lost at a weak-disorder scale, while sixfold bond-orientational order survives to a larger disorder strength and the total topological charge remains nearly locked up to a substantially larger scale. The resulting interval defines a topological buffer containing a Bragg-glass- like skyrmion regime followed by a skyrmion-glass regime. Finite-size scaling, spatial correlations, defect statistics, and spin autocorrelations support their distinct structural and glassy character. At lower fields, bond-orientational disordering nearly coincides with topological reconstruction, eliminating the skyrmion-glass window and contracting the buffer. These results identify the magnetic field as a control knob for separating crystalline disordering from topological-charge loss and establish topological buffering as a mechanism by which topological textures can remain robust in structurally disordered media.

</details>


### [144] [From Exact Diagonalization to DMRG: A Complete Numerical Study of the Transverse-Field Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28471)
*Chandra Sekhar Prayaga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对一维横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model, TFIM）的基态纠缠结构进行了自洽的数值研究，从精确对角化（exact diagonalization）小尺寸（L=8,14,20）到密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法大尺寸（L=100），采用统一的收敛判据，解析了键维度收敛伪迹，并通过两种互补途径提取临界点的中心荷（central charge）为c_eff≈0.51–0.52，与二维伊辛精确值c=1/2一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在提供一个自洽、严谨的一维横场伊辛模型基态纠缠数值研究，解决此前数据集中的键维度收敛伪迹导致的虚假不连续性，并通过精细的有限尺寸标度提取临界中心荷。

Method: 在七个系统尺寸上采用完全一致的方法论——相同的场网格和相同的收敛诊断（每个点独立运行键维度chi=100和chi=200）——结合精确对角化（L=8,14,20）与DMRG（至L=100）；通过两条互补途径（链中熵的领头对数有限尺寸标度，以及同时应用于所有系统尺寸所有键的Calabrese-Cardy公式）提取中心荷。

Result: 解析了键维度收敛伪迹并给出警示性案例；系统排除短距离晶格修正后得到有效中心荷c_eff为0.51–0.52，与精确二维伊辛值c=1/2一致；明确展示了精确对角化与DMRG在何处必须精确吻合而非仅是近似一致。

Conclusion: 该量子临界点的中心荷通过两种独立标度途径均收敛到c=1/2，并由Suzuki-Trotter量子经典对应和Widom-Kadanoff标度假设提供理论支撑；本文同时作为谨慎有限尺寸数值方法的工作范例。

Abstract: We present a self-contained numerical study of the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (TFIM), tracing its ground-state entanglement structure from exact diagonalization at small system size (L=8,14,20) through density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations up to L=100. A single, consistent methodology - identical field grid, identical convergence diagnostic (independent runs at bond dimensions chi=100 and chi=200 at every point) - is used across all seven system sizes, and we show explicitly where exact diagonalization and DMRG must agree exactly rather than merely approximately. Along the way we document and resolve a bond-dimension convergence artifact that produced a spurious discontinuity in an earlier, less systematic dataset, as a worked cautionary example for practitioners. Using the resolved dataset we extract the central charge of the transition via two complementary routes - the leading logarithmic finite-size scaling of the mid-chain entropy, and the full Calabrese-Cardy formula applied to every bond of every system size simultaneously - obtaining c_eff to 0.51-0.52 as short-distance lattice corrections are systematically excluded, consistent with the exact two-dimensional Ising value c=1/2. We review the quantum-classical (Suzuki-Trotter) correspondence that underlies this agreement and discuss how the Widom-Kadanoff scaling hypothesis transplants from the classical to the quantum problem. The manuscript is intended as both a physics result and a worked methodological example of careful finite-size numerics.

</details>


### [145] [Graph Neural Network Force Fields for Spin Dynamics in Metallic Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28537)
*Ali Rayat,Yunhao Fan,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）的磁性力场（magnetic force-field）框架，直接从电子结构计算中学习有效磁能量泛函，从而高效评估巡游自旋动力学（itinerant spin dynamics）中的自旋力矩（spin torques），并在共线、非共线和非共面磁序的代表性金属磁性体系中验证了其与直接电子模拟结果的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属磁性体中的自旋动力学由电子产生的相互作用控制，而预测此类动力学通常需要在时间演化过程中反复求解底层电子问题，构成重大计算瓶颈，亟需更高效的替代方法。

Method: 作者引入一个图神经网络磁性力场框架，直接从电子计算中学习支配巡游自旋动力学的有效磁能量泛函；该方法在概念上类比于机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials），能够高效评估自旋力矩，并捕捉巡游电子产生的非线性、空间扩展的相互作用。

Result: 在具有共线（collinear）、非共线（noncollinear）和非共面（noncoplanar）磁序的代表性金属磁性体系中，学习得到的力场能够准确重现电子产生的自旋力矩，所生成的非平衡自旋动力学与直接电子模拟结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了图神经网络作为机器学习磁性力场的强大框架，为跨越多个长度和时间尺度的非平衡磁性大规模预测性模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metallic magnets exhibit complex spin dynamics governed by electronically generated interactions. Predictive simulations of such dynamics typically require repeated solutions of an underlying electronic problem throughout the time evolution, creating a major computational bottleneck. Here we introduce a graph neural network (GNN) magnetic force-field framework that learns the effective magnetic energy functional governing itinerant spin dynamics directly from electronic calculations. Conceptually analogous to machine-learned interatomic potentials, the proposed framework enables efficient evaluation of spin torques while capturing the nonlinear and spatially extended interactions generated by itinerant electrons. We benchmark the method on representative metallic magnetic systems exhibiting collinear, noncollinear, and noncoplanar magnetic order. The learned force fields accurately reproduce electronically generated spin torques and yield nonequilibrium spin dynamics in excellent agreement with direct electronic simulations. Our results establish graph neural networks as a powerful framework for machine-learned magnetic force fields, providing a pathway toward predictive large-scale simulations of nonequilibrium magnetism across multiple length and time scales.

</details>


### [146] [Lattice composite Fermi liquid with broken inversion symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28613)
*Pavel A. Nosov,Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究半满Chern能带中具有破缺反演对称性的晶格复合费米液体（composite Fermi liquid）的输运性质，发现晶格对称性降低会暴露复合费米子固有的奇异动力学响应，包括非解析的纵向光电阻率、非互易输运以及2kF散射重整化导致的奇异直流电阻率温度依赖性，这些特征在连续量子霍尔体系（quantum Hall system）中不存在，并可在扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯等实验体系中直接检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复合费米液体框架主要在Landau量子化能级（Landau-level）设置下研究，其动力学响应可能掩盖某些固有奇异行为；通过研究半满Chern能带中破缺反演对称性的晶格复合费米液体，可以揭示在连续量子霍尔对应物中缺失的、独特的输运特征。

Method: 论文从理论上分析晶格复合费米液体的输运性质，考虑零波矢（zero wave vector）和有限波矢（finite wave vector）两种情形下反演对称性破缺的影响；分别考察规范场涨落（gauge-field fluctuations）对光电阻率的作用、有限波矢下霍尔电导率的非解析行为，以及2kF散射重整化对直流输运的影响。

Result: 在零波矢处，反演对称性破缺使规范场涨落产生非解析纵向光电阻率，其标度关系为Reρxx(ω)∼|ω|^{4/3}（对栅极屏蔽库仑相互作用而言）；在有限波矢处，反演破缺导致非互易输运（nonreciprocal transport）和霍尔电导率非解析的∼|q|依赖关系；此外，复合费米面上2kF散射的重整化会增强Umklapp弛豫，导致电阻率具有非解析的温度依赖关系。

Conclusion: 这些发现确定了晶格复合费米液体所特有的输运特征，其在连续量子霍尔对应物中不存在，可直接在扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯等近期报道零场复合费米液体证据的实验体系中检验。

Abstract: We study transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids realized in half-filled Chern bands with broken inversion symmetry. We show that reduced crystalline symmetry exposes intrinsic singular dynamical responses of composite fermions that are otherwise hidden in the conventional Landau-level setting. At zero wave vector, inversion breaking allows gauge-field fluctuations to generate a non-analytic longitudinal optical resistivity, with $\operatorname{Re}ρ^{xx}(ω)\sim |ω|^{4/3}$ for gate-screened Coulomb interactions. At finite wave vector $\mathbf{q}$, inversion breaking leads to nonreciprocal transport and a non-analytic $\sim |\mathbf{q}|$ dependence of the Hall conductivity, both of which can be probed through surface acoustic wave propagation. We also discuss a distinct mechanism for singular DC transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids: renormalization of $2k_F$ scattering at the composite Fermi surface enhances Umklapp relaxation and can lead to a non-analytic temperature dependence of the resistivity. Taken together, our results identify transport signatures of lattice composite Fermi liquids that are absent in their continuum quantum Hall counterparts and can be directly tested in ongoing experiments on twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene, where evidence for zero-field composite Fermi liquids has recently been reported.

</details>

### [147] [Semi-supervised Hopfield model: Theoretical and Numerical results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28173)
*Linda Albanese,Andrea Ladiana,Andrea Lepre*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文针对Hopfield网络构建了半监督Hebbian学习的统计力学理论：将监督与无监督Hebbian核通过混合参数λ进行凸组合作为突触耦合，通过信噪比分析和Guerra插值法推导出一阶Mattis磁化强度与高存储容量下的Replica对称淬火压力，并证明混合策略优于纯监督或纯无监督协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习实践中完全标注的数据集是稀缺资源，而半监督学习是应对标注稀缺的标准方案，但半监督Hebbian学习的统计力学理论仍属空白。本文旨在填补这一空白，为Hopfield网络建立半监督学习的严格统计力学框架。

Method: 该方法将监督与无监督Hebbian核的凸组合作为突触耦合，权重由混合参数λ∈[0,1]控制；通过信噪比（signal-to-noise）分析推导一步Mattis磁化强度与学习阈值；利用Guerra插值法在高存储容量区推导Replica对称淬火压力，并通过特征渠道分解处理监督与无监督通道产生的关联无序；最后证明淬火压力关于λ的凸性。

Result: 相图显示混合策略性能优于纯监督和纯无监督两种协议；淬火压力关于λ为凸函数，表明热力学无法选择内部混合解，因此λ必须作为学习超参数处理；所有解析结果均与大规模Monte Carlo模拟一致验证。

Conclusion: 半监督Hebbian学习在Hopfield网络中可获得严格的统计力学描述，混合策略在检索能力上占优，但最优混合比例无法由热力学自洽确定，λ应作为学习超参数人工调优。

Abstract: In the daily practice of Machine Learning, fully labeled datasets are a luxury: labels demand expensive and time-consuming human annotation, whereas raw, unlabeled data can be harvested automatically and in bulk. Semi-supervised learning, where the network jointly exploits the few labeled and the many unlabeled examples at its disposal, is the standard answer to this asymmetry, yet a statistical mechanical theory of semi-supervised Hebbian learning is still lacking. In this paper we fill this gap for the Hopfield network: we prescribe a synaptic coupling given by the convex combination, weighted by a mixing parameter λin [0,1], of the supervised and unsupervised Hebbian kernels built from the same archetypes, and we solve for the emergent computational capabilities of the resulting network. A signal-to-noise analysis yields the one-step Mattis magnetization and the learning threshold, i.e. the minimum dataset size for stable retrieval. Using Guerra's interpolation, we then derive the Replica Symmetric quenched pressure in the high-storage regime, treating the correlated disorder generated by the supervised and unsupervised channels through a particular eigen-channel decomposition. The resulting phase diagram shows that a mixed strategy outperforms both pure protocols. Finally, we prove that the quenched pressure is convex in λ, so thermodynamics cannot select an interior mixture: λis therefore a learning hyperparameter. All the analytical findings are successfully checked against extensive Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Fast and Accurate Foundation Models for Equivariant Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28461)
*Seán R. Kavanagh,Chuin Wei Tan,Menghang Wang,Marc L. Descoteaux,Gabriel de Miranda Nascimento,Ulrik Unneberg,Laura Zichi,Francesco Libbi,Norma Rivano,Austin Glover,Vivek Bharadwaj,Anders Johansson,William C. Witt,Albert Musaelian,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了等变机器学习原子间势（equivariant MLIPs）在极大数据集条件下实现高推理速度、强可扩展性与高精度的可行性，并基于NequIP与Allegro架构提出了一个基础势（foundation potential）家族，在多个社区基准测试中取得领先性能，同时显著降低了超大数据集上的训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）作为"基础模型"（foundation models）被广泛用于定向化学空间的微调，但其科学应用（如分子动力学，MD）要求同时具备高推理/训练速度与高精度。在极大数据集场景下数据效率不再关键，如何平衡速度、可扩展性与精度成为亟需解决的核心问题。

Method: 作者在等变MLIP架构（直接编码物理对称性）框架内分析速度与精度之间的权衡，并在NequIP和Allegro两种等变架构中实现了加速优化与规模化训练，构建了一族基础势模型，同时通过数据集的多样化设计来提升材料发现任务的模型精度。

Result: 所提出的基础势在材料发现、热导率预测以及近平衡力学和热力学性质等多个社区基准测试中展现了领先的推理速度、强大的可扩展性以及优异的精度；NequIP基础设施中实现的加速使得在超大数据集上训练高精度基础势的计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 研究表明，提升材料发现模型精度的重点应放在数据集多样性与过渡金属化合物能量表面的改进、一致描述上，而非单纯追求模型架构的复杂化。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a transformative tool for computational materials science and chemistry, with universal potentials trained on large and diverse datasets now routinely deployed as 'foundation models' for downstream fine-tuning in targeted chemical spaces. Many scientific applications of the resulting models, such as molecular dynamics (MD), require high inference and training speeds as well as accuracy. In this work, we examine the limits of equivariant MLIPs, which directly encode physical symmetries in model architectures, to achieve these competing targets -- particularly in the regime of extremely large datasets where data efficiency is less critical. We show how this trade-off can be addressed, and present a family of foundation potentials in the NequIP and Allegro equivariant MLIP architectures which achieve leading inference speeds and strong scalability as well as excellent accuracies across a range of community benchmarks -- spanning materials discovery, thermal conductivity prediction, and near-equilibrium mechanical and thermodynamic properties. Accelerations implemented within the NequIP infrastructure now permit training of high-accuracy foundation potentials on ultra-large datasets with dramatically reduced computational cost. Alongside, we show that efforts to improve model accuracy for materials discovery should focus on dataset diversity and improved, consistent descriptions of transition metal compound energy surfaces.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Self-avoiding polygons on a three-row square-lattice strip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27397)
*Michael von Thaden*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了带状图$S_2:=\mathbb{Z}\times\{0,1,2\}$上长度为$n$的自回避多边形（self-avoiding polygons, SAPs）计数$p^{S_2}(n)$的闭式公式，并针对由最左列和最右列垂直步数确定的自回避多边形子类型给出了相应闭式公式。对于最左列和最右列各含两个垂直步的子类型，还推导了二项式和的替代表示，并由此得到Larsen处理Gessel所提问题中出现的一条恒等式的新几何证明。此外，该子类型与整数序列A007909密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回避多边形是统计力学与组合数学中的重要研究对象，带条（strip）上的计数问题具有基础性意义。作者旨在为$S_2$带条上的自回避多边形提供闭式计数公式，并建立其与已知组合恒等式及整数序列之间的联系。

Method: 推导方法完全是初等的，采用纯组合与几何论证，避免了生成函数（generating functions）的运用。通过将不同计数表达式相互比较，作者获得了一个关于Larsen在解决Gessel问题中出现的恒等式的新几何证明。

Result: 给出了$S_2$带上自回避多边形的总数$p^{S_2}(n)$及其子类型的闭式公式；对于最左列和最右列各含两个垂直步的子类型，得到了二项式和的替代表示。证明了当$m\geq0$时，长度为$2m+6$的这一子类型自回避多边形数目等于整数序列A007909的第$m$项。

Conclusion: 通过闭式公式与二项式和的比较，给出了Larsen-Gessel恒等式的一个新几何证明；同时揭示了该特殊子类型与A007909序列之间的对应关系，为该序列提供了除组合（compositions）计数之外的几何解释。

Abstract: We give a closed formula for the number $p^{S_2}(n)$ of self-avoiding polygons (SAPs) of length $n$ on the strip $S_2:=\mathbb{Z}\times\{0,1,2\}$, together with closed formulas for those subtypes of SAPs which are determined by the numbers of vertical steps in their leftmost and rightmost columns. For the subtype whose leftmost and rightmost columns each contain two vertical steps, we also derive an alternative representation as a binomial sum. Our derivation is elementary: it is purely combinatorial and geometric and avoids generating functions. Comparing the two representations yields a new geometric proof of an identity arising in Larsen's treatment \cite{L07} of a problem posed by Gessel \cite{G95}. Finally, we show that this subtype of SAPs is closely connected to the sequence A007909. More precisely, for $m\geq0$, the number of these SAPs whose leftmost and rightmost columns each contain two vertical steps and whose length equals $2m+6$ is given by the term of this sequence with index $m$, which thereby acquires a geometric interpretation alongside the compositions it enumerates.

</details>


### [150] [From Kreweras walks to branching perimeter processes of percolated triangulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28566)
*Renan Gross,Emmanuel Kammerer,Frederik Ravn Klausen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Bernardi、Holden和Sun的结果，建立了三角剖分（triangulation）渗流（percolation）界面上的剥离过程（peeling process）的周长与对应的Kreweras游走（Kreweras walk）之间的显式几何关系，并将该关系自然推广到分支剥离探索（branching peeling exploration），从而揭示了增长-碎片化过程（growth-fragmentation process）与关联布朗游走（correlated Brownian excursions）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于揭示随机三角剖分中剥离过程与组合游走之间的内在几何联系，进而阐明增长-碎片化过程与关联布朗游走之间此前由Da Silva、Powell和Watson发现的关联背后的机制。

Method: 方法上，作者基于Bernardi、Holden和Sun的一个已知结果，通过构造显式的几何对应关系，将剥离过程沿渗流界面的周长与Kreweras游走的相应量联系起来，并将此关系从标准的剥离过程推广至分支剥离探索（branching peeling exploration）。

Result: 文中得到了剥离过程的周长与Kreweras游走之间的显式几何关系，且该关系可自然推广到分支剥离探索情形。

Conclusion: 该显式几何关系为增长-碎片化过程与关联布朗游走之间的联系提供了新的视角和解释，进一步深化了对随机三角剖分渗流界面相关随机结构之间关联的理解。

Abstract: In this note, using a result of Bernardi, Holden and Sun, we give an explicit geometric relation between the perimeter of the peeling process along the percolation interface of a triangulation, and the corresponding Kreweras walk. The relation naturally extends to the branching peeling exploration. This sheds light on the relation between the growth-fragmentation process and correlated Brownian excursions discovered by Da Silva, Powell and Watson.

</details>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Phase noise analysis and control of VO$_2$-based relaxation type oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27447)
*Artem Litvinenko,Erbin Qiu,Sambit Ghosh,Juan Andres Hofer,Akash Kumar,Jong-Guk Choi,Ivan K. Schuller,Johan Åkerman*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于VO₂的弛豫振荡器中的相位噪声（phase noise），证明低振荡频率下频谱线宽展宽源于孵化阶段（incubation phase）对热涨落（thermal fluctuations）敏感性的增强，并发现方波注入锁定比正弦波注入锁定能更有效地改善相位噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VO₂基弛豫振荡器在神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）、伊辛机（Ising machine）和信号处理等领域有广泛应用，但其运行受到热效应支配，导致额外电压涨落和较宽的频谱线宽，制约了器件性能。现有研究对这一相位噪声来源及抑制机制尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 对VO₂基弛豫振荡器进行相位噪声的系统性实验研究，分析不同类型噪声源对振荡器稳定性的影响，并比较外部方波信号与正弦波信号注入锁定两种同步方案对相位噪声的改善效果。

Result: 研究表明，低振荡频率下发生频谱线宽（spectral linewidth）展宽的根源在于孵化阶段对热涨落的敏感性增强；与正弦波注入锁定信号相比，外部方波信号的同步能够更有效地改善相位噪声。

Conclusion: VO₂弛豫振荡器的相位噪声行为受热效应驱动下的孵化阶段热涨落主导，且方波注入锁定是比正弦波注入锁定更优的相位噪声抑制策略。

Abstract: VO$_2$-based relaxation oscillators form a rapidly developing field that finds applications in neuromorphic computing, Ising machines, and numerous signal processing concepts. These oscillators operate in a deeply nonlinear relaxation regime based on rapid phase transitions between insulating and metallic states in the VO$_2$ material. This process is governed by thermal effects, which lead to additional voltage fluctuations and contribute to a considerably wide spectral linewidth in the VO$_2$-based oscillator signal. In this work, we thoroughly study the phase noise in VO$_2$-based relaxation oscillators and demonstrate that the broadening of the generation spectrum linewidth at low oscillation frequencies is caused by an increased susceptibility to thermal fluctuations during the incubation phase. We explore the types of noise affecting oscillator stability and show that synchronization with an external square-wave signal improves the phase noise more effectively than a sinusoidal-shape injection locking signal.

</details>


### [152] [Vibe-FDTR: An agent-oriented framework for reproducible frequency-domain thermoreflectance data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28200)
*Fuwei Yang,Weiheng Li,Bai Song*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: Vibe-FDTR是一个面向智能体（agent）的框架，通过将配置驱动的频域热反射（FDTR）代码包与程序化智能体技能相结合，使大语言模型（LLM）智能体能够直接从自然语言请求出发，执行可靠且可复现的FDTR热学分析。在合成单步任务和真实多步任务基准上分别达到100%和98.9%的成功率，同时大幅降低计算成本与执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频域热反射（Frequency-domain thermoreflectance, FDTR）是一种广泛用于微纳尺度热学性质测量的激光泵浦-探测技术，但其数据分析过程复杂，严重依赖领域专家的经验，且容易受到细微人为错误的影响。因此需要一种能够降低使用门槛、提高可靠性并支持自动化的FDTR分析方法。

Method: 提出Vibe-FDTR框架，该框架由两部分组成：一是配置驱动的FDTR代码包，用于确保物理和参数的一致性；二是程序化智能体技能（procedural agent skills），用于将用户意图转化为有组织、可验证的分析步骤。框架允许LLM智能体直接从自然语言请求驱动分析流程。

Result: 在包含合成单步任务和基于金包覆石墨样品测量的真实多步任务的两级控制基准上，使用Vibe-FDTR的智能体成功率分别达到100%和98.9%；相比之下，移除技能模块（Code-agent）后降至91.4%和36.7%，进一步移除领域包（仅智能体）后降至38.6%和0%。Vibe-FDTR相较于Code-agent变体将计算成本降低87.7%，并将执行时间削减超过60%。

Conclusion: 将领域代码和专家知识封装为智能体技能，为实现低门槛、自主且可信赖的热测量（thermal metrology）提供了一条有前景的路径。可选的专家模式还能通过自主灵敏度与不确定度评估支持实验规划，并为参数欠定义的任务生成基于物理的推荐。

Abstract: Frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR) is a laser pump-probe technique widely used to measure thermal properties at the micro- and nanoscale; however, it relies on a complex data analysis procedure that demands substantial domain expertise and is susceptible to subtle human errors. Here, we present Vibe-FDTR, an agent-oriented framework that enables large language model (LLM) agents to perform reliable and reproducible FDTR analyses directly from natural language requests. This framework couples a configuration-driven FDTR code package, which enforces physical and parametric consistency, with procedural agent skills that translate user intentions into organized and verifiable analysis steps. We evaluate Vibe-FDTR using a controlled benchmark with two levels: synthetic single-step tasks and real-data multi-step tasks based on measurements of gold-coated graphite samples. Across the two levels, agents using Vibe-FDTR achieve success rates of 100% and 98.9%, respectively. In sharp contrast, ablating skills (Code-agent) reduces performance to 91.4% and 36.7%, which drops further to 38.6% and 0% when the domain package is also omitted (Agent-only). Beyond success rate, Vibe-FDTR also reduces computational cost by 87.7% relative to the Code-agent variant and cuts execution time by more than 60%. Finally, an optional expert mode supports experimental planning via autonomous sensitivity and uncertainty evaluations, and formulates physically grounded recommendations for underspecified tasks. These results demonstrate that encapsulating domain code and expert knowledge into agent skills offers a promising route toward low-barrier, autonomous, and trustworthy thermal metrology.

</details>

### [153] [Ghosts that Connect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27297)
*Henrique Gomes*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将鬼场（ghost）解释为场空间主丛上的主联络（principal connection），将BRST算子解释为场空间上的竖直外微分（vertical exterior derivative），从而同时消解Faddeev–Popov程序的两个概念性谜题：鬼场的来源以及BRST对称性为何在规范固定（gauge-fixing）后仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Faddeev–Popov程序存在两个概念性谜题：其一，若规范等价构型代表相同物理，商空间F/G应足以计算物理振幅，但程序却需要引入在商空间上无对应物的反对易辅助场（鬼场），需阐明它们编码了F的何种结构；其二，规范固定本应消除局域规范对称性，但规范固定后的理论仍保留BRST——一个作为无穷小规范变换作用于规范势的残余对称性，需解释其存续原因。

Method: 遵循Dougherty（2021）和Dougherty与Read（2026）的思路，将鬼场视为F→F/G这一纤维丛（fiber bundle）上的经典内容，但具体识别为不同的结构：该丛上的一个主联络（principal connection）ϖ。据此，鬼场即ϖ，BRST算子即场空间上的竖直外微分，Maurer–Cartan方程即其竖直Cartan结构方程。Faddeev–Popov演算仅依赖ϖ的竖直内容（这也被代数解读所捕捉），而ϖ的水平内容——Vilkovisky–DeWitt纲领所依赖的部分——提供了规范固定、穿衣服（dressing）量子化及反事实比较所需的跨轨道（cross-orbit）配对，这一配对被商空间所舍弃。

Result: 两个谜题共同消解：鬼场编码了F→F/G丛上的主联络的竖直内容，BRST是保持这一跨轨道配对的刚性、竖直对称性，这正是它在规范固定后仍然存在的原因。

Conclusion: Faddeev–Popov程序的两个概念困惑并非独立问题，而是同一结构（主联络ϖ的竖直与水平内容）的两个方面；BRST的存续源于其作为保持跨轨道配对的竖直对称性这一本质角色。

Abstract: The Faddeev--Popov procedure poses two conceptual puzzles. \emph{Puzzle~(1)}: if gauge-equivalent configurations represent the same physics, the quotient $\F/\G$ should suffice to compute physical amplitudes --- yet the procedure requires anti-commuting auxiliary fields, the ghosts, with no analogue on the quotient. What structure of $\F$ do they encode? \emph{Puzzle~(2)}: gauge-fixing was supposed to eliminate local gauge symmetry, yet the gauge-fixed theory retains BRST --- a residual symmetry that acts on the gauge potential as an infinitesimal gauge transformation. Why does it survive?
  Both puzzles dissolve together. Following \textcite{Dougherty2021} and \textcite{DoughertyRead2026}, I take ghosts to encode classical content of $\F \to \F/\G$, but identify a different structure: a principal connection $\varpi$ on this bundle. The ghost is $\varpi$; the BRST operator is the vertical exterior derivative on field space; the Maurer--Cartan equation is its vertical Cartan structure equation. The Faddeev--Popov calculus draws only on $\varpi$'s vertical content, which the algebraic reading also captures; $\varpi$'s horizontal content, on which the Vilkovisky--DeWitt programme rests, supplies the cross-orbit pairing that gauge-fixing, dressing-based quantisation, and counterfactual comparison require and the quotient discards. BRST is the rigid, vertical symmetry that preserves this pairing; this is why it survives gauge-fixing.

</details>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Unification of Quantum Graph Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28024)
*Gian Luca Spitzer*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文针对量子图（quantum graph）中经典图论性质难以直接推广的问题，引入了一种关于量子点子集（subsets of a quantum set）的自然替代定义，并在此基础上将图的染色（colourings）、连通分量（connected components）、独立集（independent sets）和团（cliques）等性质统一推广到量子设置中；研究显示新定义能恢复已有概念，同时对独立集和团的定义提出了改进性变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图论中许多性质（如连通分量、独立集）基于顶点集的子集定义，而量子图中"量子点子集"的自然概念过于刚性，导致这些经典性质的量子推广缺乏统一性，同一概念甚至存在多种不等价的定义，亟需一种自然且动机充分的统一框架。

Method: 作者首先引入一种量子点子集的新替代定义，以此作为基础，将经典图论中的性质定义进行直接推广，形成统一的量子图性质定义框架；并在此框架下检验已有的染色与连通分量概念，同时提出独立集和团的新定义变体。

Result: 新框架能够恢复已有文献中关于染色和连通分量的既定概念；对于独立集和团，所提出的定义与现有定义存在差异，但解决了后者的一些反直觉性质；同时证明了在提出的框架下可以恢复一种重要的已有独立集定义。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子图中经典图论性质的统一推广提供了新的视角，既保持了与已有工作的一致性，又针对现有定义中的不足提出了修正方案，有助于推动量子图论中相关概念的系统化。

Abstract: Many properties of classical graphs are defined in terms of subsets of the vertex set. Examples include connected components, which are subsets $X \subseteq V(G)$ such that $X \times X^c$ and $E(G)$ are disjoint, or independent sets, for which $X \times X$ and $E(G)$ are disjoint. Direct generalisations of these definitions to quantum graphs are difficult to achieve, since the natural notion of subsets of a quantum set is much too rigid. As a consequence, approaches to generalising these classical properties to the quantum setting have been eclectic. In some cases, multiple inequivalent definitions of the same notion are in use. We introduce a natural and well-motivated alternative definition of subsets of a quantum set. Building on this, we propose unified definitions of quantum graph properties as straightforward generalisations of the classical definitions. We recover this way the established notions of colourings and connected components. For independent sets and cliques, our approach suggests variations that diverge from existing definitions, but address some of their counterintuitive properties. We nevertheless show how to recover an important existing definition of independent sets in our framework.

</details>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [On the triple nature of the PSR J0435+3233 system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27462)
*Paulo C. C. Freire,Colin J. Clark,Cees G. Bassa,Guillaume Voisin,Rutger van Haasteren,Lars Nieder,Benjamin W. Stappers*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究提出毫秒脉冲星（millisecond pulsar, MSP）PSR J0435+3233是一个层级三重系统（hierarchical triple），其异常高的自转减慢率（spin-down rate）由宽轨道第三体引起的加速度变化所致。层级三重计时模型拟合了全部观测数据，并回溯检测到2008年以来的费米伽马射线脉冲。第三体被识别为一颗1.2太阳质量的F型主序星，轨道偏心率约0.6、周期约70年，该系统的持续研究有望比PSR J0337+1715提供对强等效原理（Strong Equivalence Principle）违反的更紧约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSR J0435+3233的脉冲星计时星历显示其自转减慢率远高于其他毫秒脉冲星，挑战了现有对毫秒脉冲星形成与演化的理解。作者提出该高自转减慢率可能由宽轨道第三体引起的加速度变化所致，从而为这一异常现象提供解释。

Method: 采用脉冲星计时方法，结合PSR J0435+3233的射电与伽马射线观测数据，构建层级三重计时模型以确定系统性质。通过模型拟合区分脉冲星内禀自转减慢率与外轨道参数的相关性，并通过位置匹配与色指数推断识别第三体的光学对应体。

Result: 层级三重计时模型能够描述全部计时观测，并使伽马射线脉冲回溯检测至2008年费米大面积望远镜（Fermi Large Area Telescope, LAT）数据开端。尽管内禀自转减慢率因与外轨道参数相关而不确定，但较大自转减慢率被排除，内禀率比观测值低至少两个数量级，与其他银河系毫秒脉冲星一致。在距脉冲星11毫角秒处识别出一颗恒星作为第三体的光学对应体；基于1.5-2.5千秒差距的距离和颜色，推断第三体是一颗1.2太阳质量的F型主序星，沿偏心（e≈0.6）、宽约70年的轨道绕共同质心运动，轨道倾角可能较低。

Conclusion: PSR J0435+3233被确认为层级三重系统。对外层恒星的谱学测量与持续天体测量将给出质量比和轨道倾角估计，而持续的脉冲星计时可能获得比目前PSR J0337+1715更紧的强等效原理违背约束。该系统的持续研究具有明确动机与前景。

Abstract: Context. The recent pulsar timing ephemeris of PSR J0435+3233 indicates that this millisecond pulsar (MSP) has a spin-down rate that is much higher than observed in other MSPs and challenges our understanding of the formation and evolution of MSPs. Aims. We propose that this system is a hierarchical triple, and that the high spin-down rate is caused by varying acceleration due to a tertiary in a wide orbit. Methods. We use pulsar timing methods with radio and gamma-ray observations of PSR J0435+3233 to determine the system properties. Results. We find that a hierarchical triple timing model describes the timing observations of PSR J0435+3233 and that this results in the detection of gamma-ray pulsations back to the beginning of the Fermi Large Area Telescope (LAT) data in 2008. The intrinsic spin-down rate remains uncertain as it correlates with the parameters of the outer orbit, but large spin-down rates are excluded and the intrinsic rate is at least two orders of magnitude lower than the observed rate, in line with other Galactic MSPs. We identify a star located 11 mas from the pulsar position as the optical counterpart to the tertiary companion. From the 1.5-2.5 kpc distance and colours, we infer that the tertiary is a 1.2 solar mass F-type main-sequence star. Along with the pulsar binary, it orbits the common centre of mass with an eccentric (e ~ 0.6), wide (~ 70 yr) orbit that is likely seen at a low orbital inclination. Conclusions. We conclude that PSR J0435+3233 is a hierarchical triple system. We discuss the motivation and prospects for the continued study of this system. Spectral measurements of the outer star in addition to continued astrometric measurements will yield mass ratio and inclination estimates, while continued pulsar timing may yield a tighter constraint on violations of the Strong Equivalence Principle than are currently obtained from PSR J0337+1715.

</details>


### [156] [Thermal Breaking of the I-Love Universality for Hot White Dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27873)
*Jinyi Lv,Jing-Yi Wu,Hongji Chen,Kexin Jia,Weihan Sun,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用MESA恒星演化代码构建不同温度下低质量氦核与碳氧核白矮星模型，结合Clairaut-Radau方程提取内部等密度面偏心率径向变化，定量证明较高中心温度会放大偏心率变化并导致I-Love关系偏离零温Chandrasekhar模型，而冷却后关系恢复，确认温度引起的普适关系破坏本质上源于等密度面自相似性（self-similarity）的丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密恒星的普适I-Love-Q关系在引力波天文学（gravitational-wave astronomy）中有重要应用，但热效应可能使低质量白矮星（white dwarfs）中的这一关系失效，因此需要定量探究温度对普适关系破坏的物理机制及其适用条件。

Method: 使用恒星演化代码MESA构建0.15倍太阳质量的氦核（helium-core）和0.6倍太阳质量的碳氧核（carbon-oxygen core）白矮星在不同温度下的现实模型，并利用Clairaut-Radau方程定量提取内部等密度面（isodensity surfaces）偏心率（eccentricity）的径向变化。

Result: 数值结果表明，较高中心温度会放大偏心率变化，使I-Love关系偏离零温Chandrasekhar模型，而后续冷却过程可使关系恢复；温度诱导的普适关系破坏与等密度面自相似性的丧失密切相关。

Conclusion: 温度引起的I-Love-Q普适关系破坏其根本驱动机制是等密度面失去自相似性，这为明确I-Love-Q关系在致密天体（compact objects）中的适用条件提供了关键见解。

Abstract: The universal I-Love-Q relations for compact stars have significant applications in gravitational-wave astronomy, but thermal effects can break these relations in low-mass white dwarfs. In this work, we employ the stellar evolution code MESA to construct realistic models of $0.15 \, M_{\odot}$ helium-core and $0.6 \, M_{\odot}$ carbon-oxygen core white dwarfs at various temperatures. By utilizing the Clairaut-Radau equation, we quantitatively extract the radial variation of the eccentricity of internal isodensity surfaces. Our numerical results demonstrate that higher central temperatures amplify the eccentricity variation, causing the I-Love relations to deviate from the zero-temperature Chandrasekhar model, whereas subsequent cooling restores them. This confirms that the temperature-induced violation of the universal relations is fundamentally driven by the loss of self-similarity in isodensity surfaces, providing key insights into the applicability conditions of I-Love-Q relations in compact objects.

</details>


### [157] [Search for gravitational waves associated with high-energy neutrinos detected by IceCube during the third observing run of LIGO-Virgo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28539)
*Matthias Vereecken,Matteo Pracchia,Kara Merfeld,Iara Tosta e Melo,Wasim Javed,Patrick Sutton*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究针对IceCube中微子天文台在第三次观测运行（O3）期间探测到的高能中微子，搜索与之相关的通用引力波瞬变信号。通过非建模、定向搜索方法，该研究未发现统计显著的引力波信号，并对不同发射模型下潜在引力波源的距离设定了下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型的引力波瞬变目录和实时报告可能遗漏与中微子符合的弱引力波信号，因此需要一种对较弱信号敏感的搜索方法来发现常规方法可能错过的巧合事件。

Method: 采用非建模（unmodeled）、定向（targeted）搜索方法，对Advanced LIGO、Advanced Virgo和KAGRA第三次观测运行期间的数据进行分析，搜索与IceCube探测到的高能中微子相关联的通用引力波瞬变信号。

Result: 未发现统计显著的引力波信号，针对不同的发射模型给出了可能的引力波源距离的下限约束。

Conclusion: 该搜索对弱于实时报告或引力波瞬变目录中信号的引力波源具有灵敏度，能够发现典型中微子后随搜索可能遗漏的巧合事件；尽管未发现信号，所给出的距离下限为理解中微子-引力波联合发射机制提供了约束。

Abstract: We search for generic gravitational-wave transients associated with high-energy neutrinos detected by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory during the third Observing Run (O3) of Advanced LIGO, Advanced Virgo, and KAGRA. We perform an unmodeled, targeted search, which is sensitive to gravitational-wave signals weaker than those reported in real time or in the gravitional-wave transient catalog, and can thus uncover coincidences missed by typical neutrino follow-up searches. We find no statistically significant gravitational-wave signal and set lower bounds on the distance of possible gravitational-wave sources for different emission models.

</details>


### [158] [A Four-dimensional Model-agnostic Probe into the Astrophysical Origins of Binary Black Hole Subpopulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28622)
*Anarya Ray,Vicky Kalogera*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次基于LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA合作组的引力波目录数据，采用数据驱动方法重建了双黑洞（binary black hole, BBH）在四个维度（主质量、质量比、有效进动自旋参数与有效吸气自旋参数）上的联合分布，识别出四个具有不同质量范围特征的子群，并报告了在强模型参数化和低维数据驱动框架下无法触及的新相关性，从而为BBH子群的多种天体物理起源（孤立双星演化、动力学组装、层级并合等）提供了全新的模型无关约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 累积的引力波目录强烈表明BBH群体由多个子群组成，可能源于不同的演化通道，但由于双星恒星演化、核心坍缩及宿主环境等处理中的理论不确定性，缺乏稳健的理论预测。强模型化群体分析容易导致先验主导的结论，而灵活的备选方法在高维度下往往难以扩展，从而丢失天体物理上有意义的相关性信息，亟需一种既能避免先验偏差又能处理高维结构的分析框架。

Method: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的重建方法，直接对BBH的主质量（primary mass）、质量比（mass ratio）、有效吸气自旋参数（effective inspiral spin）和有效进动自旋参数（effective precessing spin）这四个关键参数的联合四维分布进行非参数化重构。该方法避免了强模型假设带来的先验偏差，同时克服了低维灵活框架在维度扩展上的困难，能够捕捉子群间的多维相关结构。

Result: 分析识别出四个覆盖不同BBH质量范围的子群，并在这些特定质量区间内发现了新的相关性。这些相关性超出了强模型化参数表示和低维数据驱动框架所能触及的范围，为不同质量区间中孤立双星演化、动力学组装和层级并合等特定子通道的丰度提供了新的见解。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了模型无关的高维数据驱动方法在解析BBH复杂群体结构上的有效性，为天文学界理解BBH子群的天体物理起源提供了新型约束，揭示了此前难以触及的子通道丰度信息，并可能为后续更精细的群体合成模型提供观测基准。

Abstract: There is strong evidence of multiple binary black hole~(BBH) subpopulations in the cumulative gravitational wave catalog by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration that likely originate through distinct evolutionary channels. The astrophysical interpretation of this complex underlying population is subject to theoretical uncertainties in treatments of binary stellar evolution, core collapse, and host environments. Due to a lack of robust predictions and the sheer diversity of plausible features, strongly modelled population analyses often lead to prior-driven conclusions. On the other hand, flexible alternatives are often difficult to scale in higher dimensions, which can lead to a loss of critical information on astrophysically meaningful correlations. In this \textit{Letter}, we present the first data-driven reconstruction of the joint four-dimensional distribution of BBH primary masses, mass ratios, effective inspiral and effective precessing spin parameters, which yields novel model-agnostic constraints on the astrophysical origins of BBH subpopulations. We characterize four distinct subpopulations spanning different ranges of BBH masses and report new correlations in these specific mass ranges that are beyond the reach of strongly modeled parametrizations and lower-dimensional data-driven frameworks. Our results unveil novel insights into the abundances of specific subchannels of isolated binary evolution, dynamical assembly, and hierarchical mergers across various mass ranges in the astrophysical BBH population.

</details>

### [159] [A Periodically Interacting Dark Sector: Signatures and Constraints from CMB and Cosmic Expansion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26174)
*Marco Antonio Cardoso Alvarez,Micol Benetti,Leila Graef,Robert Brandenberger*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的宇宙学模型，对超哈勃尺度（super-Hubble）涨落对宇宙学背景的反作用提供有效描述，该模型在现象学上可解释为能量转移符号随时间周期变化的相互作用暗区（interacting dark sector）场景，并利用CMB、BAO和Ia型超新星数据进行约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超哈勃尺度涨落的背反作用（back-reaction）是任何宇宙学场景中预期存在的普适效应，无需引入额外成分即可自然产生。作者旨在为这一效应提供有效的宇宙学模型描述，并检验其与观测数据的一致性。

Method: 作者构造了一个现象学上等价于相互作用暗区场景的宇宙学模型，其中暗区能量转移的符号随时间周期性变化。模型参数通过宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）、重子声学振荡（BAO）和Ia型超新星数据进行联合约束。

Result: 数据允许该模型中振荡相互作用具有显著幅度，但统计上并未显示出该模型相对于ΛCDM（Λ冷暗物质）模型的显著偏好。

Conclusion: 该模型能够被现有观测数据兼容，但缺乏超越ΛCDM的统计显著性证据，因此目前仍不足以构成对标准宇宙学模型的实质性替代。

Abstract: We introduce a novel cosmological model that provides an effective description of the back-reaction of super-Hubble fluctuations on the cosmological background, a generic effect expected to arise in any cosmological scenario without requiring additional ingredients. At the phenomenological level, it can be interpreted as an interacting dark sector scenario in which the sign of the energy transfer changes periodically over time. We constrain the model using CMB, BAO, and Type Ia supernova data. We find that a significant amplitude of the oscillatory interaction is allowed by the data, although there is no statistically significant preference for this model over $Λ$CDM.

</details>


### [160] [Gravitational Waves as a Source of Large-Scale White Noise: New Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27338)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Albert Stebbins*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于随机引力波（stochastic gravitational wave）背景在宇宙流体中产生的剪切效应，通过非线性模式耦合在曲率密度场中生成大尺度白噪声（LSWN），并推导出该噪声的振幅，进而将LSWN的观测非检测转化为对引力波产生红移和密度的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在利用大尺度白噪声作为新的观测探针，约束早期宇宙中引力波的产生参数（如产生红移和密度），从而对引力波的存在时间和物理过程给出严格的宇宙学限制。

Method: 基于作者前期工作中建立的LSWN框架，通过分析随机引力波背景在宇宙流体中引起的剪切场，并考虑非线性模式耦合过程，推导引力波诱导的LSWN振幅，并将其与观测非检测结果相结合以推导约束不等式。

Result: 推导出最小约束条件：${z_*}^2\,\Omega_\mathrm{GW0}^*<5\times10^7\,(f_*/\mathrm{nHz})^{3/2}$，该约束数量级上强于其他引力波约束，例如可排除近期脉冲星计时阵列探测到的引力波在红移 $z_*\sim10^8$ 之前存在。

Conclusion: 该LSWN约束是最小约束，实际早期宇宙引力波产生模型（考虑引力波源粒度和相关声波产生的LSWN）可能将约束进一步收紧数个数量级。此方法为引力波宇宙学提供了独立于直接探测的强有力间接探针。

Abstract: A stochastic gravitational wave (GW) background sources a shear in the flow of cosmic fluid which, through non-linear mode coupling, generates large-scale white noise (LSWN) in the kurvature density field. Building on the LSWN framework of our previous work, we derive the amplitude of this GW-induced LSWN and translate the observational non-detection of LSWN into bounds on the production redshift and density of gravity waves. In particular, a minimal constraint on gravity waves with $z=0$ density parameter $Ω_\mathrm{GW0}^*$ in frequency band $f_*$ generated at redshift $z_*$ must satisfy ${z_*}^2\,Ω_\mathrm{GW0}^*<5\times10^7\,(f_*/\mathrm{nHz})^{3/2}$. This, for example, precludes the gravity waves recently detected by pulsar timing arrays \cite{NANOGrav:2023hvm} from being present before $z_*\sim10^8$, long after the quark hadron phase transition. While orders of magnitude stronger than other constraints on gravity waves, this is a minimal LSWN constraint as realistic modeling of early universe gravity wave production, including the granularity of the gravity-wave sources and the LSWN produced by the associated acoustic waves would probably tighten this constraint by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [161] [GGI Lectures on Large-Scale Structure Perturbation Theory (Effective Field Theory)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28314)
*Mikhail M. Ivanov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文是关于宇宙大尺度结构（large-scale structure）非线性微扰论（non-linear perturbation theory）的系统性讲义，面向本科高年级和研究生初学者，从头构建了理解实际和红移空间中星系成团（clustering of galaxies）所需的所有关键概念。内容涵盖有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）基础、大尺度结构EFT的构建、标准微扰论的缺陷、重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations）的非线性演化、逆项与重整化、随机效应、星系偏置EFT、红移空间畸变（redshift space distortions）以及拉格朗日微扰论（Lagrangian Perturbation Theory）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是为初学者提供一套无需宇宙学或量子场论背景即可掌握的宇宙大尺度结构非线性微扰论导论，系统解释建模星系观测成团所需的所有关键要素。讲义强调从零推导所有必要概念，降低入门门槛。

Method: 作者先以教学方式引入有效场论的基本思想，随后在牛顿宇宙学（Newtonian cosmology）框架下利用对称性原理构建大尺度结构EFT。具体步骤包括：讨论标准微扰论（Standard Perturbation Theory）的缺陷、分析重子声学振荡的非线性演化及其与等效原理（equivalence principle）的联系、处理圈图的逆项（counterterms）与重整化（renormalization）、引入随机效应（stochastic effects）、发展星系偏置（galaxy bias）与红移空间畸变的EFT，最后介绍拉格朗日微扰论。

Result: 讲义系统性地阐述了从标准微扰论到EFT框架的完整方法论，并强调了红移空间随机性（redshift-space stochasticity）对当前及未来星系巡天（galaxy surveys）的重要性。文章以拉格朗日微扰论的引入作为收尾。

Conclusion: 该讲义为初学者提供了一条从基本概念出发系统掌握宇宙大尺度结构非线性微扰论的路径，特别强调了大尺度结构EFT在建模星系实际空间与红移空间成团中的核心作用。

Abstract: These notes are an introduction to non-linear perturbation theory for cosmological large-scale structure. They are aimed at undergraduate and beginning graduate students and do not require any cosmology or quantum field theory background. All necessary concepts are developed from scratch. The lectures are intended to explain all key ingredients needed to model the observed clustering of galaxies in real and redshift spaces. After a brief pedagogical introduction to the ideas of effective field theory (EFT), we develop large-scale structure EFT in the context of Newtonian cosmology using symmetry principles. We discuss in detail the shortcomings of Standard Perturbation Theory, the non-linear evolution of baryon acoustic oscillations and its relation to the equivalence principle, counterterms and renormalization of the loop diagrams, and stochastic effects. Then we develop EFT for galaxy bias and redshift space distortions. We also highlight some important facts about redshift-space stochasticity, relevant for ongoing and future galaxy surveys. Finally, we introduce Lagrangian Perturbation Theory.

</details>


### [162] [Inflation, Open Universes, and Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28445)
*Anton Chudaykin,Mikhail M. Ivanov,Renata Kallosh,Andrei Linde,Oliver H. E. Philcox,Yusuke Yamada*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用CMB数据集（Planck、SPT、ACT）与DESI星系光谱样本（含BAO与全形状聚类测量）的联合分析，研究空间曲率Ω_k与动力学暗能量（w_0、w_a参数化）对标量谱指数n_s的影响，发现小幅负曲率或随时间演化的暗能量均可使n_s降低，从而缓解当前观测数据与Starobinsky、Higgs及最简α-attractor暴胀模型之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ΛCDM模型下观测到的标量谱指数n_s与Starobinsky、Higgs以及最简α-attractor暴胀模型的预言存在紧张关系（tension），作者试图探讨扩展宇宙学模型（引入空间曲率或动力学暗能量）能否缓解这一矛盾。

Method: 采用宇宙微波背景辐射（cosmic microwave background, CMB）数据集（Planck、SPT、ACT）与DESI光谱星系样本的联合分析，同时纳入重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations, BAO）与全形状（full-shape）聚类测量；基于参数化w_0–w_a模型描述动力学暗能量，并引入空间曲率参数Ω_k，对n_s进行综合约束。

Result: 采用Planck与DESI数据得到n_s=0.9667±0.0041，加入ACT与SPT后为n_s=0.9692±0.0035；考虑动力学暗能量后n_s=0.9716±0.0032，若再叠加小幅负曲率（Ω_k≈3×10^-3）则得n_s=0.9694±0.0035。结果显示当前观测数据与Starobinsky、Higgs及最简α-attractor模型的张力仅存在于ΛCDM框架内，在扩展模型中可得到缓解。

Conclusion: 空间曲率与动力学暗能量均可显著降低n_s，使观测值更接近多种暴胀模型预言；这些发现对开放宇宙（open universe）或含动力学暗能量的暴胀场景（如量子隧穿与非标准拓扑）具有重要含义，并探讨了可使n_s任意增大的特殊α-attractor模型及α-attractor精髓（quintessence）模型，对未来DESI、DESI-II、SPHEREx、Euclid、Rubin及Roman等数据分析具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We study the impact of spatial curvature ($Ω_k$) and dynamical dark energy (parametrized by $w_0$ and $w_a$) on the spectral index $n_s$ using a combination of cosmic microwave background datasets (Planck, SPT, and ACT), and spectroscopic galaxy samples from DESI, including both BAO and full-shape clustering measurements. We show that a small negative curvature, $Ω_k\simeq 3\times 10^{-3}$, lowers the value of $n_s$, bringing it closer to predictions of the Starobinsky, Higgs, and simplest $α$-attractor inflationary models. In particular, we find $n_s= 0.9667\pm0.0041$ (using Planck and DESI data) or $n_s= 0.9692\pm0.0035$ (adding ACT and SPT). Allowing for time-evolving dark energy also reduces the spectral index, leading to $n_s=0.9716\pm0.0032$ (from the combined dataset), or $n_s=0.9694\pm0.0035$ in combination with a small negative curvature. Our results demonstrate that the tension between current observational data and the Starobinsky, Higgs, and simplest $α$-attractor models holds only for $Λ$CDM, and can be mitigated in extended cosmological models. We discuss implications of these findings for inflationary models in an open universe and/or with dynamical dark energy, including scenarios with quantum tunneling and non-standard topology. Furthermore, we briefly describe a special class of $α$-attractor models, where one can make $n_s$ arbitrarily large, and we describe the $α$-attractor quintessence model. Such models may be of particular relevance when future data from DESI, as well as DESI-II, SPHEREx, Euclid, Rubin, and Roman, becomes available.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond monomial $α$-attractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28502)
*Laura Iacconi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了超引力（supergravity）构造的α-吸引子T-模型中，超越单项式（monomial）势形式时的宇宙学后果。考虑由二次和四次项叠加的双项式（binomial）势，发现标量谱指数$n_s$呈现非普适行为，可达到0.965；同时，重加热（reheating）阶段的四次项主导会导致时间依赖的状态方程（equation of state）均量$\bar w$先为1/3后趋于0，进而对单项式大p模型的适用性提出了质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期小尺度宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）数据与DESI数据联合分析显示更偏好较大的标量谱指数$n_s$，而单项式α-吸引子T模型需要双曲正切函数的幂次$p\geq 6$才能与新观测相容。然而这一构造依赖"较低幂次在整个暴胀和重加热场范围内可忽略"的假设，本文旨在探索超越单项式形式后模型的物理后果。

Method: 作者考虑一个由二次项和四次项叠加构成的双项式势，设置四次项系数满足$0 < c+1/2 \ll 1$。通过求解重加热前几个e-folds背景动力学，分析膨胀子振荡过程中的势主导项转变及相应的状态方程变化。

Result: 研究发现双项式势下的标量谱指数$n_s$显示非普适行为，可高达0.965，其α依赖性与单项式势有本质差异。重加热阶段中，四次项可能在振荡初期主导，之后二次项接管，导致状态方程均量$\bar w$从初期的约1/3最终趋于0。要实现约4个e-folds的四次项主导重加热阶段，需要四次与二次项系数间存在巨大层级（$c\sim 10^5$，当$\alpha\gtrsim 0.1$时）。

Conclusion: 超越单项式形式的模型可导致$n_s$相对单项式势预测的非平凡偏差；同时，重加热阶段$\bar w$的结果质疑了大p单项式模型的可用性，因为假定$\bar w$由第p次幂唯一确定依赖于对超引力势的显著精细调谐（fine-tuning）。

Abstract: Recent small-scale CMB data show a preference for larger scalar spectral index, $n_s$, when combined with DESI data. Monomial $α$-attractor T-models can be reconciled with the new observations if the power of the hyperbolic tangent function is $p\geq 6$, where $p$ is even. The supergravity construction of monomial T-models with $p>2$ relies on the assumption that lower powers remain negligible over the whole field range explored during inflation and reheating. What would be the consequences of going beyond the monomial formulation? As a first step in this direction, we consider the case of a binomial potential, given by the sum of quadratic and quartic terms. When the quartic coefficient, $c$, is $0< c+1/2 \ll 1$, we find that the new model displays non-universal behavior for $n_s$, leading to values as large as $0.965$ and with an $α$-dependence that is qualitatively different from that of monomial potentials. By solving the background dynamics during the first few e-folds of perturbative reheating we show that the quartic term might dominate before the quadratic one eventually takes over as the inflaton oscillations decrease in amplitude. This leads to a time-dependent equation of state, with $\bar w\sim 1/3$ initially before ultimately $\bar w\to 0$. Obtaining a quartic-dominated reheating stage lasting $\sim4$ e-folds requires a substantial hierarchy between the quartic and quadratic terms, $c\sim 10^5$ for $α\gtrsim 0.1$. Our study highlights that models beyond the monomial form can lead to non-trivial deviations of $n_s$ from the predictions of monomial potentials. Furthermore our results for $\bar w$ during reheating call into question the use of monomial models with large $p$; assuming that $\bar w$ is uniquely determined by the $p$-th power relies on substantial fine-tuning of the underlying supergravity potential.

</details>


### [164] [Domain walls through different cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28214)
*I. Dankovsky,D. Gorbunov,S. Ramazanov,A. Vikman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过晶格模拟研究了不同早期宇宙学（从尘埃到刚体物质主导直至闵可夫斯基空间极限）下双阱势模型中畴壁（domain wall, DW）网络的演化性质，发现其演化对宇宙学状态方程（EoS）近似具有普适性：大体积内的壁面积主要由粒子视界（particle horizon）决定，而非哈勃率倒数。封闭畴壁的形成和湮灭（biased）畴壁的引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）谱特性会打破这种普适性，但在峰值附近区域对EoS的依赖性较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁作为宇宙学拓扑缺陷之一，其网络演化对早期宇宙的状态方程（equation of state, EoS）是否敏感尚不清楚，而已有研究中常假设不同宇宙膨胀历史来讨论其动力学，因此需要系统性检验DW网络演化在不同宇宙学背景下的普适性，以便为引力波探测（如脉冲星计时阵列）提供可靠的理论预测。

Method: 采用格点模拟（lattice simulations）方法，在双阱势模型中生成并演化畴壁网络，比较从尘埃（dust）到刚体物质（stiff matter）主导直至有效闵可夫斯基空间（Minkowski space）极限等不同EoS条件下的网络几何特性与引力波谱。

Result: 发现DW网络面积主要由粒子视界控制，展示了近似普适性；然而封闭DW的形成对EoS高度敏感，破坏普适性但整体贡献可忽略。湮灭DW发射的GW谱存在两种普适性破坏：红外（IR）斜率依赖EoS参数，紫外（UV）部分出现随EoS变硬的更显著平台；峰值附近区域对EoS依赖较弱。此外，针对多种原始宇宙学提供了峰值附近谱的拟合公式（fitting formulae）。

Conclusion: 粒子视界而非逆哈勃率才是DW网络相关长度及其现象学（如GW特征波长）的关键控制量；虽然EoS在部分特征上打破普适性，但对最相关的GW峰值区域影响较弱，因此由脉冲星计时阵列等途径搜索随机引力波背景时对早期宇宙EoS的敏感性有限。

Abstract: We study properties of domain walls (DWs) arising in the model with a double well potential assuming different early universe cosmologies: from dust through stiff matter domination to the limit of effective Minkowski space. Using lattice simulations we demonstrate that evolution of DW networks exhibits an approximate universality with respect to the cosmological equation of state (EoS). Namely, the wall area inside a given large volume is mainly determined by the particle horizon, while details of cosmic expansion play a subdominant role. As it follows, particle horizon rather than the inverse Hubble rate is pivotal in DW evolution defining the network correlation length and hence its phenomenology, e.g., the characteristic wavelength of emitted gravitational waves (GWs). Formation of closed DWs is shown to be very sensitive to the cosmological EoS and hence violate the universality, but their contribution to the total network is too small to change the overall picture. The universality breaking is also observed in the spectral properties of GWs from annihilating (biased) DWs: i) the IR slope of the spectrum depends on the EoS parameter; ii) there is a plateau in the UV part of the spectrum, which gets more pronounced as one stiffens the EoS. However, the dependence on the EoS is rather weak in the near peak region, which is most relevant from the viewpoint of pulsar timing arrays and other searches for the stochastic GW background. For a selection of primordial cosmologies, we provide fitting formulae for the spectra near the peak.

</details>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Nanoparticle Networks for Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27844)
*Jonas Mensing,Wilfred G. van der Wiel,Andreas Heuer*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SiO2/Si衬底上金属纳米颗粒与分子结互连的神经形态架构，通过静态控制电极将纳米颗粒网络从被动储层转变为可调非线性动力系统，并确立了三条核心设计规则以最大化计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理计算（physical computing）利用复杂动力系统实现节能数据处理，但如何有效调控纳米颗粒网络的动力学行为并最大化其计算性能仍缺乏系统性设计准则。

Method: 研究者构建了由金属纳米颗粒通过分子结在SiO2/Si衬底上互连的神经形态架构，利用周围静态控制电极引导一维电压输入进入多维信号响应，并通过分析电极对信号的路由作用提炼设计规则。

Result: 确立了三条核心设计规则：(1) 在系统截止频率附近工作可在非线性电荷隧穿与线性电容记忆间取得最优平衡；(2) 调节SiO2厚度可设定静电屏蔽长度（electrostatic screening length）并决定记忆类型——厚氧化物层缩短屏蔽长度，使网络进入持久、类非易失（non-volatile-like）状态，而小于屏蔽长度的网络仅表现衰减记忆（fading memory）；(3) 通过异质分子结引入结构无序可突破表达能力的天花板，使控制电压独立操控特定信号振幅与相位。

Conclusion: 网络的计算表达性虽随物理尺寸扩展，但最终受屏蔽长度限制；通过局部无序打破内部空间对称性可绕过此饱和，从而在动态神经形态应用中普遍最大化计算性能。

Abstract: Physical computing leverages complex dynamical systems for energy-efficient data processing. In this work, we present a neuromorphic architecture based on metallic nanoparticles interconnected by molecular junctions on a $\text{SiO}_2$/Si substrate. We demonstrate that surrounding static control electrodes transform this nanoparticle network from a passive reservoir into a tunable nonlinear dynamical system. By analyzing how these electrodes route simple one-dimensional voltage inputs into multidimensional signal responses, we establish three core design rules to maximize computational performance. First, operating near the system's cutoff frequency achieves an optimal balance between nonlinear charge tunneling and linear capacitive memory. Second, tuning the underlying $\text{SiO}_2$ thickness sets the electrostatic screening length and dictates the memory type. Thick oxide layers reduce the screening length, causing networks larger than this length to transition into a persistent, non-volatile-like regime. Conversely, networks smaller than the screening length exhibit only fading memory. Third, introducing structural disorder via heterogeneous molecular junctions overcomes inherent limits on expressivity. While a network's computational expressivity scales with its physical size, it is ultimately capped by the screening length. Breaking internal spatial symmetries with localized disorder bypasses this saturation, allowing control voltages to independently manipulate specific signal amplitudes and phases, universally maximizing performance for dynamic neuromorphic applications.

</details>
