### [166] [Brewster-anomaly delocalization for free-electron radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27894)
*Zheng Gong,Xiangfeng Xi,Hongsheng Chen,Owen D. Miller,Stefan Rotter,Xiao Lin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过布儒斯特异常退局域化与自由电子辐射的非常规相互作用，在强无序多层介质中实现了无局域化、高强度和定向的自由电子辐射，且该辐射对介质构型、光频率和电子速度均具有不变性，为粒子探测器和消色差光源开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域化效应（localization effects）在无序电子学与光子学中至关重要，其会限制自由电子与光在随机介质中的高效耦合。然而，局域化效应在自由电子-光相互作用中的复杂角色此前几乎未被探索，本文旨在克服这一关键限制。

Method: 本文利用布儒斯特异常退局域化（Brewster-anomaly delocalization）与自由电子辐射之间的非常规相互作用，在无需工程化设计的强无序多层介质中实现自由电子辐射，使布儒斯特效应阻止多次散射干涉并抵消局域化。

Result: 在强无序、未工程化设计的多层介质中，自由电子辐射可保持无局域化、高强度和定向性；该退局域化介导的辐射对随机介质构型、光频率及电子速度均表现出显著不变性。

Conclusion: 该发现为在易于制备的复杂介质中、在此前无法达到的频率下工作的粒子探测器（particle detectors）和消色差光源（achromatic light sources）提供了新的机遇。

Abstract: Localization effects are central to disordered electronics and photonics. In electronics, Anderson localization governs electron confinement in randomly perturbed lattices. Similarly, its photonic counterpart inhibits light transport via disorder -- but with a unique exception: Brewster-anomaly delocalization, where the Brewster effect prevents multiple-scattering interference and counteracts the localization. Despite extensive research in electronics and photonics separately, the intricate role of localization effects in free-electron--light interactions -- vital for lasers, accelerators, microscopy and spectroscopy, and quantum information -- remains largely unexplored. At the same time, localization effects are widely regarded as a key factor limiting the efficient coupling between free electrons and light in random media. Here we overcome this key limitation via the unconventional interplay between Brewster-anomaly delocalization and free-electron radiation. In this way, free-electron radiation can be localization-free, intense and directional even in strongly disordered, unengineered multilayers. Essentially, this delocalization-mediated free-electron radiation is remarkably invariant not only to the random-medium configuration, but also to the light frequency and the electron velocity. Our findings unlock new opportunities for particle detectors and achromatic light sources operating in easy-to-fabricate complex media at previously inaccessible frequencies.

</details>


### [167] [Spin-canting-induced Giant Nonlinear Optical Magnetochirality in a 2D Ferrotoroid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28067)
*Shian Xia,Sheng Liu,Wenhe Jia,Fanglu Qin,Xuanji Wang,Haixiang Luo,Yue Sun,Vanessa Li Zhang,Wenduo Chen,Jiazheng Qin,Cheng-Wei Qiu,Ting Yu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了在中心对称二维铁环畴（ferrotoroid）双层CrSBr中实现巨非线性光学磁手性（magnetochirality）的实验突破。通过磁场诱导的自旋倾斜态破坏对偶时间（PT）对称性，激活自旋手性驱动的i型非线性磁化率，其与本征c型SHG磁化率的相干干涉产生了旋性可磁场切换的圆偏振二次谐波信号，并展示了磁光存储与逻辑运算功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现磁场可切换的手性光发射是二维光自旋电子学（opto-spintronics）的重要目标，但传统策略在动态可调性与偏振对比度之间存在根本性权衡。非线性光学中的手性二次谐波产生（SHG）机制虽能绕过这一限制，却因严格的对称性要求而难以实验实现，亟需在实验上探索有效的实现路径。

Method: 采用中心对称的二维铁环畴双层CrSBr作为研究对象，利用磁场诱导的自旋倾斜态打破未扰动反铁磁（AFM）基态的PT对称性，激活自旋手性驱动的i型非线性磁化率；该i型磁化率与本征c型SHG磁化率发生相干干涉，产生宏观可切换圆偏振SHG信号。

Result: 实验成功观测到巨非线性光学磁手性效应，其SHG信号旋性可通过磁场切换。基于该灵敏机制，进一步发现了磁场饱和后常规线性探测无法察觉的剩余磁态，并利用这些非易失性态演示了磁光存储和逻辑运算操作。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种基于对称性驱动的一般性策略，用于调控非线性磁手性，同时为探测二维极限下细微自旋纹理提供了灵敏的光学探针，拓展了非线性光学在磁光信息处理中的应用前景。

Abstract: Achieving magnetically switchable chiral light emission is an important goal for 2D opto-spintronics. However, conventional strategies face a fundamental trade-off between dynamic tunability and polarization contrast. Nonlinear optics, particularly the emerging mechanism of chiral second-harmonic generation (SHG), offers a distinct strategy to bypass this restriction, yet its experimental realization remains elusive due to stringent symmetry requirements. Here, we report giant nonlinear optical magnetochirality in a centrosymmetric 2D ferrotoroid, bilayer (2L) CrSBr. We reveal that a field-induced spin-canting state breaks the parity-time (PT) symmetry of the unperturbed antiferromagnetic (AFM) ground state, activating a spin-chirality-driven i-type susceptibility. The coherent interference between this emergent i-type and intrinsic c-type SHG susceptibilities generates a macroscopic circularly polarized SHG signal whose helicity is magnetically switchable. Leveraging this sensitive mechanism, we uncover remanent magnetic states after field saturation that evade conventional linear probes. By exploiting the non-volatility of these states, we demonstrate magneto-optical memory and logic operations. Our work establishes a general symmetry-driven strategy for tailoring nonlinear magnetochirality, while providing a sensitive optical probe for subtle spin textures in the 2D limit.

</details>

### [168] [Information-driven stepping in dimeric transport motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27495)
*Antonio Patrón Castro,David A. Sivak*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论研究二聚体转运马达（dimeric transport motors）步进动力学的理论模型，其中两个马达头以位置依赖方式周期性切换迁移率，仅有一个头在任意时刻移动。模型在稳态下表征了平均速度、能量流和信息流，揭示了马达作为纯信息引擎（pure information engine）运作的机制，并通过粗粒化推导出朗之万（Langevin）型非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）动力学及四种驻留时间（dwell-time）分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二聚体转运马达是沿细胞骨架丝（cytoskeletal filaments）运动的纳米级蛋白复合物，其步进动力学背后的热力学与信息处理机制尚不清晰。作者旨在建立一个理论模型，从迁移率切换和化学自由能消耗的角度统一解释这类马达的速度、力特征以及实验中可观察的驻留时间分布。

Method: 构建了一个双头马达的随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）模型，其中两个马达头的布朗运动迁移率随时间位置周期性切换，保证同一时刻仅有一个头运动；通过化学自由能的耗散实现定向运动，并在稳态下分析速度、能量流与信息流；最后将该模型粗粒化（coarse-graining）为单一机械自由度，导出二阶非马尔可夫动力学。

Result: 模型表明马达作为纯信息引擎运作——各组分之间仅发生信息转导而无能量交换。对于局域切换机制，模型给出的平均速度表达式具有热力学一致性，能复现实验观测行为，并捕捉紧密耦合马达的特征性失速力（stall force）特性；粗粒化后得到四种不同的驻留时间分布，可在单分子实验中直接观测。

Conclusion: 研究发现信息转导可能通过隐式的麦克斯韦妖（Maxwell demon）操作方式，构成这些分子马达出色性能的内在机制基础。

Abstract: Dimeric transport motors are nanoscale protein complexes that move along cytoskeletal filaments. Here we introduce a theoretical model for their stepping dynamics, in which the two motor heads undergo Brownian motion with mobilities periodically switching in a position-dependent manner so that only one head moves at a time. Through consumption of chemical free energy, this mechanism produces directed motion. We characterize at steady state the motor's mean velocity and its energy and information flows. The motor operates as a pure information engine, where only information is transduced between its components, without energy exchange. For localized switching, the model yields a thermodynamically consistent expression for the mean motor velocity that reproduces experimentally observed behavior, and captures the stall force characteristic of tightly coupled motors. Finally, coarse-graining the model to a single mechanical degree of freedom produces a second-order non-Markovian dynamics, from which we compute the four distinct dwell-time distributions that can be directly observed in single-molecule experiments. Our findings highlight how information transduction, via implicit operation as a Maxwell demon, may underlie the remarkable performance of these molecular motors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Harnessing Native Chromium Oxidation for Giant Orbital Torque and Field-Free Magnetization Switching in NiFe/Cr Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27306)
*Chirag Kalouni,Abhishek Kumar,Manish Kumar Mohanta,Kanupriya,Athira Ravindran K,Damanpreet Kaur,Preet Kamal,Puru Jena,Debangsu Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了铬（Cr）的天然氧化物可将NiFe/Cr双层结构转化为无需转换层的自包含双通道轨道电流源。通过Cr(3d)-O(2p)杂化增强轨道霍尔电导率近三倍，实验测得阻尼样转矩效率高达3.9×10⁶ Ω⁻¹ m⁻¹，超过Pt一个数量级、超过Ta两个数量级，并实现1.58×10¹¹ A m⁻²的无外场磁化翻转电流密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道电流（orbital current）可实现超越传统重金属自旋霍尔（spin Hall）源效率限制的电荷-自旋转换，但此前利用轨道电流需要厚轨道霍尔材料或额外的重金属转换层。本文旨在探索一种无需额外转换层的更高效方案。

Method: 利用第一性原理计算预测表面氧化对轨道霍尔电导率的增强效应；制备天然氧化的NiFe/Cr异质结构并进行实验表征；开发包含氧化门控界面源（oxidation-gated interfacial source）的漂移-扩散模型（drift-diffusion model）以定量拟合实验数据。

Result: 表面氧化使Cr的轨道霍尔电导率提升近三倍，源于Cr(3d)-O(2p)杂化。实验测得阻尼样转矩效率达3.9×10⁶ Ω⁻¹ m⁻¹，分别超过Pt和Ta一个和两个数量级。Cr-CrOₓ界面产生的轨道电流比体轨道霍尔通道强一个数量级以上，轨道输运长度约4 nm；实现无外场磁化翻转，电流密度仅为1.58×10¹¹ A m⁻²。

Conclusion: 天然氧化被确立为一种可扩展策略，用于实现高效的轨道转矩（orbital torque）器件，无需重金属转换层即可获得优异的电荷-自旋转换性能。

Abstract: Orbital currents offer charge-to-spin conversion beyond the efficiency limit of conventional heavy-metal Spin Hall sources. However, harnessing them has so far required either thick orbital-Hall materials or additional heavy-metal conversion layers. Here, we show that the native oxide of chromium, typically regarded as parasitic, transforms a simple NiFe\Cr bilayer into a self-contained dual-channel orbital-current source without the need for any conversion layer. First-principles calculations predict a nearly threefold enhancement of the orbital Hall conductivity upon surface oxygenation, driven by Cr(3d)-O(2p) hybridization. Experimentally, naturally oxidized NiFe\Cr heterostructures exhibit a giant damping-like torque efficiency of $3.9 \times 10^{6}$ $Ω^{-1}$ m$^{-1}$, exceeding Pt (Ta) by one (two) orders of magnitude. The torque depicts a non-monotonic Cr-thickness dependence which cannot be explained by a conventional model. We have developed a drift-diffusion model with an oxidation-gated interfacial source which quantitatively reproduces the data, revealing that the Cr-CrO$_x$ interface generates orbital currents over an order of magnitude stronger than the bulk orbital Hall channel with an orbital transport length of $\approx 4$ nm. The enhanced torque enables field-free magnetization switching at $1.58 \times 10^{11}$ A m$^{-2}$, outperforming heavy-metal and CuO$_x$ benchmarks. These results establish native oxidation as a scalable strategy for realizing efficient orbital-torque devices.

</details>


### [170] [Fluctuation electrodynamics of quantum capacitance in electron bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27406)
*Dmitry Zverevich,Alberto F. Morpurgo,Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了电子双层体系（electron double layers）量子电容（quantum capacitance）的理论，证明层间相关贡献在环图（ring diagrams）级别上精确对应非推迟的Lifshitz范德华（van der Waals）表达式，并将该修正识别为Casimir压缩率（Casimir compressibility）。作者在闭合形式下获得零点儿电荷涨落对电容的贡献（含普适系数），并通过Gell-Mann-Brueckner分析给出高密度极限的精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电容是探测相互作用电子体系的热力学探针，但在低维体系中，交换和关联效应对充能成本的影响与静电学同等重要。已有理论未系统处理层间关联（interlayer correlations）对量子电容的贡献，尤其是其与Casimir型涨落的联系，因此需要建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 作者在泛函积分（functional-integral）框架下推导，证明分离依赖的地态能在环图级别上精确等价于两块导电板的非推迟Lifshitz范德华能表达式，其中反射振幅由层极化率（layer polarizabilities）构造。通过Gell-Mann-Brueckner分析给出高密度极限的普适函数，并分别应用于半导体量子阱、单层与双层石墨烯器件。

Result: 层间关联对逆电容的修正等于该能量的二阶密度导数，即Casimir压缩率；零点儿电荷涨动（即双层耦合等离激元）对电容的贡献以闭合形式获得，含普适系数，其符号显示高密度下层间关联抑制充电。单层石墨烯遵循电子气（electron-gas）模板，而双层石墨烯出现反常：能带间屏蔽（interband screening）将修正压低多个数量级并在实验相关间距范围内逆转符号。

Conclusion: 作者划定了理论的适用边界：在稀薄Wigner晶体（Wigner crystal）区域以及靠近激子凝聚（exciton condensation）的电子-空穴双层体系中，电容成为探测层间配对的探针。该工作建立了量子电容与Casimir物理之间的精确对应关系，为低维电子器件的电容测量提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Capacitance is a thermodynamic probe of interacting electrons: it measures the energy cost of moving charge between conductors, and in low-dimensional systems this cost is shaped by exchange and correlation as much as by electrostatics. We develop a theory of the quantum capacitance of electron double layers, semiconductor quantum wells as well as monolayer and bilayer graphene devices, focusing on the contribution generated by interlayer correlations. Within a functional-integral formulation we show that the separation-dependent part of the ground-state energy is, at the level of ring diagrams, exactly the nonretarded Lifshitz expression for the van der Waals energy of two conducting sheets, with reflection amplitudes built from the layer polarizabilities. The interlayer correction to the inverse capacitance is the second density derivative of this energy: a Casimir compressibility. The zero-point fluctuations that generate Casimir forces between mirrors are here the coupled plasmons of the bilayer, and their contribution to the capacitance is obtained in closed form, with a universal coefficient; its sign shows that interlayer correlations oppose charging at high density. A Gell-Mann-Brueckner analysis gives the exact high-density limit in terms of universal functions. For graphene we find that monolayers follow the electron-gas template, while bilayer graphene is anomalous: interband screening suppresses the correction by orders of magnitude and reverses its sign in the experimentally relevant range of separations. We delineate the limits of the theory, identifying the dilute Wigner-crystal regime and electron-hole double layers near exciton condensation as regimes where the capacitance becomes a probe of interlayer pairing.

</details>


### [171] [Entropic signatures of the single-impurity Kondo state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27502)
*Johann Drayne,Silvia Lüscher,Will Grant,Vahid Movahed,Tim Child,Saeed Fallahi,Geoffrey C. Gardner,Michael J. Manfra,Yaakov Kleeorin,Yigal Meir,Joshua Folk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过温度依赖的电荷传感和麦克斯韦关系（Maxwell relation），在强耦合GaAs量子点中测量了Kondo单重态（Kondo singlet）形成所伴随的自旋熵抑制，首次在热力学层面直接追踪了Kondo屏蔽过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo单重态是一种由局域自旋与费米海（Fermi sea）纠缠形成的多体态，此前主要通过量子点中的输运（transport）特征进行研究，缺乏直接的热力学测量证据。

Method: 利用温度依赖的电荷传感技术，结合麦克斯韦关系，测量第一个电子加入强耦合GaAs量子点时自旋熵的抑制变化；将dN/dT对同时测量的占据数N作图，并与数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group, NRG）计算结果对比。

Result: dN/dT对N的图像呈现不对称线形，峰值移位至N>1/2——这是Kondo屏蔽的标志性特征；该线形随温度升高而减弱，NRG计算定性复现了这一行为，但实验相对理论存在小而持续的较低占据数偏移。同一器件上独立测量电导-占据数关系，在混合价区（mixed-valence crossover）与NRG在实验不确定度内一致。

Conclusion: 本研究首次通过热力学测量直接观测了Kondo单重态形成过程中的熵抑制，为Kondo物理提供了分子输运之外的新实验途径，并验证了NRG理论在该体系中的预测能力。

Abstract: The Kondo singlet---a many-body state formed by entanglement between a localized spin and the Fermi sea---has been studied extensively through its transport signatures in quantum dots. Here we report a thermodynamic measurement of the entropy suppression associated with the formation of the Kondo singlet, using temperature-dependent charge sensing and a Maxwell relation to track the suppression of spin entropy as the first electron is added to a strongly-coupled GaAs quantum dot. Plotting $dN/dT$ against the simultaneously measured occupation $N$ reveals an asymmetric lineshape with its peak shifted to $N>1/2$---a hallmark of Kondo screening---that weakens with increasing temperature and is qualitatively reproduced by numerical renormalization group (NRG) calculations, with a small but persistent offset to lower occupation relative to the theory. An independent measurement of conductance versus occupation on the same device provides a test of these quantities through the mixed-valence crossover and matches NRG within experimental uncertainty.

</details>


### [172] [Third-order nonlinear transport in a percolative two-dimensional superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27641)
*Wenjun Liu,Chenghe Wang,Xiubin Li,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Tao Zhang,Xiao-Xiao Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报告在三层1T'-MoTe2超导体中观测到显著的三阶非线性输运现象，其非线性响应源于时间依赖金兹堡-朗道理论（time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau theory）描述的超导涨落（superconducting fluctuation），表明三阶非线性输运可作为渗透型（percolative）超导体系中超导转变的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华（van der Waals）材料中因维度降低、量子涨落增强和复杂电子-声子相互作用而产生渗透型超导性，此时正常电子与库珀对（Cooper pair）共存，提供了研究非线性输运现象的独特平台。

Method: 在位于渗透转变区间的三层1T'-MoTe2超导体中测量三阶谐波纵向电压V∥3ω，系统考察其随激发电流的变化规律，并利用时间依赖金兹堡-朗道理论对实验结果进行半定量分析。

Result: 三阶谐波纵向电压在阈值以下对激发电流呈现清晰的三次方依赖关系，其幅值和非线性系数与超导态强相关；该非线性响应可由时间依赖金兹堡-朗道理论下的超导涨落半定量地解释。

Conclusion: 三阶非线性输运是渗透型超导体系中探测超导转变的灵敏手段，为在强关联体系中探索更高阶输运现象奠定了基础。

Abstract: Percolative superconductivity frequently arises in two-dimensional van der Waals materials due to reduced dimensionality, enhanced quantum fluctuations, and complex electron-phonon interactions, providing a unique platform where normal electrons coexist with Cooper pairs. We report the observation of substantial third-order nonlinear transport in a trilayer $1T^\prime$-MoTe$_2$ superconductor within its percolative transition regime. The third-harmonic longitudinal voltage ($V_{\|}^{3ω}$) exhibits a clear cubic dependence on excitation current below a threshold, with both its magnitude and nonlinear coefficient strongly correlated with the superconducting state. This nonlinear response is semiquantitatively captured by the superconducting fluctuation within the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau theory, where nonlinear transport arises due to fluctuating Cooper pairs. Our results demonstrate that third-order nonlinear transport serves as a sensitive probe of superconducting transitions in percolative systems and establish a foundation for exploring higher-order transport phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [173] [Significant ordinary Nernst effect contribution to spin-orbit torque harmonic Hall measurements in metallic structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27813)
*Igor Lyalin,C. C. Chiang,C. L. Chien*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文指出在Cr/Co双层膜中，普通能斯特效应（ordinary Nernst effect）对二次谐波电压的贡献不可忽略，会导致自旋轨道扭矩（spin-orbit torque）效率被大幅高估，并建议在使用谐波霍尔（harmonic Hall）技术表征自旋轨道扭矩时须审慎考虑该效应，部分先前研究结果可能需要重新审视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谐波霍尔测量是量化铁磁体/普通金属双层膜中自旋轨道扭矩的常用方法，传统分析通常假设普通能斯特效应对二次谐波电压的贡献可忽略不计。本文旨在检验这一假设在Cr/Co双层膜体系中的适用性，并揭示忽略该效应可能导致的定量误差。

Method: 通过对比分析Cr/Co双层膜的谐波霍尔测量数据，考察普通能斯特效应对二次谐波电压的贡献；并通过额外实验估算Cr的能斯特系数（Nernst coefficient），与其他金属的报告值进行比较。

Result: 发现Cr/Co双层膜中普通能斯特效应不可忽略，其贡献会导致自旋轨道扭矩效率被严重夸大；估算出的Cr能斯特系数与其他金属的报告值相当，表明该效应是系统性的而非反常。

Conclusion: 普通能斯特效应在使用谐波霍尔技术量化金属中自旋轨道扭矩时必须被谨慎考虑；部分先前基于该技术的研究结果可能需要被重新检验。

Abstract: The harmonic Hall measurements are commonly used to quantify the spin-orbit torque in ferromagnet/normal metal bilayers. The contribution from ordinary Nernst effect to the second harmonic voltages is usually assumed to be negligible and omitted in the data analysis. We show that in Cr/Co bilayers the ordinary Nernst effect cannot be neglected and can lead to largely exaggerated values of spin-orbit torque efficiency. Conducting additional experiments, we estimate the Nernst coefficient of Cr and find it comparable to the values reported for other metals. Consequently, the ordinary Nernst effect should be carefully considered when employing the harmonic Hall technique to quantify spin-orbit torque in metals, while the results of some previous works might need to be re-examined.

</details>


### [174] [Scalar-spin-chirality-driven fractional Chern insulator on a kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27833)
*Shinnosuke Tsutsumi,Koji Kudo,Kentaro Nomura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究证明在具有非共面磁序（noncoplanar magnetic order）的kagome磁体中可实现分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）态。研究表明，相对带色散更强的电子-电子相互作用可在更宽的标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality）范围内稳定FCI态，并通过基态简并度、热力学极限下的有限能隙及磁通插入下的谱流等特征对其进行了表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数陈绝缘体是无外磁场下即可出现的分数量子霍尔态的晶格类比，寻找可实现FCI的物理平台是凝聚态物理中的重要课题。本文探索kagome磁体中的非共面磁序所诱导的标量自旋手性能否作为实现FCI的机制。

Method: 在模型中同时纳入电子-电子相互作用与带色散（band dispersion）效应，系统研究了二者相对强度对FCI态形成的影响。通过计算与代表性态的重叠（overlap）、识别基态简并度（ground-state degeneracy）、确认热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下的有限能隙，以及追踪多次磁通量子插入下的谱流（spectral flow）等途径，对涌现的FCI态进行全面表征。

Result: 研究发现，相对于带色散更强的相互作用可在更广泛的标量自旋手性参数范围内稳定FCI态。计算结果通过基态简并度、有限能隙和磁通插入谱流等多重判据确认了FCI态的涌现。

Conclusion: 具有标量自旋手性的kagome磁体可成为实现分数陈绝缘体态的有前景的平台，为无需外磁场的分数量子霍尔效应研究提供了新的候选材料体系。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) are the lattice analogs of the fractional quantum Hall states, emerging even without an external magnetic field. In this work, we demonstrate the emergence of the FCI states in a kagome magnet with a noncoplanar magnetic order that induces a finite scalar spin chirality. By incorporating in our model both electron-electron interactions and the effect of band dispersion, we find that stronger interactions relative to the band dispersion stabilize the FCI state over a broader range of scalar spin chirality. We characterize the emergent FCI state by calculating overlap with representative states, identifying the ground-state degeneracy and the finite energy gap in the thermodynamic limit, and tracking the spectral flow under multiple flux-quantum insertions. Our results suggest that kagome magnets with scalar spin chirality can be promising platforms for realizing FCIs.

</details>


### [175] [Giant nonlinear Hall effect in a Pt/ferrimagnetic insulator bilayer under Zeeman-exchange frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27848)
*Takayuki Shiino,Matteo Fettizio,Weronika Janus,Can Onur Avci*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了在具有厚度方向组分梯度的铝取代铽铁石榴石中，Zeeman-交换阻挫（Zeeman-exchange frustration）与相邻铂层耦合产生巨大的非线性霍尔响应。在磁补偿点附近，场致自旋翻转转变伴随异常大的高阶奇次谐波电压，其中第三、五、七次谐波幅度可与一次谐波相当。研究表明焦耳热是谐波响应的参数驱动源，宏观自旋链模拟验证了电流诱导的热调制可周期性切换界面铁磁化状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性磁相互作用可以产生亚稳态或不稳定态，使磁系统对外部微扰高度敏感。作者旨在探索如何利用这种阻挫效应将弱热扰动转换为大的非线性电响应，为补偿铁磁体（compensated ferrimagnets）中的非线性磁输运开辟新途径。

Method: 实验上，作者制备了具有厚度方向组分梯度的Al取代铽铁石榴石（terbium iron garnet）薄膜，并在其相邻沉积Pt层进行输运测量。通过系统地改变外加磁场、温度和电流，测量了各阶谐波电压信号。理论上，采用宏观自旋链（macrospin-chain）模拟，研究电流诱导热调制对界面铁磁化状态切换的动力学过程。

Result: 在磁补偿附近实现场致自旋翻转转变时，观察到第三、五、七次谐波幅度达到与一次谐波相当的水平，呈现异常大的高阶奇次谐波响应。磁场、温度和电流依赖关系共同表明焦耳热（Joule heating）是该谐波响应的参数驱动机制。宏观自旋链模拟成功复现了观测到的谐波信号，证实电流诱导的热调制可周期性切换界面铁磁化在交换主导态与Zeeman主导态之间。

Conclusion: 阻挫效应能够将弱热扰动转化为大非线性电响应，为设计基于补偿铁磁体的非线性磁输运器件提供了可行路径，展示了阻挫磁系统中热-电耦合的新物理机制。

Abstract: Competing magnetic interactions can create metastable or unstable states and render magnetic systems highly susceptible to external perturbations. Here we show that Zeeman-exchange frustration in an Al-substituted terbium iron garnet with a compositional gradient across its thickness gives rise to a giant nonlinear Hall response in an adjacent Pt layer. Near magnetic compensation, a field-induced spin-flip transition is accompanied by unusually large higher-order odd harmonic voltages, with the third, fifth, and seventh harmonics reaching amplitudes comparable to that of the first harmonic. The field, temperature, and current dependences collectively identify Joule heating as the parametric drive of the harmonic response. Macrospin-chain simulations further show that current-induced thermal modulation periodically switches the interfacial Fe magnetization between exchange- and Zeeman-dominated states and reproduces the observed harmonic signals. These results demonstrate how frustration can convert a weak thermal perturbation into a large nonlinear electrical response, providing a route to nonlinear magnetotransport in compensated ferrimagnets.

</details>


### [176] [Hyperchaos in a Magnetic Nanodisk Driven by Ferromagnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27884)
*A Kolli,Hugo Merbouche,Salvatore Perna,Claudio Serpico,Damien Rontani,Grégoire de Loubens*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁模拟与时间序列分析相结合，系统地研究了垂直磁化纳米圆盘在铁磁共振非线性区间的混沌动力学行为。研究发现了从稳定周期轨道到奇怪吸引子的拓扑转变，并在实验可及的控制参数空间内证实了该系统可表现出多达三个正李雅普诺夫指数的超混沌动力学。通过模式投影技术，作者揭示了动力学复杂度与参与动力学的量子化自旋波模式数量之间的关联，论证了磁子自旋电子纳米器件可作为非常规信息处理的通用熵源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纳米尺度磁性系统中，非线性铁磁共振驱动的混沌动力学行为尚未被充分理解，特别是其与自旋波模式之间的定量关系仍不清楚。作者旨在通过精细的系统研究，验证这一看似简单的纳米系统能否产生高维混沌，并阐明复杂度产生背后的物理机制，为利用磁子自旋电子器件进行非常规信息处理提供理论基础。

Method: 作者采用大规模微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）与时间序列分析（time-series analysis）相结合的方法，在完整的控制参数空间内扫描系统行为。利用李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponents）量化动力学复杂度，并通过模式投影技术（mode projection technique）将时域动力学分解到量子化自旋波模式，建立复杂度与参与模式数目之间的定量关联。

Result: 研究发现该系统在实验可及的控制平面大范围内可表现出超混沌（hyperchaotic）动力学，最多同时存在三个正李雅普诺夫指数。动力学复杂度的产生与参与动力学的量子化自旋波模式数量直接相关，实现了从稳定周期轨道到奇怪吸引子的拓扑转变的系统映射。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了磁子-自旋电子（magnon-spintronic）纳米器件作为通用熵源（entropy source）的可行性，可用于非常规信息处理（unconventional information processing）。该工作为纳米磁性系统在混沌计算与随机资源生成等新兴领域的应用提供了物理依据。

Abstract: We investigate the chaotic dynamics driven in the nonlinear regime of ferromagnetic resonance of an out-of-plane magnetized nanodisk in detail. By combining extensive micromagnetic simulations with time-series analysis across the control parameter space, we map the topological transitions from stable periodic orbits to strange attractors and quantify the dynamical complexity. Despite the simplicity of our nanoscale system, we evidence that it can exhibit hyperchaotic dynamics with up to three positive Lyapunov exponents in vast regions of the control plane accessible to experimental studies. Using a mode projection technique, we unveil that the generated complexity is related to the number of quantized spin-wave modes participating in the dynamics. Our findings establish magnon-spintronic nanodevices as versatile entropy sources for unconventional processing of information.

</details>


### [177] [Probing the Potential Profile of Twisted Bilayer Graphene via Fabry-Pérot Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27948)
*Curtis McDowell,Alina Mreńca-Kolasińska,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Ming-Hao Liu,Thiti Taychatanapat*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用法布里-珀罗（Fabry-Pérot）干涉方法探测大角度扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）的内部电势分布，将名义上单极性区（unipolar regime）的反常电阻振荡归因于器件中意外局部掺杂形成的隐藏空腔，并通过零场干涉图谱和磁输运测量确定了该不均匀性的位置与尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大角度扭转双层石墨烯器件中，局部掺杂等非均匀性会形成隐藏的空腔结构，干扰对器件本征性质的理解，但这些内禀缺陷难以直接探测。作者旨在开发一种简单、非侵入性的方法，用于识别弹道器件中的局部缺陷和内部势垒。

Method: 作者使用法布里-珀罗干涉来探测器件的内部电势轮廓；通过分析零场干涉图案确定不均匀性的位置和大小；结合磁输运测量（magnetotransport）区分局部空腔模式与全局共振，依据其不同的磁场依赖关系；最后利用提取的电势轮廓进行仿真以复现实验特征。

Result: 研究成功定位并量化了器件中由意外局部掺杂形成的隐藏空腔；磁输运测量支持了该模型，能够区分局部空腔模式与全局共振；基于提取电势轮廓的仿真结果与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 干涉光谱学（interference spectroscopy）可作为一种简单、非侵入性的探针，用于识别弹道器件中的局部缺陷和内部电势势垒，为大角度扭转双层石墨烯及其他弹道器件中的内部结构诊断提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We use Fabry-Pérot interference to probe the internal potential profile of large-angle twisted bilayer graphene. We trace anomalous resistance oscillations in the nominally unipolar regime to a hidden cavity formed by unintentional local doping in our device. By analyzing zero-field interference patterns, we determine the location and size of this inhomogeneity. Magnetotransport measurements support the model, distinguishing local cavity modes from global resonances through their different magnetic dependence. Simulations using our extracted profile reproduce the experimental features. Our results highlight interference spectroscopy as a simple, non-invasive probe for identifying local defects and internal potential barriers in ballistic devices.

</details>


### [178] [An iterative method bridging DFT, disorder averaging, and experiment in intercalated materials: application to Au-intercalated graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28296)
*Poonam Kumari,Alberto Zobelli,Igor de Melo Froldi,Adeline Crepieux,Laurent Simon,Cristina Bena*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、紧束缚模型（tight-binding）、自洽T矩阵近似（self-consistent T-matrix approximation, SCTMA）和角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）实验的通用迭代方法，用以构建插层系统的有效模型，并将其应用于Au团簇插层石墨烯体系，成功重现了实验观测到的范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity）展宽和色散中的扭结（kink）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 插层（intercalation）可强烈改变宿主材料的电子色散结构，但现有方法难以系统构建插层系统的有效模型；作者旨在开发一种将第一性原理计算、模型模拟与实验测量相结合的通用迭代方案，以识别插层系统中关键的微观自由度。

Method: 方法包含三个核心步骤：利用DFT识别相关微观自由度并约束选定模型参数；基于tight-binding框架构建模型；通过SCTMA处理无序平均，并将计算结果与ARPES实验对比以进一步精化模型参数。该迭代流程被应用于Au团簇插层石墨烯体系。

Result: 该方法成功重现了Au团簇相的主要ARPES特征，包括范霍夫奇点的展宽以及色散中扭结状特征的出现。分析表明，关键的微观成分为插层物特定轨道与石墨烯态之间的杂化（hybridization），以及插层诱导的局域散射势（local scattering potential）。

Conclusion: 该迭代方法为构建插层系统的有效模型提供了通用框架，能够准确捕捉实验可观测特征并揭示其背后的关键微观机制，在异质插层材料的电子结构研究方面具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Intercalation can strongly modify the electronic dispersion of a host material, as directly revealed by angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). We develop a general iterative method combining density functional theory (DFT), tight-binding (TB), disorder averaging within the self-consistent T-matrix approximation (SCTMA), and experiment, to construct an effective model of the intercalated system. DFT identifies the relevant microscopic degrees of freedom and constrains selected model parameters, while comparison of SCTMA calculations with experiment guides their further refinement. We apply this method to graphene intercalated with Au clusters and show that it reproduces the main ARPES signatures of the Au-cluster phase, including the broadening of the V12an Hove singularity and the emergence of kink-like features in the dispersion. The essential microscopic ingredients identified by the analysis are the hybridization between selected intercalant orbitals and the graphene states, together with an intercalation-induced local scattering potential.

</details>


### [179] [Effects of gold cluster intercalation in graphene: stationary waves and modified QPI features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28297)
*Cristina Bena,Marion Cranney,Poonam Kumari,Alberto Zobelli,Laurent Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究解释了外延石墨烯（epitaxial graphene）在碳化硅（SiC）衬底上因金（Au）插层（intercalation）而形成的团簇相中观测到的异常驻波和准粒子干涉（quasiparticle-interference, QPI）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观察发现，Au插层在SiC上外延石墨烯下方产生团簇相，其驻波和QPI特征集中在石墨烯M点附近，现有理论无法解释这一异常现象，需要建立新的散射机制模型。

Method: 提出Au插层位于石墨烯空心位（hollow site）下方，在六个周围碳原子上诱导局部散射势；采用T矩阵（T-matrix）方法处理这一环状散射体，并结合局部态密度（local-density-of-states, LDOS）计算对比团簇上与团簇外的态密度差异。

Result: 环状散射体在倒空间产生中心位于M点附近的椭圆QPI结构，与实验傅里叶变换扫描隧道谱（FT-STS）测量一致；QPI特征自然产生实空间观测到的近似驻波图案；LDOS对比的符号和幅度强烈依赖能量，与实验观测吻合。

Conclusion: Au插层在石墨烯空心位下方形成的环状散射势是解释实验观测异常QPI和驻波特征的关键物理机制，理论计算与实验数据在QPI结构、驻波图案和LDOS能量依赖三方面均取得一致性。

Abstract: Gold intercalation beneath epitaxial graphene on SiC produces a cluster phase with unusual standing waves and quasiparticle-interference (QPI) features concentrated near the graphene M points. We show that this can be explained by Au intercalation below graphene hollow sites, which induces a local scattering potential on the six surrounding carbon atoms. Within a T-matrix treatment, this ring-like scatterer produces elliptical QPI structures centered near M, in agreement with the experimental FT-STS measurements. We further show that these QPI features naturally generate the nearly stationary standing-wave patterns observed in real space. Finally, we compute the local-density-of-states contrast on and off a small cluster and show that its sign and magnitude are strongly energy dependent, consistent with the experimental observations.

</details>


### [180] [Current-based RF charge sensing in a carbon nanotube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28313)
*Marta Cagetti,Stefan Forstner,Victor Champain,Roger Tormo-Queralt,Christoffer B. Møller,Sergio L. De Bonis,Chandan Samanta,Elsa Vázquez-Rodriguez,Eneko Mateos-Madinabeitia,David A. Czaplewski,Adrian Bachtold*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于悬浮碳纳米管的电流模式电荷传感器（current-mode charge sensor），在1.25 MHz的RLC谐振回路（RLC tank circuit）共振频率下工作，电荷灵敏度达0.15 μe/√Hz，并用于对同一纳米管中静电定义的双量子点（double quantum dot, DQD）进行测量，实现了单次读取，信噪比达17，超过现有技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超灵敏电荷检测是量子电子学中的重要工具，广泛应用于量子信息处理和凝聚态物理探测。然而现有方案要么需要阻抗匹配的谐振电路，要么要求放大器与样品之间达到毫米级距离，两者都增加了系统复杂性和器件设计约束，亟需更简洁的电荷检测方案。

Method: 作者引入了一种电流模式电荷传感器，集成在悬浮碳纳米管（suspended carbon nanotube）中，工作在RLC谐振回路的1.25 MHz共振频率下。利用该传感器对同一纳米管中静电定义的双量子点进行电荷态测量，并执行单次测量（single-shot readout）实验。

Result: 该传感器实现了0.15 μe/√Hz的电荷灵敏度；对双量子点进行测量后获得了高度规则的电荷稳定性图（charge stability diagram）；在3.56 μs的积分时间内完成单次电荷态读取，10^7次测量中无错误判定，信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）达17，超过了当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的电流模式碳纳米管电荷传感器在灵敏度、读取速度和可靠性上均达到或超越现有技术，同时简化了实验装置结构，为量子电子学中高精度电荷探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Ultra-sensitive charge detection is a widely used tool for quantum electronics with applications in quantum information processing and in probing the physics of condensed matter systems. Existing approaches require either an impedance-matched resonant circuit, or millimeter-scale proximity between amplifier and sample, both adding complexity and constraining device design. In this work, we introduce a current-mode charge sensor in a suspended carbon nanotube, operating at the $1.25$ MHz resonance of an RLC tank circuit and achieving a charge sensitivity of $0.15~μe/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. We utilize it to measure a double quantum dot (DQD) electrostatically defined in the same nanotube, revealing a highly regular charge stability diagram. We perform single-shot readout of the DQD charge state at an integration time of $3.56~μ\mathrm{s}$, without any false assignments over $10^{7}$ measurements and a signal-to-noise ratio of 17 exceeding the state of the art.

</details>


### [181] [Anomalous metal and superconducting phases in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28425)
*Anna Okounkova,Abigail Sohm,Tobias Faehndrich,Manish Kumar,Derek Waleffe,Jiaqiang Yan,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Joshua Folk,Matthew Yankowitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了菱面体石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）在WSe₂衬底上的超导行为，发现栅极空间中同时存在零电阻超导区域和有限饱和电阻区域，后者重现了薄膜超导体中"反常金属"（anomalous metal）态的关键现象学特征，为理解这一缺乏理论解释的量子态提供了超高洁净、可调控的新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在石墨烯基体系中，许多栅极调谐的超导相中电阻不随温度降低而消失，而是饱和于有限值，这一反常行为缺乏公认的理论解释，且以往观测平台的均一性无法排除外因干扰，因此需要更洁净、可复现的体系来系统研究该现象。

Method: 作者在WSe₂衬底上的菱面体石墨烯中系统扫描栅极空间，通过改变温度、垂直磁场、面内磁场和电流等参数，系统表征零电阻超导与有限饱和电阻区域的行为及其转变边界。

Result: 在零磁场下，栅极空间中出现孤立的有限饱和电阻"口袋"与零电阻超导区域共存，两者表现出高度相似的现象学特征；小面内磁场会扩展并合并这些口袋而不改变其本质行为，在毫开尔文基温下产生零电阻与有限电阻状态之间的锐利边界。

Conclusion: 该有限电阻态重现了薄膜超导中被观测数十年但缺乏公认理论解释的反常金属态的关键现象学特征，超高洁净菱面体石墨烯的可调谐性和可复现性有力排除了外因解释，为理解这一量子态提供了新平台。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superconductivity is now well established in graphene-based systems, with many such realizations showing evidence for unconventional pairing. Yet in several of the gate-tuned phases that otherwise exhibit clear signatures of superconductivity, the resistance does not vanish as temperature is lowered, instead saturating at a finite value. Here we report a systematic study of this behavior in rhombohedral graphene on a WSe$_2$ substrate, finding regions of gate space with zero-resistance superconductivity alongside others with finite saturation resistance. At zero magnetic field, these regions appear as isolated pockets in gate space that otherwise exhibit strikingly similar phenomenology, including abrupt transitions to the normal state as temperature, perpendicular magnetic field, and current are raised above critical values. A small in-plane field expands and merges these pockets without qualitatively altering their behavior, producing a sharp boundary at millikelvin base temperature between states of zero or finite resistance. The finite-resistance state reproduces key phenomenology associated with the anomalous metal, a state that has been observed in thin-film superconductors for decades but lacks an accepted theoretical explanation. The tunability and reproducibility of ultra-clean rhombohedral graphene place strong constraints on extrinsic explanations and provide a new platform for understanding this behavior.

</details>


### [182] [Observation of long-lived spin order in nanoconfined water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28480)
*Rohma Khan,Kang Xu,Nathaniel Jeffries,Ankit Bhardwaj,Boya Radha,Daniela Pagliero,Carlos A. Meriles*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用氮空位（NV）中心核磁共振（NMR）光谱技术，对5.6纳米通道内受限水的动力学行为进行了温度依赖性探测。实验发现系统在整个温度范围内保持液态且扩散能力被强烈抑制，并观测到质子共振偶尔转变为分裂达数十千赫兹的双峰结构，该现象被初步归因于由光激发溶剂化电子引发的长寿命顺磁电荷复合物所介导的超精细相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度受限液体的行为与体相液体显著不同，但在受控条件下研究其动力学在实验上极具挑战性，因此需要开发能够在纳米几何结构中直接探测液体性质的方法。

Method: 采用氮空位（NV）中心核磁共振（NMR）光谱技术，对5.6纳米通道中受限的水进行随温度变化的测量；利用系统受限导致的强烈扩散抑制，直接检测其1H NMR谱。

Result: 受限水在整个研究的温度范围内保持液态且扩散能力显著被抑制；质子共振偶尔转变为具有数十千赫兹分裂的双峰，该特征被初步归因于由激光照明期间光注入的溶剂化电子所催生的长寿命顺磁电荷复合物介导的超精细相互作用。

Conclusion: 间歇性出现的双峰特征暗示受限液体中存在一个亚稳态，该亚稳态由一个延伸至整个受限液体区域的、相关的电荷水合复合物群体组成。

Abstract: Liquids confined to nanometer-scale geometries exhibit behavior that departs markedly from their bulk counterparts, yet studying their dynamics under controlled conditions remains experimentally challenging. Here, we use nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy to probe water confined in 5.6 nm channels as a function of temperature. The system remains liquid throughout the investigated temperature range and exhibits strongly suppressed diffusivity, enabling direct detection of its 1H NMR spectrum. Occasionally, the proton resonance transforms into a doublet with a splitting of several tens of kilohertz, which we tentatively attribute to hyperfine interactions mediated by long-lived paramagnetic charge complexes, in turn seeded by solvated electrons optically injected during laser illumination. The intermittent appearance of this feature suggests a metastable state comprising a correlated population of charge-hydration complexes extending throughout the confined liquid.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Wavefunction-based periodic quantum chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28554)
*Hong-Zhou Ye,Timothy C. Berkelbach*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提供了一篇关于周期性量子化学计算的教育性综述教程，覆盖从平均场到关联理论的方法，详述了周期性库仑相互作用、基函数、k点采样、密度拟合等关键技术，并给出了Hartree-Fock、微扰理论和耦合簇等方法的方程及其有限尺寸误差分析。目标是降低凝聚态体系高精度分子量子化学计算这一跨学科领域的入门门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度分子量子化学为凝聚态体系提供了有前景的计算工具箱，但该领域因融合分子量子化学、固态物理和数值方法等多学科内容而入门困难。作者希望编写一篇系统性的教学教程，帮助研究者顺利过渡到周期性体系的高精度计算。

Method: 论文以教学教程形式系统阐述周期性量子化学计算：首先详细讨论周期性库仑相互作用的微妙之处，重点聚焦于Ewald求和方法；随后描述平面波（plane waves）和周期性原子轨道线性组合（periodic linear combinations of atomic orbitals）两种最常用的周期性单电子基函数，并给出所有哈密顿量积分公式；接着说明k点采样（k-point sampling）作为超胞基函数的对称性适配及计算成本节省方法，以及密度拟合（density fitting）等近似技术；最后呈现若干示例周期性量子化学方法（包括Hartree-Fock理论、微扰理论和耦合簇理论）的工作方程，并讨论其有限尺寸误差及向热力学极限的收敛行为，同时简要提及局域关联和量子嵌入（quantum embedding）理论。

Result: 该教程提供了周期性量子化学从理论基础到实际计算方法的完整框架，给出了所有必要的哈密顿量积分公式和各方法的显式工作方程，并阐明了有限尺寸误差的来源及其向热力学极限的收敛行为。同时指出局域关联和量子嵌入理论因其适配晶格平移对称性而尤其适用于周期性体系。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的教学叙述，本文降低了高精度分子量子化学方法应用于凝聚态体系的入门难度，为相关领域的研究者提供了一份全面且实用的参考指南。

Abstract: High-accuracy molecular quantum chemistry offers a promising toolbox for applications to condensed-phase systems, but this field is difficult to enter due to its combination of topics from molecular quantum chemistry, solid-state physics, and numerical methods. Aiming to ease this transition, we present a comprehensive, pedagogical tutorial on periodic quantum chemistry calculations, using both mean-field and correlated theories. The subtleties of periodic Coulomb interactions are discussed in detail, focusing on the Ewald summation approach. We describe the two most popular periodic, one-electron basis functions---plane waves and periodic linear combinations of atomic orbitals---and we give formulas for all Hamiltonian integrals. Next, we explain the use of $k$-point sampling as a symmetry adaptation of supercell basis functions and the associated savings in computational costs as well as the use of density fitting and related approximations to further reduce costs. We present the working equations of a few example periodic quantum chemistry methods, including Hartree-Fock theory, perturbation theory, and coupled-cluster theory, and we discuss their finite-size errors and convergence to the physically relevant thermodynamic limit. Finally, we briefly discuss local correlation and quantum embedding theories, which are especially appropriate for periodic systems due to their lattice translational symmetries.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Nonlinear stability and instability of rotating Riesz star solutions of the compressible Euler-Riesz equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27925)
*Samuel R. Charles*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了可压缩欧拉-里斯（Euler–Riesz）方程组的旋转稳态解（称为旋转里斯星，rotating Riesz stars），在质量次临界（mass-subcritical）和质量超临界（mass-supercritical）两种体制下分别证明了其存在性与非线性稳定性或不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩欧拉-里斯方程组在恒星动力学、等离子体物理和数学生物学等广泛物理现象中具有重要应用价值。理解旋转稳态解的存在性、稳定性与不稳定性，是深入研究该方程组模型物理行为的关键基础。

Method: 在次临界体制下，采用适应轴对称设定的集中紧性（concentration compactness）论证证明非线性稳定性；在超临界体制下，利用角动量分布的超齐次性假设在多方物态（polytropic setting）下证明存在性，并通过分析质量保持标度（mass-preserving scalings）上自由能的凹性来建立不稳定性。

Result: 在次临界体制下证明了旋转里斯星的存在性与非线性稳定性；在超临界体制下证明了存在性并将理论推广到超出小角速度范围，同时建立了其不稳定性。结果表明旋转的对稳定性影响取决于里斯相互作用（Riesz interaction）的奇异性，既可能起稳定作用也可能起去稳定作用。

Conclusion: 旋转对可压缩欧拉-里斯方程组稳态解的稳定效应呈双面性：旋转可以带来稳定化（stabilising）或去稳定化（destabilising）效果，具体取决于里斯相互作用的奇异性程度。

Abstract: The compressible Euler--Riesz equations arise in the modelling of a wide range of physical phenomena, including stellar dynamics, plasma physics, and mathematical biology. We study rotating steady states of the attractive compressible Euler--Riesz equations, which we call rotating Riesz stars, and establish existence and nonlinear stability or instability results in both the mass-subcritical and mass-supercritical regimes. In the mass-subcritical regime, we prove the existence of rotating Riesz stars under suitable subhomogeneity assumptions on the angular momentum profile. We then establish their nonlinear stability through a concentration compactness argument adapted to the axisymmetric setting. Rotation creates new compactness difficulties, most notably the possibility that minimising sequences are tight along rings whose radii diverge to infinity. In the mass-supercritical regime, we prove existence in the polytropic setting under suitable superhomogeneity assumptions on the angular momentum profile, thereby extending the theory beyond the small angular velocity regime. The proof requires a careful analysis of mass-preserving scalings, which are more delicate than in the non-rotating case. Finally, by analysing the concavity of the free-energy along these scalings, we establish the instability of the resulting mass-supercritical rotating Riesz stars. Our results show that rotation can have either a stabilising or a destabilising effect, depending on the singularity of the Riesz interaction.

</details>


### [185] [On Sirakov's equal-frequency uniqueness conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28279)
*Hong-Ge Chen,Yong Liu,Juncheng Wei,Wen Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在N=2,3维空间中，等频率双组分三次薛定谔系统（cubic Schrödinger system）在弱耦合范围（weak-coupling range）内恰好存在一个正解（模同时平移），该解由唯一正径向解的同步态（synchronized state）经平移构造而成，从而解决了Sirakov的等频率唯一性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sirakov曾提出等频率唯一性猜想，即在等频率条件下双组分三次薛定谔系统在弱耦合区间内只有唯一正解。本文旨在通过严格的数学证明来彻底解决这一长期悬而未决的唯一性问题。

Method: 关键难点在于排除归一化分量之比为非恒定的径向解。在归一化后，两个分量满足具有公共势函数的标量方程。作者构建了一个带权重的Pohozaev泛函（weighted Pohozaev functional）并附加修正项，证明修正后的泛函及相关权重泛函均严格为正；将这些符号性质与径向通量恒等式（radial flux identity）以及与分量之比相关的辅助商式相结合，最终迫使系统进入同步状态。

Result: 对于N∈{2,3}且0<μ₁≤μ₂、0<β<μ₁的参数范围，证明了系统在H¹(R^N)×H¹(R^N)中模同时平移意义下恰有一个正解。该唯一解是同步态，由R^N中方程-Δw+w=w³的唯一正径向解构造得出。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了Sirakov在弱耦合范围内的等频率唯一性猜想，确认了在等频率条件下此类系统解的结构具有唯一性，并给出了证明中关键的分析工具——加权Pohozaev泛函与修正项的符号性质。

Abstract: Let $N\in\{2,3\}$, $0<μ_1\leqμ_2$, and $0<β<μ_1$. We prove that the equal-frequency two-component cubic Schrödinger system \[
  -Δu+u=μ_1u^3+βuv^2,
  \qquad
  -Δv+v=μ_2v^3+βu^2v
  \quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^N \] has exactly one positive solution in $H^1(\mathbb{R}^N)\times H^1(\mathbb{R}^N)$ modulo simultaneous translations. More precisely, every positive solution is a simultaneous translate of the synchronized state constructed from the unique positive radial solution of $-Δw+w=w^3$ in $\mathbb{R}^N$. This settles Sirakov's equal-frequency uniqueness conjecture throughout the weak-coupling range.
  The main difficulty in the proof is to exclude radial solutions for which the ratio of the normalized components is nonconstant. After normalization, the two components satisfy scalar equations with a common potential. We construct a weighted Pohozaev functional for the system together with a correction term and prove that both the corrected functional and the associated weighted functional are strictly positive. Combining these sign properties with a radial flux identity and an auxiliary quotient associated with the component ratio forces synchronization.

</details>


### [186] [Harmonic, radial, and shell stability of the weighted Einstein constraints on the sphere at infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28599)
*Bruno Le Floch,Philippe G. LeFloch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究任意维数球面上定义的四阶与二阶偏微分算子，这些算子来源于线性化爱因斯坦约束算子及其伴随算子的加权复合。作者建立了加权庞加莱（Poincaré）、柯恩（Korn）与哈迪（Hardy）不等式，将这些不等式与谐波稳定性（harmonic stability）、径向稳定性（radial stability）和壳层稳定性（shell stability）联系起来，从而完成了任意维数中任意小孔径胶合锥（gluing cones）的引力屏蔽（gravitational shielding）问题程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在严格建立谐波、径向和壳层稳定性条件，这些条件是作者在伴生论文中控制爱因斯坦约束方程解的渐近行为的关键工具。该工作完成了A. Carlotto与R. Schoen提出的引力屏蔽程序，涵盖任意维数中任意小孔径的胶合锥情形。

Method: 作者引入加权庞加莱、柯恩和哈迪不等式作为核心分析工具，用于证明三种稳定性条件。在证明过程中，构造了所谓壳层泛函（shell functionals）的哈密顿泛函与动量泛函，并证明其满足单调性与半强制（semi-coercivity）性质。

Result: 在任意维数下，作者证明了稳定性条件对广泛一类局域化函数成立，涵盖任意小局域化域（即任意小孔径胶合锥）以及极端情形——整个球面（即无局域化）。同时，研究了相关几何常数的行为。

Conclusion: 该定理完成了任意维数下任意小孔径胶合锥的引力屏蔽程序，并指出壳层泛函的结构与其它曲率相关几何问题中出现的泛函存在潜在类比，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We consider fourth-order and second-order partial differential operators localized on domains of the sphere in arbitrary dimension. These operators arise as weighted compositions of the linearized Einstein constraint operators and their adjoints, and played a key role in our resolution of the optimal localization problem in general relativity, also referred to as the gravitational shielding problem. To control the asymptotic behavior of solutions to Einstein's constraints in our companion paper (preprint arXiv:2312.17706), we introduced the notions of harmonic, radial, and shell stability. Harmonic stability controls the borderline harmonic modes, radial stability governs the radial evolution of spherical averages, and shell stability controls the coupled radial-angular evolution of solutions. In the present paper, we establish that these stability properties follow from weighted Poincaré, Korn, and Hardy inequalities. Furthermore, in arbitrary dimension, we investigate the behavior of the associated geometric constants, and conclude that the stability conditions hold for a broad class of localization functions; the theory applies to arbitrarily small localization domains, corresponding to gluing cones with arbitrarily small aperture. At the opposite extreme, our conditions also hold on the entire sphere, corresponding to the absence of localization. This completes, for gluing cones of arbitrarily small aperture in every dimension, the program initiated by A. Carlotto and R. Schoen on gravitational shielding and the construction of solutions enjoying super-harmonic decay estimates. In our proofs, we introduce Hamiltonian and momentum functionals, which we call shell functionals, and show that they enjoy monotonicity and semi-coercivity properties; their structure also suggests possible analogies with functionals arising in other curvature-related geometric problems.

</details>

### [187] [Bose-Einstein condensation and superfluidity on a fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27470)
*Vira Shyta,Flavio S. Nogueira,Ashley M. Cook*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个致密二维空间——非交换（模糊）球面上的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）和超流性。研究发现非交换性有利于有序相的形成，从而增强BEC和超流性；在模糊球面上，理想气体和弱相互作用玻色气体的BEC临界温度均高于普通交换球面S²的情况。超流密度计算显示，在球面大半径极限下，模糊球面上的正常流体分数呈线性温度依赖（~T），而交换球面表现为二维行为（~T³），这种线性依赖与铜氧化物高温超导体中的植村定律（Uemura's law）相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根据Hohenberg定理，二维中任何正温度下都不可能发生BEC；但二维有限温度下超流性确实存在，源于Galilei不变性的破缺。作者希望探究非交换几何对BEC和超流性的影响，因为非交换性引入了额外的尺度并可能改变系统的热力学性质。

Method: 将标量玻色场提升为N×N矩阵，在非交换（模糊）球面上分析BEC和超流性。分别研究理想气体和弱相互作用玻色气体中的BEC临界温度；通过考察超流响应和涡旋集体坐标来研究超流密度；将涡旋缺陷的定义推广到本质上非交换的球面。

Result: 非交换性有利于有序相的形成，增强了BEC和超流性。在模糊球面上，理想气体和弱相互作用玻色气体的BEC临界温度均高于交换球面S²。在大球面极限下，模糊球面的正常流体分数呈线性温度依赖（~T），而交换S²表现为二维的~T³行为；这一线性依赖与非交换性直接相关。

Conclusion: 模糊球面具有与平面上定义的热力学极限不同的极限，这一差异对BEC和超流性具有实验意义。超流密度计算表明，由非交换性直接导致的线性温度依赖使人联想到铜氧化物高温超导体中的植村定律，暗示非交换几何可能在高温超导现象中具有潜在相关性。

Abstract: According to Hohenberg's theorem, Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) in two dimensions is impossible for any temperature $T>0$. By contrast, superfluidity does occur in two dimensions at finite temperatures; it emerges due to the breaking of Galilei invariance. Here we consider BEC and superfluidity on a compact two-dimensional space taking the form of a non-commutative ("fuzzy") sphere, where the scalar bosonic fields are promoted to $N\times N$ matrices. The dimension $N$ is related to the non-commutativity parameter of space and introduces an additional scale into the system. We find that non-commutativity favors ordered phases and so enhances BEC and superfluidity. We analyze BEC in ideal and weakly interacting Bose gases on a fuzzy sphere, finding in each case that the critical temperature of BEC is greater compared to that found in the case of a commutative sphere $S^2$. Then we investigate the superfluid response of weakly interacting Bose systems. To account for vortices in a superfluid, we show that, even on an ordinary sphere, the collective coordinates of vortices induce non-commutativity. With this in mind, we extend the definition of vortex defects to an inherently non-commutative sphere studied here, where the notion of a point is untenable. The non-commutativity is expected to be experimentally relevant to BEC and superfluidity since the fuzzy sphere has a thermodynamic limit distinct from the one defined over a plane, unlike the $S^2$ case. The significance of this difference is illustrated by the superfluid density calculation indicating that, in the large sphere limit, the normal fluid fraction on the fuzzy sphere yields a linear in $T$ dependence, while on a commutative $S^2$ it exhibits the usual two-dimensional $\sim T^3$ behavior. This linear dependence, arising directly from non-commutativity, is reminiscent of Uemura's law in cuprate high-$T_c$ superconductors.

</details>


### [188] [Observation of Moiré Time Crystal in Floquet-driven Rydberg Atomic Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27999)
*Shuai Shi,Dong-Yang Zhu,Yu Yang,Chu-Rong Pan,Jing-Wen Tang,Ya-Peng Zhang,Yan-Li Zhou,Wei-Tao Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Bang Liu,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了一种"莫尔时间晶体"（Moiré time crystal），通过双色驱动场结合长程里德堡相互作用与耗散，在量子多体系统中观测到类似莫尔条纹的超长拍频周期模式，并绘制了系统的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔时间晶体是一种由两个不同频率相干干涉产生的非平衡量子相，其标志性的超长拍频周期在时间域上对应空间莫尔条纹的映射，但此前从未有实验验证。作者旨在填补这一实验空白。

Method: 实验上施加具有两个不同频率的双色驱动场，利用长程里德堡（Rydberg）相互作用与耗散的协同作用，构建出梳状莫尔模式（beat-note comb），该模式叠加了次谐波周期性（subharmonic periodicity）与基频。作者系统改变其中一个驱动频率以观察光谱中莫尔模式的错位，并扫描激光失谐量以绘制系统相图。

Result: 实验成功观测到独特的梳状莫尔拍频模式，其随驱动频率变化在光谱中呈阶梯状错位排列。相图表明存在一个稳健区域，其中莫尔时间序在激光失谐扰动下仍能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该莫尔时间晶体为探索涌现的慢快动力学（slow-fast dynamics）与合成时空对称性提供了可控平台，并为在驱动量子多体系统中工程化复杂时间序开辟了新途径。

Abstract: A Moiré time crystal is a non-equilibrium quantum phase emerging from the coherent interference of two distinct frequencies, at least one being the intrinsic oscillation of a symmetry-broken time crystal. Its hallmark is an ultra-long beat period, reflecting a time-domain mapping of the Moiré fringes that arise from mismatched spatial lattices. However, to date, no experimental realization of such a Moiré time crystal has been reported. In this work, by applying a bichromatic driving field with two distinct frequencies, we demonstrate that the interplay between long-range Rydberg interactions and dissipation gives rise to a unique comb-like Moiré pattern characterized by a beat-note comb, which superimposes subharmonic periodicity and fundamental frequencies. This Moiré pattern formed by two mismatched drives is staggered in the spectrum as the frequency of one driver changes. We experimentally map the phase diagram of the system and identify a robust region where the Moiré temporal order persists against perturbations in laser detuning. The reported Moiré time crystal not only provides a controllable platform for exploring emergent slow-fast dynamics and synthetic space-time symmetries but also opens avenues for engineering complex temporal order in driven quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [189] [FFLO transition and quantum criticality in polarized Fermi gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28335)
*Francesco Pirolo,Leonardo Pisani,Pierbiagio Pieri*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过图解t-matrix方法研究二维费米气体中从极化正常态到FFLO态的零温量子相变。作者发现标准非自洽理论因严重违反Luttinger定理而产生非物理相图，因此引入最小自洽扩展以恢复定理满足性，进而在全相互作用范围内获得物理自洽的相图，并系统表征了FFLO量子临界行为，同时将二维与三维系统的临界性质统一于费米面嵌套的几何图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准非自洽t-matrix理论在描述二维费米气体FFLO转变时严重违反Luttinger定理，导致计算出的相图不具备物理意义。作者旨在克服这一缺陷，建立物理自洽的理论框架以正确描述FFLO量子临界现象。

Method: 采用图解t-matrix（diagrammatic t-matrix）方法研究零温下的FFLO量子相变；引入最小自洽扩展（minimal self-consistent extension）以恢复Luttinger定理的满足性同时保留原始公式的解析简洁性；通过准粒子衰减率、准粒子权重、动量分布以及费米子与玻色子自由度的临界动力学表征量子临界行为；并在Hertz-Millis框架下分析三维情形中顶点修正（vertex corrections）的无关性。

Result: 标准非自洽理论因严重违反Luttinger定理产生非物理相图；引入最小自洽扩展后在全相互作用范围内获得了物理一致的相图。二维与三维系统的临界性质可统一于基于多数与少数费米面嵌套的几何图像。三维情形下顶点修正为无关项，FFLO量子相变属于平均场普适类（mean-field universality class），与巡游反铁磁体（itinerant antiferromagnets）相类似。

Conclusion: 本文建立的自洽t-matrix框架为计算二维费米气体的FFLO相图提供了可靠方法，并将二维与三维系统中FFLO量子临界行为统一纳入费米面嵌套的几何视角。三维情形下FFLO量子相变被归入平均场普适类，为理解该转变的普适性质提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the zero-temperature transition from the polarized normal phase to the FFLO state in a two-dimensional Fermi gas by means of a diagrammatic t-matrix approach. We first show that the standard non-self-consistent theory produces an unphysical phase diagram because of a severe violation of the Luttinger theorem. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a minimal self- consistent extension that largely restores compliance with the Luttinger theorem while preserving the analytical simplicity of the original formalism. This leads to a physically consistent phase diagram over the whole interaction range. Building on this improved description, we characterize the quantum critical behavior of the FFLO transition through the quasiparticle decay rates, quasiparticle weights, momentum distributions, and the critical dynamics of both fermionic and bosonic degrees of freedom. We further compare the two- and three-dimensional systems, showing that their critical properties can be understood within a unified geometrical picture based on the nesting of the majority and minority Fermi surfaces. Finally, for the three-dimensional case, we demonstrate within the Hertz-Millis framework that vertex corrections are irrelevant, thereby placing the FFLO quantum phase transition in the mean-field universality class, in close analogy with itinerant antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [190] [Hubbard physics with ultracold polar molecules: on-site interaction energies for shielded molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28533)
*Carlin Stewart-Wiese,Joy Dutta,Jeremy M. Hutson*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了微波屏蔽的超冷极性分子对在同一晶格位点上的在位相互作用能$U$，发现其行为与超冷原子截然不同：对于弱晶格$U$可为负值，但随着晶格强度增加$U$会跨过零点变为正值。这一行为在非束缚对和两体束缚态中均存在，后者为探索多占据的强偶极Hubbard物理提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷极性分子与原子在不同维度上展现出丰富的相互作用物理，但分子之间强烈的各向异性偶极-偶极相互作用及其短程排斥核会导致同一位点上两个分子的运动之间存在强关联，使得在位相互作用能$U$的性质与原子情形显著不同，值得系统研究。

Method: 采用适用于微波屏蔽（microwave shielding）的二维有效势（2-dimensional effective potentials）来刻画同晶格位点上的分子对：该势在长程具有偶极-偶极（dipole-dipole）特征，但当两个分子靠近时具有非常大的排斥核心（repulsive core）；在此有效势框架下计算不同晶格强度下非束缚分子对及两体束缚态（two-body bound states）的在位相互作用能$U$。

Result: 发现与超冷原子截然不同的行为：存在一些态在弱晶格下$U$为负值，但随着晶格强度增大，$U$跨越零点变为正值；该行为在未束缚的分子对和两体束缚态中都被观察到。

Conclusion: 同一位点上的分子对由于短程排斥核心与长程偶极相互作用的共同作用，会产生可调符号的在位相互作用能$U$；两体束缚态提供了实现具有多格点占据（multiple site occupancy）的强偶极Hubbard物理（strongly dipolar Hubbard physics）的新途径。

Abstract: We explore on-site interaction energies $U$ for pairs of shielded ultracold molecules on the same lattice site. We use 2-dimensional effective potentials appropriate for microwave shielding, which have dipole-dipole character at long range but feature a very large repulsive core when the two molecules come close together. This causes very strong correlation between the motions of two molecules on the same site. We find behavior very different from that for ultracold atoms: in particular, there are states for which $U$ is negative for weak lattices but crosses zero to positive values as the lattice strength increases. This behavior is found for both unbound pairs and 2-body bound states. The latter will give access to previously unexplored types of strongly dipolar Hubbard physics with multiple site occupancy.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Relativistic transport near moving interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28569)
*Lorenzo Gavassino*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了相对论性介质中平面表面匀速运动时局域化的线性扰动，通过拉普拉斯变换和传播子表示，给出了边界层、尾迹和激波渐近尾部现象的统一几何描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同物理场景下（运动障碍物、热边界、外部源）的近表面扰动看似不同，缺乏统一的理论框架来描述边界层、尾迹和激波渐近尾部等界面局域化现象。

Method: 采用拉普拉斯变换（Laplace-transform）表述，将界面解表示为纯虚频率和纯虚波数模式的叠加；关键结果是通过$\{i\omega,ik\}$平面上的直线$i\omega=vik$来选择允许模式，随界面速度$v$变化，该直线扫描谱空间，提供统一的几何描述。

Result: 推导出界面解的传播子表示，揭示了界面局域化现象背后的几何特征；该方法适用于任意界面速度，并已应用于相对论流体力学（relativistic hydrodynamics）和动理学理论（kinetic theory）。

Conclusion: 给出了一个统一的几何框架，能够描述不同物理场景下界面局域化扰动的共同结构，为相对论介质中的边界层、尾迹和激波尾部现象提供了普适描述。

Abstract: We study linear disturbances localized near planar surfaces moving at constant velocity $v$ in relativistic media. Depending on the physical setting, the surface may represent a moving obstacle, a thermal boundary, or an external source, providing a unified description of boundary layers, wakes, and the asymptotic tails of shock waves. The central result is a propagator representation of the interface solution that yields a geometric characterization of these phenomena. Using a Laplace-transform formulation, we show that the solution is a superposition of modes with purely imaginary frequency and wavenumber. For a given interface velocity, the admissible modes are selected by the line $iω=vik$ in the $\{iω,ik\}$ plane. As $v$ varies, this line sweeps across the spectrum, providing a unified geometric description of interface-localized solutions for arbitrary interface velocities. We illustrate the formalism with applications to relativistic hydrodynamics and kinetic theory.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Spectral problems on open-book structures with singularly perturbed density: the limit operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28040)
*Yuriy Golovaty,Delfina Gòmez,Maria-Eugenia Pèrez-Martìnez*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类开放书结构（open-book structures）在装订处附近存在质量密度扰动时振动渐近分析中出现的谱问题。极限问题由耦合宏观与微观分量的非自伴块算子矩阵（non-self-adjoint block operator matrix）控制，作者描述了该算子的谱并完整刻画了其特征空间与根子空间，证明了广义特征向量构成的链长度至多为二，并给出了Jordan块存在与否及其数目的显式判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放书结构在实际中具有几何与振动分析的重要性，而当质量密度在装订处受到扰动时，经典的渐近分析会产生非平凡的极限谱结构。理解该极限算子的谱性质对于完整描述这类结构的振动行为至关重要。

Method: 作者从开放书结构振动的渐近分析出发，将极限问题归结为一个非自伴块算子矩阵的谱问题。通过将该算子矩阵的宏观与微观分量彼此耦合，系统地分析其谱点，并利用代数结构来刻画特征空间与根子空间，进而研究广义特征向量的链结构以及Jordan块的形成条件。

Result: 论文完整地描述了该非自伴块算子矩阵的谱，并完全刻画了其特征空间与根子空间。进一步证明了广义特征向量构成链（generalized eigenvector chains）的长度至多为二，并导出了一个显式判据来确定Jordan块的存在性及其个数。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个非平凡的例子：一族作用在变化Hilbert空间上的自伴算子（self-adjoint operators），其极限谱行为却由一个具有真实Jordan结构的非自伴算子来描述，展示了自伴算子族的极限谱结构可能呈现非自伴特征这一重要现象。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral problem arising in the asymptotic analysis of vibrations of open-book structures with a mass density perturbed near the binding. The limiting problem is go\-ver\-ned by a non-self-adjoint block operator matrix coupling the macroscopic and microscopic components of the model. We describe the spectrum of this operator and completely characterize its eigenspaces and root subspaces. We further prove that generalized eigenvectors form chains of length at most two and derive an explicit criterion for the existence and number of Jordan blocks. This model provides a nontrivial example of a family of self-adjoint operators acting in varying Hilbert spaces whose limiting spectral behavior is described by a non-self-adjoint operator with a genuine Jordan structure.

</details>


### [193] [New universality classes associated to fractals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28379)
*Benjamin Eichinger,Alexander Kheifets,Milivoje Lukić,Peter Yuditskii*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文发现了正交多项式零点局部行为中一类新的普适性类（universality class），其中不存在单一的极限核，而是出现极限环（limit cycle），这类新现象适用于具有奇异测度（singular measure）的模型，如三分之一康托测度（middle third Cantor measure）和实Julia集上的平衡测度，这是关于具有奇异谱的近周期算子（almost periodic operator）Christoffel-Darboux核局部行为的首个结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交多项式零点的局部行为由Christoffel-Darboux（CD）核的局部标度行为决定，此前所有研究的行为均由标度极限描述，不同的极限核对应不同的普适性类。本文旨在揭示一类新的普适性类——其中不存在单一极限核而是极限环，以适用于康托谱和测度的分形行为，填补关于具有奇异谱的近周期算子CD核局部行为研究的空白。

Method: 本文针对两个具有奇异测度的标准模型展开分析：三分之一康托测度以及实Julia集上扩张多项式（expanding polynomial）的平衡/均衡测度。同时，在二次迭代的固定点处分析标度函数（scaling function）和Christoffel函数（Christoffel function）的渐近行为，并研究该情形下的极限链（limit chain），通过Martin函数（Martin function）的渐近性质（所谓M型）对其参数化。

Result: 作者证明，在上述两个标准模型中，CD核的局部标度行为由极限环而非单一极限核描述，从而确立了新的普适性类。在二次迭代的固定点处，n次多项式零点的局部标度精确为n^{-1/α}阶，其中α为测度的局部维数（local dimension）。此外，文中证明了该极限链可由其关于Martin函数的渐近行为（M型）参数化。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出了具有奇异谱的近周期算子CD核的局部行为描述，也是此类模型在固定点处的首次分析，为理解奇异测度下正交多项式零点的普适性行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The local behavior of zeros of orthogonal polynomials is determined by the local scaling behavior of Christoffel-Darboux (CD) kernels. All previously studied behaviors are described by scaling limits, and different values of the limit kernel correspond to different universality classes. In this paper, we describe new universality classes in which instead of a single limit kernel, there is a limit cycle. These are naturally suited to Cantor spectra and to fractal behaviors of the measure. We show that these new phenomena occur for two canonical models with singular measures: the middle third Cantor measure and the balanced/equilibrium measure on a real Julia set of an expanding polynomial. In particular, this is the first result on the local behavior of CD kernels for an almost periodic operator with singular spectrum.
  As a complementary result, we describe the asymptotics of the scaling function, and the Christoffel function, at a fixed point of a quadratic iteration. This is the first such analysis for an almost periodic model with singular spectrum. It allows us to conclude that, at the fixed point, the local scaling of zeros of the polynomial of degree $n$ is precisely of order $n^{-1/α}$, where $α$ is the local dimension of the measure. We also study the limit chain in this case, and prove that it can be parametrized by its asymptotics with respect to a Martin function (so-called $M$-type); this is the first result of this kind for a chain with a singular measure.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Hubble tension problem encompassed by phase-space quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27285)
*Alex E. Bernardini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在Weyl–Wigner量子力学框架下重新审视哈勃张力（Hubble tension）问题，在极小超空间（minisuperspace）框架中通过解析推导爱因斯坦–弗里德曼方程（Einstein–Friedmann equation）的残余量子修正，证明量子效应能够抑制早期与晚期宇宙学预测之间的哈勃张力分歧，并涵盖曲率及暗区（dark sector）修正等广义量子宇宙学情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃张力是当前宇宙学中早期宇宙与晚期宇宙对哈勃常数（Hubble constant）测量不一致的核心难题，作者希望探索超越标准宇宙学框架的解析方案，尤其关注量子宇宙学（quantum cosmology）中的相空间表述能否提供解决该问题的途径。

Method: 论文采用Weyl–Wigner量子力学框架，在广义相空间场景中构建扩展公式化方案；在极小超空间框架中利用（一般的）局域化相空间量子态描述量子宇宙学，并解析推导爱因斯坦–弗里德曼方程中的残余量子修正项。

Result: 解析结果表明，残余量子修正能够有效抑制早期与晚期哈勃常数预测之间的张力分歧；该方案在标准ΛCDM宇宙学模型内解决哈勃张力问题的同时，也扩展到包含曲率和暗区修正的广义量子宇宙学情景。

Conclusion: 量子相空间框架下的Weyl–Wigner方法为哈勃张力问题提供了一种解析的、统一的解决途径，其适用性不限于标准ΛCDM模型，可推广至更广义的量子宇宙学场景。

Abstract: Analytical solutions encompassing the so-called Hubble tension problem are revisited through the framework of Weyl--Wigner quantum mechanics and discussed in the context of generalized phase-space scenarios of quantum cosmology. After reviewing the nature of the problem and its recent developments, an extended formulation constructed within the quantum phase-space framework to address the Hubble tension is proposed. For the quantum cosmology described in the minisuperspace framework through (generic) localized phase-space quantum states, when residual quantum corrections to the Einstein--Friedmann equation are analytically derived, quantum effects are shown to suppress the Hubble tension divergence between early- and late-time predictions. Besides addressing the Hubble tension problem within the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model, our approach encompasses generalized quantum cosmological scenarios that also include curvature and dark sector modifications.

</details>


### [195] [Nonlinear Dynamics near the Threshold of Gravitational Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27343)
*Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟标量场在自身引力作用下的坍缩过程，研究围绕平直时空的微扰展开（perturbative expansion）在何种条件下失效。研究发现，当过程的最大光度达到约10⁻²普朗克光度时，数值结果即偏离微扰预测，并表现出驱动频率红移和幂律谱振幅等非线性效应，后者与近似的离散自相似性（discrete self-similarity）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰理论是建模和解释引力动力学（如双黑洞并合）的基本工具，因此精确理解其有效范围至关重要。标量场自引力坍缩提供了一个干净的实验台来研究微扰展开的适用界限。

Method: 通过改变标量场初始振幅，在近似平直时空中散射的微扰区与有限时间内形成黑洞的非微扰区之间平滑过渡。作者采用数值相对论模拟，考察包含次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order）效应的微扰展开在平直时空框架下的精度。

Result: 模拟显示，当过程的最大光度L_peak达到约10⁻²普朗克光度时，数值结果在黑洞形成之前就开始偏离微扰预测。非线性效应表现为驱动频率的红移（redshift）以及幂律形式的谱振幅，且该谱振幅与近似的离散自相似性一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解微扰展开在更现实的强引力现象（如非球对称坍缩和高速双黑洞并合）中的失效极限迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Perturbation theory is an essential tool to model and interpret gravitational dynamics, for example, binary black hole mergers. Therefore it is also crucial to precisely understand its regimes of validity. The collapse of a scalar field under its own self-gravity provides a clean laboratory to study these questions. By varying the field's initial amplitude we can transition smoothly between a perturbative regime, where the field scatters in an approximately flat spacetime; and a nonperturbative regime, where a black hole forms in finite time. In this work, we use numerical relativity simulations of this set-up to investigate the accuracy of a perturbative expansion around flat spacetime, including next-to-next-to-leading order effects. Our simulations show deviations from these perturbative predictions before black hole formation, once the maximum luminosity of the process is sufficiently large, $L_{peak} \sim 10^{-2} L_{Planck}$. We characterize these nonlinear effects including a redshift of the driving frequency and a power-law spectral amplitude, which we show is consistent with approximate discrete self-similarity. These results provide a step forward towards understanding the limits of perturbative expansions in more realistic strong-gravity phenomena such as non-spherical collapse and high-velocity black hole mergers.

</details>


### [196] [Inflation in unimodular loop quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27344)
*Steffen Gielen,Rita B. Neves*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在单模圈量子宇宙学（unimodular loop quantum cosmology）框架下研究暴胀（inflation），以单模时间而非自由无质量标量场作为时间演化坐标，推导了常数势和二次势等情形下的解析及数值解，并展示了α吸引子势下的反弹模型可与观测兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的圈量子宇宙学以自由无质量标量场作为时间钟，其几何意义不够明确且依赖于具体物质内容；单模设定提供了具有清晰几何意义、与物质内容无关的自然薛定谔时间演化，因此值得在暴胀研究中系统探索。

Method: 该论文首先综述单模圈量子宇宙学并评论与完整（单模）圈量子引力的可能联系；然后聚焦半经典有效方程，在常数势等简单情形下推导解析解，强调单模时间坐标的使用；最后对二次势、Starobinsky暴胀和α吸引子势等具有现象学意义的模型进行数值求解，并比较不同的初始条件选择。

Result: 获得了常数势等简单情形下的解析解，并给出二次势和Starobinsky暴胀等模型的数值结果。特别地，选择α吸引子势可以构造由动能或势能主导的反弹（bounce）模型，这些模型与观测兼容，并可能包含量子引力区域的可观测印记。

Conclusion: 单模时间坐标提供了一种天然且几何意义明确的时间演化框架，使得暴胀子无需充当时间钟；在此框架下，α吸引子势可产生与观测兼容的反弹模型，并有望留下量子引力效应的可观测特征。

Abstract: We study inflation in the setting of unimodular loop quantum cosmology, where time evolution is defined in unimodular time rather than with respect to a free, massless scalar field as is standard in loop quantum cosmology. The unimodular setting leads to a natural Schrödinger time evolution in a time coordinate with clear geometric meaning, defined independently of any particular matter content; an inflaton can be included but is not needed as a clock. We review the unimodular version of loop quantum cosmology and comment on possible connections to full (unimodular) loop quantum gravity. Then, focusing on semiclassical effective equations, we derive analytical solutions in simple cases such as a constant potential, emphasising the use of a unimodular time coordinate. We also discuss numerical solutions for phenomenologically interesting cases such as a quadratic potential and Starobinsky inflation, comparing different possible choices of initial conditions. In particular, we show that choosing an $α$-attractor potential allows for models of a bounce either dominated by kinetic or potential energy, which are compatible with observations while potentially including observable imprints of the quantum-gravity regime.

</details>


### [197] [Gauge vs (hidden) physical symmetries of FLRW cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27351)
*Andrea Calcinari,Adrià Delhom,Federico Greco,Daniele Oriti,Néstor Rivero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究平坦FLRW几何与任意数量n个自由无质量标量场耦合的极小超空间（minisuperspace）模型中，规范对称性与（隐藏的）物理对称性之间的关系，证明共形Killing矢量生成的守恒荷是Dirac可观测量的极大共形代数，并揭示了Eisenhart-Duval提升中对称代数具有规范依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义协变理论中，坐标时间演化是规范变换，因此规范固定描述中显现的对称性不一定是物理动力学的对称性；而去参数化（deparametrisation）虽移除规范对称性，却可能隐藏依赖于所选物理时钟的物理对称性。作者旨在建立分离规范对称性与物理对称性的框架，并恢复简化相空间描述结构性地无法看到的守恒荷。

Method: 作者将平坦FLRW几何与任意数量的自由无质量标量场耦合，分析极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量及其生成的守恒荷，证明其构成Dirac可观测量。随后在多种规范下重审Eisenhart-Duval提升，比较不同规范中显现对称代数的大小，并通过去参数化将提升后的荷映射为规范不变的Dirac可观测量。

Result: 极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量生成的守恒荷是Dirac可观测量，其Poisson代数为极大共形代数𝔠𝔬𝔫𝔣(n,1)≃𝔰𝔬(n+1,2)，将先前单场结果推广到任意n。在特定的调和规范（harmonic gauge）下，显现对称代数扩大为薛定谔代数（Schrödinger algebra），后者并非物理对称性；去参数化后，荷始终实现共形代数的子代数，在调和规范下可完全复现。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了极小超空间模型中分离规范对称性与物理对称性的通用框架，表明显现对称代数依赖于规范选择，而物理对称性由共形代数刻画；该框架在存在势能项时仍然适用。

Abstract: In generally covariant theories evolution in coordinate time is a gauge transformation, so that a symmetry made manifest in a gauge-fixed description need not be a symmetry of the physical dynamics. Deparametrisation, in turn, removes gauge symmetries but may hide physical symmetries, in particular those dependent on the chosen physical clock. We study the relation between gauge and (hidden) physical symmetries in flat FLRW geometry coupled to an arbitrary number $n$ of free massless scalar fields. We show that conformal Killing vectors of the minisuperspace metric generate conserved charges which are Dirac observables--hence gauge-invariant--and whose Poisson algebra is the maximal conformal algebra $\mathfrak{conf}(n,1)\simeq\mathfrak{so}(n+1,2)$, extending previous single-field results to arbitrary $n$. We then revisit the Eisenhart-Duval lift in a family of gauges and show that the manifest symmetry algebra is gauge dependent, enlarging to the Schrödinger algebra (which is thus not a physical symmetry) in the distinguished harmonic gauge where the gauge-fixed minisuperspace metric becomes flat. Further, deparametrisation maps the lifted charges to gauge-invariant Dirac observables, which always realise a subalgebra of the conformal algebra and reproduce it in full in the harmonic gauge. These results establish a framework for separating gauge from physical symmetries in minisuperspace models, recovering charges to which reduced phase-space descriptions are structurally blind, and remaining applicable in the presence of potentials.

</details>


### [198] [Quark Stars in $f(T,\mathcal{T}) $ Gravity: Structure, Stability, and Observational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27365)
*Takol Tangphati,Ayan Banerjee,Izzet Sakalli,Sayantan Ghosh,Aseel Smerat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(T,𝒯)引力框架下研究夸克星（quark star）的结构特性，使用MIT袋模型描述退禁闭夸克物质（deconfined quark matter），求解修正后的恒星结构方程，发现最大质量随耦合常数的变化呈非单调行为，峰值约为2.02倍太阳质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克星作为完全由退禁闭夸克物质组成的假想致密天体，为检验广义相对论之外的引力理论提供了理想平台。本文旨在研究f(T,𝒯)引力（一种扭转（torsion）直接与能量-动量张量迹相耦合的teleparallel理论）下的夸克星结构。

Method: 采用标准MIT袋模型描述夸克物质，在f(T,𝒯)引力框架下求解修正后的恒星结构方程（modified stellar structure equations），并在耦合常数的全部容许范围内系统地追踪质量、半径、致密度（compactness）和表面红移（surface redshift）随耦合的变化。

Result: 最大质量对耦合的依赖呈非单调特征：先高于广义相对论值，在中等正耦合处达到约2.02倍太阳质量的峰值，随后在耦合逼近结构方程奇异的临界值时急剧下降。两倍太阳质量脉冲星约束（two-solar-mass pulsar constraint）在有限的正耦合窗口内得到满足。

Conclusion: 候选稳定分支上的所有构型均满足因果性条件（causality），且低于广义相对论标准下的致密度和表面红移基准值，表明f(T,𝒯)引力能够容纳符合观测约束的夸克星构型。

Abstract: Quark stars-hypothetical compact stars made entirely of deconfined quark matter-offer a clean testing ground for gravity beyond general relativity. We study their structure in $f(T,\mathcal{T})$ gravity, a teleparallel theory in which torsion is coupled directly to the trace of the energy-momentum tensor through a single constant coupling. Using the standard MIT bag description of quark matter, we solve the modified stellar structure equations and follow how the mass, radius, compactness, and surface redshift respond as the coupling is varied across its full admissible range. The maximum mass turns out to depend on the coupling in a non-monotonic way: it rises above the general relativity value, peaks near 2.02 solar masses at a moderate positive coupling, and then falls steeply as the coupling approaches a critical value at which the structure equations become singular. The two-solar-mass pulsar constraint is satisfied within a finite window of positive couplings. All configurations on the candidate stable branch satisfy causality and remain below the standard general-relativistic compactness and surface-redshift benchmarks.

</details>


### [199] [Structural morphology and the gravitational arrow of time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27526)
*Julian Barbour,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了零能量、零线动量、零角动量扇区中牛顿N体问题（N=5000）的两个平面中心构型（central configurations）的全局簇团对比度（variety）与局部几何形态之间的关系。尽管两个构型的无量纲多样性差异仅1.686%，但它们的六近邻各向异性（six-neighbour anisotropy）均值相差156.8%，且较高多样性构型中有18.1%的粒子满足A₆>0.5，而较低者中该比例为零。由此表明标量型全局量无法单独唯一决定局域形态，需要将局域关系可观测量作为互补描述层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BKM引力箭头（gravitational arrow）由全局标量多样性V的长时行为刻画，但V是否足以唯一确定系统形态尚不明确。中心构型作为同一尺度不变景观的临界形状，可用于受控探测V所编码的结构信息。

Method: 采用两个平面N=5000中心构型的数值粒子坐标数据，以无量纲多样性V衡量全局簇团对比度，以六近邻各向异性A₆量化局域形态，并测试不同邻域尺寸（4≤k≤12）及无纲近距离鲁棒性检验。

Result: 两个构型的V仅差1.686%，但平均A₆相差156.8%（0.1331至0.3419）；较高多样性构型中18.1%的粒子满足A₆>0.5，较低者中为零。各向异性排序在所有测试邻域尺寸下持续成立，且主要对比度在无纲近距离鲁棒性检验中保持。

Conclusion: 全局标量V不唯一决定局部几何组织，相近的全局多样性可与截然不同的局部形态共存。因此需要局域关系可观测量作为补充描述层次，为静态形状空间景观与真实Janus点历史的形态学之间建立定量桥梁。

Abstract: The Newtonian $N$-body problem in the zero-energy, zero-linear-momentum, and zero-angular-momentum sector provides a time-reversal-invariant setting in which generic complete solutions possess a Janus point and exhibit a gravitational arrow on both branches away from it. The dimensionless variety $V$, a global scale-invariant measure of clustering contrast, is not pointwise monotonic but fluctuates while growing between rising bounds away from the Janus region. Central configurations are critical shapes of the same scale-invariant landscape and therefore provide controlled probes of the structural information encoded by $V$. We investigate this question for two planar $N=5000$ central configurations using the numerical particle-coordinate data. Local morphology is quantified by the six-neighbour anisotropy $A_6$, which ranges from $0$ for an isotropic local environment to $1$ for an effectively one-dimensional one. Although the varieties of the two configurations differ by only $1.686\%$, their mean anisotropies differ by $156.8\%$, from $0.1331$ to $0.3419$. Moreover, $18.1\%$ of the particles in the higher-variety configuration satisfy $A_6>0.5$, whereas none do so in the lower-variety configuration. The anisotropy ordering persists for all tested neighbourhood sizes $4\le k\le12$, and the principal contrast survives a dimensionless close-pair robustness test. For this pair of critical shapes, nearby values of the global variety therefore coexist with markedly different local geometrical organization. Thus the scalar quantity whose long-term behavior characterizes the BKM gravitational arrow does not, by itself, uniquely specify morphology. This identifies local relational observables as a complementary level of description and provides a quantitative bridge between the static shape-space landscape and morphology along genuine Janus-point histories.

</details>


### [200] [Tidal Love numbers of multi-state Boson stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27587)
*Xin-Lei Zhao,Jun-Ru Chen,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算由基态和第一激发态复标量场构成的多态玻色子星（multi-state boson stars, MSBSs）的潮汐洛夫数（tidal Love numbers），发现电性洛夫数在特定参数阈值下由正转负，而磁性洛夫数始终为负且绝对值较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多态玻色子星是玻色星（boson stars）的一种推广形式，此前研究中尚未系统考察其潮汐响应特性。理解其潮汐洛夫数的符号与取值规律，有助于区分不同类型的致密天体并深化对引力波信号中潮汐形变效应的认识。

Method: 论文基于基态与第一激发态复标量场构建多态玻色子星模型，在同步与非同步频率条件下对背景解进行分类（单支型与双支型），并考察场函数、ADM质量与束缚能等性质；随后对包含稳定解的支路计算四极（ℓ=2）电性与磁性潮汐洛夫数。

Result: 电性潮汐洛夫数起初为正，随后在参数满足 $\tilde{\mu}_1 > 0.891$ 或 $\tilde{\omega}_0 > 0.777$ 时突然转为负值；在更小的参数取值下电性洛夫数保持为正。磁性潮汐洛夫数始终为负，且其绝对值小于电性洛夫数的绝对值。

Conclusion: 多态玻色子星的潮汐响应呈现独特的符号翻转现象，电性与磁性洛夫数的差值可作为该类天体的可观测特征，为引力波天文学中区分玻色星与其他致密天体提供理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we calculate the tidal Love numbers of multi-state boson stars (MSBSs) composed of ground state and first excited state complex scalar fields. Under synchronized and nonsynchronized frequency conditions, the background solutions of MSBSs are classified into single-branch and double-branch types. The field functions, ADM mass, and binding energy of different solutions are discussed. We then calculate the quadrupolar ($\ell=2$) electric and magnetic tidal Love numbers for branches containing stable solutions. Our results show that the electric tidal Love numbers are initially positive and then suddenly transition to negative values. This phenomenon occurs when the parameters satisfy $\tildeμ_1 > 0.891$ or $\tildeω_0 > 0.777$; for smaller values of these parameters, the electric Love numbers remain positive. The magnetic tidal Love numbers are always negative, with absolute values smaller than those of the electric tidal Love numbers.

</details>


### [201] [Constrained thermodynamics and geodesic observables of an effective non-commutative Kerr-like black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27707)
*H. Hassanabadi,L. A. López,N. Bretón,L. M. Nieto,S. Zare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究非对易背景下有效克尔型黑洞（Kerr-like black hole）的视界结构、约束热力学与测地线性质。变形通过质量依赖的类电荷贡献改变径向几何，同时保持测地线方程的可分离性。研究发现变形使特征光子轨道向内移动并缩小阴影尺寸，内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）也产生向内偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易（non-commutative）量子引力效应对旋转黑洞几何与热力学性质的影响，理解变形参数如何改变视界存在条件、极端温度构型以及可观测的引力透镜特征。

Method: 通过有效克尔型度规引入质量依赖的类电荷形变项，保持测地线方程可分离性；分别求出视界存在条件、极端零温构型、静止极限面与能层（ergoregion）；在固定非对易变形参数下区分几何霍金量（Hawking quantities）与约束态空间共轭变量，推导正则与巨正则热容；数值求解球形光子区域、赤道光环、阴影边界及内稳定圆轨道，并计算不变量光子频移。

Result: 确定视界存在条件及极端零温构型；非对易变形使特征光子轨道向内移动并减小阴影整体尺寸；内稳定圆轨道中顺行与逆行分支均产生向内偏移；通过将发射体轨道方向与光子切向发射方向视为独立物理选择，得到不变量光子频率移动。

Conclusion: 非对易背景下的有效克尔型黑洞在几何、热力学和测地线性质上均呈现出可区分的变形效应，尤其体现在光子轨道、阴影尺寸和稳定圆轨道位置的内移上，表明该模型可作为检验非对易量子引力效应的候选天体物理探针。

Abstract: We investigate the horizon structure, constrained thermodynamics, and geodesic properties of an effective Kerr-like black hole in a non-commutative background. Deformation modifies the radial geometry through a mass-dependent charge-like contribution, while preserving the separability of the geodesic equations. We determine the conditions for horizon existence, identify the extremal zero-temperature configuration, and analyze the stationary-limit surfaces and the ergoregion. Special attention is paid to the thermodynamic interpretation of the model, where the geometric Hawking quantities are distinguished from the conjugate variables associated with the constrained state space at fixed non-commutative deformation parameter. The canonical and grand-canonical heat capacities are derived to characterize their ensemble-dependent local thermal behavior. We also obtain the spherical photon region, equatorial light rings, and shadow boundary, showing that the deformation shifts the characteristic photon orbits inwards and reduces the overall size of the shadow.
