Timelike circular motion is studied through the innermost stable circular orbit, where non-commutative correction produces an inward shift of both the prograde and retrograde branches. Finally, invariant photon frequency shifts are obtained by treating the emitter's orbital direction and the photon's tangential emission direction as independent physical choices.

</details>


### [202] [Degenerate Limits of Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27801)
*Bekir Baytaş,Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在哈密顿联络动力学（Hamiltonian connection dynamics）框架下研究标量-张量引力理论的退化极限，通过空间三标场（spatial triad）的局域标度变换实现退化，推导出退化标量-张量引力理论的约束条件并讨论其物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量-张量引力理论在退化极限下的动力学行为尚未在联络动力学表述中被系统研究，需要建立该极限下的数学框架并理解其物理后果。

Method: 采用哈密顿联络动力学表述，通过对空间三标场施加局域标度变换（local scaling transformation）来实现退化极限，并由此推导所有几何量和几何关系在该极限下的退化形式。

Result: 推导出了退化标量-张量引力理论的约束条件（constraints），并对其物理含义进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 通过局域标度变换可以在联络动力学框架中一致地实现标量-张量引力理论的退化极限，所获得的约束条件为该理论在退化情形下的动力学提供了基础。

Abstract: We study the degenerate limits of the scalar-tensor theory of gravity in its Hamiltonian connection dynamics formulation. The degenerate limit of the spatial triad is effectuated by a local scaling transformation of the triad, which leads to the degenerate limits of all other geometric quantities and relations in the connection dynamics. We derive the constraints for the degenerate scalar-tensor theory of gravity and discuss their physical implications.

</details>


### [203] [Foldy--Wouthuysen Transformation of the Generalized Dirac Equation in Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27889)
*Sabutay Ugur,Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称遥平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）背景下广义狄拉克方程（generalized Dirac equation）的非相对论极限，通过Foldy–Wouthuysen变换推导了至1/m²阶的有效哈密顿量，发现除了传统的引力对应项（动能、自旋轨道、达尔文相互作用）外，还产生了直接自旋-引力、各向异性自旋-动量-引力及潮汐自旋-动量耦合等新型低能相互作用通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统狄拉克方程仅考虑黎曼几何背景下自旋-引力相互作用，而广义自旋联络（generalized spinor connection）包含完整Clifford代数量基，可引入非度量性（non-metricity）扇区的额外耦合。理解这些额外耦合在低能极限下的物理效应，是探索对称遥平行引力新现象的关键。

Method: 在重合规范（coincident gauge）下采用弱场史瓦西（Schwarzschild）各向同性坐标几何，构造广义狄拉克哈密顿量；随后连续执行Foldy–Wouthuysen变换直至1/m²阶，保留引力势及其空间导数的一阶项，获得块对角化的有效哈密顿量。

Result: 块对角哈密顿量除包含动能、自旋-轨道及达尔文相互作用的引力对应项外，还展现出由广义自旋联络产生的额外算符结构，天然涌现出直接自旋-引力耦合、各向异性自旋-动量-引力耦合及潮汐自旋-动量耦合。针对地球弱引力场中电子的量级分析进一步验证了逆质量展开截断的合理性。

Conclusion: 对称遥平行背景下的广义狄拉克方程在低能极限下产生涉及费米子自旋、动量及引力场空间导数的新相互作用通道。所得有效哈密顿量为约束广义自旋联络中额外耦合参数提供了可检验的现象学框架。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of the generalized Dirac equation in a weak, static, and spherically symmetric background of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The underlying generalized spinor connection incorporates the complete Clifford-algebra basis and introduces additional couplings to the non-metricity sector beyond those of the conventional Dirac theory. Working in the coincident gauge and adopting the weak-field Schwarzschild geometry in isotropic coordinates, we derive the corresponding generalized Dirac Hamiltonian and perform successive Foldy--Wouthuysen transformations up to order $1/m^2$, retaining terms to first order in the gravitational potential and its spatial derivatives. The resulting block-diagonal Hamiltonian contains not only the expected gravitational counterparts of the kinetic, spin--orbit, and Darwin interactions, but also additional operator structures generated by the generalized spinor connection. In particular, direct spin--gravity, anisotropic spin--momentum--gravity, and tidal spin--momentum couplings arise naturally from the generalized metric-affine interaction. We further perform an order-of-magnitude analysis for an electron in the Earth's weak gravitational field to justify the adopted truncation of the inverse-mass expansion. These results demonstrate that the generalized Dirac equation in a symmetric teleparallel background gives rise to new low-energy interaction channels involving the fermion spin, momentum, and spatial derivatives of the gravitational field. The resulting effective Hamiltonian provides a framework for exploring phenomenological constraints on the additional couplings entering the generalized spinor connection.

</details>


### [204] [Towards long and accurate numerical relativity waveforms of binary black holes beyond general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27991)
*Guillermo Lara,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Nils Deppe,Lawrence E. Kidder,Geoffrey Lovelace,Sizheng Ma,Alexandra Macedo,Jordan Moxon,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,William Throwe,Nils L. Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文展示了将谱方法与“固定方程”（fixing-the-equations）方法相结合，在标量高斯-博内引力（scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity）这一真实的广义相对论之外的理论中生成了文献中最长的数值相对论波形，并量化了相位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越广义相对论（beyond GR）的数值相对论（numerical relativity, NR）模拟对于未来利用引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）检验引力的发展至关重要，但此前在替代引力理论中缺乏与GR相当的高质量长波形。

Method: 采用谱方法（spectral methods）与“固定方程”方法相结合的技术路线，针对移对称（shift-symmetric）版本的标量高斯-博内引力理论，模拟等质量、无自旋、低偏心率的黑洞双星系统，并在未来零无穷远（future null infinity）处提取引力和标量波形。

Result: 在40多个GW周期（20多圈轨道）后，相位误差被量化在约1弧度以内；同时发现该替代理论中的GW相位修正与爱因斯坦理论可区分，且导致比GR更早的并合时间。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着超越GR的NR方法向GR领域的最先进水平迈进，为后续与后牛顿（post-Newtonian）理论的精确比较以及校准超越GR的波形模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Numerical relativity (NR) simulations of compact binaries in theories beyond general relativity (GR) will be pivotal for the continued development of future tests of gravity with gravitational waves (GWs). In this Letter, we show that the combination of spectral methods and the "fixing-the-equations" approach allows us to produce the longest waveforms in the literature for a genuine beyond-GR theory, thus bringing NR methods for alternative theories of gravity closer to the state-of-the-art in GR. For concreteness, we focus on the well-known shift-symmetric version of scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity, a theory postulating the existence of an additional dynamical scalar and describing black holes (BHs) different from the Kerr solution. We extract the gravitational and scalar waveforms at future null infinity for equal-mass, nonspinning, eccentricity-reduced BH binaries, and quantify the phase errors to be $\lesssim$ 1 rad after 40+ GW cycles (20+ orbits). We also show that the GW phase corrections in this alternative theory are distinguishable from Einstein's theory and lead to an earlier coalescence time than in GR. Obtaining such waveforms is a stepping stone to perform precise comparisons with Post-Newtonian theory and to calibrate waveform models beyond GR.

</details>


### [205] [High-accuracy drivers to simulate black hole binaries beyond general relativity with the fixing-the-equations approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28003)
*Guillermo Lara,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Nils Deppe,Lawrence E. Kidder,Geoffrey Lovelace,Sizheng Ma,Alexandra Macedo,Jordan Moxon,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,William Throwe,Nils L. Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文采用"fixing-the-equations"方法在数值相对论（numerical relativity，NR）代码SpECTRE中实现了标量高斯-博内（scalar Gauss-Bonnet，sGB）引力下的长时间高精度数值相对论波形（waveforms），通过引入新的共动驱动器方程（comoving driver equations）恢复全耦合理论的精确（准）稳态解，并获得了相误差≲O(1)弧度、跨越近40个引力波（gravitational wave，GW）周期且自然包含记忆效应（memory contributions）的波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标量高斯-博内引力中，直接求解全耦合偏微分方程进行长时间数值模拟存在数值不稳定性或效率问题，需要借助"fixing-the-equations"方法来产生长时长、高精度的数值相对论波形，以满足引力波天文学对理论模板的需求。同时，已有方法在张量场情形下可能出现标量化处理导致的伪增长等不期望行为，需要更适当的推广。

Method: 在基于赝谱间断伽辽金（pseudo-spectral discontinuous Galerkin）方案的NR代码SpECTRE中实现"fixing-the-equations"方法；引入新型共动驱动器方程族以利用准圆双星系统的近似对称性，恢复全耦合理论的精确（准）稳态解；针对张量情形改进了驱动器方程的张量化推广；结合最先进的偏心率约减（eccentricity reduction）和带柯西特征演化（Cauchy Characteristic Evolution）的波提取技术。

Result: 单黑洞解与解析预测吻合；早期旋进阶段双星的固有黑洞量对驱动器方程中的时间尺度相对不敏感；验证了将张量分量作标量处理会导致长时间尺度的不期望行为（如黑洞自旋的伪增长）；提出的张量共动驱动器避免了此类问题；在偏心率≲10⁻³和近40个引力波周期条件下获得相误差≲O(1)弧度的波形，且波形自然包含记忆贡献。

Conclusion: 该实现成功将"fixing-the-equations"方法扩展到张量场情形，提供了适用于标量高斯-博内引力的长时间高精度数值相对论波形生成框架，为未来引力波模板库构建奠定了基础。

Abstract: We implement the "fixing-the-equations" approach [Phys.Rev.D 96 (2017) 8, 084043] in spectre, an NR code using a pseudo-spectral discontinuous Galerkin scheme, to produce long and accurate NR waveforms in the well-known shift-symmetric version of scalar Gauss-Bonnet (sGB) gravity. To achieve this, we introduce a new family of comoving driver equations that exploits the approximate symmetries of quasicircular binary systems and is designed to recover the exact (quasi-)stationary solutions of the fully-coupled theory. We validate our single black hole (BH) solutions against analytic predictions and show that, even for binary BHs in the early inspiral, the intrinsic BH quantities are relatively insensitive to the timescales entering the driver equation. Attention is given to the prescription of driver equations for tensors, for which we give an example of how treating tensor components as scalars can lead to undesired behaviour over long timescales, including spurious growth of the BH spins. A more appropriate generalization to the tensor case is given for the comoving driver, which is shown to avoid these issues. Overall, our implementation leverages state-of-the-art methods for eccentricity reduction and wave extraction with Cauchy Characteristic Evolution to simulate systems with eccentricity $\lesssim 10^{-3}$. We obtain waveforms with phase errors $\lesssim \mathcal{O}(1) \, \mathrm{rad}$ over almost 40 GW-cycles, which naturally incorporate memory contributions.

</details>


### [206] [Quartic Scalar Clouds on Fixed Kerr Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28044)
*Hendrik Mennenga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究固定克尔（Kerr）和史瓦西（Schwarzschild）背景下，具有无界标量势的复标量场的稳态非线性云。在克尔背景上发现了满足同步条件 ω=mΩ_H 的有限振幅 Q 云（Q-clouds），占据参数平面上的二维区域，并在振幅趋零时退化为线性云。四阶势区域可持续至 Ω_H=0，此时解成为史瓦西背景上的静态非线性标量云。此外还计算了归一化类轴子余弦势的存在域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索固定黑洞背景下非线性标量场云（scalar clouds）的存在性，特别是超越线性摄动层级、考虑无界标量势时非线性解的数学结构与参数空间特征。

Method: 在固定克尔和史瓦西背景上，对具有无界标量势（unbounded scalar potential）的复标量场求解稳态非线性方程，通过同步条件（synchronisation condition）约束解的角频率，并系统扫描参数平面以确定解的存在域。

Result: 在克尔背景上发现了有限振幅的 Q 云，满足同步条件且占据 (Ω_H, M_K) 平面上的二维区域；当振幅趋于零时还原为线性云。四阶势（quartic domain）的解可延伸到 Ω_H=0 情形，此时同步频率和诺特荷（Noether charge）均消失，解退化为史瓦西背景上的静态非线性标量云。还确定了归一化类轴子余弦势（axion-inspired cosine potential）的存在域。

Conclusion: 非线性标量云在固定黑洞背景下普遍存在，其参数空间具有二维连续结构，且与线性摄动解有明确的极限对应关系；不同势函数形式（四阶势与余弦势）均支持此类解的存在。

Abstract: We study stationary nonlinear clouds of a complex scalar field on fixed Kerr and Schwarzschild backgrounds, with an unbounded scalar potential. On Kerr backgrounds we find finite-amplitude Q-clouds satisfying the synchronisation condition $ω=mΩ_H$. They occupy two-dimensional regions of the ($Ω_H,M_K$) plane and reduce to the corresponding linear clouds as the scalar field amplitude tends to zero. The quartic domain can continue to $Ω_H=0$, where both the synchronised frequency and the Noether charge vanish and the solutions become static nonlinear scalar clouds on a fixed Schwarzschild background. We also compute the existence domain for a normalised, axion-inspired cosine potential.

</details>


### [207] [Signatures of kinetic gravity braiding in cosmological probes of the gravitational field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28207)
*Ahmad Nouri-Zonoz,Farbod Hassani,Julian Adamek,Emilio Bellini,Martin Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用相对论性N体模拟代码KGB-evolution，沿过去光锥构建了动力学引力编织（kinetic gravity braiding, KGB）模型的可观测信号，包括弱引力透镜汇聚、Shapiro时间延迟、ISW-RS效应和引力红移，并与k-essence模型及线性微扰理论预言进行比较。研究发现导数耦合会改变引力势的振幅与时间演化，在ISW-RS信号和弱透镜小尺度上产生数个至数十个百分点的尺度依赖偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGB模型作为广义修正引力理论的一种，其对引力势和可观测信号的影响尚未在相对论性光锥探针中被系统研究。本文旨在通过数值模拟检验KGB模型在各类相对论性宇宙学探针中的可观测特征，并评估线性微扰理论的适用范围。

Method: 作者使用相对论性N体模拟代码KGB-evolution生成光锥输出，计算弱引力透镜汇聚、Shapiro时间延迟、ISW-RS效应和引力红移等直接探测引力场的观测量；构建全天图与角功率谱，并与k-essence模型及线性微扰理论预言进行比较。

Result: 导数耦合同时改变引力势的振幅和时间演化，产生从百分之几到数十个百分点的尺度依赖偏差；ISW-RS信号表现出最大的分数响应，在高多极矩下与k-essence的差异达数十个百分点；弱引力透镜在小尺度（多极矩约10^2至10^3）出现约10%至12%的增强偏离。

Conclusion: 线性微扰理论能准确描述大尺度行为，但非线性效应在小尺度上变得重要，尤其对ISW-RS信号和汇聚信号，因此可靠的理论预言必须纳入非线性效应。

Abstract: We study the observational signatures of kinetic gravity braiding (KGB) models in relativistic cosmological probes constructed along the past light cone. Using the relativistic $N$-body code KGB-evolution, we generate light-cone outputs and compute several observables that directly probe the gravitational field, including weak gravitational lensing convergence, Shapiro time delay, the integrated Sachs-Wolfe and Rees-Sciama (ISW-RS) effects, and gravitational redshift. Full-sky maps and angular power spectra of these quantities are constructed and compared with $k$-essence models and predictions from linear perturbation theory. We find that the derivative coupling between the scalar field and the metric modifies both the amplitude and the time evolution of the gravitational potentials, producing scale-dependent deviations ranging from a few percent to tens of percent. In particular, the ISW-RS signal exhibits the largest fractional response, as the slower decay of the Weyl potential suppresses the KGB signal in the ISW-dominated regime, whereas nonlinear evolution reverses this trend at higher multipoles, producing differences of tens of percent relative to $k$-essence. Weak gravitational lensing also provides a strong complementary probe and, for the model considered here, exhibits clear deviations from the $k$-essence prediction at small scales with enhancements up to $\sim 10$-$12\%$ at multipoles $\ell \sim 10^2$-$10^3$. Our results show that linear perturbation theory accurately describes the large-scale behaviour, while nonlinear effects become important at smaller scales, particularly for the ISW-RS signal and, more moderately, for the convergence, and must therefore be included for reliable theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [208] [Homogeneous and Isotropic Linearized Gravity as a Caldeira--Leggett System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28209)
*Pelayo V. Calzada,Pedro Bargueño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了均匀各向同性线性化引力（linearized gravity）与Caldeira–Leggett开放系统模型之间的等价关系，将引力纳入系统加储库（system-plus-reservoir）形式框架。通过消除储库自由度，将线性化引力方程约化为关于度规微扰（metric perturbation）的广义Langevin方程，并利用Laplace方法分析所得动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为引力理论建立与成熟的开放系统形式主义之间的联系，使得可以借鉴已有的系统加储库方法来处理引力扰动，并为进一步的约化量子化和随机引力动力学研究提供自然框架。

Method: 证明均匀各向同性线性化引力（带最小耦合自由度）与Caldeira–Leggett开放系统模型在数学上等价；利用该等价关系，通过消去储库自由度将线性化引力方程约化为广义Langevin方程，环境影响被凝聚在谱密度（spectral density）中；随后采用Laplace方法分析所得动力学方程。

Result: 成功证明了线性化引力与Caldeira–Leggett模型的等价关系，得到了描述度规微扰的广义Langevin方程，并扩展了其他开放系统研究中已有的分析结果。

Conclusion: 该等价关系为减少维度的量子化（reduced quantization）和随机引力动力学（stochastic gravitational dynamics）提供了自然的理论设定，表明开放系统框架可有效应用于引力系统的动力学分析。

Abstract: We establish the equivalence between homogeneous and isotropic linearized gravity with minimally coupled degrees of freedom and the Caldeira--Leggett model of open systems. This connects gravitation to an established system-plus-reservoir formalism, which we use throughout. Eliminating the reservoir reduces the equations of linearized gravity to a generalized Langevin equation for the metric perturbation, with the environmental influence condensed in the spectral density. The equivalence also yields a natural setting for reduced quantization of the perturbation and stochastic gravitational dynamics. We analyze the resulting dynamics with Laplace methods, extending results presented in other open-system settings.

</details>


### [209] [Logarithmic corrections to black hole entropy from minimum-assumptions discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28249)
*Pelayo V. Calzada,Ana Alonso-Serrano,Ernesto Contreras,Pedro Bargueño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在不可知论（agnostic）和最小假设条件下，提出一个通用模型，以唯一确定熵面积定律（entropy area law）及其固定的对数修正项（logarithmic correction term）。该模型将视界（horizon）离散化为普朗克尺度（Planck-scale）的粗粒化不可区分几何单元，熵项的形式由统计组合计数（statistical combinatorial counting）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型中熵面积定律的对数修正项的推导依赖于具体理论假设，缺乏一个在最小假设下统一、确定性地推导该修正项的通用框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将视界离散化为普朗克尺度单元，这些单元代表粗粒化的、不可区分的几何结构；在不可知论和最小假设框架下，通过统计组合计数确定熵项的解析形式，从而唯一推导出面积定律及其固定的对数修正项。

Result: 该模型能够在最小假设下唯一确定熵面积定律及其对应的固定对数修正项，并且文中对比了该模型的假设与已知提供固定对数熵修正的模型之间的异同。

Conclusion: 统计组合计数本身即足以决定熵项形式，无需强加额外的物理假设；与已知模型对比有助于阐明各假设在确定熵修正项中的作用。

Abstract: We introduce here a general model, under agnostic and minimum assumptions, to uniquely find the entropy area law with a corresponding fixed logarithmic correction term. In this approach, the horizon is discretized into generic Planck-scale cells representing coarse-grained indistinguishable geometric structures, and it is just the statistical combinatorial counting that determines the form of the entropy terms. We highlight the role of the assumptions and their comparison with known models providing fixed logarithmic contributions to the entropy.

</details>


### [210] [Ab Initio Real-Time Gravitational-Wave Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28265)
*David Yallup,Metha Prathaban,James Alvey,Thomas C. K. Ng,Thibeau Wouters,Nikhil Sarin,Will Handley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GPU原生、专门面向引力波参数估计的嵌套采样核（nested sampling kernel），结合Slice-within-Gibbs结构，在典型长时双中子星信号上实现了单GPU中位数12分钟、四GPU分片5分钟的参数估计，并通过外差压缩将中位墙钟时间降至89秒，推动随机采样方法向实时引力波参数估计迈进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波推断问题需要快速且可靠的参数估计，而传统随机采样方法在现代GPU硬件上的性能潜力尚未被充分挖掘。作者希望探索在保持完整物理波形计算的前提下，将随机采样技术推向实时或近实时应用的可行边界。

Method: 方法基于GPU原生的嵌套采样内核，其核心采用Slice-within-Gibbs（SwiG）结构以实现快速混合，并在此框架上研究如何最大程度地发挥基线随机采样技术在现代GPU硬件上的计算能力。此外引入外差（heterodyning）技术对数据进行压缩，以进一步缩短计算时间。

Result: 对于LIGO和Virgo探测器网络中典型长时双中子星信号，在完整未压缩数据上进行三探测器网络推断，单GPU中位时间约为12分钟；在四设备分片下可降至5分钟。利用外差压缩数据后，注入活动（injection campaign）的中位墙钟时间降至89秒，短于数据段本身的时长；对GW170817进行含进动自旋和潮汐波形（precessing spin, tidal waveforms）的推断约需2分钟。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了当前基线随机采样技术结合GPU原生实现和SwiG结构，配合完整物理波形计算与无信息先验启动，能够显著接近实时引力波参数估计的目标，为未来高频次引力波事件的高效处理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a specialised GPU-native nested sampling kernel targeting rapid parameter estimation for gravitational wave inference problems. Building upon a Slice-within-Gibbs (SwiG) structure for rapid mixing, we investigate how far we can push baseline stochastic sampling techniques on modern GPU hardware. We demonstrate that for typical long-duration binary neutron star signals observed by the LIGO and Virgo detectors, we can achieve well calibrated posterior inference on the full uncompressed data of a three detector network in a median of twelve minutes on a single GPU. This falls to five minutes when sharded across four devices. Utilising heterodyning to compress the data reduces the median wall time across an injection campaign to 89 seconds -- less than the length of the segment itself -- and enables inference with precessing spin, tidal waveforms on GW170817 in around two minutes. This pushes stochastic sampling techniques using full physical waveform calculations, launched from an uninformed prior state, towards real-time gravitational wave parameter estimation.

</details>


### [211] [Post-Newtonian Roche-Lobe-Overflow Prescription for Compact Binary Mass Transfer and the Corresponding Gravitational Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28315)
*Shuai Zhang,Jie Yang,Zi-Han Zhang,Shenghua Yu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从一阶后牛顿（post-Newtonian）流体力学方程出发，推导了后牛顿洛希势（Roche potential）及相应的洛希瓣溢流（Roche lobe overflow）质量转移处方，并将质量随时间变化纳入双星动力学，计算了对引力波能量、角动量通量和远场极化波形的修正，最后应用于典型致密超紧致双星系统，发现质量转移对动力学演化有重要影响，在引力波观测中主要体现为长时间观测累积的相位漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双星系统中的质量转移是许多天体物理现象的核心，包括致密相互作用双星的演化，然而此前对后牛顿框架下质量转移效应及其对引力波信号影响的系统处理尚不完善，需要构造相应的理论框架。

Method: 从共转参考系（corotating frame）中的一阶后牛顿流体力学方程出发，推导一阶后牛顿洛希势，构建相应的后牛顿形式洛希瓣溢流质量转移处方；随后将分量质量随时间的变化纳入双星动力学，计算对运动方程、引力波能量与角动量通量以及远场极化波形的后牛顿修正；最后将模型应用于代表性超紧双星系统。

Result: 研究发现质量转移在致密双星的动力学演化中可发挥重要作用；对于引力波观测而言，其主要效应表现为在长时间观测中累积的长期（secular）相位漂移。

Conclusion: 该后牛顿质量转移模型为理解致密双星演化和引力波观测数据提供了理论工具，质量转移导致的相位漂移效应应被纳入未来引力波数据分析中。

Abstract: Mass transfer in binary systems is central to many astrophysical phenomena, including the evolution of compact interacting binaries. Starting from the first post-Newtonian hydrodynamic equations in the corotating frame, we derive the first post-Newtonian Roche potential and construct the corresponding post-Newtonian form of the Roche lobe overflow mass transfer prescription. We then include the time dependence of the component masses in the binary dynamics and compute the associated corrections to the equations of motion, gravitational-wave energy and angular-momentum fluxes, and far-zone polarization waveforms. Finally, we apply the model to representative ultracompact binary systems. We find that mass transfer can play an important role in the dynamical evolution of compact binaries. For gravitational-wave observations, its main effect appears as a secular phase drift accumulated over long observation times.

</details>


### [212] [The BGV Theorem and the Null Convergence Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28412)
*William H. Kinney*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究零收敛条件（Null Convergence Condition, NCC）与Borde-Guth-Vilenkin（BGV）定理之间的关系。在无剪切、无涡旋且正交于类时测地线汇的膨胀时空切片下，当NCC成立且空间曲率非正（³R ≤ 0）时，BGV定理成立；但在存在剪切或非测地线穿行等一般情形下，NCC与非正曲率不再足以保证BGV定理成立，需要更一般的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGV定理是宇宙学中关于类空超曲面无法具有过去完备性的重要结果，其成立条件与时空能量条件（尤其是零收敛条件）的关联程度尚不明确。作者旨在厘清在何种几何条件下NCC足以推出BGV定理，以及在剪切或非测地线情形下该结论如何失效。

Method: 论文采用解析几何分析的方法，先考虑膨胀时空被正交于切向类时测地线汇切片的情形（剪切与涡旋为零），推导BGV定理成立所需的条件；随后引入剪切（shear）和非测地线穿行（non-geodesic threading）的推广情形，分析局域膨胀率如何依赖剪切与加速度同局域空间单位矢量的收缩项。

Result: 在无剪切、无涡旋且呈直角正交切片的情形下，NCC成立且空间曲率非正（³R ≤ 0）时BGV定理成立。在存在剪切或非测地线穿行的一般情形下，进入BGV构造的局域膨胀不仅取决于标量膨胀，还包含剪切与加速度同空间单位矢量的收缩贡献，此时NCC与非正曲率不再充分。

Conclusion: 作者在共动曲率扰动下的永恒膨胀（eternal inflation）背景下讨论了结果的物理意义，并陈述了一个适用于渐近过去de Sitter永恒膨胀的更一般版本的BGV条件。

Abstract: We examine the relationship between the Null Convergence Condition (NCC) and the Borde-Guth-Vilenkin (BGV) Theorem. We first show that, for an expanding spacetime foliated orthogonally to a timelike geodesic congruence with vanishing shear and vorticity, the BGV Theorem follows when the NCC holds and the spatial curvature is non-positive, ${}^3\mathcal{R} \leq 0$. The situation becomes more complex in the presence of shear, or non-geodesic threading of the spacetime. In these cases, the local expansion that enters the BGV construction depends not only on the local scalar expansion, but acquires terms given by the contraction of the shear and acceleration with locally defined spatial unit vectors. In the general case, null convergence and non-positive curvature are no longer sufficient to guarantee that the BGV Theorem holds. We discuss the result in the context of eternal inflation in the presence of comoving curvature perturbations and state a more general version of the BGV condition relevant to asymptotically past-de Sitter eternal inflation.

</details>


### [213] [Dynamics of compact binary systems in massive scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28448)
*Iris van Gemeren,Tanja Hinderer,Stefan Vandoren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用后牛顿近似（post-Newtonian approximation）计算了有大质量标量场的有质量标量-高斯-博内特理论（massive scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory, sGB）中无自旋黑洞双星系统的动力学，首次纳入高阶曲率与标量质量耦合的修正，得到圆轨道和偏心轨道在1PN阶的运动方程、质心变换和束缚能解，并证明标量质量修正对规范不变的束缚能具有同号和反号项，在微扰极限下总贡献为反号，略微降低束缚能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密双星系统的内旋（inspiral）过程可在强场区域探测引力，从而检验广义相对论（General Relativity）可能的高阶曲率修正。宇称不变的二次曲率修正可由sGB理论描述，其中标量场与曲率标量动态耦合，可在黑洞周围产生标量凝聚。考虑标量场的质量是自然的选择，并会带来与这一额外标度相关的新物理现象。

Method: 本文在有质量sGB理论框架下，采用后牛顿近似方法，计算无自旋黑洞双星系统的动力学。推导并求解了运动方程、质心变换以及圆轨道和偏心轨道束缚能（binding energy）至1PN阶的解析解，首次包含了高阶曲率与标量质量耦合的修正。最终解析表达式假设标量质量远小于双星总质量，并展开至该质量比率的二次方阶。

Result: 标量质量对规范不变束缚能的修正包含同号和反号符号项的贡献，在微扰极限下整体呈现反号贡献，使束缚能略微减小。该效应在高质量比率和大偏心率的双星系统中最为显著。大多数计算对一般标量质量均有效。

Conclusion: 本文的方法和结果可作为计算此类双星系统产生的引力波（gravitational waves）的基础，为后续在强场引力探测中检验高阶曲率修正及其标量质量效应提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Inspiraling binary systems of compact objects probe gravity in strong-field regimes, thereby exploring potential higher curvature corrections to General Relativity. Parity-invariant quadratic corrections can be described by scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (sGB) theory, which involves a scalar field dynamically coupled to curvature scalars and can give rise to scalar condensates around black holes. Considering a mass for the scalar field is natural and leads to new phenomenology related to this additional scale. We compute the dynamics of a binary system of nonspinning black holes in massive sGB using the post-Newtonian (PN) approximation. We obtain solutions for the equations of motion, center-of-mass transformation, and binding energy for circular and eccentric orbits up to 1PN order, where for the first time the higher curvature coupled to scalar mass corrections are included. While most of our calculations are valid for generic scalar masses, the final explicit expressions assume that the mass is small compared to the total mass of the binary and expand to quadratic order in this ratio. We show that the scalar mass corrections to the gauge-invariant binding energy come with same and opposite sign order terms, contributing an overall opposite sign contribution in the perturbative limit, decreasing the binding energy slightly. The effects are largest for binary systems with high mass ratio and large eccentricity. Our methods and results will also be useful as a basis for computing the gravitational waves sourced by such systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Suppressed Quantum Effects of Weakly Coupled Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27313)
*Yunjia Bao,Dhong Yeon Cheong,Nicholas L. Rodd,Joey Takach,Lian-Tao Wang,Kevin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了精密实验中检测弱耦合波（如轴子暗物质和引力辐射）的非经典（nonclassical）效应的两个严重障碍：探测器无法分辨基本模式而只能耦合到粗粒化的"有效"模式，以及所有非经典效应都被弱耦合的额外幂次抑制。作者证明这些抑制对正交分量和数统计、纠缠以及退相干均成立，并以轴子腔体卤化物探测器（axion cavity haloscope）为实例说明，结论适用于多种超轻暗物质搜索，并排除了通过引力波观测确立引力量子化的提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密实验日益针对弱耦合波（包括轴子暗物质和引力辐射），这些波通常被描述为经典场，但它们可能存在于没有经典对应的量子态中。作者希望探讨能否检测这些非经典效应，并揭示实际可行性存在的基本物理障碍。

Method: 作者从一般性原理出发，给出两种与模式占据数（mode occupancy）无关的非经典效应检测障碍的严格证明。他们分别从探测器分辨率的限制（有效模式粗粒化）和弱耦合导致的非经典效应幂次抑制两个角度展开分析，并将其应用于正交分量统计、数统计、纠缠和退相干等具体场景。

Result: 结果表明，真实探测器因无法分辨场的基本模式而耦合到粗粒化的有效模式，这往往会抹去非经典效应；同时，所有非经典效应都因弱耦合而受到额外幂次抑制，使其比波本身更难检测。虽然原则上可通过高度压缩（squeezed）的探测器态等量子资源克服抑制，但所需参数远超当前实验能力。

Conclusion: 作者以轴子腔体卤化物探测器为明确实例验证结论，并指出其适用性涵盖多种超轻暗物质（ultralight dark matter）搜索；该结论还排除了从引力波观测中建立引力量子化的提议。

Abstract: Precision experiments increasingly target weakly coupled waves, including axion dark matter and gravitational radiation. Such waves are commonly described as classical fields, yet they could exist in quantum states with no classical counterpart. We exhibit two severe obstructions to detecting nonclassical effects, both independent of the mode occupancy. First, realistic detectors cannot resolve the fundamental modes of a field; instead they couple to coarse-grained "effective" modes, which often washes out nonclassical effects. Second, all nonclassical effects are suppressed by extra powers of the weak coupling, making them much harder to detect than the waves themselves. We prove this in general, and explicitly show how the suppression arises for quadrature and number statistics, entanglement, and decoherence. The suppression can in principle be overcome given suitable quantum resources, such as highly squeezed detector states, but the required parameters are far beyond current experimental capabilities. We use the axion cavity haloscope as an explicit example, although our conclusions apply to many ultralight dark matter searches, and rule out proposals to establish the quantization of gravity from observations of gravitational waves.

</details>


### [215] [The Very Nearly Right Theory of Flavor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27315)
*Nima Arkani-Hamed,Carolina Figueiredo,Lawrence J. Hall,Claudio Andrea Manzari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过"九链纹理（nine-link textures）"参数化味数据空间，发现CKM矩阵幺正三角形（unitarity triangle）的角度接近π的简单分数并非巧合，而是源于Yukawa矩阵的特殊结构。这些纹理定义了一个"Yukawa三角形（Yukawa triangle）"，在领头阶与幺正三角形一致，超越领头阶则产生可计算的偏差，为下一代实验提供了可检验的精确预言，同时稀疏纹理配合自发CP破缺可解决强CP问题（strong CP problem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKM矩阵幺正三角形的角度$(α, β, γ)$观测值接近$(π/2, π/8, 3π/8)$，这些π的简单分数暗示存在连接味物理与自发CP破缺的潜在理论，但幺正三角形作为Yukawa矩阵的复杂函数，难以直接与底层味理论建立联系。本文旨在构建一个Yukawa矩阵的简单图像，使这一经验观察与理论建立直接关联。

Method: 作者将十维味数据空间参数化为"九链纹理"：秩满且总共只有九个非零元素的$Y_{u,d}$矩阵，仅含单一CP破缺相。通过对所有此类纹理的十个参数进行味数据拟合，研究其结构特性；同时利用$Y_{u,d}$的矩阵元自然地构造"Yukawa三角形"，将其与幺正三角形在领头阶及更高阶进行对比分析。

Result: 拟合结果显示CP相紧密聚集在$π/8$的整数倍附近。Yukawa三角形的矩阵元在大多数情形下在领头阶与小味参数下与幺正三角形一致，但在超越领头阶时两三角形出现差异，从而给出$(α, β, γ)$偏离$(π/2, π/8, 3π/8)$的精确预言。这些预言可被下一代实验测量明确排除或强有力地确认。此外，九链纹理的稀疏性使其行列式自然为实，结合自发CP破缺可解决强CP问题。

Conclusion: 9链纹理为味结构与CP破缺提供了简洁的理论框架，既统一解释了幺正三角形角度接近π简单分数的经验事实，又给出了可被下一代实验检验的高阶修正预言，同时为解决强CP问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: A striking empirical observation about the CKM matrix is that the angles of the unitarity triangle $(α, β, γ)$ are very close to $(π/2, π/8, 3 π/8) $, simple fractions of $π$ that are suggestive of an underlying theory linking flavor and spontaneous CP violation. However, relating this empirical observation to an underlying theory of flavor is challenging, since the unitarity triangle is a complicated function of the Yukawa matrices. In this letter we present a simple picture for the Yukawas where this direct link is possible. We begin by parametrizing the ten-dimensional space of flavor data via "nine-link textures", full-rank $Y_{u,d}$ matrices with a total of nine non-zero entries, with a single CP violating phase. Fitting the ten parameters of all such textures to the flavor data reveals a wonderful surprise: the CP phases cluster tightly around multiples of $π/8$! This happens because the entries of $Y_{u,d}$ naturally define a "Yukawa triangle", in most cases identical to the unitarity triangle at leading order in small flavor parameters. Most interestingly, these two triangles are not the same beyond leading order, yielding precise predictions for $(α, β, γ)$ with calculable deviation from $(π/2, π/8, 3 π/8)$, which can be decisively excluded or strongly confirmed by the next generation of experimental measurements of the angles. The 9-link textures are sparse and their determinants are naturally real, which taken together with spontaneous CP violation can resolve the strong CP problem.

</details>


### [216] [The inseparable three and four tops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27323)
*Gauthier Durieux,Hesham El Faham,Rikkert Frederix,Davide Pagani,Marco Zaro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LHC观测中四顶夸克产生（tttt）与三顶夸克伴随W玻色子产生（tttW）的不可分离简并问题，计算了完整的次领头阶（NLO）修正，并引入了一种新颖的窗口移除方案（window-removal prescription），实现了对tttW+tttt联合过程在NLO精度下的一致预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC合作组在测量四顶夸克产生时发现其与三顶夸克产生存在显著简并，而已有的非共振计算中的共振移除方法在离壳顶夸克极限下会破坏规范不变性并产生幺正性违反，或涉及动量重置的高度任意性，因此需要新的解决途径。

Method: 计算了tttW伴随产生的完整NLO修正（包含所有QCD和电弱（EW）修正）；引入一种新颖的窗口移除方案（window-removal prescription），在NLO精度下对不可分离的tttW+tttt过程产生一致预测；同时研究了额外硬中心b喷注的理想化否决（veto）条件。

Result: 联合预测的包容性产率比纯壳上tttt产生高10%以上；理想化b-喷注否决可抑制共振tttt图及其与非共振tttW图的干涉，定义相对纯净的tttW类信号区；形式上次领头耦合阶的数值在领头阶（LO）重要，但相应的次领头NLO修正大部分在包容性和微分层面相互抵消，因此完整NLO预测可由前三个LO耦合阶加领头QCD NLO修正很好地近似。

Conclusion: 在实验比较中应采用联合预测而非纯壳上tttt预测，因为实践中无法隔离纯壳tttt分量；该联合预测为实验选择提供了更准确的基准。

Abstract: In measurements of four-top-quark production ($tttt$), LHC collaborations observe a significant degeneracy with three-top-quark production. We compute the dominant three-top-production mode, namely associated production with a $W$ boson ($tttW$), at complete next-to-leading order (NLO), including all possible QCD and electroweak (EW) corrections. Beyond leading order (LO), $tttW$ production with the radiation of an additional $b$-flavoured quark contributes to the same final state as $tttt$ production with a $t \to bW$ decay. Away from the on-shell top-quark limit, the usual overlap removal of resonant contributions in the non-resonant computation either breaks gauge invariance and generates unitarity violation or involves a significant arbitrariness in the required reshuffling of momenta. To overcome these issues, we introduce a novel window-removal prescription that produces consistent predictions for the inseparable $tttW+tttt$ process, with both components described at NLO accuracy. We argue that such a joint prediction should be used in comparisons with experimental selections targeting $tttt$ production, since the on-shell $tttt$ component can not be isolated in practice. Such a joint prediction has an inclusive rate more than 10% higher than the purely on-shell $tttt$ one. We also study an idealised veto on additional hard and central $b$-jet radiation, which suppresses the contributions of resonant $tttt$ diagrams as well as their interference with non-resonant $tttW$ ones and therefore defines a relatively pure $tttW$-like signal region. Formally subleading coupling orders are numerically important at LO, while the corresponding subleading NLO corrections largely cancel both inclusively and differentially. Consequently, the complete-NLO prediction is well approximated by retaining the first three LO coupling orders together with the leading QCD NLO correction.

</details>


### [217] [Precise predictions for double Higgs production in association with a vector boson in Effective Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27334)
*Ramona Gröber,Michał Ryczkowski,Gioia Sacchi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了夸克诱导的Vhh（V=W±,Z）伴随产生过程在SMEFT（截断至维度六）和HEFT（手征展开领头阶）框架下的NNLO QCD修正结果，提供了13.6和14.0 TeV下的包含尺度、PDF和α_s不确定性的预言，并发现W±hh产生的QCD修正几乎与EFT依赖因子化，而Zhh产生因胶子诱导贡献而对EFT系数有更强的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子对（Higgs boson pair）伴随弱规范玻色子产生过程是探测新物理的有效探针，但此前缺乏完整的NNLO QCD预言，需要结合有效场论（EFT）框架提供高精度理论预测。该过程对希格斯自耦合和电弱规范耦合的灵敏度使其成为精确检验标准模型的重要渠道。

Method: 计算基于夸克诱导产生过程的因子化结构：将过程分解为离壳矢量玻色子的Drell-Yan产生及其随后衰变为Vhh，在Zhh道中补充了在NNLO QCD首次进入的圈诱导gg→Zhh贡献。结果以数值系数形式给出，允许对任意EFT参数在考虑范围内快速求值。

Result: 在质心能量√s=13.6和14.0 TeV下提供包含尺度、PDF和α_s不确定性的包含性预言。发现对于W±hh产生，QCD修正很大程度上与EFT依赖因子化，导致K因子近乎平坦；相比之下，Zhh产生由于胶子诱导分量的存在，对EFT系数表现出更强的依赖性。

Conclusion: W±hh和Zhh两个道在EFT敏感性上存在显著差异，Zhh道中的胶子诱导贡献增强了其对新物理EFT系数的依赖，使其成为更有鉴别力的探针。

Abstract: We present next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) QCD predictions for Higgs boson pair production in association with a weak gauge boson, $pp \to Vhh$ with $V=W^\pm,Z$, in effective field theory descriptions of new physics. We consider both the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), truncated at dimension six, and the Higgs Effective Field Theory (HEFT) at leading order in the chiral expansion. The calculation builds on the factorisation of the quark-induced production process into Drell-Yan production of an off-shell vector boson and its subsequent decay into $Vhh$, supplemented in the $Zhh$ channel by the loop-induced $gg \to Zhh$ contribution that first enters at NNLO QCD. We provide inclusive predictions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ and $14.0$ TeV, including scale, PDF, and $α_s$ uncertainties, and express the results in terms of numerical coefficients that allow fast evaluations for arbitrary EFT parameters in the considered ranges. We find that, for $W^\pm hh$ production, QCD corrections largely factorise from the EFT dependence, leading to almost flat $K$-factors. In contrast, $Zhh$ production shows a stronger dependence on the EFT coefficients because of the gluon-induced component.

</details>


### [218] [Photon-Tagged Energy Flow in Inclusive Endpoint $B$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27367)
*Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种 B 衰变标记能量关联器（B-decay tagged energy correlator, BTEC），用于解析包含性 B 衰变末态区域中能量流的角结构，聚焦于 \overline B\to X_s\gamma 的直接光子贡献。在领头幂次和天然共线角尺度下，推导出涉及标准硬系数、B 介子形状函数和新的测量夸克喷注函数（measured quark jet function）的因子化关系，未引入新的领头幂次非微扰函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的包含性 B 衰变研究主要关注光子能谱，缺乏对末态反冲喷注角结构的敏感度。BTEC 旨在补充这一角结构信息，并为检验领头幂次端点因子化的自洽性以及区分直接 O_7 幂次修正和分辨光子效应提供新的探针。

Method: 在领头幂次（leading power）和天然共线角尺度 τ∼s∼QΛ_QCD 下，推导出 BTEC 的因子化关系，涉及标准硬系数、B 介子形状函数和测量夸克喷注函数；在单圈精度下计算该测量喷注函数并验证其角积分可还原标准包含性夸克喷注函数。

Result: 在 Bosch–Lange–Neubert–Paz 中心参数集下，基准计算表明在固定角截断 τ_c=2–4 GeV² 下存在 3–16% 的迁移（migration）效应；利用独立约束的形状函数，角累积量可提供领头幂次端点因子化的闭合检验，并对直接 O_7 幂次修正和分辨光子效应敏感。

Conclusion: BTEC 为包含性反冲喷注的角结构提供了超出普通光子能谱的补充信息，在能量流分布存在差异时有望改善信号与本底的区分能力。

Abstract: We introduce a $B$-decay tagged energy correlator (BTEC) to resolve the angular structure of energy flow in inclusive $B$ decays in the endpoint region, focusing on the direct-photon contribution to $\overline B\to X_sγ$. At leading power and at the natural collinear angular scale $τ\sim s\sim QΛ_{\rm QCD}$, we derive a factorization relation involving the standard hard coefficient and $B$-meson shape function together with a new measured quark jet function; no new leading-power nonperturbative function is introduced. We calculate the measured jet function at one-loop accuracy and verify that its angular integral reproduces the standard inclusive quark jet function. For the central Bosch--Lange--Neubert--Paz parameter set, an illustrative benchmark gives $3 - 16\%$ migration outside the fixed angular cuts $τ_c=2 - 4~{\rm GeV}^2$. With an independently constrained shape function, the angular cumulatives provide a closure test of leading-power endpoint factorization and are sensitive to direct-$O_7$ power corrections and resolved-photon effects. The BTEC thereby adds information on the angular structure of the inclusive recoil jet beyond the ordinary photon spectrum and may improve signal--background discrimination when their energy-flow profiles differ.

</details>


### [219] [Magnetic dipole moments as probes of doubly-bottom molecular pentaquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27653)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD光锥求和规则（QCD light-cone sum rules）结合光子分布振幅（photon distribution amplitudes），计算了双底五夸克（doubly-bottom pentaquark）分子态在 BΣb、BΣb* 和 B*Σb 构型中的磁偶极矩（magnetic dipole moments），发现其大小对内部自旋结构和夸克组成高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双底五夸克态是强子物理中的前沿研究对象，其内部结构（尤其是分子态构型）尚不明确，磁偶极矩作为一种敏感的电磁观测量，可用于区分不同内部自旋结构，并为未来实验提供可检验的预言。

Method: 采用QCD光锥求和规则（QCD light-cone sum rules，LCSR）框架，引入光子分布振幅（photon distribution amplitudes），对 J^P=½⁻ 和 ¾⁻ 的双底五夸克态在 BΣb、BΣb* 与 B*Σb 三种分子构型下的磁偶极矩进行计算。

Result: 计算结果表明：在 BΣb 构型中，轻夸克扇区（light-quark sector）贡献占主导；在 BΣb* 构型中，磁矩主要由重底夸克（heavy bottom quark）决定；而在 B*Σb 分子态中，轻、重扇区贡献相加，导致磁偶极矩显著增强。

Conclusion: 磁偶极矩对分子型双底五夸克态的内部结构具有灵敏的探测能力，其计算结果为未来的实验研究提供了可测试的理论预言。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetic dipole moments of doubly-bottom pentaquark states with spin-parities $J^P=\tfrac{1}{2}^-$ and $\tfrac{3}{2}^-$, interpreted as hadronic molecules in the $BΣ_b$, $BΣ_b^{*}$, and $B^{*}Σ_b$ configurations. The analysis is performed within the framework of QCD light-cone sum rules employing photon distribution amplitudes. Our results demonstrate a strong sensitivity of the magnetic dipole moments to the internal spin structure and quark composition of the states. In particular, the light-quark sector provides the dominant contribution in the $BΣ_b$ configuration, while the magnetic moment of $BΣ_b^{*}$ is largely governed by the heavy bottom quark. For the $B^{*}Σ_b$ molecular state, both light and heavy sectors contribute constructively, leading to a significantly enhanced magnetic dipole moment. These findings indicate that magnetic dipole moments constitute a sensitive probe of the internal structure of molecular-type doubly-bottom pentaquarks and provide testable predictions for future experimental studies.

</details>


### [220] [The Conformal Origin of the Lepton Flavor Mixing Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19454)
*Cora Aigle,Alex Jourjine*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用味旋理论（flavor spin theory）的形式体系，以一种新颖方式重新推导了TM1轻子味混合因子的分解，基于U_PMNS矩阵第一行元素的一个此前未报道的近似恒等式。所得的混合矩阵是无质量标准模型在电弱相变前共形对称性的直接结果。论文还推导出CP破坏相位与三个混合角之间的关系，并建议TM1分解应采用(19/12)π作为CP破坏相位值，使得TM1的全部四个参数可用根式（radicals）表达，有助于区分混合参数的经典值与量子修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在味旋理论框架下，需要一种更简洁、更自然的推导方式来理解TM1轻子味混合因子的分解结构，并探索其与标准模型基本对称性之间的深层联系。同时，推导CP破坏相位与混合角间的解析关系，有助于厘清混合参数的经典来源与量子修正的分离问题，进而阐明质量生成机制。

Method: 利用味旋理论的形式体系重新推导TM1混合因子分解，核心基于一个此前未报道的涉及U_PMNS第一行元素的近似恒等式。推导出CP破坏相位与三个混合角之间的解析关系，并将该关系应用于实验数据，以确定CP破坏相位的具体取值。

Result: TM1混合矩阵被证明是无质量标准模型在电弱相变前共形对称性（conformal symmetry）的直接结果；CP破坏相位被确定为(19/12)π，在此取值下TM1的全部四个参数均可用根式形式表达。

Conclusion: 该结果有望在味旋理论框架下帮助分离混合参数的经典值与量子修正，进而有助于阐明质量生成机制（mass generation mechanism）。

Abstract: Using the formalism of the flavor spin theory, we re-derive the TM1 lepton flavor mixing factor decomposition in a novel way, using a previously unreported near identity involving the elements of the first row of U_PMNS. The resulting mixing matrix is a direct consequence of the conformal symmetry of the massless SM before the EW phase transition. In addition to the previously reported results, we derive a relation between the CP violating phase and the three mixing angles. Application of the relation to the experimental data data suggests the TM1 decomposition should use (19/12)Pi as the value for the CP violating phase. With this assumption all four parameters of TM1 may be expressed in radicals, which might help in the separation of the classical values of the mixing parameters from their quantum corrections in the framework of the flavor spin theories. This in turn could help in clarification of the mass generation mechanism.

</details>


### [221] [A Material Frame: Hard Recoils from Slow Force Carriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27253)
*Francesco Serra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将标准模型（Standard Model）修改为具有物理纵向光子分量、传播速度c_L远小于1的理论。该修改破坏规范不变性与洛伦兹对称性，但没有引入新场或新能标；理论具有光滑的c_L→0极限，可恢复普通规范理论预言。暗物质探测器给出的强约束为c_L≲10^{-60}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在标准模型中引入一个物理的、低速传播的纵向光子分量所产生的理论后果，检验该修改对规范不变性和洛伦兹对称性的影响，并评估其与实验观测的相容性。

Method: 通过将光子纵向分量设为物理可传播的慢模（速度c_L远小于光速），破坏规范不变性和洛伦兹对称性，定义优先参考系；借助伽利略高阶形式对称性（Galilean higher-form symmetry）保护光子质量，并分析带电粒子在该框架下的切连科夫辐射（Cherenkov radiation）与反冲效应，利用暗物质探测器对核反冲的灵敏度导出约束。

Result: 理论具有光滑的c_L→0极限，新模与标准物质退耦并恢复普通规范理论预言。带电粒子会切连科夫辐射这些慢模并遭受强烈反冲；反冲率随c_L减小而下降，但动量转移保持粒子动量量级。暗物质探测器给出c_L≲10^{-60}的约束，比速度差约束强数十个数量级。

Conclusion: 在如此极端的约束下，c_L更应理解为反冲率因子而非物理上相关的速度。慢模几乎在空间固定，使得参考系等效于能吸收带电粒子动量的物质介质。

Abstract: We modify the Standard Model by making the longitudinal component of the photon physical, propagating with small speed $c_L \ll 1$. This modification breaks gauge invariance and Lorentz symmetry, defining a preferred reference frame. No new fields or scales are introduced, and the photon mass is protected by a Galilean higher-form symmetry. The theory has a smooth $c_L\to0$ limit: the new modes decouple from standard matter and ordinary gauge theory predictions are recovered. Experimental constraints are severe. Charged particles Cherenkov-emit these slow modes, experiencing hard recoils against the preferred frame. The recoil rate decreases with $c_L$, but the momentum transfer remains of the order of the particle's momentum. Dark matter detectors, sensitive to keV-scale nuclear recoils, imply an order-of-magnitude bound $c_L \lesssim 10^{-60}$, tens of orders of magnitude stronger than speed-difference bounds. At such values, $c_L$ is better understood as a recoil-rate factor rather than a physically relevant speed. The slow modes, practically fixed in space, make the reference frame a material medium capable of absorbing momentum from charged particles.

</details>


### [222] [The Dark Dimension meets the Axiverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27314)
*Kevin Langhoff,Maria Ramos,Mario Reig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暗维度（dark dimension）场景与轴子景观（axiverse）相结合的宇宙学后果。研究表明，在规范扇区实现于膜（branes）上时，闭弦轴子（closed string axions）的Kaluza-Klein激发塔与引力子激发塔共同在暗维度中传播，由此产生两类效应：轴子塔与标准模型等离子体的相互作用可显著改变塔态丰度的冻结产生（freeze-in production），从而在特定参数区间内提供全部暗物质；同时不同塔之间的碎裂与能量再分配导致可见能量被稀释N倍，有助于规避强宇宙学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗维度场景中引力子KK塔的宇宙学后果已被广泛研究，但闭弦轴子塔的贡献尚未被系统考察。作者希望理解轴子塔的存在如何修改暗维度暗物质的产生机制，以及如何解释最简单的暗维度模型为何会遭受强宇宙学约束而被排除。

Method: 作者将暗维度场景与轴子景观结合，分析膜上规范扇区产生闭弦轴子KK塔的物理后果。他们计算冻结产生过程对轴子衰变常数（axion decay constant）和再加热温度（reheating temperature）的依赖关系，并建立不同塔之间碎裂与能量再分配的连续近似（continuum approach），将问题简化为一维积分求积（quadrature）以辅助数值计算。

Result: 冻结产生到引力子塔与轴子塔的过程可在轴子衰变常数$10^{12}\,\text{GeV}\lesssim f_a\lesssim 10^{16}\,\text{GeV}$且再加热温度$5\,\text{MeV}\lesssim T_{\rm RH}\lesssim 10\,\text{GeV}$区间内提供全部暗物质。当存在$N\gg1$个轴子塔时，任何衰变塔向标准模型注入的可见能量被稀释因子$N$所抑制，从而规避强宇宙学约束。

Conclusion: 轴子塔的存在为暗维度暗物质提供了新的产生通道，即经由冻结产生机制；其对标准模型的能量注入稀释效应为最简暗维度模型受约束的问题提供了一个简单实现方案。作者发展连续近似方法处理塔碎裂问题，为后续数值研究提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We explore the cosmological implications of combining dark dimension scenarios with an axiverse. If gauge sectors are realized on branes, towers of Kaluza-Klein (KK) excitations of closed string axions can propagate through the dark dimension in addition to the tower of graviton excitations. This modifies cosmology in two ways. First, if any of these axion towers interact with the standard model (SM) plasma, they can significantly alter the freeze-in production of the cosmological abundance of tower states. Freeze-in to graviton and axion towers can provide all of dark matter (DM) for an axion decay constant in $10^{12} \text{ GeV }\lesssim f_a\lesssim 10^{16}\,\text{ GeV }$ and reheating temperatures $5\text{ MeV }\lesssim T_{\rm RH} \lesssim O(1)\,\text{ GeV }$. Second, different towers fragment into each other and redistribute energy; each tower's fraction of energy at late times is fixed by their interactions. If there are $N\gg1$ axion towers, the energy visibly injected into the SM by any decaying tower is diluted by a factor of $N$. This suppression offers a simple realization of how dark dimension dark matter can avoid strong cosmological constraints which rule out the simplest models. In the process of this exploration we develop a continuum approach to evaluating tower fragmentation which offers insight and aids numerical calculations by reducing the problem to quadrature.

</details>


### [223] [LISA Reconstruction Landscape for Metastable Cosmic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28592)
*Satyabrata Datta,Rome Samanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用激光干涉仪空间天线（LISA）从亚稳态宇宙弦（metastable cosmic strings）重构随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds）。在真空隧穿基准下，网络通过世界面上的单极子对零温成核（zero-temperature nucleation）衰变，有限寿命在南带宽内留下红外尾部（infrared tail）和向稳定弦状高频平台（high-frequency plateau）的过渡。通过贝叶斯分析（Bayesian analysis）在参数空间 (Gμ, κ_CS) 中映射可探测性、不确定性、相关性，结果表明重构受寿命依赖的光谱特征支配，可区分背景探测与参数重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在明确LISA不仅能够探测亚稳态宇宙弦产生的随机引力波背景，还能如何进一步区分"探测背景"与"重构参数"两个层次，并考察网络寿命的可探测性。

Method: 采用合成LISA数据，包含仪器噪声和未解析的天体物理前景（astrophysical foregrounds），在参数空间 (Gμ, κ_CS) 中进行贝叶斯分析，并对比两种银河前景模板（灵活模板与简化tanh模板）以评估建模敏感性。

Result: 当LISA采样到尾部与平台之间的过渡区时，数据包含振幅与形状信息，可恢复弦张力（string tension）和亚稳态尺度，后验虽可能因振幅—寿命权衡而相关，但占据小参数区域；无特征光谱仅约束有限参数组合或由先验主导；高信噪比平台状光谱即使过渡不显著，仍可通过残余寿命依赖保留对 κ_CS 的部分敏感性。

Conclusion: 结果表明LISA可在超越探测的层面上，探究底层弦网络的寿命参数，为亚稳态宇宙弦引力波信号的可识别性提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: We study reconstruction of stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds from metastable cosmic strings with the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). In the vacuum-tunneling benchmark, the network decays via zero-temperature nucleation of monopole pairs on the string worldsheet. Initially following cosmic-string scaling, loop production is suppressed after a decay time linked to efficient loop breaking and network collapse. This finite lifetime imprints an infrared tail and a transition toward a stable-string-like high-frequency plateau. Using synthetic LISA data with instrumental noise and unresolved astrophysical foregrounds, we perform Bayesian analysis in the $(Gμ,κ_{\rm CS})$ parameter space. We map detectability, uncertainties, correlations, and localization to distinguish background detection from parameter reconstruction. Reconstruction is governed by lifetime-dependent spectral features in the LISA band. When LISA samples the transition between tail and plateau, data contain amplitude and shape information, enabling recovery of string tension and metastability scale. Posteriors may remain correlated, reflecting an amplitude--lifetime trade-off, yet occupy a small parameter region. By contrast, featureless spectra constrain only limited parameter combinations or become prior dominated. High-SNR plateau-like spectra can retain partial sensitivity to $κ_{\rm CS}$ through residual lifetime dependence even when the transition is not prominent. Finally, we assess sensitivity to Galactic foreground modeling by comparing a flexible template with a reduced tanh template. Our results show where LISA can move beyond detection to probe the lifetime of the underlying string network.

</details>


### [224] [Systematic derivation of the Boltzmann equation for electroweak baryogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28321)
*Motoi Endo,Yushi Mura,Tenta Tsuji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子场论（quantum field theory）框架下推导了电弱重子生成（electroweak baryogenesis）的玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）。通过逐阶代数求解Kadanoff-Baym方程，纳入CP破坏的泡壁背景场梯度效应，并提取描述等离子体中准粒子态的壳上解，最终推导出味对角准粒子模的玻尔兹曼方程。该推导推广了此前的场论处理，保留了自能修正，在忽略这些修正时可回归已知的玻尔兹曼方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱重子生成中的玻尔兹曼方程此前缺乏系统且透明的场论推导框架，尤其是对自能修正（self-energy corrections）的处理尚不完整。作者旨在发展一种适用于多味玻色子与费米子系统的统一推导方法，使推导过程更透明、适用范围更广。

Method: 作者首先在CP破坏泡壁背景场中逐阶（order by order）代数求解Kadanoff-Baym方程，纳入背景场的梯度效应；随后应用适用于电弱重子生成的近似与假设，提取解的壳上（on-shell）部分，用以描述等离子体中传播的准粒子态；最后基于这些壳上解，在忽略描述不同味模之间量子相干性的非对角分量后，推导味对角准粒子模的玻尔兹曼方程。

Result: 推导出的玻尔兹曼方程保留了自能修正，推广了以往的场论处理方法；当忽略这些自能修正时，所得结果与此前已知的玻尔兹曼方程一致，验证了推导的正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了系统性且透明的推导途径，适用于多味玻色子（bosonic）和费米子（fermionic）系统的电弱重子生成动力学描述。

Abstract: We derive the Boltzmann equation for electroweak baryogenesis within a field-theoretical framework. We first algebraically solve the Kadanoff--Baym equations by incorporating the effects of gradients of the CP-violating bubble-wall background order by order. We then apply approximations and assumptions appropriate for electroweak baryogenesis and identify the on-shell parts of the solutions, which describe quasiparticle states propagating in a plasma. Using these on-shell solutions, we derive the Boltzmann equations for flavor-diagonal quasiparticle modes, while neglecting the off-diagonal components describing quantum coherence between different flavor modes. Our derivation generalizes previous field-theoretical treatments by retaining self-energy corrections, and our results are consistent with the known Boltzmann equations when these corrections are neglected. This framework provides a systematic and transparent derivation applicable to multi-flavor bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [225] [Why 4D? Spontaneous Dimensional Selection from Gauge Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28615)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，杨-米尔斯（Yang–Mills）耦合在四维以下具有相关（relevant）性、在四维为边缘（marginal）性、在四维以上为无关（irrelevant）性，这种临界性（criticality）可能导致四维宏观维度的自然选择。作者通过一个具体的竞赛跑道模型（racetrack model），在不预设非紧致四维时空的前提下，展示四维时空可通过规范临界性诱导的超对称破缺（supersymmetry breaking）和超引力（supergravity）效应经半径子稳定化（radion stabilization）自发实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于一个深刻的观测：杨-米尔斯耦合的维度依赖性质（在四维以下相关、四维边缘、四维以上无关）暗示四维可能具有某种特殊性，这或许可以解释为何我们观测到的宏观时空恰好是四维的。

Method: 作者采取的概念证明（proof of principle）方法如下：构建一个在高维额外维度（extra-dimensional）场景中常见的竞赛跑道模型，但关键突破在于不预设非紧致（noncompact）四维时空仅作为背景。在该可控模型中，通过规范临界性（gauge criticality）诱导超对称破缺，并辅以超引力效应的作用，实现半径子的稳定化（radion stabilization），从而自发地产生四维时空。

Result: 作者在无需预先指定四维时空的条件下，于一个良好可控（well-controlled）的模型框架内证明：四维时空可以自发实现，其机制是规范临界性诱导的超对称破缺结合超引力效应共同驱动半径子的稳定化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明杨-米尔斯耦合的临界性可能成为宏观时空维度选择（dimension selection）的动力学根源，为解释四维时空为何在宏观尺度上被优选提供了一个具有原理验证意义的理论方案，并为额外维度场景中的维度确定问题提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Noting that the Yang--Mills coupling is relevant below four dimensions, marginal in four dimensions, and irrelevant above four dimensions, I propose that this criticality can lead to the selection of four macroscopic dimensions. As a proof of principle, I present a concrete racetrack model of the type commonly studied in extra-dimensional scenarios, but without assuming a noncompact 4D spacetime. I find, within a well-controlled model without specifying the 4D, that 4D spacetime is spontaneously realized through radion stabilization via gauge-criticality-induced supersymmetry breaking and supergravity effects.

</details>
